// This is a really long function. LOTS OF IMPORTANT STUFF GOES HERE.
// For modded characters, make sure to comment before/inside with tons of trailing //@@@...@@@//.

//_ChrNo for the character #

function Battle_Std::GetStdMoveTable( _ChrNo=0, ) {

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
//Create MV data for everyone
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
local CHRMAX = 50; // Important Character Count for modded ones
local chrparam = {};

chrparam.Param <- {}; //Frame params
chrparam.Get <- function( tbl={} ) //get the chara ID no
{
	local checktype = tbl.type;
	local checkchrnum = tbl.chrnum;
	
	if( checktype in Param )
	{
		local ret = Param[checktype][checkchrnum];
		btl_debPrint_CharacterMake("y’è‹`zchrparam:"+checktype+" chr:"+checkchrnum+" -> "+ret);
		return ret;
	}
	else
	{
		btl_debError_CharacterMake("yŒxzchrparam‚Å–¢’è‹`:"+checktype+" chr:"+checkchrnum);
		return 0;
	}
}
	
{	//Dash "stiffness", when this time (F) has elapsed, it will transition from DashWait to Dash_F. Pattern doesn't change
	local type = "ƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…‚Å‚©‚©‚èd’¼‚e";
	chrparam.Param[type] <- array(CHRMAX, 4);
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Hyd] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Lin] = 3;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Wal] = 8;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Car] = 7;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Ori] = 5;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Gor] = 6;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Mer] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Vat] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Set] = 6;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Yuz] = 6;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Hil] = 6;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Elt] = 3;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Nan] = 3;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Bya] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Aka] = 5;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Cha] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Wag] = 5;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Enk] = 4;

	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Udu] = 5;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Ogr] = 6;

}

{	// Dash Unstoppable F
// Time from when the dash hold is released until you can stop
// How long you cannot move from when you can act, to when you can stop
// Useless (dashblocking exists) unless you have FrameID100 and an Mv that can stop
// If this is long, you won't be able to make small movements
// If it's too short, it will be a little difficult to perform a dash light attack with inertia
// If you reach the stopping motion, it will reset (you can guard), so it's OK if it's longer than "Dash Unblockable F"
	local type = "ƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…’â~•s”\‚e";
	chrparam.Param[type] <- array(CHRMAX, 4);
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Hyd] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Lin] = 6;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Wal] = -1; //no suspension
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Car] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Ori] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Gor] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Mer] = 8;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Vat] = -1; //no suspension
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Set] = 3; //Bad dashblock!
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Yuz] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Hil] = 12;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Elt] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Nan] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Bya] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Aka] = -1; //no suspension
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Cha] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Wag] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Enk] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Lnd] = 5;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Mik] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Pho] = 5;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Kag] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Tsu] = 4;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Kuo] = 7;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Udu] = 9;
	chrparam.Param[type][Def_ChrNo_Ogr] = 4;

local val_GroundRecoverEffectType = chrparam.Get( { type="’nãó‚¯g’…’nƒGƒtƒFƒNƒgƒ^ƒCƒv", chrnum=_ChrNo } ); 
local callLandEffect = function() : (val_GroundRecoverEffectType)
{
	switch( val_GroundRecoverEffectType )
	{
	case 1: // ƒƒŒƒ“‚Æ‚©d‚¢
		BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat=290 } );
		BSound.SE_Play( { type=_SeType_Player, num=25 } );
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { time=20, type=0 } );
		break;
	default: // ’Êí
		BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat=20 } );
		break;
	}
}

	{ WaitPAT = 51, timeline = [ { delay=186, SE=101, time=186 }, ], }, // ï¿½uï¿½×–ï¿½ï¿½ï¿½ï¿½ï¿½ï¿½ï¿½È‚ï¿½cï¿½Ğ•tï¿½ï¿½ï¿½ï¿½Ü‚Å‚ï¿½ï¿½Iï¿½v
	{ WaitPAT = 50, timeline = [ { delay=124, SE=102, time=124 }, ], }, // ï¿½uï¿½cï¿½cï¿½ï¿½ï¿½Ì—Í‚ÅIï¿½ï¿½ç‚¹ï¿½Ä‚ï¿½ï¿½Iï¿½v
	{ WaitPAT = 51, timeline = [ { delay=146, SE=103, time=146 }, ], }, // ï¿½uï¿½Iï¿½ï¿½ï¿½Ì—Ícï¿½cï¿½ï¿½ï¿½ï¿½ï¿½Ä‚ï¿½ï¿½Iï¿½v

//CommonStandbyParams‚ÌƒGƒ‰[ƒ`ƒFƒbƒNˆ—
//time‚æ‚è‚à‹É’[‚Édelay‚ª­‚È‚¢‚Æš–‹‚ª‚©‚Ô‚Á‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚Ì‚Å‚»‚ê‚ğ’²‚×‚é
//time‚Æparam‚Ì·‚à’²‚×‚é
if( 0 && Def_Dbg_LocalDebugMode ){
	local limit_sa = 0;//10;
	local chr_mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
	foreach( slot, val in CommonStandbyParams )
	{
		//slot = P0ƒLƒƒƒ‰”Ô†
		//
		//val = ”z—ñ?
		if( typeof val == "array" )
		{
			_dpn("ƒLƒƒƒ‰:"+slot+" chr_mvs:"+chr_mvs.CharaNo);
			local check_voice_len = ( slot == chr_mvs.CharaNo)? 1 : 0; // ©ƒLƒƒƒ‰‚µ‚©æ“¾‚Å‚«‚È‚¢‚ñ‚Å‚Ë
			
			for( local i=0; i<val.len(); i++ )
			{
				if( "timeline" in val[i] )
				{
					local tl = val[i].timeline;
					_dpn("TL["+i+"]");
					if( typeof tl != "array" ) break;
					
					local time = [0,0]; // ‰¹ºÄ¶ŠÔ
					local delay = [0,0]; // ƒAƒjƒƒfƒBƒŒƒCŠÔ
					for( local j=0; j<tl.len(); j++ )
					{
						local id = ( "TAG" in tl[j] )? tl[j].TAG : 0;
						if( "delay" in tl[j] ) delay[id] += tl[j].delay;
						if( "time" in tl[j] ) time[id] += tl[j].time;
						local se = ( "SE" in tl[j] )? tl[j].SE : 0;
						
						if( check_voice_len && se )
						{
							local param = BSound.SE_GetUserParam( { type=_SeType_Player, num=se, } );//‰¹º‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€”‚ğæ“¾
							local file_size = BSound.SE_GetBufferSize( { type=_SeType_Player, num=se } );
							local calc_frame = ((file_size / (48 * 2))*60)/1000;
							
							_dpn("se:"+se+" param:"+param+" -> "+calc_frame);
							
							if( abs(param-calc_frame) > 1 )
							{
								_dpn("ƒGƒ‰[[[");
							}
						}
					}

					//HHHpl1‚Á‚Ä‚ ‚éH
					for( local pl=0; pl<2; pl++ )
					{
						local sa = time[pl]+limit_sa - delay[pl];
						if( time[pl] > 0 && sa > 0 )
						{
							_dpn(" š"+pl+1+"PŠÔ:"+delay[pl]+" -> "+time[pl]+" ŠÔ•s‘«:"+sa );
						}
					}
				}
				if( "duet_timeline" in val[i] )
				{
					local tl = val[i].duet_timeline;
					local enemy_num = val[i].Enemy; // ‚È‚º‚©”z—ñ
					if( typeof enemy_num == "array" ) enemy_num = enemy_num[0]; // ‚Ä‚«‚Æ‚¤
					local check_enemy_voice_len = ( enemy_num == chr_mvs.CharaNo)? 1 : 0; // ©ƒLƒƒƒ‰‚µ‚©æ“¾‚Å‚«‚È‚¢‚ñ‚Å‚Ë
					
					_dpn("DTL["+i+"]");
					if( typeof tl != "array" ) break;
					
					local time = [0,0]; // ‰¹ºÄ¶ŠÔ
					local delay = [0,0]; // ƒAƒjƒƒfƒBƒŒƒCŠÔ
					for( local j=0; j<tl.len(); j++ )
					{
						local id = ( "PL" in tl[j] )? tl[j].PL : 0;
						if( "delay" in tl[j] ) delay[id] += tl[j].delay;
						if( "time" in tl[j] ) time[id] += tl[j].time;

						local time = ( "time" in tl[j] )? tl[j].time : 0;
						local delay = ( "delay" in tl[j] )? tl[j].delay : 0;
						if( time == 0 )
						{
							time = delay;
						}
						
						local se = ( "SE" in tl[j] )? tl[j].SE : 0;
						
						if( check_voice_len && se && id == 0 )
						{
							local param = BSound.SE_GetUserParam( { type=_SeType_Player, num=se, } );//‰¹º‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€”‚ğæ“¾
							local file_size = BSound.SE_GetBufferSize( { type=_SeType_Player, num=se } );
							local calc_frame = ((file_size / (48 * 2))*60)/1000;
							
							_dpn("se:"+se+" param:"+param+" -> "+calc_frame+" time:"+time);
							
							if( abs(param-calc_frame) > 1 )
							{
								_dpn("ššššššššššššššššššššƒGƒ‰[[[1");
							}
							if( abs(time-param) > 1 )
							{
								_dpn("ššššššššššššššššššššƒGƒ‰[[[2");
							}
						}
						if( check_enemy_voice_len && id == 1 && se )
						{
							local param = BSound.SE_GetUserParam( { type=_SeType_Player, num=se, } );//‰¹º‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€”‚ğæ“¾
							local file_size = BSound.SE_GetBufferSize( { type=_SeType_Player, num=se } );
							local calc_frame = ((file_size / (48 * 2))*60)/1000;
							
							_dpn("XX se:"+se+" param:"+param+" -> "+calc_frame+" time:"+time);
							
							if( abs(param-calc_frame) > 1 )
							{
								_dpn("šššššššššššššššššššššƒGƒ‰[[[3");
							}
							if( abs(time-param) > 1 )
							{
								_dpn("ššššššššššššššššššššƒGƒ‰[[[4");
							}							
						}
					}
					for( local pl=0; pl<2; pl++ )
					{
						local sa = time[pl]+limit_sa - delay[pl];
						if( time[pl] > 0 && sa > 0 )
						{
							_dpn(" š"+pl+1+"PŠÔ:"+delay[pl]+" -> "+time[pl]+" ŠÔ•s‘«:"+sa );
						}
					}				
				}
			}
			_dpn("");
		}
		else
		{
			// –¢’è‹`ƒLƒƒƒ‰‚¾‚Æ0‚ª“ü‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚Å‚±‚±‚É‚­‚é
			// _dpn("ƒ^ƒCƒv:"+typeof val+" : "+slot+" -> "+val );
		}
	}
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ƒQ[ƒ€‘S‘Ì‚ÌŠ„‚è‚İŠÖ”
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
//function Std_MoveTable::GeneralInterrupt( info )
Std_MoveTable.GeneralInterrupt <- function( info )
{
	// info ‚Í BMvTbl::MvInterruptInfo ƒNƒ‰ƒX
	switch( info.type ) // type‚Å•ªŠò
	{
	case _InterruptType_Ko_Atk: // KO‚µ‚½
		//print( "\n_InterruptType_Ko_Atk" );
		
		Battle_Std.GS_AddFlag( def_PP_GS_KoAtk ); // ‰½‚©‚µ‚ç‚ÅKO‚µ‚½‚Ì‚ğ‹L‰¯
		break;
	case _InterruptType_Ko_Def: // KO‚³‚ê‚½
		//print( "\n_InterruptType_Ko_Def" );
		//ƒXƒ[‚É‚·‚é
		BMvEff.Slowmotion_Set( { type=1, time=120, power=6666 } ); //KOƒXƒ[‚ğ‘¼‚Ì“o˜^‚ğ‘SÁ‹‚µ‚Ä’Ç‰Á
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { type=2, time=40, clear=1 } ); //‘¼‚Ì—h‚ç‚µ‚ğ~‚ß‚Ä‰æ–Ê—h‚ç‚µ

		//ƒLƒƒƒ‰•Ê‚É‚¢‚¶‚ê‚éƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg‚ğˆê‰ŒÄ‚Ô
		BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_KOImpact_Object" } );
		
		//ƒI[ƒ‰‚Æ‚©‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½‚çÁ‚·
		Battle_Std.PcAuraEffect_AllAuraEnd(); //‚j‚n‚³‚ê‚½ƒLƒƒƒ‰‚Í‚±‚Ìƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ÅƒI[ƒ‰‚ğÁ‚·
		
		BSound.SE_Play( { type=_SeType_Normal, num=71 } ); //‚j‚nŒø‰Ê‰¹
		break;
	case _InterruptType_Judge: // Judge
		// _dpn( "_InterruptType_Judge" );
		// 1P‚Æ2P‚Å‚»‚ê‚¼‚êˆ—‚ª“ü‚é
		// 
		
		if( Def_Sys_CelesVorpal )
		{
			Battle_Std.GS_DelFlag( def_PP_GS_CelecVorpal ); // ƒZƒŒƒXƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
			//‚±‚Ìˆ—‚ª‚È‚¢‚ÆCVPŒã‚ÉƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚µ‚ÄŸ‚ÌVP‚ªCVP‚ÉŒ©‚¦‚é
		}
		//ƒWƒƒƒbƒW‚ÌŒ‹‰Ê‚ğæ“¾‚µ‚ÄƒyƒJ‚ç‚¹‚é
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ‘€ìe‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		if( player.push() )
		{
			local grd_judge_status = BMvEff.GRD_GetJudgeResult();
			Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Increment(101,"—¼Òƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‰ñ”");
			
			if( grd_judge_status > 0 )
			{
				// ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚É‚È‚Á‚½‚±‚Æ‚ªŠm’è
				Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Increment(100,"ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‰ñ”");//©•ª‚ÌVP‰ñ”
				
				/*
				// ƒŠƒAƒ‹ƒ^ƒCƒ€‚Å‘ª’è‚µ‚æ‚¤‚Æ‚·‚é‚ÆA1P„2P‚Ì‡‚ÅŒv‘ª‚·‚é‚Ì‚Å‚¿‚å‚Á‚ÆƒYƒŒ‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚Ì‚ÅQl’ö“x‚É
				Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Calc( {
					title="ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹Šm—¦", 
					molecule=Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Count("ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‰ñ”"), denominator=Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Count("—¼Òƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‰ñ”")
				} );
				*/
			
				//ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹Šm—¦‰ñ”/‡”
				//‚±‚ÌŒvZ®‚¾‚ÆAƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv•ú’u‚Æ‚©‚Å—¼ÒVP‚É‚È‚è‘±‚¯‚é‚Æ‚·‚²‚¢‚±‚Æ‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤
				//—~‚µ‚¢’l‚Æ‚µ‚Ä‚ÍA‡’†‘Šè‚æ‚è‚Ç‚ê‚­‚ç‚¢VP‚ª‚Æ‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚©A‚Å100%‚ğ–Úw‚·Œ`‚É‚µ‚½‚¢
				//©•ª‚Ì‰ñ”/(©•ª‚Ì‰ñ”+‘Šè‚Ì‰ñ”)=Šm—¦
				//‚±‚ê‚ğ‡”•ª‹L‰¯‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚«AƒtƒBƒ‹ƒ^‚Å‚«‚éŒ`‚ª—Ç‚³‚»‚¤
				
				//—¼ÒVP‚Ì‚Æ‚«AŒvZ‚ª‡‚í‚È‚­‚È‚é‚Ì‚ÅAˆø‚«•ª‚¯‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚Í”íVP‰ñ”‚Í‰ÁZ‚µ‚È‚¢
				
				// ƒgƒŒƒ‚‚ÌƒŠƒXƒ^[ƒg‚ÉVP‚·‚é‚ÆFX‰‰o‚ª“ü‚Á‚Ä‚â‚©‚Ü‚µ‚¢‚Ì‚ÆAGRD‚à‘‚¦‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚Á‚Ä“s‡‚ªˆ«‚¢‚Ì‚Åˆ—‚ğ••‚¶‚ß‚éˆ—
				local is_restart_first = ( Def_Sys_NoPlayTrainingRestartUpsetEffect && Battle_Std.GS_CheckFlag( def_PP_GS_ImmediatelyAfterRestart ) )? 1 : 0;
				
				//d—l•ÏX‚ÅƒŠƒXƒ^[ƒg’¼Œã‚Ìó‹µ‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é–Í—l
				//–¾‚ç‚©‚ÈƒŠƒXƒ^[ƒg’¼Œã‚Á‚Û‚¢‚Æ‚±‚ë‚àŒŸ’m‚·‚é‚æ‚¤‚É
				if( Def_Sys_NoPlayTrainingRestartUpsetEffect )
				{
					local mvname = BMvTbl.GetMvName();
					local change_mvname = BMvTbl.ChangeMv_GetMvName();
					local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
					// _dpn((1+BMvTbl.GetPlayerSide())+"P: mvname:"+mvname+" change_mvname:"+change_mvname+" mvs.MvCount:"+mvs.MvCount);
					if( mvname == "Mv_StandbyNoMotionPass")
					{
						is_restart_first = 1;
					}
					if( change_mvname == "Mv_StandbyNoMotionPass" && mvs.MvCount <= 1 )
					{
						is_restart_first = 1;
					}
				}
				
				// _dpn("is_restart_first:"+is_restart_first);
				
				if( !is_restart_first )
				{
					
					Battle_Std.SetCharaFlash_GRDJudgeFlash();
					//W’†‚í‚Á‚©
					//‚í‚Á‚©ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‘¤‚©‚çƒI[ƒ‰‚ÍŒÄ‚Î‚ê‚Ü‚·iÀ•W‚Æƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ÌŠÖŒWãd•û‚È‚¢j
					local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=-200, datatype=1, start_pat=190, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
					if( eff.push() )
					{
						BMvEff.SetObjectFlags({ flags=_ObjFlags_ParentMove|_ObjFlags_NoGround|_ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll });
						
						eff.pop();
					}
					if( Def_Dbg_NoPlayUpsetSE == 0 ) BSound.SE_Play( { type=_SeType_Normal, num=75 } ); //GRDŸ—˜Œø‰Ê‰¹‚ğÄ¶
				}
				
				// ƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚Ìˆ—
				// GRD‚ª6ŒÂˆÈã‚¾‚Á‚½‚ç‘S•”©•ª‚Ì‚à‚Ì‚É‚·‚é
				// ƒgƒŒƒ‚‚Ìİ’è‚à‚ ‚é‚Ì‚ÅA‘S•”©•ª‚Ì‚à‚Ì‚É‚·‚é‚Ì‚ÍGRD‚ª6ŒÂˆÈã‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚¾‚¯
				if( Def_Sys_CelesVorpal )
				{
					local is_celes_vp = 0; // ƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹VP‚É‚È‚Á‚½‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
					local grd_stock = BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } );
					local enemy_grd_stock = 0;
					local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
					if( enemy.push() )
					{
						enemy_grd_stock = BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } );
						enemy.pop();
					}
					local grd_overenemy = ( grd_stock > enemy_grd_stock )? 1 : 0; // GRD‚ª‘Šè‚æ‚è‘½‚¢‚©
					
					local stage_st = BMvTbl.GetMvStageStatus().IsTrainingBattle(); // 1-Training  2-Tutorial  3-Mission
					local grd_countover = 0; // GRD‚ğ‘S•”©•ª‚Ì‚à‚Ì‚É‚·‚é‚©
					
					// GRD‚ª6ŒÂ‚æ‚è‘½‚¢‚Æ‚«‚ÍƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹VP‚¾‚µGRD‚ğ‘S•”©•ª‚Ì‚à‚Ì‚É‚·‚é
					// ‚½‚¾AƒgƒŒƒ‚‚ÌƒŠƒXƒ^[ƒg‚Í‚±‚Ìƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚Å‚Í0‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚ÅŒŸ’m•s‰Â
					// is_restart_first ‚Å’e‚¢‚½•û‚ªˆ—‚Í“Ç‚İ‚â‚·‚¢‚ªA•|‚¢‚Ì‚Åˆê’U‚±‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü
					// GRD6ˆÈã‚È‚çƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹‚É‚È‚é‚Í‚¸‚¾‚ªAƒgƒŒƒ‚‚ª—¼ÒVP‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚Í‰ñ”ğ‚·‚é@¦•Ğ‘¤VP‚Í‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü‚Ì•û‚ªg‚¢‚â‚·‚¢
					// ‚¨Œİ‚¢GRD6‚Å—¼ÒƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹‚É‚È‚é‚Ì‚à‚Ü‚¸‚¢‚Ì‚ÅœŠO
					// —¼ÒVP‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚ÍuGRD VORPALv‚Í‚¨Œİ‚¢‚Éo‚È‚¢d—l
					if( grd_stock >= 6 && grd_overenemy && BMvEff.TM_IsNormalVorpalGRDMode() != 110 ) // 1:©•ªVP 2:‘ŠèVP 101:©•ªVP&©•ª‘¤ 102:‘ŠèVP&‘Šè‘¤ 110:—¼‘¤VP
					{
						is_celes_vp = 1;
						grd_countover = 1;
					}
					
					if( BMvEff.TM_IsCelestialVorpalGRDMode() >= 100 ) // 1:©•ªCVP 2:‘ŠèCVP 101:©•ªCVP&©•ª‘¤ 102:‘ŠèCVP&‘Šè‘¤
					{
						// _dpn("¦ƒgƒŒƒ‚İ’è‚ÅƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹‰»");
						is_celes_vp = 1;
					}
					
					if( grd_countover && stage_st )
					{
						local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
						if( enemy.push() )
						{
							local enemyIsVorpal = ( BMvEff.GRD_GetJudgeResult() > 0 )? 1 : 0;
							enemy.pop();
							
							if( enemyIsVorpal )
							{
								// ƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹‚É‚·‚é‚ÆA©•ª‚ªƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹VP‚Å‘Šè‚ªVP‚Æ‚¢‚¤“ä‚Èó‘Ô‚É‚È‚é‚Ì‚Å‰ñ”ğ‚·‚é
								// _dpn("¦‚¨‚»‚ç‚­ƒgƒŒƒ‚‚Ì—¼ÒVP‚Ìİ’è‚È‚Ì‚ÅGRD’D‚¤‚Ì‚ğ‰ñ”ğ");
								grd_countover = 0;
							}
						}
					}
					
					if( grd_countover ) // GRD‚ª‚½‚­‚³‚ñ‚ ‚Á‚Ä’D‚¤‚Æ‚«@grd_overenemy‚Ìƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚Í•s—v‚¾‚ªˆê‰c‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­
					{
						local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
						if( enemy.push() )
						{
							if( grd_overenemy )
							{
								BMvEff.GRD_UseStock( { val=12 } );//‹ó‚É‚·‚éi‘S•”’D‚í‚ê‚é‚½‚ßj
							}
							enemy.pop();
							
							// GRD‚ğ‘S•”©•ª‚Ì‚à‚Ì‚É‚·‚é
							if( grd_overenemy )
							{
								BMvEff.GRD_AddValue( { val=10000*12, pressure=0, boundplus=1 } ); // GRD‚ğÅ‘å‚É
							}
						}
					}
					
					// ƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹‚ÌŒø‰Ê”­“®
					if( is_celes_vp )
					{
						// _dpn("šƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹VP");
						BMvEff.AttackInfoString_Set({ word=def_AISW_CelesVorpal,} );

						// ƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹Eƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚Ìˆ—
						Battle_Std.GS_AddFlag( def_PP_GS_CelecVorpal ); // ƒZƒŒƒXƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚È‚Ì‚ğ‹L‰¯
						
						BMvTbl.SetCelestialVorpalMode(1); // ƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹‚ÈUŒ‚—ÍƒAƒbƒv
						
						// ƒŠƒXƒ^[ƒg‚ÍƒgƒŒƒ‚‚ÌGRD‚ÌŒÂ”İ’è‚ªƒYƒŒ‚é‚Ì‚Å‰½‚à‚µ‚È‚¢
						// ƒgƒŒƒ‚ŒÀ’è‚¾‚ªƒZƒŒƒXƒeƒBƒAƒ‹VP‚¾‚¯‚ÇGRD‚ª6ŒÂ–¢–‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚Í’ÊíVP“¯—l‚ÉGRD‚ğ1‚Â‘‚â‚·
						if( !is_restart_first && !grd_countover )
						{
							BMvEff.GRD_AddValue( { val=10000, pressure=0, boundplus=1 } ); // VPŒø‰Ê‚Æ‚µ‚ÄGRD‚ğ1‚Â‘‚â‚·
						}
					}
					else
					{
						// ’Êíƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹
						// _dpn("š’ÊíVP");

						// ƒŠƒXƒ^[ƒg‚ÍƒgƒŒƒ‚‚ÌGRD‚ÌŒÂ”İ’è‚ªƒYƒŒ‚é‚Ì‚Å‰½‚à‚µ‚È‚¢
						if( !is_restart_first )
						{
							BMvEff.GRD_AddValue( { val=10000, pressure=0, boundplus=1 } ); // VPŒø‰Ê‚Æ‚µ‚ÄGRD‚ğ1‚Â‘‚â‚·
						}
						BMvTbl.SetCelestialVorpalMode(0); // ’Êí‚Ì’l‚É–ß‚·
					}
				}
				
				// ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚Ì‰‰o
				// KOŒã‚Éo‚·‚à‚Ì‚Å‚à‚È‚¢
				if( !Battle_Std.CharaisKO() && !is_restart_first )
				{
					BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_CVPCutinGrp" } );

					// ƒ{ƒCƒX‚ÉŠÖ‚µ‚Ä‚Í”íƒRƒ“ƒ{’†‚È‚Ç‚ÍÄ¶‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
					if( Def_Dbg_NoPlayUpsetSE == 0 && !Battle_Std.CheckPlayerisDamage() )
					{
						// ƒgƒŒƒ‚‚Å‚Í’§”­İ’è‚ğQÆ
						// ƒ~ƒbƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚Æ‚©‚Å‚ÍÄ¶‚µ‚È‚¢
						// ‘Îí’†‚Í‚Æ‚¤‚º‚ñÄ¶‚·‚é
						local stage_st = BMvTbl.GetMvStageStatus().IsTrainingBattle(); // 1-Training  2-Tutorial  3-Mission
						local appeal = BMvEff.TM_GetCharaGauge(Def_TMCG_EnableAppeal);//’§”­‚Ì—L–³
						if( stage_st == 0 || ( stage_st == 1 && appeal ) )
						{
							local isDying = ( BMvTbl.GetMvStatus().flags & _MvStFlag_DangerHPDef )? 1 : 0; // •m€ó‘Ô
							
							// (1<<3) : ÅŒã‚ÉÄ¶‚µ‚½SE”Ô†‚ğXV‚µ‚È‚¢
							if( isDying )
							{
								if( !Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹_•m€", flags=(1<<3) }) )
								{
									Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹", flags=(1<<3) });
								}
							}
							else
							{
								Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹", flags=(1<<3) });
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			player.pop();
		}
		break;
	}
}


//’´‹Z’†‘±‚·‚éƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg
//’´‹Z‚Å‚Ì”wŒi‚ğØ‚è‘Ö‚¦‚½‚è‚·‚é
Std_MoveTable.Mv_SPAfterImage_KanriObject <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender| _ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );

		Battle_Std.GS_AddFlag( def_PP_GS_SPKanriObjFlag ); // ˆÃ“]’†‚Æ‚¢‚¤ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ—§‚Ä‚é
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ‘€ìe‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		local skillmove = 0;
		local spmove = 0;
		local object_daburi = 0; // ‚±‚ÌƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg‚Ì‚QŒÂ–Ú‚ªo‚Ä‚«‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Á‚Û‚¢‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		
		local obj_mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		local obj_mvs_MvCount = obj_mvs.MvCount+1; // ’´‹Z’†‘±‚·‚éMv‚ÌMvCount(ŒÄ‚Ño‚µ•ª1F’x‚¢‚Ì‚Å‘«‚·)
		
		if( player.push() )
		{
			skillmove = ( Battle_Std.MoveCode.CheckFlag( def_MC_Skill ) );
			spmove = ( Battle_Std.MvAction.CheckFlag( def_MC_SPAction ) );
			
			player.pop();
		}
		
		if( obj_mvs_MvCount == def_FL_SPCutinStopTime ) // ˆÃ“]”²‚¯‚½ƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO
		{
			// _dpn("ˆÃ“]‚¨‚í‚è");
			Battle_Std.GS_DelFlag( def_PP_GS_SPKanriObjFlag ); // ˆÃ“]‚ğ”²‚¯‚½‚Ì‚Åƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğÁ‚·
		}
		else if( obj_mvs_MvCount > def_FL_SPCutinStopTime )
		{
			if( Battle_Std.GS_CheckFlag( def_PP_GS_SPKanriObjFlag ) )
			{
				object_daburi = 1; // ‚QŒÂ–Ú‚Å‚Ä‚é‚Á‚Û‚¢
			}
		}

		if( skillmove && spmove ) //•KE‹Z‚Å‚r‚oˆµ‚¢
		{
		}
		else
		{	
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
		if( object_daburi )
		{
			// _dpn("ƒ_ƒuƒŠ‚ÅI—¹");
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100);
		}
	}
	function Finalize()
	{
		if( BMvTbl.GetFinalizeCode() != 100 ) // ƒ_ƒu‚è‚ÅI—¹‚ÌˆÈŠO
		{
			local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ‘€ìe‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
			if( player.push() )
			{
				BMvEff.PcAfterImage_Clear(); //c‘œ‚ÌÁ‹
				BMvEff.CutInProc_Erase(); //ƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½‚çÁ‹‚·‚é
				
				player.pop();
			}
		}
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

//‹óU‚èƒK[ƒhŠÖŒW
Std_MoveTable.Mv_VGuard_Stand <- 
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=1, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // Mv’†s“®‰Â”\‚É‚·‚é

		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		if( s.DataPattern!=def_PAT_VGuard_S )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_VGuard_S); // ”CˆÓ
			BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(100); // s“®‰Â”\‚ÌƒK[ƒhƒ|[ƒY‚Ö
			BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(def_PAT_VGuard_Time);
		}
		else //“¯‚¶ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚Å‚àFrameID‚ğƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		{
			if( (s.FrameID==120 || s.FrameID==130 ) ) //”²‚¯‚Ä‚½
			{
				BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(100); // s“®‰Â”\‚ÌƒK[ƒhƒ|[ƒY‚Ö
			}
		}		
		
		Battle_Std.InitCharaVector(); //InitCharaVector‚ÅXMAX‰Šú‰»‚·‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚µ‚½‚Ì‚Å‚±‚Á‚¿‚Å‚n‚j
		//Battle_Std.InitVector(); //ƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…‚ÌŠµ«‚ªæ‚Á‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚Ì‚Å‚±‚¤‚·‚é
	}
	function Update_Std() // 
	{
		local type = BMvTbl.VGuard_CheckKeep();
		if( type != 0 )
		{
			BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(def_PAT_VGuard_Time); // ‚à‚Ç‚·
			if( type == 1 ); // ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚ÜŒp‘±
			else if( type == 2 ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(2); // ‚µ‚á‚ª‚İ‚Ö
			else
			{
				// —áŠOA•|‚¢‚Ì‚ÅI‚í‚è
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			}
		}
		else
		{
			if( BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(-1) == 0 ) // ŠÔ‚ğæ“¾‚µ0‚¾‚ÆI—¹
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			}
		}
		//
		//print("\nKeep_Stand" + BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(-1) ); //-1‚Åæ“¾
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(0); // 0‚É‚·‚é
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_VGuard_StandEnd", [2,"Mv_VGuard_Crouch"]); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...	
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(0); // 0‚É‚·‚é
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_VGuard_Crouch <- 
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=1, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // Mv’†s“®‰Â”\‚É‚·‚é

		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		if( s.DataPattern!=def_PAT_VGuard_C )
		{	
			//print("\nˆá‚¤ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚È‚Ì‚Åİ’è");
			BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_VGuard_C); // ”CˆÓ
			BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(100); // s“®‰Â”\‚ÌƒK[ƒhƒ|[ƒY‚Ö		
			BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(def_PAT_VGuard_Time);
		}
		else //“¯‚¶ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚Å‚àFrameID‚ğƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		{
			if( (s.FrameID==120 || s.FrameID==130 ) ) //”²‚¯‚Ä‚½
			{
				BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(100); // s“®‰Â”\‚ÌƒK[ƒhƒ|[ƒY‚Ö
			}
		}
		Battle_Std.InitCharaVector(); //InitCharaVector‚ÅXMAX‰Šú‰»‚·‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚µ‚½‚Ì‚Å‚±‚Á‚¿‚Å‚n‚j
		//Battle_Std.InitVector(); //ƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…‚ÌŠµ«‚ªæ‚Á‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚Ì‚Å‚±‚¤‚·‚é
	}
	function Update_Std() // 
	{
		local type = BMvTbl.VGuard_CheckKeep();
		if( type != 0 )
		{
			// Œp‘±
			BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(def_PAT_VGuard_Time); // ‚à‚Ç‚·
			if( type == 1 )  BMvTbl.SetFinalize(1); // —§‚¿‚Ö
			else if( type == 2 ); // ‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚ÜŒp‘±
			else
			{
				// —áŠOA•|‚¢‚Ì‚ÅI‚í‚è
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			}
		}
		else //Œp‘±‚µ‚È‚¢
		{
			if( BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(-1) == 0 ) // ŠÔ‚ğæ“¾‚µ0‚¾‚ÆI—¹
			{
				BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(0); // 0‚É‚·‚é
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			}
		}
		// 
		//print("\nKeep_Crouch" + BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(-1)+" type:"+type );
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(0); // 0‚É‚·‚é
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_VGuard_CrouchEnd", [1,"Mv_VGuard_Stand"]); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...	
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.VGuard_Time(0); // 0‚É‚·‚é
	}
}

{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);

		// ‚Ü‚Ÿ‚¢‚¢‚©‚Èc‚Á‚Ä‚±‚Æ‚Åest‚©‚ç‚ÍŒ¸‚ç‚³‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é

		local stop_frame = 6; // ©•ª‚É‚¾‚¯‚©‚©‚éƒqƒbƒgƒXƒgƒbƒv
		local muteki_frame = 19+stop_frame; // –³“GŠÔ@‘Š‘Å‚¿‚ª‘½‚¢‚Ì‚Å+5F‚µ‚½@1.12 15+stop_frame
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=muteki_frame, muteki_dageX=muteki_frame, muteki_nage=muteki_frame, muteki_nageX=muteki_frame } );
		
		// ƒK[ƒLƒƒƒ“‰‰o‹­‰»
		BMvEff.SetStopTime( { time=7, stopme=0 } );
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { time=14, type=0, } );
		
		// ©•ª‚ÉƒqƒbƒgƒXƒgƒbƒv‚ğ‚©‚¯‚Ä”­¶‚ğ’x‚­‚·‚é
		local hitst = BMvTbl.MvHitStatus();
		hitst.Count = stop_frame;
		BMvTbl.SetMvHitStatus( hitst );				
		
		// ª 2A‚Ìd’¼·-3F‚Ü‚Å‚ÍƒK[ƒh‚³‚ê‚é”­¶
		
		//”O‚Ì‚½‚ßŠÖŒW
		BMvTbl.SetAsStatusFlag( { normal=_CancelFlag_None, special=_CancelFlag_None, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
		BMvTbl.SetAsFlag( { as_flags=_AsFlag_ChainShift, time=0, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_SysAtk ); // ƒVƒXƒeƒ€“I‚ÈUŒ‚
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
	}
	function HitInterrupt_Std()
	{
		if( Battle_Std.CheckDamageTiming() )
		{
			local muteki_frame = 254; // –³“GŠÔ
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=muteki_frame, muteki_dageX=muteki_frame, muteki_nage=muteki_frame, muteki_nageX=muteki_frame } );
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		// –³“GÁ‹
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=0, muteki_dageX=0, muteki_nage=0, muteki_nageX=0 } );
	}
}

{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		BMvTbl.SetPattern( def_PAT_GuardCancelAtk ); // ƒpƒ^[ƒ“•ÏX
		Battle_Std.InitCharaVector();
		//ƒtƒ‰ƒbƒVƒ…‚Æ‚©ƒQ[ƒWÁ”ï‚Æ‚©
		
		BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { mode=_SpGauge_UseBuffer } ); //‚r‚oƒQ[ƒW—\–ñ•ª‚ğÁ”ï
		
		//GuardCanselƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‚æ‚Ñ
		//GuardCansel

		datatype=1, pat="GuardCansel", angle="rand",
		objectflags=_ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll|_ObjFlags_NoGround
		});

		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒK[ƒLƒƒƒ“" }); //”­“®ƒ{ƒCƒX
		
		//ƒAƒiƒEƒ“ƒX•\¦
		BMvEff.AttackInfoString_Set({ word=def_AISW_GuardCancel,} );
		
		BMvTbl.SetPrio( _CharaPrio_Near ); // ƒvƒ‰ƒCƒIƒŠƒeƒBè‘O‚É
		
		//”­“®‚ÉGRD‚ğÁ”ï
		local stock = BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } );
		BMvEff.GRD_UseStock( { val=stock } ); // ‘S•”Á”ï
		BMvTbl.SetLP(9,stock); // ”­“®‚ÌƒXƒgƒbƒN‚ğ‹L‰¯
		
		BMvEff.GRD_ClearVorpal( 0 ); // ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚ğ‰ğœ‚·‚é

		local stop_frame = 3; // ©•ª‚É‚¾‚¯‚©‚©‚éƒqƒbƒgƒXƒgƒbƒv
		local muteki_frame = 15+stop_frame; // –³“GŠÔ
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=muteki_frame, muteki_dageX=muteki_frame, muteki_nage=muteki_frame, muteki_nageX=muteki_frame } );
		
		// ƒK[ƒLƒƒƒ“‰‰o‹­‰»
		BMvEff.SetStopTime( { time=7, stopme=0 } );
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { time=14, type=0, } );
		
		// ©•ª‚ÉƒqƒbƒgƒXƒgƒbƒv‚ğ‚©‚¯‚Ä”­¶‚ğ’x‚­‚·‚é
		local hitst = BMvTbl.MvHitStatus();
		hitst.Count = stop_frame;
		BMvTbl.SetMvHitStatus( hitst );				
		
		// ª 2A‚Ìd’¼·+0F‚Ü‚Å‚ÍƒK[ƒh‚³‚ê‚é”­¶
		
		//”O‚Ì‚½‚ßŠÖŒW
		BMvTbl.SetAsStatusFlag( { normal=_CancelFlag_None, special=_CancelFlag_None, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
		BMvTbl.SetAsFlag( { as_flags=_AsFlag_ChainShift, time=0, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
		
		// BMvTbl.Achievement_Unlock(6); // yƒgƒƒtƒB[zˆÀ‘S‚ÈØ‚è•Ô‚µ
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_SysAtk ); // ƒVƒXƒeƒ€“I‚ÈUŒ‚
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
	}
	function HitInterrupt_Std()
	{
		if( Battle_Std.CheckDamageTiming() )
		{
			local muteki_frame = 254; // –³“GŠÔ
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=muteki_frame, muteki_dageX=muteki_frame, muteki_nage=muteki_frame, muteki_nageX=muteki_frame } );
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		// –³“GÁ‹

Std_MoveTable.Mv_ConvertChargeEff <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BSound.SE_Play( { num=239 } ); //ƒRƒ“ƒZƒ“ƒgƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“Œø‰Ê‰¹Ä¶‚Å‚¾‚µ
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‰Šú‰»
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local id = Battle_Std.GetPlayerFrameID();
		local mv = Battle_Std.GetPlayerMvName();
		
		local mvcnt = BMvTbl.GetLP(0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^æ“¾
		BMvTbl.AddLP(0,1); //1Fi‚ß‚é
		
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		if( mvcnt%50==49 )
		{
			BSound.SE_Play( { num=238 } ); //ƒRƒ“ƒZƒ“ƒgƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ƒ‹[ƒv{ƒtƒF[ƒhŒø‰Ê‰¹Ä¶		
		}
		
		if( id==256 && mv=="Mv_ConvertCharge_Modori" )
		{
			BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(256);
			BMvEff.EraseObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_ParentMove|_ObjFlags_EraseParentPatChange|_ObjFlags_EraseParentDamage } );	
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();	
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BSound.SE_Stop( { type=_SeType_Normal, num=239, fadetime=10 } ); // ƒRƒ“ƒZƒ“ƒg‚r‚dƒtƒF[ƒhƒAƒEƒg
	}
}

//ƒRƒ“ƒZƒ“ƒgƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“
Std_MoveTable.Mv_ConvertCharge <- 	//ƒRƒ“ƒo[ƒg
{
	function Init_Std() //
	{
		//‹¤’Êˆ—
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		
		BMvTbl.ClearHitStatus(); //ƒqƒbƒgî•ñ‚ÌƒNƒŠƒAi‹óU‚èƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹‰Â”\ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ‚½‚Ä‚Ä‚é‚½‚ß•K—vj

		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Convert);
		local rc = BMvEff.GetHanteiRect( { check=[ _Hantei_Etc, 0 ], flags=_HanteiFlag_NoMukiChange | _HanteiFlag_Tool  } );
		if( rc.sx != _Hantei_Error ) // ‘¶İ‚·‚é‚©
		{
			local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=rc.sx, y=0, mvname="Mv_ConvertChargeEff", datatype=1, start_pat=84, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
			if( eff.push() )
			{
				BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll|_ObjFlags_ParentMove|_ObjFlags_EraseParentPatChange|_ObjFlags_EraseParentDamage } );	
				BMvTbl.SetPosition( { y=0 } );
				eff.pop();
			}
		}
		Battle_Std.InitVector(); // ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»

		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒRƒ“ƒZƒ“ƒgƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“" });
		
		//D˜A‘Å‚ÅCSo‚¹‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‹óU‚èCS‰Â”\‚É
		BMvTbl.SetAsFlag( { as_flags=_AsFlag_ChainShift, time=60, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangePattern|_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 1, def_MC1_ChainShiftOK ); // DD‚ÅCS‚ªo‚¹‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 1, def_MC1_CmdLvDZ_OK ); // “¯‰Ÿ‚µ‚Å‘¼‚Ìs“®‚ÉˆÚ‚ê‚és“®
		
		BMvTbl.SetWallCount(0); // –³“GƒoƒO‘Îô‚É•ÇƒoƒEƒ“ƒh‰ñ”‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg‚·‚é
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‰Šú‰»
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0); //‘O‰ñ‚ÌMvCount
		
		// ƒ‹[ƒv‚Ì”²‚¯æ‚ğ—\–ñ
		local stopframe = BMvEff.GetFrameIDNum( 256 );
		if( stopframe != -1 ) // FrameID‚ª‚ ‚é‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		{
			BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=def_PAT_Convert, frame=stopframe, rest=4 } );
		}
		
		// ‚±‚ê‚ÍŒã”¼s“®‰Â”\‚É‚È‚é‚µo‚È‚¢‚Ù‚¤‚ª©‘R
		// Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.CallLoopEndCache_FrameID( def_PAT_Convert, 256, 10 ); // ƒLƒƒƒbƒVƒ…—\–ñPat, —\–ñFrameID, ‰½F–ˆ‚ÉŒÄ‚Ô‚©
	
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		
		//ƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ğ‚¿‚å‚Á‚ÆÂ‚­Œõ‚ç‚¹‚é
		if( s.MvCount%4==0 )
		{
			Battle_Std.SetCharaFlash_ConvertCharge();
		}
		
		// 1F‚É‚P‰ñ‚Ìˆ—
		if( s.CallCount == 0 )
		{
			// GRD‘‰ÁŠÖŒW
			//ƒ`ƒƒ[ƒW’†‚ÌGRD‘‰ÁŠî–{’l
			local mGRDAddVal = def_GRD_Convert_Frame;
			local eGRDAddVal = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_Enemy;
			
			local okure_hosei = 0;
			if( Def_Sys_DelayedConvertChargeDemerit )
			{
				local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “G‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
				if( enemy.push() )
				{
					local emv = BMvTbl.GetMvName();
					local emvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();

					enemy.pop();
					
					local p_cnt = s.MvCount;
					local e_cnt = emvs.MvCount;
					
					// 1P¨2P‚Ìˆ—‡‚Å‚â‚â‚±‚µ‚¢‚Ì‚Å1F–Ú‚Í”ò‚Î‚·
					// 2P‘¤‚©‚çŒ©‚é‚Æ1P‚Íˆ—Ï‚İ‚È‚Ì‚ÅA1F•ªŒ¸‚ç‚µ‚ÄŒvZ‚·‚é
					
					if( emv == "Mv_ConvertCharge" && p_cnt >= 1 && e_cnt >= 1 )
					{
						
						local pside = BMvTbl.GetPlayerSide();
						
						if( pside == 1 )
						{
							e_cnt--; // 1FŒ¸‚ç‚·
						}
						
						local cnt_sa = p_cnt - e_cnt; // Ÿ‚Á‚Ä‚é‚©
						
						// ­‚µ‚¾‚¯ƒ}[ƒWƒ““ü‚ê‚é ‚ ‚é’ö“xFˆÈã’x‚ê‚Ä‚½‚çŸ‚Ä‚È‚­‚·‚é
						// ‚ ‚Ü‚èƒ}[ƒWƒ“‚ª‚ ‚é‚ÆAŒ©‚Ä‚©‚çD‚Åˆø‚«•ª‚¯‚É‚Á‚Äs‚«‚â‚·‚­‚È‚é‚ªA‚»‚ê‚à–Ê”’‚¢‚©‚à‚È‚Ì‚Å‚æ‚­‚ ‚é26F‚É‚·‚é
						if( cnt_sa <= -26 )
						{
							// _dpn("’x‚ê•â³‚ ‚è");
							okure_hosei = 1;
						}
						// _dpn(format("%dP “¯‚¶\‚¦:%3d - %3d = %d",(pside+1), p_cnt, e_cnt, cnt_sa) );
					}
				}
			}
			
			local enemyBoundAirUkemiOk = 0; // “G‚ª‹ó’†óg‰Â”\‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©i‚â‚ç‚ê‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚Í•Ê‚É’²‚×‚é•K—v‚ ‚èj
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				local ebs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
				enemy.pop();
				
				enemyBoundAirUkemiOk = ebs.AirUkemi;
			}
			local enemyIsDamage = Battle_Std.CheckEnemyisDamage();
			
			local useDamageGRD = (enemyIsDamage && !enemyBoundAirUkemiOk)? 1 : 0; // ƒ_ƒ[ƒW‚ÌGRD‘‰Á‚ğQÆ‚·‚é‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©

			local comboConcentrationFrame = 0;
			if( Def_Sys_Concentration_EnemyDamageCheck && useDamageGRD )
			{
				comboConcentrationFrame = BMvTbl.AddPP(def_PP_ComboConcentrationCounter,1); // ”­“®‚µ‚½ŠÔ‚ğ‹L‰¯‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚­
			}
			
			if( BMvEff.Liberate_Get()!=_SpGaugeMode_Normal ) //ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt’†
			{
				mGRDAddVal = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_VeilOff;
				eGRDAddVal = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_VeilOff_Enemy;
			}
			if( BMvEff.GRD_GetBreak( 1 ) != 0 ) //‘Šè‚ªƒuƒŒƒCƒN’†
			{
				// _dpn("‘Šè‚ªƒuƒŒƒCƒN’†");
				mGRDAddVal = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_EnemyisBreak;
				eGRDAddVal = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_EnemyisBreak_Enemy;
			}
			else if( BCMDTbl.GetComboGauge()==0 ) //©•ª‚ÌEXS‚ª‹ó
			{
				mGRDAddVal = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_NoEXS;
				eGRDAddVal = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_NoEXS_Enemy;		
			}
			else if( enemyIsDamage ) // ‹Œd—l
			{
				mGRDAddVal = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_EnemyisDamage;
				eGRDAddVal = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_EnemyisDamage_Enemy;
			}

			// ƒ_ƒ[ƒW‚Í—Dæ“x‚ğÅ‘å‚Åƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚µ‚ÄA¡‚Ì‚â‚Â‚Æ”äŠr‚µ‚Äˆ«‚¢•û‚ğ‘I‚Ô
			if( useDamageGRD && Def_Sys_Concentration_EnemyDamageCheck )  //‘Šè‚ª‚â‚ç‚ê•ó‚¯g•s‰Â”\‚È‚Æ‚«
			{
				local mGRDAddVal_Damage = mGRDAddVal;
				local eGRDAddVal_Damage = eGRDAddVal;

				// _dpn("comboConcentrationFrame:"+comboConcentrationFrame);
				
				// ƒRƒ“ƒ{’†‚Ì‘ƒRƒ“ƒZŠÔ‚ªˆê’è’l‚ğ‰z‚¦‚½‚ç‚Ç‚ñ‚Ç‚ñŒø‰Ê‚ªŒ¸‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚­
				local limitFrame = 25;
				local maxFrame = 70;
				if( comboConcentrationFrame >= limitFrame )
				{
					if( comboConcentrationFrame > maxFrame ) comboConcentrationFrame = maxFrame;
					comboConcentrationFrame = comboConcentrationFrame - limitFrame; // 0`60(90-60)
					local conceFramePar = comboConcentrationFrame * 100 / (maxFrame-limitFrame); // ’·‚¢‚Æ”’l‚ª‘‚¦‚é
					// _dpn("conceFramePar:"+conceFramePar);

					mGRDAddVal_Damage = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_EnemyisDamage - (def_GRD_Convert_Frame_EnemyisDamage*80/100)*conceFramePar/100;
					eGRDAddVal_Damage = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_EnemyisDamage_Enemy - (def_GRD_Convert_Frame_EnemyisDamage_Enemy*80/100)*conceFramePar/100;
				}
				else
				{
					mGRDAddVal_Damage = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_EnemyisDamage;
					eGRDAddVal_Damage = def_GRD_Convert_Frame_EnemyisDamage_Enemy;
				}
				
				// ˆ«‚¢•û‚ğ‘I‚Ô
				// _dpn(" mGRDAddVal Damage:"+mGRDAddVal_Damage+" "+mGRDAddVal);
				// _dpn(" mGRDAddVal Damage:"+eGRDAddVal_Damage+" "+eGRDAddVal);
				
				if( mGRDAddVal > mGRDAddVal_Damage ) mGRDAddVal = mGRDAddVal_Damage;
				if( eGRDAddVal < eGRDAddVal_Damage ) eGRDAddVal = eGRDAddVal_Damage; // ‚±‚Á‚¿‚Íƒ}ƒCƒiƒX‚È‚Ì‚Å•„†‹t
			}
			
			if( s.MvCount<def_FL_Shield_SPCostStartFrame )
			{
				//‚Å‚©‚©‚è‚Í‚½‚Ü‚è‚É‚­‚­
				mGRDAddVal = mGRDAddVal * 25 / 100; //®”‰»:0.25;
				eGRDAddVal = eGRDAddVal * 45 / 100; //®”‰»:0.45;
			}
			else
			{
				// ‚Å‚©‚©‚èˆÈ~‚Å·‚ªo‚Ä­‚µ‹Ù’£Š´‚ğ‚½‚¹‚é
				if( Def_Sys_DelayedConvertChargeDemerit && okure_hosei )
				{
					//okure_hosei
					// mGRDAddVal = mGRDAddVal * 70 / 100;
					eGRDAddVal = eGRDAddVal * 70 / 100; // ‘Šè‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚·ƒpƒ[‚¾‚¯Œ¸‚é
				}
			}
			
			// ‹——£‚ª—£‚ê‚Ä‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÍŒø‰Ê‚ª‰º‚ª‚é
			if( Def_Sys_Concentration_CheckEnemyKyori )
			{
				local enemy_kyori = abs( Battle_Std.GetEnemyDistance()/128 );

				local apart_kyori = enemy_kyori = enemy_kyori - 640;
				if( apart_kyori <   0 ) apart_kyori =   0;
				if( apart_kyori > 640 ) apart_kyori = 640;
				
				local apart_par = apart_kyori * 100 / 640; // 0`100 ŒvZ—p‚Ì‚Ç‚ê‚¾‚¯—£‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚é‚©
				// Å’á‚Å30%‚Ü‚Å—‚¿‚é
				
				if( Def_Sys_Concentration_CheckEnemyKyoriWithGRDStock )
				{
					local grd_stock = BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } ); // ¡‰½ŒÂ‚ ‚éH
					// if( grd_stock>11 ) grd_stock = 11; //Å‘å
					if( grd_stock>6 ) grd_stock = 6; //Å‘å6ŒÂ”»’è‚Ål‚¦‚é
					
					// GRDŒÂ”‚É‚æ‚é‰e‹¿“xiGRD‚ª­‚È‚¢‚Æ‚«‚Í‹——£•â®‚Ì‰e‹¿‚ğó‚¯‚É‚­‚¢j
					local stock_par_ar =[
					20, // 0
					40, // 1
					60, // 2
					80, // 3
					100, // 4
					100, // 5
					100, // 6
					];
					local stock_par = stock_par_ar [ grd_stock%stock_par_ar.len() ];
					// _dpn("grd_stock:"+grd_stock+" stock_par:"+stock_par+" apart_par:"+apart_par);
					apart_par = apart_par * stock_par / 100;
					// _dp(" -> "+apart_par);
				}
				
				local calc_par = 100 - 70 * apart_par / 100
				// _dpn("GRD1:"+mGRDAddVal+", "+eGRDAddVal);
				mGRDAddVal = mGRDAddVal * calc_par / 100;
				eGRDAddVal = eGRDAddVal * calc_par / 100;
				// _dpn("GRD2:"+mGRDAddVal+", "+eGRDAddVal);

				// _dpn("calc_par:"+calc_par);
			}
			
			// _dpn("ŒvZŒã:"+mGRDAddVal+" / "+eGRDAddVal+" time_bonus_val:"+time_bonus_val );
			// _dpn("mGRDAddVal:"+mGRDAddVal+" eGRDAddVal:"+eGRDAddVal);
			
			Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue({ val=Battle_Std.GetGRD_AddValue(mGRDAddVal), pressure=0 }); //©•ª‚Ì‘‰Á
			Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue({ val=Battle_Std.GetGRD_AddValue(eGRDAddVal), pressure=0, target=1 }); //‘Šè‚ÌŒ¸­

			if( Def_Sys_Concentration_SubExs )
			{
				Battle_Std.SetSpGauge_ConvertChargeFU( {value=def_SP_ConvertCharge_Frame,} ); //ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“’†‚ÌEXSÁ”ï
			}
			
			//‚»‚ê‚Æ‚Í•Ê‚É
			//©•ª‚ÌƒGƒŠƒA‚Ü‚ÅNH‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚éGRD‚Í‹­‚­Œ¸‚ç‚·
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “G‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				local enemyGRD_count = BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } );
				if( enemyGRD_count>=6 ) 
				{
					BMvEff.GRD_AddValue( { val=-600, } ); //ŒÅ’è’l‚ÅŒ¸‚é
				}
				
				enemy.pop();
			}
			
			if( Def_Sys_Concentration_AddTS )
			{
				// ƒRƒ“ƒZ’†‚ÍTS‚Ì‘¬“x‚ğ•ÏX‚µ‚Ä‚İ‚é
				// VP‚¾‚¯‘‚­‚·‚é‚Æ‚©‚·‚é‚ÆB–†‚È‚Ì‚ÅAƒVƒ“ƒvƒ‹‚É‚¿‚å‚Á‚Æ‘‚­‚·‚éŒø‰Ê‚É‚µ‚½
				// local speed = BMvEff.GRD_TS_SetPlusValue( { val=120 } ); // 2”{‘¬“x
				BMvEff.GRD_TS_AddValue( { val=Def_Sys_Concentration_AddTS } );
			}
			
			
			// ƒp[ƒeƒBƒNƒ‹ŠÖŒW
			// ‚Â‚Ô‚ğo‚·
			if( s.MvCount%3==0 ) //‹C‚¿Œy‚­‚·‚é
			{
				local muki = BMvEff.Random_Limit(2); //BMvTbl.SetMuki( muki )
				local iRnd = BMvEff.Random_Limit(100); //0`99 Šî–{ƒ‰ƒ“ƒ_ƒ€’l
				local posx = iRnd;
				local vecy = (-2300 + (iRnd*15))*150/100;
				local vecx = (posx*3)*150/100;
				local frame = 45;
				if( muki==1 )
				{
					posx*=-1;
				}
				local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=posx, y=-25, mvname="", datatype=1, start_pat=27, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
				if( eff.push() )
				{
					BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll|_ObjFlags_NoGround } );	
					if( muki==1 ) BMvTbl.SetMuki( _Direction_Reverse );
					BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=vecx, y=vecy, addx=0, addy=-vecy/frame, flags=_Vector_Div } );
					BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(20);
					
					eff.pop();
				}
			}
		}
		
		// 1F‚É2‰ñˆÈã‚â‚Á‚Ä‚à‚¢‚¢d—v‚Èˆ—
		switch( s.FrameID )
		{
		case 100: //ƒ‹[ƒvŠJn
			break;
		case 200: //ƒ‹[ƒv•ªŠò
			BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(100);
			break;
		}
		
		if( s.MvCount>def_FL_ConvertMinTime && !BMvTbl.CheckCommandString( [ "@H,0D@" ] ) )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256);
		}
		
		// Ver1.03‚Å‚Ì–³“GƒoƒO–h~—p
		// ¡‚Í‚à‚¤•s—v‚¾‚ªA–³ŒÀ‚É‘±‚¯‚ç‚ê‚éƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚Í‚»‚ê‚È‚è‚ÉŠëŒ¯‚È‚Ì‚Åˆê‰c‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­
		if( s.MvCount>def_FL_ConvertMaxTime )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256);		
		}
		
		//200225:ƒRƒ“ƒZ‚Å2P‚ª•K‚¸Ÿ‚Á‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚Ì‘Î‰
		BMvEff.GRD_SetSpFlag(1);
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral", [256,"Mv_ConvertCharge_Modori"]); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...	
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( s.MvCount>4 )
		{
		}
		else
		{
			Battle_Std.PlayerSE_StopLastPlaySound( 0 ); //’¼‘O‚ÉÄ¶‚µ‚½‚r‚d‚ğ’â~iƒRƒ“ƒZƒ“ƒgƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“j
		}
	}
}

//ƒRƒ“ƒZƒ“ƒgƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚Ì–ß‚è
//ˆê‰ƒXƒNƒŠƒvƒg‚Å‚às“®‰Â”\‚É‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚±‚¤
Std_MoveTable.Mv_ConvertCharge_Modori <-
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.JumpFrameIDEX(256, def_PAT_Convert, 0); //İ’èFrameID, ã‘‚«Pattern, ¸”sFinalizeCode
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=1, time=64, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangePattern|_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); //s“®‰Â”\‚É•ÏX
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}	
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ‚â‚ç‚êŠÖŒW
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ›“Áê‚È‚à‚Ì
// Mv_Bound_0023 “Š‚°”²‚¯‰Ÿ‚µ•Ô‚µUŒ‚
// Mv_Bound_0024 “Š‚°”²‚¯‚ç‚êƒK[ƒhƒ|[ƒY

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Bound <- 
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.BoundInit();		
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.BoundUpdate();
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local useval = (BMvEff.GRD_GetJudgeResult()>0)? ( (BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } )>=6)? def_GRD_BoundFU_GRD6Vorpal : def_GRD_BoundFU_Vorpal) : def_GRD_BoundFU;
		Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue({ val=useval, pressure=0, boundplus=1 });	
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		//”²‚¯æ‚ª–³‚¢‚©‚àH
		//ó‘Ô‚ÌˆÚs‚¾‚¯s‚¤
		BtlMvStd.SetBoundFinalize();
		// print("\nMv_Bound Finalize()");
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		if( Def_Rule_GroundBoundSlideKeep )
		{
			//’nãó‘Ô‚Å‚ÍŠµ«‚ğc‚·
			if( BMvTbl.CheckPosState( _PosState_Ground  ) )
			{
				local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector( { flags=_Vector_Bound } );
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=vec.x, addx=-vec.x/10, flags=_Vector_DivKeep } );
				// print("\n vec.x:"+vec.x);
			}
		}
		BtlMvStd.ClearBound(); // ó‘Ô‰ğœ(ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ª–³‚­‚È‚é)
		// print("\nMv_Bound LastUpdate()");		
	}
}

//•ß‚Ü‚ê’†‚ÍMv_Bound‚Å‚Í‚È‚­Mv_Bound_Capture‚É‚È‚è‚Ü‚·(–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€Init‚·‚é)
//Mv_Bound_Capture‚Í–¼‘O‚¾‚¯‚ÅBound‚Å‚Í‚È‚¢‚½‚ßABoundŠÖŒW‚Å‰½‚©‚µ‚æ‚¤‚Æ‚·‚é‚ÆŠë‚È‚¢
//Mv_Bound_Capture‚Æ‚¢‚¤ó‘Ô‚ğæ“¾‚Å‚«‚é‚¾‚¯‚Æl‚¦‚½‚Ù‚¤‚ª‚¢‚¢
//ƒGƒ‰[‚ª‚¤‚´‚¢‚Ì‚Åˆê‰’è‹`‚·‚é‚¯‚Ç‰½‚à‚µ‚È‚¢
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Bound_Capture <- 
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		if( BMvTbl.ChangeMv_GetMvName() != "Mv_Bound_Capture" )
		{
			// print("\nMv_Bound_Capture Init()");
			// ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»‚¾‚¯s‚¤
			// ‚Â‚©‚İ¬—§¨‘OMv‚ÌLastUpdate‚ÅƒxƒNƒgƒ‹İ’è¨‚±‚ÌMv‚Å“®‚«‚Á‚Ï‚È‚µ‚É‚È‚é
			Battle_Std.InitVector();
			
			// ‚â‚ç‚êƒ{ƒCƒXÄ¶ˆ—
			local sound_st = BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_SoundStatus);
			if( sound_st&def_PP_SS_NoBoundVoice )
			{
			}
			else if( sound_st&def_PP_SS_NoBoundVoiceOnce )
			{
			}
			else
			{
				Battle_Std.PlayerSE_StopLastPlaySound( 1 ); //’¼‘O‚ÉÄ¶‚µ‚½‰¹º‚ğ’â~
			}
			BMvEff.CutInProc_Erase(); //ƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½‚çÁ‹‚·‚é
			
			local anno_flags = BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_ImpactAnnounce);
			BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_ImpactAnnounce,0);//‚·‚®‰Šú‰»
			Battle_Std.DrawPunishAnnounce( anno_flags ); //‚â‚ç‚êƒAƒiƒEƒ“ƒXˆ—
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		// Finalize‚·‚é‚Æ–³ŒÀƒ‹[ƒv‚µ‚Ä—‚¿‚é‚µA‚»‚à‚»‚à‰½‚à‚µ‚È‚¢‚Å‚¨‚­
		// BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Bound_Capture" );
	}
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Guard
// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Guard <- 
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		Battle_Std.InitVector(); // ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
		BtlMvStd.SetGuardStatus();
		//print("\nMv_Bound Init()\n");
		
		// ƒK[ƒhƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹—pˆ—
		BMvTbl.SetLP(9,0); //ƒK[ƒh‚µ‚Ä‚©‚ç‰½ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€Œo‰ß‚µ‚½‚©

		BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0); // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğŸ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Å”­“®‚·‚é
		BMvTbl.SetLP(6,0); // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒhó‘Ô‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‰½ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚½‚Á‚½‚©BƒK[ƒhŠJn‚Å‚Ì‚İƒŠƒZƒbƒgB2‰ñ–Ú‚Ì”­“®‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½ê‡Œp‘±B
		BMvTbl.SetLP(7,0); // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®‰ñ”
		//BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_ShieldStatus,0); //ƒV[ƒ‹ƒhƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒX‚Í‚±‚±‚Å‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetLP(8,0); // ƒK[ƒhƒtƒ[ƒg‚Å–³“GŠÔ‚Ì‚Â‚¢‚½ƒK[ƒh‚¾‚æ
		
		//ƒK[ƒh‚Ìƒ{ƒCƒX‚ğ‘I‚ñ‚ÅÄ¶
		local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
		if( bs.isBound )
		{
			//_dm("pow:"+bs.Power+" def_BS_Pow_LV2"+def_BS_Pow_LV2);
			local bs_power = bs.Power;
			
			if( bs_power&def_BS_Pow_LV1 )
			{
				// _dpn("A");
				Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒK[ƒhã" });
			}
			else if( bs_power&def_BS_Pow_LV0 )
			{
				// º–³‚µ
			}
			else if( bs_power&def_BS_Pow_LV3 )
			{
				// _dpn("C");
				Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒK[ƒh‹­" });
			}
			else
			{
				//def_BS_Pow_LV2 == 0 ‚È‚Ì‚Åƒtƒ‰ƒO‚Ìƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚Íâ‘Î‚É0‚É‚È‚é‚©‚çelse‚É’u‚­
				// _dpn("B");
				Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒK[ƒh’†" });
			}
			
			if( bs_power&def_BS_Pow_GuardFloat ) 
			{
				// ƒK[ƒhƒtƒ[ƒg‚È‚Ì‚Å–³“G‚É‚µ‚Ü‚·
				_dp("\n ƒK[ƒhƒtƒ[ƒg‚È‚Ì‚Å–³“G‚ğƒZƒbƒg");
				BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=64, muteki_dageX=64 } ); // •|‚¢‚Ì‚ÅŠÔ‚Í’Z‚ß
				BMvTbl.SetLP(8,1); // –³“G‚ğƒZƒbƒg‚µ‚½‚±‚Æ‚ğ‹L‰¯
			}
		}

		//ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷‚Ìˆ—
		if( BMvEff.GuardSP_Success() )
		{
			// _dm("ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷i‹Œ‚d‚wj");
			
			// ƒoƒŠƒAƒK[ƒhiƒV[ƒ‹ƒhj‚ª¬Œ÷‚µ‚½‚Æ‚«‚Ìˆ—
			Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷" });	
			
			//ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚É¬Œ÷
			BMvEff.AttackInfoString_Set({ word=def_AISW_SPGuard,} );									
			
			Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_ShiledSuccess ); // ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷
			
			
			Battle_Std.SetShieldSuccessGRDEffect(0); // ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷‚É‚æ‚éGRD‚ÆTS‚Ìˆ—
			
			//‹——£‚ğ—£‚ê‚â‚·‚­‚·‚é
			local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector( { flags=_Vector_Bound }); //‘‚â‚·‚½‚ß¡‚ÌƒK[ƒhƒxƒNƒgƒ‹æ“¾
			if( vec.x<=0 ) //—£‚ê‚éƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚Ì‚İ‘‚â‚·
			{
				local isGround = BCMDTbl.CheckPosState( _PosState_Ground ); //def_VEC_GuardSP_Xvec_Air
				local useXvec = (isGround)? def_VEC_GuardSP_Xvec : def_VEC_GuardSP_Xvec_Air;//’nã‚Æ‹ó’†‚Å•Ê‚ÌŒW”‚ğg‚¤
				local useXmax = (isGround)? def_VEC_GuardSP_Xmax : def_VEC_GuardSP_Xmax_Air;//’nã‚Æ‹ó’†‚Å•Ê

				//ŒW”‚ğ‚©‚¯‚ÄÅ‘å’l‚ğ§ŒÀ
				//_dp("\n nowvecx:"+vec.x);
				vec.x = vec.x * useXvec/100;
				if( vec.x<useXmax) vec.x = useXmax;
				vec.addx = vec.addx * useXvec/100;
			
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=vec.x, addx=vec.addx, flags=_Vector_Bound } );
			}
			
			//ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚É‚æ‚éŠÔ’â~iƒqƒbƒgƒXƒgƒbƒvj
			if( def_FL_ShieldStopTime )
			{
				BMvEff.SetStopTime( { time=def_FL_ShieldStopTime, stopme=2 } );
			}
			
			local enemy_atk_is_airatk = 0;
			local enemy_atk_is_shield_steady = 0;
			
			//‰£‚ç‚ê‚½‘Šè‚Ìs“®‚ªƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvUŒ‚‚¾‚Á‚½‚çƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹•s‰Â‚ÆƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚³‚ê‚½ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ—^‚¦‚é@{@¦‚Å‚«‚È‚¢F‹ó’†ƒK[ƒh‰Â”\‚É‚·‚éi‘½’iƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ß‚ñ‚Ç‚­‚³‚¢j
			local enemy_LastDmgChr = BMvCore.GetLastDamageCharaData( 0 ); // ‰£‚ç‚ê‚½“Gî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
			if( enemy_LastDmgChr.push() )
			{
				Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_Shielded ); // ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğæ‚ç‚ê‚½Mv‚Æ‚·‚é
				
				local grd_add = Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 7, def_MC7_ShieldWeakGRDAdd );//”íƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÅGRD‚ª‘½‚­‘‚¦‚éUŒ‚
				local grd_add_comp = Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 7, def_MC7_CompletedShieldWeakGRDAdd );//”íƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÅGRD‚ª‘½‚­‘‚¦‚éUŒ‚‚ğˆ—Ï‚İ‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
				
				Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_CompletedShieldWeakGRDAdd ); // ˆ—Ï‚İ‚Æ‚¢‚¤‚±‚Æ‚É‚·‚é
				
				local grd_add_timing = (!grd_add_comp && grd_add); // ‚±‚Ìƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚Åê—pGRD‘‰Áˆ—‚ğ“ü‚ê‚é
				
				Battle_Std.SetShieldSuccessCancelEffect(); // ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷‚É‚æ‚éƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹§ŒÀˆ—

				local isJumpAtk = Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 1, def_MC1_AtkJump );
				if( isJumpAtk ) //ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvUŒ‚‚È‚ç
				{
					enemy_atk_is_airatk = 1;
				}
				
				if( Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 2, def_MC2_ShieldSteady ) )
				{
					enemy_atk_is_shield_steady = 1;
				}
				
				enemy_LastDmgChr.pop();
				
				if( grd_add_timing )
				{
					//GRD‘‰ÁH
					// _dpn("ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚É‚æ‚éGRD‘‰Á:"+def_GRD_Shield_Success_WeakBonus);
					Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue( { val=def_GRD_Shield_Success_WeakBonus, boundplus=1 } );
					
					local enemy_LastDmgPlayer = BMvCore.GetLastDamageCharaData( 1 ); // ‰£‚ç‚ê‚½“Gî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
					if( enemy_LastDmgPlayer.push() )
					{
						BMvEff.GRD_AddValue( { val=def_GRD_Shield_Success_WeakBonus_Enemy, pressure=0 } ); //val:10000‚Å‚PƒXƒgƒbƒN
						
						enemy_LastDmgPlayer.pop();
					}
				}
			}
			
			// ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÉƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚·
			if( Def_Sys_ShieldSuccess_MinusVecTime )
			{
				if( BCMDTbl.CheckPosState(_PosState_Air) )
				{
					BMvEff.SetBoundSt( { addtime = -6 } );
				}
				else
				{
					if( Def_Sys_AirAtkShieldSubVecTime && enemy_atk_is_airatk )
					{
						local bound_time = BMvEff.SetBoundSt( { addtime = 0 } ); // Œ»İ’l
						if( bound_time >= 17 ) bound_time = 17; // ‹­ƒK[ƒh‚®‚ç‚¢‚ğŒÀŠE’l‚É‚·‚é
						local set_bound_time = bound_time - 6;
						if( set_bound_time <= 8 ) set_bound_time = 8; // ãƒK[ƒh-3F‚æ‚è‚ÍŒ¸‚ç‚È‚¢
						// _dpn("ƒV[ƒ‹ƒhƒK[ƒhd’¼:"+set_bound_time);
						BMvEff.SetBoundSt( { settime = set_bound_time } );
						
					}
					else if( enemy_atk_is_shield_steady )
					{
						BMvEff.SetBoundSt( { addtime = -2 } ); // ­‚µ‚¾‚¯Œ¸‚ç‚·
					}
					else
					{
						BMvEff.SetBoundSt( { addtime = -3 } );
					}
				}
			}			
			
			if( Def_Sys_Shield_EraseEnemyUniqCancel )
			{
				local enemy_LastDmgMainChr = BMvCore.GetLastDamageCharaData( 1 ); // ‰£‚ç‚ê‚½“G‚Ì–{‘Ì‚ğ“¾‚é
				
				if( enemy_LastDmgMainChr.push() )
				{
					if( Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 7, def_MC7_ActiveEraseEnemyUniqCancel ) )
					{
						if( Battle_Std.MoveCode.CheckFlag( def_MC_EnableCansel_SkilltoAtk ) )
						{
							Battle_Std.MoveCode.DelFlag( def_MC_EnableCansel_SkilltoAtk );
							_dpn("[ƒV[ƒ‹ƒhŒø‰Ê] •KE‹Z„’Êí‹Z ‚ğ•s‰Â‚É•ÏX");
						}
						
						if( Battle_Std.MoveCode.CheckFlag( def_MC_EnableCansel_SkilltoSkill ) )
						{
							Battle_Std.MoveCode.DelFlag( def_MC_EnableCansel_SkilltoSkill );
							_dpn("[ƒV[ƒ‹ƒhŒø‰Ê] •KE‹Z„•KE‹Z ‚ğ•s‰Â‚É•ÏX");
						}
					}

					enemy_LastDmgMainChr.pop();
				}
				
			}
			
			
			if( Def_Sys_HajiShield_EnemyNockBack )
			{
				// ‰æ–Ê’[”w•‰‚Á‚Ä‚½‚ç@•@’[‚Ö‚ÌƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ª“ü‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚½‚ç
				if( Battle_Std.GetHaimenStageHajiDistance() <= 0 && vec.x < 0 )
				{
					if( enemy_LastDmgChr.push() )
					{
						local nosend_vector = Battle_Std.MoveCode.CheckFlag(def_MC_NoSend_ScriptKnockBackVecor);
						
						enemy_LastDmgChr.pop();
						
						if( nosend_vector==0 )
						{
							local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “Gî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
							if( enemy.push() )
							{
								local divvec = BMvTbl.GetVector( { flags=_Vector_DivKeep } );
								if( divvec.x == 0 ) // ‰½‚àƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ªİ’è‚³‚ê‚Ä–³‚¢‚¾‚¯‚É‚·‚é
								{
									local nockbackvec = vec.x * 200 / 100;
									if( nockbackvec < -4000 ) nockbackvec = -4000; // ãŒÀ‚«‚ß‚Æ‚­
									BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=nockbackvec, addx=-nockbackvec/6, flags=_Vector_DivKeep } );
									// _dp("\n nockbackvec:"+nockbackvec);
								}
								enemy.pop();
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		// ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚É‚æ‚Á‚Äƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚Ä‚¨‚­
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		local nowpat = mvs.DataPattern;
		if( nowpat == def_PAT_GuardS ) Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_StatusStd );
		if( nowpat == def_PAT_GuardC ) Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_StatusCro );
		if( nowpat == def_PAT_GuardA ) Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_StatusAir );
		
		// ‚È‚ñ‚©ƒGƒ‰[‚ªo‚â‚·‚¢‚Ì‚Åhint—\–ñ‚ğ“ü‚ê‚½
		if( Def_Rule_GuardPat_HitStopAnime )
		{
			BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=nowpat, frame=1, rest=2 } );
			BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=nowpat, frame=2, rest=4 } );
			BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=nowpat, frame=3, rest=6 } );
			BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=nowpat, frame=4, rest=8 } );
			BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=nowpat, frame=5, rest=10 } );
		}
	}
	function Update_Std() // 
	{
		// ƒK[ƒhƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹—pˆ—
		local updateCnt = BMvTbl.AddLP(9,1); // ƒqƒbƒgƒXƒgƒbƒv‚àŠÜ‚ß‚Ä‰½ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€Œo‰ß‚µ‚½‚©‰ÁZ
		
		if( Def_Rule_GuardPat_HitStopAnime )
		{
			//ƒK[ƒh’†ƒqƒbƒgƒXƒgƒbƒv‚àƒAƒjƒ‚³‚¹‚é
			local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			local bst = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
			local nowFra = BMvTbl.Frame_Proc( 0, _ValAdd );
			// _dp("\n updateCnt:"+updateCnt+" bst:"+bst.BoundTime );
			if( mvs.FrameID == 10 && bst.isBound && bst.BoundTime == 0 && ( nowFra >= 0 && nowFra < Def_Rule_GuardPat_HitStopAnime ) )
			{
				//ƒK[ƒh‚Ìƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“•ƒqƒbƒgƒXƒgƒbƒv’†
				//6–‡ƒ‹[ƒv‚ÌƒK[ƒhƒ|[ƒYA3F–‡‚ÉŠG‚ği‚ß‚é
				//0 0
				//1 0
				//2 0
				//3 1
				//4 1
				//5 1
				//6 2 ...
				local guardFra = (updateCnt/5)%Def_Rule_GuardPat_HitStopAnime;
				// _dpn(nowFra+" -> guardFra:"+guardFra );
				BMvTbl.Frame_Proc( guardFra, _ValSet );
			}
		}
		
		//’nãó‘Ô‚Å‚Í•„†‚ª”½“]‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
		if( BMvTbl.CheckPosState( _PosState_Ground  ) )
		{
			//¡ƒvƒ‰ƒX‚ÌƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ÅAŸ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ªƒ}ƒCƒiƒXƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚©H
			local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector( { flags=_Vector_Bound });
			if( (( vec.x<0 ) && ( (vec.x + vec.addx)>=0 )) || (( vec.x>0 ) && ( (vec.x + vec.addx)<=0 )) )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, addx=0, flags=_Vector_Bound } );
			}
		}

		// SPó‘Ô‚Å‚ÌƒK[ƒh‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚ÅGRD‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€–ˆ‘‰Á’l‚ğ•Ï‚¦‚é
		// BMvEff.GuardSP_Success()‚Ì–ß‚è’l
		// 0 ‰½‚à‚È‚µ
		// 1 ¬Œ÷
		// -1 ƒuƒŒƒCƒN‹Z‚ğƒK[ƒh‚µ‚½
		// ƒK[ƒh¬—§‚ÉXV‚³‚êAŸ‚ÌƒK[ƒh¬—§‚Ü‚Å•Ï‰»‚µ‚Ü‚¹‚ñ
		local noChange = Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 1, def_MC1_GuardGRDChangeStop );
		if( !noChange )
		{
			if( BMvEff.GuardSP_Success() )
			{
				local useval = (BMvEff.GRD_GetJudgeResult()>0)? ( (BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } )>=6)? def_GRD_GuardSP_Frame_GRD6Vorpal : def_GRD_GuardSP_Frame_Vorpal) : def_GRD_GuardSP_Frame;
				Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue({ val=useval, pressure=0,boundplus=1 });
			}
			else
			{
				local useval = (BMvEff.GRD_GetJudgeResult()>0)? ( (BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } )>=6)? def_GRD_Guard_Frame_GRD6Vorpal : def_GRD_Guard_Frame_Vorpal) : def_GRD_Guard_Frame;
				Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue({ val=useval, pressure=0,boundplus=1 });
			}
		}


		//ƒWƒƒƒXƒg‚ÅƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğ¬Œ÷‚·‚é‚ÆAƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚µ‚½’¼Œã‚ÉƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ªo‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤
		//ƒK[ƒh‚ÌMv‚É“ü‚Á‚½’¼Œã•ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷’†‚ÍŒŸ’m‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚éc‚Æ
		//‚Í‚è‚Á‚Ï‚Ì‚ ‚Æo‚»‚¤‚Æ‚µ‚Ä‚à‚Å‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅA’¼‘O‚ÌMv‚ªƒV[ƒ‹ƒh1F‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚Ío‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
		//1‰ñ‚Ì“ü—Í‚ÅAƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¨ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚Æ‚È‚é‚Ì‚ª‚æ‚­‚È‚¢
		// local t_mvs_cnt = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus().MvCountFrame;
		// local justSpGguard = 0;//(t_mvs_cnt == 0 && BMvEff.GuardSP_Success() );
		// _dp("\n ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN:"+t_mvs_cnt+", "+BMvEff.GuardSP_Success()+" H:"+justSpGguard );
		local cmd_info0 = BCMDTbl.GetCmdInfoEx(0);
		local last_mvname = BMvTbl.ChangeMv_GetMvName();
		local isJustTiming = (cmd_info0.GetFrame() == 0 && (last_mvname=="Mv_Barrier_Std" || last_mvname=="Mv_Barrier_Cro" || last_mvname=="Mv_Barrier_Air") );
		// _dp("\n frame:"+cmd_info0.GetFrame()+" Mv:"+last_mvname+" -> "+isJustTiming );
		
		//•’Ê‚ÌƒK[ƒh‚Ì‚ÍƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚Ì“ü—Íƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		//ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÌƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒh‚ªŒã‚ë+D‚È‚Ì‚ÅA“Š‚°ƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒh‚Æ”í‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚ÄA–\”­‚ğ–h‚®‚½‚ß‚É1F’x‚ç‚¹‚é
		if( BMvEff.GuardSP_Get() != 1 && !isJustTiming ) // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒhó‘Ô‚Å‚È‚¢A•’Ê‚ÌƒK[ƒh‚Ì‚Æ‚«iƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®‚·‚é‚Æset‚Ì’lj
		{
			local grd = BMvEff.GRD_GetBreak( 0 ); //GRD‚ÌƒuƒŒƒCƒNó‘Ô‚ğæ“¾
			local notGRDBreak = !( grd==1 || grd==255 ); //ƒuƒŒƒCƒNorƒI[ƒo[ƒuƒŒƒCƒNˆÈŠO‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
			
			//ƒK[ƒh’†“Š‚°”²‚¯‚ğ“ü—Í‚µ‚½‚ÉƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ªo‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é@‚»‚Ì‚P
			if( BMvTbl.CheckCommandString( { command=[ "A+D" ], lastdelay=1, } ) )
			{
				BMvEff.GuardSP_Set( { val=0 } ); // ƒoƒŠƒAƒK[ƒhó‘Ô‰ğœ
				BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0); //ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh“ü—Íƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğÁ‚·				
			}
			else if( notGRDBreak ) //ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÍGRDƒuƒŒƒCƒN’†‚Ío‚¹‚È‚¢
			{
				local guard_shield = BMvTbl.GetLP(5); //Ÿ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ÅƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®‚·‚é‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©ƒtƒ‰ƒO(‚¨‚µ‚Á‚Ï‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€)
				
				/*
				// ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÌƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒh‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚½‚Ì‚Å‚±‚Ìˆ—‚Í•s—v
				// ‚¿‚å‚Á‚Æ“ËŠÑˆ—‚Á‚Û‚¢‚¯‚ÇA‚Ü‚Ÿ‘åä•v‚¾‚×
				if( BMvTbl.CheckCommandString( [ "214D" ] ) && BMvEff.GetPlayerMuteki( 0 ) == 10 && BMvEff.GetPlayerMuteki( 1 ) == 10 )
				{
					// _dp("\n ƒK[ƒhƒXƒ‰ƒXƒgo‚µ‚½‚¢I");
				}
				else
				*/
				if( BMvTbl.CheckCommandString( [ "4+D","7+D","1+D" ] ) || BMvTbl.TM_GetCancelShieldTiming() ) //D‚ğ‰Ÿ‚µ‚½‚çŸ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€—p‚Éƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ‚½‚Ä‚é
				{
					BMvTbl.AddLP(5,1); //Ÿ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Å”½‰
					//©•ª‚ª‹ó’†‚Ì‚Í‹ó’†“Š‚°”²‚¯–³‚¢‚Ì‚Å1F’x‚ç‚¹‚ğ–³‚­‚»‚¤i‚Ü‚Ÿ‚ ‚éƒLƒƒƒ‰‚à‚¢‚é‚¯‚ÇA‹óƒK‚Ìó‹µ‚à‚È‚¢‚µj
					//‚±‚ê‚Å–§“x‚Ì‚‚¢ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvUŒ‚‚à˜A‘±ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚Å‚«‚é‚æ
					if( BCMDTbl.CheckPosState( _PosState_Air ) )
					{
						guard_shield = BMvTbl.GetLP(5); //1F–Ú‚©‚çƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”½‰‚³‚¹‚é
					}
				}
				else
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0); //ƒ_ƒ‚È‚çƒŠƒZƒbƒg
				}
				
				local guard_shield_canseled = (BMvTbl.GetLP(7) >= 1 && BMvTbl.GetLP(6) > 4); // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®‰ñ”1‰ñˆÈã•”­“®ŠÔ‚à4FˆÈã
				if( guard_shield >= 1 && !guard_shield_canseled ) // Dƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğ‘O‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Å‰Ÿ‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚½‚çƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®•ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹‚µ‚Ä‚È‚©‚Á‚½‚ç
				{
					//ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®ˆ—
					
					BMvEff.GuardSP_Set( { val=1 } ); // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒhó‘Ô‚ÉƒZƒbƒg
					BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0); // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh“ü—Íƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğÁ‚·
					
					Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_GuardShiled ); // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒhó‘Ô
					
					// ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‚ÍƒŠƒZƒbƒg‚µ‚È‚¢BƒK[ƒhó‘Ô‚É‚È‚Á‚½“_‚Å‚µ‚©ƒŠƒZƒbƒg‚³‚ê‚È‚¢B
					// BMvTbl.SetLP(6,0); // ‰½ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®‚µ‚½‚©‚ÌƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^ƒŠƒZƒbƒg
					
					BMvTbl.AddLP(7,1); // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®‰ñ”‚ğ‰ÁZ

					//ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®’¼Œã‚ÉI—¹‚·‚éê‡‚à‚ ‚é‚Ì‚ÅƒRƒXƒg‚È‚Ç‚Í Mv_GuadShieldEff ‚Åˆ—‚·‚é‚æ‚¤‚É•ÏX
					//“Áê”»’è‚ÌÀ•W‚ÉƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒhƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‚ğ•\¦
					local rc = BMvEff.GetHanteiRect( { check=[ _Hantei_Etc, 1 ], flags=_HanteiFlag_NoMukiChange | _HanteiFlag_Tool  } );
					if( rc.sx != _Hantei_Error ) // ‘¶İ‚·‚é‚©
					{			
						BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=rc.sx, y=rc.sy, datatype=1, start_pat=74, mvname="Mv_GuadShieldEff", flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
					}
					
					// ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚Ì“®ì’†‚ğ”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚É‚·‚é
					if( Def_Sys_SetCounterHitFlagToShield )
					{
						BMvTbl.SetCounterHitFlag( { val=1, time=9999, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );//”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚é
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		if( BMvEff.GuardSP_Get() == 1 ) //ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh’†‚Ìˆ—
		{
			local guard_shield_cnt = BMvTbl.AddLP(6,1); // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒhó‘Ô‚ÌƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‰ÁZ
			//ƒK[ƒh’†“Š‚°”²‚¯‚ğ“ü—Í‚µ‚½‚ÉƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ªo‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é@‚»‚Ì‚Q
			
			// print("\n guard_shield_cnt:"+ guard_shield_cnt);
		
			//A+D‚Ì“¯‰Ÿ‚µ‚ğD¨A‚Æ‚µ‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¢Aæ‚ÉƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ª”­“®‚µ‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚Á‚½ê‡‚ÍA‚±‚±‚Å“Š‚°ƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒh‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚µ‚Ä‰ğœ
			if( guard_shield_cnt <=2 && BMvTbl.CheckCommandString( { command=[ "A+D" ], lastdelay=1, } ) )
			{
				BMvEff.GuardSP_Set( { val=0 } ); // ƒoƒŠƒAƒK[ƒhó‘Ô‰ğœ
				BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0); //ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh“ü—Íƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğÁ‚·
				BMvEff.SetCharaFlash( { color = 0xAAAAFF, type = 0, time = 0 } ); //ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh“ü—Í‚Å‚Ì”­Œõ‚ğÁ‚·
			}
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std() : (callLandEffect)
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		
		//‹ó’†ƒK[ƒh’†‚É’…’n‚µ‚½‚ç—§‚¿ƒK[ƒhƒ|[ƒY‚ÉˆÚs
		if( mvs.isLanding )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_GuardS);
			//YƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚¾‚¯‰Šú‰»i’…’nE”O‚Ì‚½‚ßj
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { y=0, addy=0, flags=_Vector_Normal|_Vector_Div|_Vector_Bound|_Vector_Keep|_Vector_DivKeep } );
			//’…’nƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‚ğŒÄ‚Ô
			callLandEffect(); // ’…’nƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg
			
			// ƒK[ƒhƒtƒ[ƒg‚©‚ç‚Ì’…’n‚¾‚Á‚½‚ç‘ÅŒ‚–³“G‚ğíœ
			if( BMvTbl.GetLP(8) ==1 ) // –³“G‚ğƒZƒbƒg‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½
			{
				// ‘ÅŒ‚–³“G‚ğíœ
				BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=0, muteki_dageX=0 } );
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		//ó‘Ô‚ÌˆÚs‚¾‚¯s‚¤
		BtlMvStd.SetGuardFinalize();
		//print("SetGuardFinalize()");
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		//ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğ”­“®‚µ‚½‚ªUŒ‚‚ğ‚Æ‚ê‚È‚©‚Á‚½ê‡‚Ì‚İGRDƒRƒXƒg‚ğÁ”ï‚·‚é
		//ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ğ“ü—Í‚µ‚Ä‚Ä@‚©‚Â@ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚Í‚È‚¢‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		if( BMvEff.GuardSP_Get()!=0 && BMvEff.GuardSP_Success()!=1 )
		{
			//_dm("ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒhƒRƒXƒgÁ”ï");
			
			//¸”s‚É‚æ‚éƒRƒXƒgÁ”ï
			Battle_Std.UseGRDStock( def_GRD_GuardShieldCost, def_GRD_GuardShieldNotEnough_Enemy); // Á”ïGRD”, ‘Šè‘‰ÁGRD’l(’PˆÊˆá’ˆÓ)
			
			_dp("\n ¸”sƒV[ƒ‹ƒhI");
			Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 1, def_MC1_ShieldFailed ); // ¸”sƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚È‚Ì
		}
		
		if( Def_Rule_GroundGuardSlideKeep )
		{
			//’nãó‘Ô‚Å‚ÍŠµ«‚ğc‚·
			if( BMvTbl.CheckPosState( _PosState_Ground  ) )
			{
				local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector( { flags=_Vector_Bound } );
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=vec.x, addx=-vec.x/10, flags=_Vector_DivKeep } );
				// print("\n vec.x:"+vec.x);
			}
		}		
		
		// ƒK[ƒhƒtƒ[ƒg‚Å•‚‚¢‚ÄA‹ó’†‚¾‚Á‚½‚ç‹óƒK•s”\‹Z‚Åƒnƒ‚ç‚ê‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É–³“GŠÔ‚ğƒZƒbƒg‚·‚é
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(8) ==1 && BMvTbl.CheckPosState( _PosState_Air ) ) // ƒK[ƒhƒtƒ[ƒg‚Å–³“G‚ğƒZƒbƒg‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½,‹ó’†‚¾‚Á‚½
		{
			// ”²‚¯‚é‚Æ‚«‚É­‚µ‘ÅŒ‚–³“G‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚éiƒnƒ‰ñ”ğj
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=def_FL_ModoriGuardFloatDagekiMuteki, muteki_dageX=def_FL_ModoriGuardFloatDagekiMuteki } );
		}
		
		BtlMvStd.ClearGuard(); // ó‘Ô‰ğœ
		Battle_Std.Reversal.SetTime(); //ƒŠƒo[ƒTƒ‹ó•t{”»’èƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg¶¬
		//print("ClearGuard()");
		
	}
}

//ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg
//LP‚ª1‚ÌŠÔ‚Í¬Œ÷ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚µ‚ÄA¬Œ÷ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‚à‚±‚Á‚©‚çŒÄ‚Ô
Std_MoveTable.Mv_GuadShieldEff <-
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_ParentMove } );
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //0: ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚Ì¬Œ÷‚ğƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚·‚é 1:‚à‚¤ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚µ‚È‚¢
		
		//ŒÄ‚Ño‚µŒãA‚»‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€“à‚ÉI—¹‚µ‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤ƒP[ƒX‚ª—L‚é
		if( BMvTbl.CheckMvUniqueID( { datatype=_DataType_Player } ) == 0 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
		else
		{
			//ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®‚ªŠm’è‚µ‚½
			//‚±‚±‚ÅƒQ[ƒW‚Æ‚©Á”ï‚·‚é‚æ‚¤‚É•ÏX 18/6/13
			local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
			if( player.push() )
			{
				BSound.SE_Play( { type=_SeType_Normal, num=193 } ); //ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®‰¹

				local guardshield_flashtime = 10;
			
				//”­“®‚ÌƒQ[ƒWÁ”ï
				if( Def_Sys_GuardShield_UseEXS )
				{
					BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { value=def_SP_GuardShield_Init } );
				}
				
				if( Def_Sys_GuardShield_PlusVecTime )
				{
					local bound_time = BMvEff.SetBoundSt( { addtime = 0 } ); // Œ»İ’l
					local set_bound_time = bound_time + Def_Sys_GuardShield_PlusVecTime;
					if( set_bound_time < Def_Sys_GuardShield_PlusVecTimeMin ) set_bound_time = Def_Sys_GuardShield_PlusVecTimeMin;
					guardshield_flashtime = set_bound_time;
					
					// BMvEff.SetBoundSt( { addtime = Def_Sys_GuardShield_PlusVecTime } ); // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®‚·‚é‚Æd’¼‚ª‘‰Á‚·‚é
					_dpn("ƒK[ƒhŠÔ:"+bound_time+" -> "+set_bound_time);
					BMvEff.SetBoundSt( { settime = set_bound_time } ); // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”­“®‚·‚é‚Æd’¼‚ª‘‰Á‚·‚é
				}
				
				//©•ª‚ğ­‚µ—£‚ê‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
				//ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒhƒK[ƒh’†‚Ì‚İ‚¾‚©‚çDIV‚ğ‚¢‚¶‚Á‚Ä‚à‘åä•v‚È‚Í‚¸
				//‘Šè‚ğ‚¢‚¶‚é‚Ì‚Í‰½‚©•|‚¢B’nã‚Ì‚İ‚É‚·‚éH
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=-1400, addx=200, flags=_Vector_DivKeep } );//UNIst x:-1000, add:200
				
				Battle_Std.SetCharaFlash_GuardSPCommand( guardshield_flashtime ); //”­“®‚Ì”­Œõ

				player.pop();
			}
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local mode = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
		
		switch( mode )
		{
		case 0: //Mv‚Ì•Ï‰»‚ğƒ`ƒFƒbƒNB•Ï‚í‚Á‚½‚ç‚à‚¤ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚Í¬Œ÷‚·‚é‚Í‚¸‚ª‚È‚¢B
			local mv = Battle_Std.GetPlayerMvName();
			if( mv!="Mv_Guard" )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1)
				// print("\nƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷ƒ`ƒFƒbƒNI—¹");
				return;
			}
			//Mv‚ª•Ï‚í‚ç‚¸ƒK[ƒh‚r‚o‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‚½‚çƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‚ğŒÄ‚Ô
			
			local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ‘€ìe‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
			if( player.push() )
			{
				local guardSP_get = BMvEff.GuardSP_Get(); //SPƒK[ƒhiƒoƒŠƒKjó‘Ô‚Ìæ“¾
				local guardSP_success = BMvEff.GuardSP_Success(); //SPƒK[ƒhiƒoƒŠƒKjó‘Ô‚ÅƒK[ƒh‚ª¬—§‚µ‚½‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
				local guardSP_effcallok = (guardSP_get==0 && guardSP_success); //SPƒK[ƒhiƒoƒŠƒKjó‘Ô‚¶‚á‚È‚¢ƒK[ƒh‚Å‚©‚ÂSPƒK[ƒh‚ª¬—§
				
				local is_end = ( guardSP_effcallok || guardSP_get!=1 )? 1 : 0;
				if( is_end )
				{
					BMvEff.SetCharaFlash( { type = 0 } ); //”­Œõ‚ğ~‚ß‚é
				}
				
				player.pop();
				
				if( guardSP_effcallok )
				{
					// print("\nƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷");
					local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat=72, } );
					if( eff.push() )
					{
						BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_ParentMove|_ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll } );
						BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseParentNull } ); // ‚±‚ê‚ª‚È‚¢‚Æ_ObjFlags_ParentMove‚ª‚¨‚©‚µ‚­‚È‚é
						eff.pop();
					}
					BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1);
				}
				if( is_end )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); // Á–Å‚Ö
				}
			}
			
			break;
		case 1: //‚l‚–‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚½orƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‚ğ‚à‚¤‚æ‚ñ‚¾Œã
			break;	
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

local val_StdTyouhatsuType = chrparam.Get( { type="—§‚¿’§”­ƒ^ƒCƒv", chrnum=_ChrNo } ); 

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Neutral <- // ƒjƒ…[ƒgƒ‰ƒ‹‘Ò‹@
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{	
		// Mv_Standby‚©‚ç‚«‚½‚Æ‚«‚Í“¯‚¶PAT‚É‚µ‚È‚¢
		// ‚±‚Ìˆ—–ß‚Á‚Ä‚­‚é‚½‚Ñ‚É‚·‚é‚Ì‚¿‚å‚Á‚ÆŒ™‚ÈŠ´‚¶‚ª‚ ‚é
		if( BMvTbl.ChangeMv_GetMvName() == "Mv_Standby" && BMvTbl.GetMvStatus().DataPattern == def_PAT_Neutral )
		{
			// _dpn("‰½‚à‚µ‚È‚¢");
		}
		else
		{
			BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Neutral);
		}
		
		Battle_Std.InitCharaVector(); //Šµ«‚ğc‚µ‚½ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetFinalizeCode(0); //‰Šúó‘Ô‚ª0‚Å‚È‚¢{GetFinalizeCode‚Å•ªŠò‚µ‚Ä‚é‚Ì‚Å‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=1, time=255, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // Mv’†s“®‰Â”\‚É‚·‚é
		
		BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_TMP0,0); // ’§”­‘Ò‹@
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std() : (val_StdTyouhatsuType)
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=1, time=32, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangePattern|_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // ƒ‹[ƒv‚ÉŠÜ‚ß‚és“®‰Â”\
		
		if( BMvTbl.CheckFurimuki() ) // U‚èŒü‚«ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256); // ƒR[ƒh256‚ğU‚èŒü‚«‚Æ‚µ‚ÄFinalize
		}


		// ’Êí‚ÆAƒgƒŒ[ƒjƒ“ƒOiƒLƒƒƒ‰ŒÅ—L‚Å’§”­—LŒø‚Ì‚İj‚É’§”­‚ğs‚¤
		// ƒ`ƒ…[ƒgƒŠƒAƒ‹‚Æƒ~ƒbƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ÍœŠO
		local stage_st = BMvTbl.GetMvStageStatus().IsTrainingBattle(); // 1-Training  2-Tutorial  3-Mission
		local appeal = BMvEff.TM_GetCharaGauge(Def_TMCG_EnableAppeal);//’§”­‚Ì—L–³
		if( stage_st == 0 || ( stage_st == 1 && appeal ) )
		{
			local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
			//’§”­Ä¶ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
			switch( val_StdTyouhatsuType )
			{
			case 0: // ’§”­–³‚µ
				break;
			case 1: // ’§”­‚ ‚èEƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ ‚è
				if( BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_TMP0) <= 0 && s.MvCount>def_FL_NeutralWaitMin && s.MvCount%def_FL_NeutralWaitStep==0 && BMvTbl.GetFinalizeCode() == 0 ) //U‚èŒü‚«’†‚Í”½‰‚µ‚È‚¢‚Æ‚©
				{
					if( BMvEff.Random_Limit(100) < 15 ) //”­“®Šm—¦
					{
						if (BMvTbl.JumpFrameID( 512 )!= -1 ) //‚ ‚Á‚½H
						{
							BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_TMP0,10); // 10FŒã‚É’§”­‚ğs‚¤—\–ñ
							Battle_Std.CallSkillSoonCache( 512, 10 );
						}
					}
				}
				break;
			case 2: // ’§”­‚ ‚èEƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“–³‚µ ¦b’è–¢À‘•
				// if( Def_Dbg_NoPlayUpsetSE == 0 ) Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="’§”­" });
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		if( BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_TMP0)>0 )
		{
			// _dp("\n ’§”­—\–ñ:"+BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_TMP0) );
			local left_frame = BMvTbl.AddPP(def_PP_TMP0,-1);
			if( left_frame <=0 )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(512); // —§‚¿‘Ò‹@ƒAƒjƒ‚Ö
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral", [256,"Mv_Furimuki_S"], [512,"Mv_NeutralWait"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}

local footstepA_senum = chrparam.Get( { type="‘«‰¹‚`_‚r‚d”Ô†", chrnum=_ChrNo } );
local footstepB_senum = chrparam.Get( { type="‘«‰¹‚a_‚r‚d”Ô†", chrnum=_ChrNo } );



//Param0‚ª10‚Ì‘«‰¹‚ğÄ¶‚·‚é
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Null_PlayFootstepSE <- 
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_ParentMove|_ObjFlags_NoRender|_ObjFlags_EraseParentPatChange } );
		BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseParentNull|_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0);
		
		//ƒfƒoƒbƒO—pŠÔŠu•\¦
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0);
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std() : ( footstepA_senum, footstepB_senum)
	{
		local my_st = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( my_st.CallCount!=0 )
		{
			return;
		}
		
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
		if( player.isdone() )
		{
			local playtiming = 0;
			if( player.push() )
			{
				if( Battle_Std.GetUpdateParam0() == 10 )
				{
					playtiming = 1;
				}
				player.pop();
			}

			if( playtiming==1 )
			{
				local setype = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
				BMvTbl.SetLP(0, 1-setype ); // 0 1 0 1 ...
				switch( setype )
				{
				case 0:
					if( footstepA_senum != -1 ) BSound.SE_Play( { type=_SeType_Normal, num=footstepA_senum } );
					break;
				case 1:
					if( footstepB_senum != -1 ) BSound.SE_Play( { type=_SeType_Normal, num=footstepB_senum } );
					break;
				}
				// ƒfƒoƒbƒO
				local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
				local aida = mvs.MvCount - BMvTbl.GetLP(1);
				BMvTbl.SetLP(1,mvs.MvCount);
				if( aida != 0 )
				{
					// print("\n‚r‚dŠÔŠuF"+aida+" setype:"+setype+" mvs.MvCount:"+mvs.MvCount);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}

local walk_vectorar = chrparam.Get( { type="•à‚«ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹", chrnum=_ChrNo } );
local walk_f_vector = { start=walk_vectorar[0], loop=walk_vectorar[1] };
local walk_b_vector = { start=walk_vectorar[2], loop=walk_vectorar[3] };

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Walk_F <- // ‘Oi
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.InitCharaVector(); //Šµ«‚ğc‚µ‚½ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Walk_F);
		Battle_Std.Call_FootStepSE(); // //‘«‰¹Ä¶ŠÄ‹ƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std() : (walk_f_vector)
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.MvCount == 0 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=walk_f_vector.start, y=0, addx=0, addy=0, flags=_Vector_Normal } );
		}
		else if( mvs.MvCount == 2 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=walk_f_vector.loop, y=0, addx=0, addy=0, flags=_Vector_Normal } );
		}		
		
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=1, time=32, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangePattern|_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // ƒ‹[ƒv‚ÉŠÜ‚ß‚és“®‰Â”\
		if( BMvTbl.CheckStickHold( (1<<0) ) || !BMvTbl.CheckStickHold( (1<<6) ) )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_WalkStop_F" );
	}
}

// ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚ÌƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“
// VORPAL‚ÌƒAƒiƒEƒ“ƒX‚Æ‚ ‚í‚¹‚é‚½‚ßAŒü‚«‚Å‚Í‚È‚­1P‚Æ2P‚ÌƒTƒCƒh‚Å•ª‚¯‚é
// ‹}‚Éo‚Ä‚«‚Ä‚Ñ‚Á‚­‚è‚·‚é‚Æ‚¢‚¤ˆÓŒ©‚à‚ ‚Á‚½‚Ì‚ÅA­‚µƒtƒF[ƒhƒCƒ“—v‘f‚ğ‹­‚ß{ˆÊ’u‚ğƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“Šñ‚è‚É•ÏX
Std_MoveTable.Mv_CVPCutinGrp <- //ƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“ ƒXƒNƒ[ƒ‹‚Ì‰e‹¿‚ğó‚¯‚È‚¢
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoCamera|_ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll|_ObjFlags_NoGround } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(996);
		BMvTbl.SetPrio( _CharaPrio_Parent_BG  );//BGè‘O
		
		if( Def_Sys_VorpalCutinPosType == 0 )
		{
			local pside = BMvTbl.GetPlayerSide();
			local pos_muki = ( pside == 0)? 1 : -1;
			local grp_muki = ( pside == 0)? _Direction_Right : _Direction_Left;
			// _dpn("psidepside:"+pside);
			// BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=-520*BMvTbl.GetMuki()*128, y=-8*128 } );
			BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=-520*pos_muki*128, y=-8*128 } );
			BMvTbl.SetMuki( grp_muki );
		}
		else
		{
			local muki = BMvTbl.GetMuki();
			BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=-520*muki*128, y=-8*128 } );
		}

		// ƒtƒF[ƒhƒCƒ“•­‚µˆÃ‚­‚µ‚Ä‚Ñ‚Á‚­‚è‚µ‚É‚­‚­’²®
		if( Def_Sys_VorpalCutinDrawType )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetDrawAlpha( { val=0, time=255, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // ƒtƒF[ƒhƒCƒ“İ’è
			BMvEff.SetCharaColor( { color=0xCCCCCC, type=3, time=255 } ); // ­‚µˆÃ‚­‚·‚é
		}
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		local mvcount = mvs.MvCount;
		if( mvcount == 13 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { y=-10 } );
		}
		
		// ƒtƒF[ƒhƒCƒ“‚Å‚Ñ‚Á‚­‚è‚µ‚É‚­‚­’²®
		if( Def_Sys_VorpalCutinDrawType )
		{
			local fadeInFrame = 30;
			if( mvcount < fadeInFrame )
			{
				local par = mvcount * 100 / fadeInFrame;
				local alpha = 128 + (127 * par / 100);
				// _dpn("par:"+par+" alpha:"+alpha);
				BMvTbl.SetDrawAlpha( { val=alpha, time=255, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
			}
			else if( mvcount == fadeInFrame )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetDrawAlpha( { val=255, time=0, flag=0 } ); // ’Êí•`‰æ‚É–ß‚·
			}
		}
		
		// KOŒã‚Í‚·‚®‚ÉÁ‚¦‚é
		if( Battle_Std.CharaisKO() )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize();
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}			
}

// EXƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“‚ÌŠG‚¾‚¯

//b’èƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“ˆ—
Std_MoveTable.Mv_CutinObject <- //ƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“ ƒXƒNƒ[ƒ‹‚Ì‰e‹¿‚ğó‚¯‚È‚¢
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		local effcallflag = false;
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0) == 0) //‚Ü‚¾o‚Ä‚È‚¢
		{
			//“Áê”»’è‚ğŒ©‚Â‚¯‚½‚çƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“W’†ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‚ğ‚»‚±‚Éo‚·
			local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ‘€ìe‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
			if( player.push() )
			{
				local rc = BMvEff.GetHanteiRect( { check=[ _Hantei_Etc, 0 ], flags=_HanteiFlag_NoMukiChange | _HanteiFlag_Tool  } );
				if( rc.sx != _Hantei_Error ) // ‘¶İ‚·‚é‚©
				{	
					local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=rc.sx, y=rc.sy, mvname="", datatype=1, start_pat=43, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
					if( eff.push() )
					{
						BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_ParentMove|_ObjFlags_EraseParentPatChange|_ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll } );	
						eff.pop();
					}
					effcallflag = true;
				}
				player.pop();
			}
			if( effcallflag ) BMvTbl.SetLP( 0, 1); //ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒgo‚µ‚½			
		}
		else
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		if( s.MvCount>60 ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}			
}

local chp_DashJumpKansei = chrparam.Get( { type="ƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvŠµ«", chrnum=_ChrNo } );

//’nãƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv‚Ì—£—¤‚©‚ç’…’n‚Ü‚Å‘±‚·‚éƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg
Std_MoveTable.Mv_JumpKanseiCheckObject <- //ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv’†‚ÌŠµ«iû‘©ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚¶‚á‚È‚­‚·‚éj
{
	function Init_Std() : (chp_DashJumpKansei)
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender } );	
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } )
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( { y=1024, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } );
		
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[î•ñæ“¾
		if( player.push() )
		{
			local v = BMvTbl.GetVector( { flags=_Vector_DivKeep } );
			
			local use_dashJumpKansei = chp_DashJumpKansei;
			//_dpn("ƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvŠµ«:"+use_dashJumpKansei);
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=v.x*use_dashJumpKansei/100, addx=0, flags=_Vector_DivKeep } ); //ƒƒ‹ƒuƒ‰ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv

			player.pop();
		}
	}
	function Update_Std() // 
	{
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[î•ñæ“¾
		if( player.push() )
		{
			local playerisground = ( (BMvTbl.CheckPosState( _PosState_Ground ))==1 );
			if( playerisground )	
			{
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, addx=0, flags=_Vector_DivKeep } ); //ƒƒ‹ƒuƒ‰ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvI—¹
			}
			player.pop();
		
			if ( playerisground )	
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
				BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_JumpStatus,0); //ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒX‚ğ‰Šú‰»
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}	
}

{
	BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
	local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector( { flags=_Vector_DivKeep } );
	Battle_Std.InitCharaVector(); //Šµ«‚ğc‚µ‚½ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
	BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=vec.x, addx=-vec.x/20, flags=_Vector_DivKeep } ); //ƒƒ‹ƒuƒ‰ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv
	BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=def_FL_JumpWaitNageMuteki, muteki_nageX=def_FL_JumpWaitNageMuteki } ); //ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvˆÚs‚Í“Š‚°–³“G‚Éİ’è
	
	// BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0);
	
	BMvTbl.AirSkill_Begin(); // ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv€”õƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Ì‚Í‚¶‚ß‚ÉŒÄ‚Ô

	Battle_Std.PassBeforeMoveCodeEx( 2, def_MC2_DashKansei ); // ƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…Šµ«ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğˆø‚«Œp‚®
}

// ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv‚Å‹ó’†‚É•‚‚¢‚½‹¤’Êˆ—
local func_JumpFlightInit = function()
{
	BMvTbl.AirSkill_Check(); // ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvŠJnƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Ì‚Í‚¶‚ß‚ÉŒÄ‚Ô
	
	Battle_Std.PassBeforeMoveCodeEx( 2, def_MC2_DashKansei ); // ƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…Šµ«ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğˆø‚«Œp‚®
	Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 1, def_MC1_Jump ); // ‹ó’†‘®«
	
	Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_LightLegsMuteki );//¬‘«‘®«‚Ö‹óU‚è—\’è‚ÌMV
	BMvTbl.SetHitCheckFlag( { type=0, val=Def_HitCheckFlag_LightLegs, time=255, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // ¬‘«–³“G‚ÉƒXƒJ‚é
	
	BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_JumpFrame,0);//—£—¤‚©‚ç‰½ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚©‚ğ‹L‰¯‚·‚é
	Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 3, def_MC3_EnableJumpFramePP ); // def_PP_JumpFrame‚ª—LŒø‚ÈMV
}

//“ñ’iƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv‚Å•‚‚¢‚½‚Æ‚«‚Ì‹¤’Êˆ—
local func_MultiJumpInit = function()
{
	local angle = Battle_Std.CCharaVector_GetToolAngle();
	local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=0, mvname="", datatype=1, start_pat=233, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
	if( eff.push() )
	{
		BMvTbl.SetAngle({ angle=angle });
		
		eff.pop();
	}
	
	BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=0, mvname="Mv_JumpKanseiCheckObject", flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
	BMvTbl.SetAirJumpOK(0);  //Ä“xƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv‚Å‚«‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
	BMvTbl.AddAirJumpCount(1);  //‰ñ”‚ğ‰ÁZ
	
	Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_LightLegsMuteki );//¬‘«‘®«‚Ö‹óU‚è—\’è‚ÌMV
	BMvTbl.SetHitCheckFlag( { type=0, val=Def_HitCheckFlag_LightLegs, time=255, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // ¬‘«–³“G‚ÉƒXƒJ‚é
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_41236SP_ABC <-
{

// ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv‘Ò‹@
Std_MoveTable.Mv_JumpWait_F <- 
{
	function Init_Std() : (func_JumpWaitInit)
	{
		func_JumpWaitInit();
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Jump_F);
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std() : (func_JumpWaitFU)
	{
		if( BMvTbl.CheckPosState( _PosState_Air ) == 1) //‹ó’†‚Éi‚ñ‚¾
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}		
		func_JumpWaitFU();
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_nageX=0 } ); //”²‚¯‚é‚Æ‚«‚É‰Šú‰»
		if( BMvTbl.CheckPosState( _PosState_Air ) == 1) //‹ó’†‚Éi‚ñ‚¾
		{
			BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="", datatype=1, start_pat=255, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );		
			BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=0, mvname="Mv_JumpKanseiCheckObject", flags=_Position_ToolShift } );		
		}
		
		// ƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv‚Å‚©‚©‚è•KE‹Z‚ÅƒX[‚Á‚ÆŠŠ‚é‚Ì‚ª‹­‚¢‚Ì‚Å•â³
		if( !BMvTbl.FromFinalize() )
		{
			local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector( { flags=_Vector_DivKeep } );
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=vec.x*100/150, addx=-vec.x/10,flags=_Vector_DivKeep } ); // û‘©ƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…Šµ«‚ğŒ¸­
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( s.MvCount<4 ) _dem("yŒxzƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvˆÚsƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ª["+(s.MvCount-1)+"F]‚µ‚©‚ ‚è‚Ü‚¹‚ñ")

		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Jump_F" ); //

		//‘O‚Ìs“®‚ªƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹‚¾‚Á‚½‚çÁ”ï
		//‚±‚±‚ÅÁ”ï‚µ‚È‚¢‚ÆƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹‚ğƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹‚µ‚½‚Æ‚«‚Éƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ª‚½‚Á‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤
		if( BMvTbl.ChangeMv_GetMvName() == "Mv_JumpCanselWait_F" )
		{
			Battle_Std.EnemyDamageFlag_Add( def_DF_JumpCanseled ); // uƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹‚ğ‚³‚ê‚½v‚ğ‘Šè‚É‹L‰¯
		}	
	}
}

local val_CroTyouhatsuType = chrparam.Get( { type="‚µ‚á‚ª‚İ’§”­ƒ^ƒCƒv", chrnum=_ChrNo } ); 


Std_MoveTable.Mv_Crouch_Wait <- // ‚µ‚á‚ª‚İ‘Ò‹@
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		// BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto); // ‚±‚ê•s—vB‚ ‚é‚Æ‚µ‚á‚ª‚İU‚èŒü‚«”­¶‚µ‚È‚¢‚Å‚µ‚å
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Crouch_Wait);
		Battle_Std.InitCharaVector(); //Šµ«‚ğc‚µ‚½ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //’§”­ó‘Ô‚©ƒtƒ‰ƒO@0:’Êí 1:’§”­
		BMvTbl.SetFinalizeCode(0); //‰Šúó‘Ô‚ª0‚Å‚È‚¢{GetFinalizeCode‚Å•ªŠò‚µ‚Ä‚é‚Ì‚Å‰Šú‰»
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std() : (val_CroTyouhatsuType)
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=1, time=32, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangePattern|_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // ƒ‹[ƒv‚ÉŠÜ‚ß‚és“®‰Â”\
		
		// ƒgƒŒƒ‚“I‚È‚â‚ÂˆÈŠO‚È‚ç’§”­‚ğs‚¤
		if( BMvTbl.GetMvStageStatus().IsTrainingBattle() == 0 )
		{
			switch( val_CroTyouhatsuType )
			{
			case 0: // ’§”­–³‚µ
				break;
			case 1: // ’§”­‚ ‚èEƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ ‚è
				local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
				
				if( s.MvCount>def_FL_NeutralWaitMin && s.MvCount%def_FL_NeutralWaitStep==0 && BMvTbl.GetFinalizeCode() == 0 && BMvTbl.GetLP(0)==0 )
				{
					local r = BMvEff.Random_Limit(100);
					if( r<20 ) //”­“®Šm—¦
					{
						if (BMvTbl.JumpFrameID( 512 )!= -1 ) //‚ ‚Á‚½H
						{
							BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); //’§”­ó‘Ô‚Ö
							if( Def_Dbg_NoPlayUpsetSE == 0 ) Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="’§”­" });
						}
					}
				}
				if( Battle_Std.GetUpdateFrameID( s ) == 900 && BMvTbl.GetLP(0)==1 )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //’§”­ó‘Ô‰ğœ
				}		
				break;
			}
		}
	}	
	function Update_Std() // 
	{
		// ‰º•ûŒü‚Å‚È‚¢‚Æ‚«—§‚¿ã‚ª‚é
		if( !BMvTbl.CheckStickHold( (1<<1) | (1<<2) | (1<<3) ) )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); // ƒR[ƒh0‚ğ—§‚¿ã‚ª‚è‚Æ‚µ‚ÄFinalize
		}
		else if( BMvTbl.CheckFurimuki() ) // U‚èŒü‚«ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256); // ƒR[ƒh256‚ğU‚èŒü‚«‚Æ‚µ‚ÄFinalize
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Crouch_toN", [256,"Mv_Furimuki_C"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}
