const DBGM_ForwardShift = 1; // ƒfƒoƒbƒO—p‚É«”\‚ğ•\¦‚·‚éˆ—

// ƒNƒŠ[ƒsƒ“ƒOƒGƒbƒW
Std_MoveTable.Mv_ForwardShift <-
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.InitVector(); //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_ForwardShift);
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 1, def_MC1_ChainShiftOK ); // ƒc[ƒ‹‚ÅCS‰Â‚È‚ç‹óU‚èCS‰Â”\

		Battle_Std.Sousai.Init_NoHitFlag( 2 ); // ‘ŠE
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
		
		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,1,0); // UŒ‚‚ğó‚¯~‚ß‚½‰ñ”@¦•ÊMV‚©‚ç‚àQÆ‚ ‚è
		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,2,0); // ƒRƒXƒg‚ğÁ”ï‚µ‚½‚©
		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,3,0); // d’¼‚Ü‚Åi‚ñ‚¾
		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,4,0); // Œ¸‚ç‚·d’¼ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ 0`4
		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,5,BMvTbl.GetMuki()); // ŠJn‚ÌŒü‚«

		if( DBGM_ForwardShift )
		{
			local pos = BMvTbl.GetPosition();
			BMvTbl.SetLP(0,pos.x);//ŠJnÀ•W
			BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0);//–³“GF
			BMvTbl.SetLP(2,0);
			BMvTbl.SetLP(3,0);//d‚È‚èoŒ»F
			BMvTbl.SetLP(4,0);//d‚È‚èoŒ»À•W
		}
		
		// Def_Sys_EnemyAntenStopNoCancel ‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚±‚Ìƒtƒ‰ƒO‚Í”íˆÃ“]‚ÅÁ‚¦‚Ü‚·
		// CS‚âEX‹ZŒ©‚Ä‚©‚çCS‚ğ•s‰Â‚É‚·‚é‚½‚ß
		BMvTbl.SetAsFlag( { as_flags=_AsFlag_ChainShift, time=8, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
		
		//3D‚Å1F“–g‚È‚Ì‚ÅƒYƒ‰‚µ‰Ÿ‚µ‚Å‚«‚é‚Æ‹­‚·‚¬‚é
		//‚Å‚à3D>A‚Å“Š‚°‚ğ“ü‚ê‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚±‚Æ‚à‘½X‚ ‚Á‚ÄƒXƒgƒŒƒX‚ª‚â‚Î‚¢
		//3DD‚ÌCS‚ğ‰Â”\‚É‚·‚é‚È‚çA‚±‚ê‚àƒeƒNƒjƒbƒN‚Æ‚µ‚Äc‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚¢‚¾‚ë‚Á‚Ä‹C‚ª‚·‚éi“–g¬—§‚©‚ç“Š‚°‚ªo‚È‚¯‚ê‚Î‚¢‚¢j
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 1, def_MC1_CmdLvDZ_OK ); // “¯‰Ÿ‚µ‚Å‘¼‚Ìs“®‚ÉˆÚ‚ê‚és“®
		
		//“Š‚°‚Éã‚­‚µ‚½‚¢‚Ì‚Å“Š‚°–³“G‚Ííœ‚·‚éiƒŠƒoƒT‚Ì–³“G‚ğÁ‚·—pj
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_nageX=0 } ); // ‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è‚È‚Ç‚Ì“Š‚°–³“G‚ğÁ‚·
	}
	function UseCost()
	{
		local cs_flag = BCMDTbl.CheckCancel( _SkillType_ChainShift ); // CS‰Â”\‚Èó‘ÔH
		if( BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,1) == 0 && !cs_flag ) // UŒ‚‚ğó‚¯~‚ß‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢¸”s@•@CS‰Â”\‚ÈŠÔ‚ÍƒRƒXƒgÁ”ï‚µ‚È‚¢
		{
			if( BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,2) == 0 ) // ƒRƒXƒg‚ğÁ”ï‚µ‚½‚©
			{
				BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,2,1); // ƒRƒXƒg‚ğÁ”ï‚µ‚½‚©
				BMvEff.GRD_UseStock( { val=2 } );
				// _dpn("ƒRƒXƒgÁ”ï");
			}
		}
	}
	function Start_Std()
	{
		// ‰ñ”ğ‚ÌƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‚ğŒÄ‚Ô
		// ƒYƒ‰‚µ‰Ÿ‚µ‚È‚Ç‚à‚ ‚é‚µƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚Í­‚µ’x‚ç‚¹‚é
		// 1F–Ú‚ÉŒõ‚é‚Ì‘O’ñ‚Ìì‚è‚Íƒ[ƒ‹ƒoƒbƒN“I‚É‚àNG
		local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat="dodge_move" } );
		if( eff.push() )
		{
			BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseParentNull|_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv } );
			BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround|_ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll } );
			eff.pop();
		}
		BMvEff.SetCharaFlash( { color = 0x4444FF, type = 0, time = 10 } );
		Battle_Std.PcAfterImage_DoudgeInit(); //c‘œƒZƒbƒg
		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="‘O•û‰ñ”ğ" });//ƒ{ƒCƒXÄ¶
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		// «”\‚ğ‘SƒLƒƒƒ‰‚Å‘µ‚¦‚é
		// ˆÚ“®—Ê‚Æd‚È‚è‚ÌÁ‚·ƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ğ“ˆê(‹ŒƒnƒCƒh‚È‚Ç‚Æ“¯‚¶«”\)
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.MvCount==11 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=700, addx=400, flags=_Vector_Normal } );

			BMvEff.SetExist( { level=_Exist_NoKasanariHantei, mode=_ExistMode_Add } ); //d‚È‚è”»’è–³‚µ
		}
		if( mvs.MvCount==15 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=5800, addx=-400, flags=_Vector_Normal } );
			BMvTbl.SetVector_MaxX( 1500 );
		}
		if( mvs.MvCount==21 )
		{
			BMvEff.SetExist( { level=_Exist_NoKasanariHantei, mode=_ExistMode_Erase } ); //d‚È‚è”»’è–ß‚µ
		}
		if( mvs.MvCount==26 )
		{
			Battle_Std.InitCharaVector();

			// ˆÈ‰º‚Ìˆ—‚ğ“ü‚ê‚È‚¢‚Æ“r’†‚ÅŒü‚«‚ª•Ï‚í‚éƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ÅƒxƒNƒgƒ‹û‘©‚ÌMaxX‚ª‚¤‚Ü‚­“®‚©‚È‚¢
			local muki = BMvTbl.GetMuki();
			local start_muki = BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,5); // ŠJn‚ÌŒü‚«
			local muki_change = (muki!=start_muki)? -1 : 1;
			
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=1500*muki_change, flags=_Vector_Normal } );
			BMvTbl.SetVector_MaxX(0);
		}
		if( mvs.MvCount==31 )
		{
			Battle_Std.InitCharaVector();
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		local mvcount = mvs.MvCount;
		// _dpn("mvcount:"+mvcount);
		
		//  1`20F ‘ŠEi‘ŠE”»’è‚Í1`28F‚Éƒc[ƒ‹ã•t‚¢‚Ä‚¢‚éj
		//  1` 8F ã‚¢‘ŠE
		// 29`40F d’¼(‘ŠE‚Å‰ñ”ğ‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚½‚çƒK[ƒh‰Â”\)

		// •‚‚­ƒ^ƒCƒv‚Í‘ŠE‚É¬Œ÷‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚Ä‚à‹ó’†ƒK[ƒh•s”\‹Z‚ğd‚Ë‚ç‚ê‚Ä”í’e‚·‚é
		// ’nãƒ^ƒCƒv‚Í‘ŠE‚É¬Œ÷‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚Ä‚à“Š‚°‹Z‚ğd‚Ë‚ç‚ê‚Ä”í’e‚·‚é
		// •‚‚­ƒ^ƒCƒv‚Ì•û‚ªŠî–{“I‚É‚Íã‚¢
		
		// ‘ŠE‚É¬Œ÷‚·‚é‚ÆA‘ŠE”»’è‚ª‚ ‚éƒtƒŒ[ƒ€’†‚Í‘ÅŒ‚–³“G‚ª“ü‚é
		// 1`20F
		// XXX:’e‘ŠE‚³‚¹‚ÄCS„‰£‚è‚É‚¢‚­‚Æ‚«‚Ìˆá˜aŠ´‚ª‚ ‚é
		// 17F‚Ü‚Å‚É‚·‚é‚ÆŠ´Šo‚Æ‚µ‚Ä‚ÍŠÉ˜a‚Í‚³‚ê‚»‚¤‚Å‚Í‚ ‚é
		
		local sousai_hantei_frame = 0;
		local rc = BMvEff.GetHanteiRect( { check=[ _Hantei_Etc, 2 ], flags=0 } );
		if( rc.sx != _Hantei_Error )
		{
			sousai_hantei_frame = 1; // ‘ŠE”»’è‚ª‚ ‚éŠÔ
			if( mvs.MvCount >= 20 )
			{
				//1`20F‚®‚ç‚¢‚É‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚©‚È‚¢‚ÆAˆÚ“®æ‚É‹Z‚ğ‚¨‚¢‚Ä‚à‘ŠE‚³‚ê‚Ä‚æ‚­‚È‚¢Š´‚¶
				sousai_hantei_frame = 0; // ’·‚·‚¬
			}
		}
		local sousai_count = BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,1); // UŒ‚‚ğó‚¯~‚ß‚½‰ñ”
		
		if( sousai_hantei_frame )
		{
			if( sousai_count > 0 )
			{
				BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=2, muteki_dageX=2 } );
			}
			else
			{
				Battle_Std.Sousai.FrameUpdate_NoHitFlag( 2 );
			}
		}
		else if( BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,3)==0 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,3,1); // d’¼‚Ü‚Åi‚ñ‚¾
			
			// sousai_hantei_frame‚ª20F‚®‚ç‚¢‚È‚Ì‚ÅA‚ ‚Æ20F‚ ‚é
			// ’â~‚ÌŠG‚Í10F‚®‚ç‚¢
			
			
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0, muteki_dageX=0, muteki_nageX=0 } );
			Battle_Std.Sousai.Erase();

			UseCost(); // ƒRƒXƒgÁ”ï
			
			// ‚±‚ê‚ğÁ‚·‚Æ3D’†‚Í‚¸‚Á‚Æ”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚É‚È‚é
			// ‹à“Š‚°ƒRƒ}“Š‚°‚È‚ñ‚©‚àŠm’è‚·‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚È‚é
			BMvTbl.SetCounterHitFlag( { val=0, time=32, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );//”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚ğÁ‚·
			
			//“Áê”»’è‚ª‚È‚¢‚Æ‚±‚ë‚Íd’¼‚Ì‚Í‚¸
			//“–g‚É¬Œ÷‚µ‚Ä‚½‚çAd’¼‚¾‚¯‚ÇƒK[ƒh‚Í‰Â”\‚É‚µ‚Ä‚İ‚½‚¢
			if( sousai_count > 0 )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetAsFlag( { as_flags=_AsFlag_GuardRev, time=64, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
				
				Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.DelFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒK[ƒh‚Å‚«‚é‚Ì‚ÅƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®‚È‚Ì‚ğ‰ğœ
				

				_dpn("Œ¸‘¬...");
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=-640, addx=64, flags=_Vector_DivKeep } ); // Œ¸‘¬
			}
			else
			{
				_dpn("‰Á‘¬!");
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=1280, addx=-128, flags=_Vector_DivKeep } ); // ‰Á‘¬
			}
		}
		
		if( DBGM_ForwardShift )
		{
			//d‚È‚è‚ªoŒ»‚·‚éˆÊ’u‚ğƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
			local kasanari_rc = BMvEff.GetHanteiRect( { check=[ _Hantei_Kasanari, 0 ], flags=0 } );
			if( kasanari_rc.sx == _Hantei_Error )
			{
				local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
				local pos = BMvTbl.GetPosition();
				local start_pos_x = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
				
				local move_len = abs(pos.x - start_pos_x);
				
				BMvTbl.SetLP(3,mvs.MvCount);//d‚È‚è‚ª‚È‚¢ŠÔXV
				BMvTbl.SetLP(4,move_len);//d‚È‚è‚ª‚È‚¢ŠÔXV
			}
		}
	}
	function HitInterrupt_Std()
	{
		local mvhs = Battle_Std.Sousai.HitInterrupt( { hitstop = 0, noeffect=0, nosound=1, noquake=1, nocommanddelay=1 } );
		if( mvhs )
		{
			if( Def_Sys_SetGuardPlus_ForwardShiftDodge )
			{
				local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
				if( enemy.push() )
				{
					BMvEff.SetGuardPlusFlag(1<<0); // ‹t•ûŒü‚Å‚àƒK[ƒh‚ª‚Å‚«‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚È‚é
					enemy.pop();
				}
			}

			local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			local mvcount = mvs.MvCount;//1F`
			local pow_atemi = (mvcount >= 8)? 1 : 0; // ‹­‚¢ƒo[ƒWƒ‡ƒ“‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©@1:8FˆÈ~‚Éæ‚Á‚½ê‡@2:–³“G‹Z‚âƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvUŒ‚‚ğæ‚Á‚½
			
			// mvcount : 0`19‚Ì‚Í‚¸
			//  0` 8:ã‚¢
			//  9`12:•’Ê
			// 13`19:‹­‚¢
			
			local minus_frame = mvcount - 13;
			_dpn("mvcount:"+mvcount+" minus_frame:"+minus_frame);
			if( minus_frame <  0 ) minus_frame =  0;
			if( minus_frame >  4 ) minus_frame =  4;
			_dp(" -> "+minus_frame );
			BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,4,minus_frame); // Œ¸‚ç‚·d’¼ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ 0`4
			
			// ‘Šè‚ª–³“G‹Z‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚Íí‚É‹­‚¢ƒo[ƒWƒ‡ƒ“‚Æ‚È‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
			// ‘Šè‚ªƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvUŒ‚‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚à‹­‚­‚·‚é
			if(1)
			{
				// ‘ÅŒ‚‚Æ’e‚ª“¯F‚Éƒqƒbƒg‚·‚é‚Æ‘ÅŒ‚”»’è‚É‚È‚é
				// ‚»‚Ì‚½‚ß’e–³“G‚ª‚Â‚©‚È‚¢–â‘è‚ª‚ ‚é‚ªA‚»‚ñ‚È‚É‹N‚«‚é‚í‚¯‚Å‚Í‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅOK‚Æ‚·‚é
				local atk_core = BMvCore.GetLastHitCharaData(0); // 0:‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü‚Ì‘Šè‚ğæ“¾ 1:‰£‚ç‚ê‚½‘Šè‚Ìˆê”Ôe‚ğæ“¾
				if( atk_core.push() )
				{
					local is_muteki_atk = 0;
					local isJumpAtk = 0;
					local is_about_fireball_atk = ( BMvTbl.GetHitCheckFlag(1)&_HitCheckFlag_FireBall );
					
					if( atk_core.isPlayer() )
					{
						if( Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 7, def_MC7_Muteki ) )
						{
							is_muteki_atk = 1;
						}
						if( Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 1, def_MC1_AtkJump ) )
						{
							isJumpAtk = 1;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if( BMvTbl.CheckMvUniqueID( { datatype=_DataType_Player } ) != 0 )
						{
							local e_player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
							if( e_player.push() )
							{
								// _dm("e‚ğŒ©‚é");
								if( Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 7, def_MC7_Muteki ) )
								{
									is_muteki_atk = 1;
								}
								if( Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 1, def_MC1_AtkJump ) )
								{
									isJumpAtk = 1;
								}
								e_player.pop();
							}
						}
					}
					
					Battle_Std.SetShieldSuccessCancelEffect(); // ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh¬Œ÷‚É‚æ‚éƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹§ŒÀˆ—
				
					atk_core.pop();
					
					if( is_muteki_atk )
					{
						_dpn("–³“G‹Z‚ğæ‚Á‚½‚Ì‚Å‹­‚¢ƒo[ƒWƒ‡ƒ“‚É•Ï‰»");
						pow_atemi = 2; // ‹­‚¢ƒo[ƒWƒ‡ƒ“‚É‚·‚é
					}
					if( isJumpAtk )
					{
						_dpn("ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvUŒ‚‚ğæ‚Á‚½‚Ì‚Å‹­‚¢ƒo[ƒWƒ‡ƒ“‚É•Ï‰»");
						pow_atemi = 2; // ‹­‚¢ƒo[ƒWƒ‡ƒ“‚É‚·‚é
						BMvEff.SetStopTime( { time=6, bounderase=1 } );
					}
					if( is_about_fireball_atk )
					{
						// 3D©‘Ì‚ğ’e–³“G‚É‚·‚é‚Ì‚à‚ ‚è‚¾‚ªA‰ñ”ğæ‚É’e‚ÅƒK[ƒhŠm‚ğæ‚è‚½‚¢‚Æ‚«‚É‚â‚â¢‚é
						_dpn("’e‚Á‚Û‚¢‚à‚Ì‚ğæ‚Á‚½‚Ì‚Ås“®I—¹‚Ü‚Å’e–³“G");
						BMvTbl.SetHitCheckFlag( { type=0, val=_HitCheckFlag_FireBall, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // ’e–³“G
					}
				}
			}
			
			// “–g¬Œ÷‚ÉCS‚È‚Ç•s‰Â‚É‚·‚é
			{
				//3D‚Å‘ŠE‚µ‚ÄA‚³‚ç‚ÉCS‚·‚é‚Ì‚Í‹­‚·‚¬‚é‚Ì‚ÅCS•s‰Â‚É‚·‚é
				BMvTbl.SetAsFlag( { as_flags=_AsFlag_ChainShift, time=0, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
				
				// “¯—l‚É‘ŠE‚©‚ç“Š‚°‚Æ‚©‚â‚Î‚·‚¬‚é‚Ì‚Å“¯‰Ÿ‚µ‚ÌƒYƒ‰‚µ‚Í•s‰Â‚É
				Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.DelFlag( 1, def_MC1_CmdLvDZ_OK ); // “¯‰Ÿ‚µ‚Å‘¼‚Ìs“®‚ÉˆÚ‚ê‚és“®
				
				BMvTbl.SetCounterHitFlag( { val=0, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );//s“®’†‚ÌƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚ğÁ‹
			}
			
			
			{
				local add_frame = 4; // ©•ª‚É‰ÁZ‚·‚éƒqƒbƒgƒXƒgƒbƒv
				if( !pow_atemi )
				{
					
					// ‘Šè‚Æ‚Ì‹——£‚ª‰“‚¢ê‡Aaddframe‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚µ‚Ä«”\ƒ_ƒEƒ“‚ğŒyŒ¸
					local kyori = Battle_Std.GetEnemyDistance();
					
					//3D‚ª480ƒhƒbƒg‚®‚ç‚¢i‚Ş(“–g¬Œ÷‚Í400ƒhƒbƒg)
					//“–g¬Œ÷‚Å400ƒhƒbƒg‚¾‚Æ‚µ‚ÄA“Š‚°‚Æ‚©A‚ªŠm’è‚µ‚»‚¤‚ÈŠÔ‡‚¢‚Åd’¼‚ªŒ¸‚ç‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚µ‚Ä‚é
					local min_kyori = (400+150)*128; // ¡‚ÌŠÔ‡‚¢‚ª‚±‚ê‚­‚ç‚¢‚Ü‚Å‚È‚ç‹­‚­‚µ‚Ä—Ç‚¢
					local max_kyori = min_kyori + 150*128;

					_dpn("kyori:"+kyori/128);
					
					if( kyori > min_kyori )
					{
						if( kyori > max_kyori ) kyori = max_kyori;
						local par = 100 - ( (kyori-min_kyori) * 100 / (max_kyori-min_kyori) );
						add_frame = add_frame * par / 100;
						
						_dpn("par:"+par+" add_frame:"+add_frame+" min:"+min_kyori/128+" max:"+max_kyori/128);
					}
					
					if( add_frame > 0 )
					{
						BMvTbl.SetHitStop( add_frame, _ValAdd ); // ©•ª‚ÌƒqƒbƒgƒXƒgƒbƒv‚¾‚¯L‚Î‚·
						//L‚Î‚µ‚½ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚¾‚¯–³“G‚É‚µ‚È‚¢‚Æ‘½’iŒn‚ª‘ŠE‚Æ‚ê‚È‚¢‚Ì‚Å–³“G‚É•ÏX
						BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=add_frame } );
						
					}
					
				}
				
				//­‚µˆÃ“]‚µ‚ÄƒXƒ[Š´‚ğo‚·
				//add_frame‚ª‘å‚«‚¢‚Ù‚Ç©•ª‚ªã‚¢ŠÔ’â~‚ª’Z‚­Œ©‚¦‚½‚Ù‚¤‚ª©‘R‚È‚Ì‚Å‚±‚¤‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é
				BMvEff.CutInProc_Set({ time=[0,10-add_frame,10], cutin_mv="",erasetype=2, bgtype=1 });

				
				// “ü—Í‚ª˜R‚ê‚ÄƒXƒgƒŒƒX‚àŒ‹\‚ ‚é‚Ì‚ÅAŠÔ’â~‚Æ‚©ƒXƒ[‚ª—~‚µ‚¢
				// Å‰‚ÍƒXƒ[ã‚ßE“r’†‹­‚ßE‚à‚Æ‚É–ß‚é‚İ‚½‚¢‚È‹““®‚ªˆê”Ô©‘R‚É‚È‚è‚»‚¤‚¾‚ª
				// BMvEff.Slowmotion_Set( { time=60, power=6666, power_minus=10000/60 } );
				// ‘€ì‚Ìƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ÉƒYƒŒ‚ª‚¨‚«‚Ä‚¨Œİ‚¢‚ª”½Œ‚‚Æ‚©‚µh‚¢‚Ì‚à‚æ‚­‚È‚¢
				// ‰ñ”ğ¨UŒ‚‚Æ‚¢‚¤‚Æ‚±‚ë‚ÅƒXƒs[ƒhŠ´‚à—~‚µ‚¢
			}
			
			local sousai_count = BMvTbl.AddLPEx(1,1,1); // UŒ‚‚ğó‚¯~‚ß‚½‰ñ”
			if( sousai_count == 1 )
			{
				BMvEff.AttackInfoString_Set({ word=def_AISW_SPGuard,} ); // ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚Å‚Í‚È‚¢‚¯‚Ç‹ß‚¢Œø‰Ê‚ªo‚Ä‚é‚Ì‚ÅƒAƒiƒEƒ“ƒX‚ğo‚·
				
				Battle_Std.SetShieldSuccessGRDEffect(1); // GRD‘‰ÁE‘Šè‚ÌGRD‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚·ETS‚ği‚ß‚é
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.PcAfterImage_Clear(); //c‘œ‚ÌÁ‹
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0, muteki_dageX=0, muteki_nageX=0 } );
		
		// BMvEff.SetCharaFlash( { time = 0 } ); // ‚±‚ê‚ğŒÄ‚Ô‚Æ”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚Ì‚Æ‚«ƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ªŒõ‚ç‚È‚¢
		
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoKasanariHantei, mode=_ExistMode_Erase } );

		if( Def_Sys_SetGuardPlus_ForwardShiftDodge )
		{
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				// ‚±‚Ìˆ—‚¾‚Æ‘Šè‚Ì•\— –³Œø‹Z‚ğ•\— ‚ ‚è‚ÅƒK[ƒh‚·‚é‚±‚Æ‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‚ ‚Ü‚è‚æ‚­‚È‚¢
				BMvEff.SetGuardPlusFlag( 0 ); // U‚èŒü‚«‚ÌƒK[ƒh•ûŒüC³‚ğ–³Œø‰»‚Æ‚©FX‚ğÁ‚·
				enemy.pop();
			}
		}
		
		UseCost(); // ƒRƒXƒgÁ”ïi‰½“x‚à“ü‚é‚ª1‰ñ‚Ì‚İˆ—j
		
		if( DBGM_ForwardShift )
		{
			local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			local pos = BMvTbl.GetPosition();
			local start_pos_x = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
			
			local kasanari_frame = BMvTbl.GetLP(3);
			local kasanari_len = BMvTbl.GetLP(4);
			
			local move_len = abs(pos.x - start_pos_x);
			local total_frame = mvs.MvCount; // ‘S‘ÌF
			_dpn("‘S‘Ì:"+total_frame+" ˆÚ“®‹——£:"+move_len/128+" d‚È‚è:"+kasanari_frame+"F "+kasanari_len/128); // ƒhƒbƒg‚É•ÏŠ·
		}
	}
}
	
//“Š‚°
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Throw_F <-
{
	function CheckForwardShiftCatch()
	{
		// 3D‚ÌˆÚ“®’†‚Å‚Â‚©‚ñ‚Å—Ç‚¢uŠÔ‚Í1
		// “–g‚É¬Œ÷‚µ‚Ä‚é‚Æ‚«‚Í–³—‚â‚è‚Â‚©‚Ş‚±‚Æ‚ª‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
		local emv = BMvTbl.GetMvName();
		if( emv == "Mv_ForwardShift" )
		{
			local nage_muteki = BMvEff.GetPlayerMuteki( 1 ); // “Š‚°–³“G
			local sousai_count = BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,1); // UŒ‚‚ğó‚¯~‚ß‚½‰ñ”
			
			if( !nage_muteki && sousai_count==0 )
			{
				//_dpn("‚Â‚©‚ñ‚Å—Ç‚¢");
				return 1;
			}
			else
			{
				//_dpn("“–g¬Œ÷");
				return 0;
			}
		}
		//_dpn("‚»‚à‚»‚à•Ês“®");
		return 0;
	}
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		// _dp("\n Mv_Throw_F_Init()");
		
		Battle_Std.InitCharaVector() //Šµ«‚ğc‚µ‚½ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
		//Battle_Std.InitVector(); // ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Throw_F);
		BMvTbl.ClearHitStatus(); // ƒqƒbƒgî•ñ‚ÌƒNƒŠƒA
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_EnableTech );//“Š‚°”²‚¯‰Â”\‚Ès“®
		Battle_Std.ThrowTech.SetThrowMvFlag(); //“Š‚°‘®«Mv‚ğİ’è
		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_Throw ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(“Š‚°)‚ğİ’è
		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_GRDBreak_FatalDmgHosei ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(GRDƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚ÅUŒ‚—ÍƒAƒbƒv)‚ğİ’è
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 1, def_MC1_CmdLvDZ_OK ); // “¯‰Ÿ‚µ‚Å‘¼‚Ìs“®‚ÉˆÚ‚ê‚és“®
		// Battle_Std.SetNoMovableMove(); // “®‚¯‚È‚¢MV@¦ƒtƒŠ[ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ª‚ ‚éƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ª‘½‚¢‚Ì‚Å”p~
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_InActive );
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
		// Battle_Std.InitAtkSkillMv(); // ’Êí‹Z‚â•KE‹Z‚Ì‹¤’Ê‰Šú‰»@¦“ü‚ê‚é‚×‚«HiƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚à‚ ‚èj
		
//		Battle_Std.DelPSFlag( def_PP_PS_isThrowMv ); //“Š‚°‘®«ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğíœ
		//BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //“Š‚°ƒXƒJ‚Ü‚Åi‚ñ‚¾•‰¹ºÄ¶Ï‚İ
		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,0,0); //3D‚Ö‚Ì‹­ˆø“Š‚°‚©@1:‹­ˆø“Š‚°@10:‚³‚ç‚ÉƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚Å’Í‚ñ‚¾‚©
		
		BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=def_PAT_Throw_F_TechWait, frame=0, rest=2 } ); // “Š‚°‚Â‚©‚İ•ªŠòæ—\–ñ		
		BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=def_PAT_Throw_F_Hit, frame=0, rest=6 } ); // “Š‚°¬—§æ—\–ñ
		
		if( BMvTbl.GetCmdNumber()==def_CN_Throw_F && Battle_Std.GS_CheckFlag( def_PP_GS_SlideDashThrow ) ) // ƒXƒ‰ƒCƒh“Š‚°
		{
			_dp("\n ššƒXƒ‰ƒCƒhƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…“Š‚°šš");
			Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 1, def_MC1_GenFlag ); // Mv_Throw_F + def_MC1_GenFlag == ƒXƒ‰ƒCƒh“Š‚°
		}
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_TechCheck );//“Š‚°”²‚¯‚³‚ê‚é‹Z
		
		Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Increment(110,"’nã“Š‚°g—p");
		
		BMvTbl.SetFinalizeCode(0); //GetFinalizeCode‚Å•ªŠò‚µ‚Ä‚é‚Ì‚Å‰Šú‰»
	}
	function HitInterrupt_Std()
	{
		//‚±‚Ì“_‚Å‘Šè‚Íƒ_ƒ[ƒWó‘Ôc‚Å‚Í‚È‚¢i‚Â‚©‚İ–½—ß“ü‚Á‚Ä‚È‚¢j
		//‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚è‚Í“–‘R“ü‚Á‚Ä‚­‚é
		local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “Gî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		
		local isBoundOrMutekiCapture = false;//‹à“Š‚°
		local enemyIsGuardShield = false; // ƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh
		
		if( enemy.push() )
		{
			local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
			isBoundOrMutekiCapture = ((bs.isCapture&(1<<1))!=0); //Bound or Muteki Capture
			if( Def_Sys_GuardShieldThrow_NoGouinNageHosei && isBoundOrMutekiCapture && BMvEff.GuardSP_Get() )
			{
				// ‹à“Š‚°”»’è‚É‚È‚Á‚½‚Æ‚«A‘Šè‚ªƒV[ƒ‹ƒhó‘Ô‚Å‚ ‚é‚È‚çƒK[ƒhƒV[ƒ‹ƒh”»’è‚Æ‚µ‚Ä•â³‚ğ‚©‚¯‚È‚¢
				enemyIsGuardShield = true;
			}
			//print("\nhs.isCapture"+bs.isCapture+" isBoundOrMutekiCapture:"+isBoundOrMutekiCapture);
			enemy.pop();
		}
		if( isBoundOrMutekiCapture && !enemyIsGuardShield ) //BM’Í‚İi‹à“Š‚°j‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		{
			// _dm("Bound‚©“Š‚°–³“G’†‚ğ‚Â‚©‚ñ‚¾‚Ì‚Å•â³‚ğ‚©‚¯‚Ü‚·");

			// Battle_Std.DrawDebugAttackInfo("! Gouin Nage");	
			local ret = Battle_Std.SetThrowHitFinalize(512); // ƒqƒbƒg•ªŠòƒtƒ@ƒCƒiƒ‰ƒCƒY
			if( ret )
			{
				if( Def_Sys_ThrowF_BMCaptureHoseiType == 0 )
				{
					_dpn("‹à“Š‚°‚Ì•â³:"+def_HOSEI_BoundMutekiCapture);
					//ƒRƒ“ƒ{•â³‚ ‚è
					BMvEff.ComboView_Set( { val=def_HOSEI_BoundMutekiCapture, type=1 } ); // æZ
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			Battle_Std.SetThrowHitFinalize(256); // ƒqƒbƒg•ªŠòƒtƒ@ƒCƒiƒ‰ƒCƒY
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()//
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		//local mvcnt = BMvTbl.GetLP(0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^æ“¾
		//BMvTbl.AddLP(0,1); //1Fi‚ß‚é
		
		if( s.MvCount>=4+2 && (BMvTbl.GetLP(1)==0) ) //“Š‚°¸”sŠm’èŒã•ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ª‚½‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢
		{
			_dpn("“Š‚°ƒXƒJ‚è");
			BMvTbl.SetLP(1,1); //Ä¶Ï‚İ
			Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="“Š‚°ƒXƒJ‚è" });
			Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.DelFlag( 7, def_MC7_EnableTech );//“Š‚°”²‚¯‰Â”\‚Ès“®
			Battle_Std.ThrowTech.DelThrowMvFlag_NextFrame(); //1FŒã‚É“Š‚°‘®«ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğ1FŒã‚Éíœ
			
			//“Š‚°ƒXƒJ‚è‚ÌGRDŒ¸­
			// ‚±‚ê‚Í‘Šè‚ª‚â‚ç‚ê’†‚Å‚àŒ¸‚ç‚·B‚È‚ñ‚Æ‚È‚­B
			Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue({ val=def_GRD_ThrowMiss, pressure=0, boundplus=0,target=0 });
		}
		
		// ‘Šè‚ª3D‚ÅˆÚ“®’†‚Å‹ß‚¢ˆÊ’u‚É‚¢‚½‚ç–³—‚â‚è‚Â‚©‚Ş
		// ‚Â‚©‚Şƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ÍUŒ‚”»’è‚ªo‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Æ‚«
		// ‚±‚Ìd—l‚ğ“ü‚ê‚éê‡AŠ®‘S‚Èƒ[ƒvƒ^ƒCƒv‚Í‚â‚ß‚Ä“à•”ƒf[ƒ^‚ÍƒxƒNƒgƒ‹ˆÚ“®‚É‚·‚é•K—v‚ª‚ ‚é
		// DamageImpact‚Æ‚©‚É“ü‚ç‚È‚¢‚Ì‚Å’ˆÓ
		if( Def_Sys_ThrowCounterToForwordShift )
		{
			if( Battle_Std.CheckHanteiAttackExist() && BMvTbl.GetLP(1)==0 && BMvTbl.GetFinalizeCode()==0 ) // “Š‚°ƒXƒJ‚Éi‚ñ‚Å‚È‚¢
			{
				local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
				if( enemy.push() )
				{
					// ‹à“Š‚°‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚Íæ“¾•s‰Â”\
					// local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
					// local isBoundOrMutekiCapture = ((bs.isCapture&(1<<1)) != 0); //Bound or Muteki Capture
					
					local enemy_catch_ok = CheckForwardShiftCatch();
					
					local counter = BMvTbl.GetCounterHitFlag(); // ’Í‚Ş’¼‘O‚Ì‘Šè‚Ì”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğŒ©‚é
					// _dpn("counter:"+counter);

					enemy.pop();
					
					if( enemy_catch_ok )
					{
						// ‹——£‚ª‹ß‚©‚Á‚½‚ç–³—‚â‚è‚Â‚©‚Ş
						// ’nã‚Æ‚©‹ó’†‚Æ‚©ŠÖŒW‚È‚¢‚µA‘Šè‚ªŒã‚ë‚É‚¢‚Ä‚àŠÖŒW‚È‚¢
						local posst = BMvEff.GetPointStatus( { target=enemy } );
						local kyori = posst.pos_x*BMvTbl.GetMuki();
						
						// _dpn("‹——£:"+kyori/128 );
						
						// ‚¾‚¢‚½‚¢•W€‚Ì“Š‚°ŠÔ‡‚¢‚Í200ƒhƒbƒgi”»’è150{‘Šè‚Ìd‚È‚è‚ª50j
						// if( kyori > -120*128 && kyori < 170*128 ) // ‚±‚ê‚¾‚Æ3D‚Åd‚È‚è‚ªÁ‚¦‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Æ‚«ƒXƒJ‚è‚â‚·‚©‚Á‚½
						if( kyori > -120*128 && kyori < 230*128 ) // 170ƒhƒbƒg‚©‚çŠg‘å
						{
							local cap = BMvEff.CapturePlayer( { target=enemy } );
							if( cap )
							{
								_dpn("‹­ˆø‚Â‚©‚İ");
								BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256);
								BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,0,1); //3D‚Ö‚Ì‹­ˆø“Š‚°‚Å’Í‚ñ‚¾
								
								if( counter )
								{
									// _dpn("ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚È‚Ì‚ğ‹L‰¯");
									BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,0,10); //3D‚Ö‚Ì‹­ˆø“Š‚°‚ÅƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚Å’Í‚ñ‚¾
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		//BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‰Šú‰»
		
		local fin_code = BMvTbl.GetFinalizeCode();
		if( fin_code == 256 || fin_code == 512 )
		{
			// ’Í‚İ‚É‘Šè‚Ìs“®‚ğ‚İ‚Ä•ªŠò
			// 3DˆÚ“®‚ğ‚Â‚©‚ñ‚Å‚½‚çƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚³‚¹‚é
			// ’Êí“Š‚°‚Å3D‚ğ’Í‚ñ‚¾‚Æ‚«A‹­ˆø“Š‚°‚Å‚È‚¢‚Æ‚«‚ÍƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚µ‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚³‚¹‚È‚¢‚Æ‘Ê–Ú
			local forward_catch_status = BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,0); //3D‚Ö‚Ì‹­ˆø“Š‚°‚Å’Í‚ñ‚¾‚© 1:’Êí@10:ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				local enemy_catch_ok = CheckForwardShiftCatch();
				local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
				
				enemy.pop();
				
				local counterhit = 0;
				if( forward_catch_status == 0 )
				{
					if( mvs.flags&_MvStFlag_CounterDef )
					{
						_dpn("’Êí’Í‚İ‚È‚Ì‚Å•’Ê‚ÉƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[ˆ—‚ª‚³‚ê‚é");
						counterhit = 1;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					counterhit = (forward_catch_status==10)? 1 : 0;
				}
				
				
				if( enemy_catch_ok )
				{
					// d—l
					// ’Êí“Š‚°‚Å3D‚ğ’Í‚ñ‚¾‚çŠ„‚ê‚é‚¯‚ÇA‘ÅŒ‚‚Å‚ÍŠ„‚ê‚È‚¢
					
					// ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚¿‚á‚ñ‚ÆŒ©‚È‚¢‚Æ‘Ê–Ú
					// 3D’†‚ğ’Í‚ñ‚¾‚ç‹­ˆø‚ÉƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚É‚µ‚½‚­‚Ä‚àA’Êí‚Ì’Í‚İ‚ÅƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ª“ü‚Á‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚Ì‚Å‘Ê–Ú
					// if( counterhit )
					if( enemy_catch_ok ) // ‚±‚ê‚ğ‚â‚é‚Æ‚«‚Ípunishƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ª“ü‚ç‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
					{
						BMvEff.SetCounterAnnounce(); // ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[ƒAƒiƒEƒ“ƒX•\¦
						Battle_Std.GRDBreak_Attack( { Nage=1, NoHosei=1 } );
						Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_EnemyTechImpossible );//“Š‚°”²‚¯•s‰Â‚É‚·‚é
						
						if( enemy.push() )
						{
							Battle_Std.SetCharaFlash_Counter(); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚É‚æ‚é”­Œõ

							if( forward_catch_status == 0 )
							{
								Battle_Std.PP_AddFlag(def_PP_ImpactAnnounce,def_PP_IA_Counter); // —áŠO“I‚Éˆ—EƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ğo‚³‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É
							}
							else
							{
								// Impact“ü‚ç‚È‚¢‚Ì‚Åˆ—‚ğ“ü‚ê‚é
								Battle_Std.EnemyCharaBattleActivity_Increment(50,"ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[ƒqƒbƒg");
								Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Increment(51,"”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[ƒqƒbƒg");
							}
							
							Battle_Std.GRDBreak_Damage( { Nage=1 } );
							enemy.pop();
						}
					}
					/*
					// ‚±‚Á‚¿‚àˆê‰ˆ—‚ğŠˆ‚©‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­Bcounterhit‚Ì‚İƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚É‚·‚é‚È‚ç“ü‚ê‚é‚±‚Æ
					else
					{
						BMvEff.SetPunishAnnounce(); // ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…ƒAƒiƒEƒ“ƒX•\¦
						Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_EnemyTechImpossible );//“Š‚°”²‚¯•s‰Â‚É‚·‚é
					}
					*/
				}
			}
		}
		
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral", [256,"Mv_Throw_F_TechWait"], [512,"Mv_Throw_F_TechWait2"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.ThrowTech.DelThrowMvFlag_NextFrame(); //1FŒã‚É“Š‚°‘®«ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğ1FŒã‚Éíœ
	}
}


Std_MoveTable.Mv_Throw_F_TechWait <- //‚Â‚©‚ñ‚Å‚©‚ç“Š‚°”²‚¯‚ğ‘Ò‚Â‚Ü‚Å`•ªŠò
{	
	function Init_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.InitVector(); // ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»(ˆê‰)
		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_Throw ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(“Š‚°)‚ğİ’è
		Battle_Std.ThrowTech.SetMuteki(); //‚Â‚©‚İ’†‚Í©•ª‚à‘Šè‚à–³“G‚É‚·‚é	
		Battle_Std.PlayerSE_StopLastPlaySound( 1 ); //’¼‘O‚ÉÄ¶‚µ‚½‰¹º‚ğ’â~
		Battle_Std.SetNoMovableMove(); // “®‚¯‚È‚¢MV
		
		//‘O‚Ìs“®‚Ì‚Â‚©‚İ‚Ìó‹µ‚ğƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		Battle_Std.PassBeforeMoveCodeEx( 7, def_MC7_EnemyTechImpossible );
		if( Battle_Std.ThrowTech.CheckTechMissFrame() || Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 7, def_MC7_EnemyTechImpossible ) )
		{
			Battle_Std.ThrowTech.CheckTechImpossible(); // d’¼’†‚¾‚Á‚½‚çFXİ’è
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(512); //“Š‚°”²‚¯‚Å‚«‚È‚¢ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€’†‚È‚Ì‚ÅI‚í‚ë‚¤
		}
		else //“Š‚°‚Ê‚¯¸”sƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Å‚Í‚È‚¢“Š‚°
		{
			Battle_Std.EnemyCharaBattleActivity_Increment(121,"“Š‚°‚Å‚Â‚©‚Ü‚ê‚½‰ñ”");
			
			//’Í‚ñ‚¾‘Šè‘¤‚É“Š‚°‚ç‚ê‰ñ”‚ğ‰ÁZ
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetCaptureCharaData();
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				BMvEff.AddSkillCount( _SkillCount_Throw ); // —š—ğƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‚ğ‰ÁZ
				
				enemy.pop();
			}
			BMvEff.ThrowChara_SetCamera( 1 ); //“Š‚°‚Ä‚¢‚éƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ğƒJƒƒ‰ˆ—‚ÉŠÜ‚ß‚é
		}
		
		Battle_Std.ThrowTech.SetPos(); //‚Â‚©‚Ş‘O‚Ì©•ª‚Æ‘Šè‚ÌÀ•W‚ğ‹L‰¯‚·‚é
		
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Throw_F_TechWait); //“Š‚°”²‚¯‘Ò‹@ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚É•ÏX(‚±‚±‚Å‘Šè‚Í‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚é)
		
		Battle_Std.ThrowTech.SetThrowParam(); //ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒgÀ•W‚É‘Šè‚ğ‚â‚ç‚êŠG‚ÅƒZƒbƒg
		
		//‘Šè‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚Á‚Ä‚©‚ç‚Ìˆ—
		Battle_Std.ThrowTech.ShiftOverGamenHajiX(); //‘Šè‚ª‰æ–ÊŠO‚É‚¢‚½‚ç©•ª‚ÌÀ•W‚²‚Æ•â³‚ğ‚©‚¯‚é	
		Battle_Std.ThrowTech.DrawThrowEffect(0); //ƒqƒbƒgÀ•W‚É‚Â‚©‚İƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg•\¦ 1:‹à“Š‚°
		
		// ‚Ü‚¾“Š‚°”²‚¯‰Â”\‚È‚Ì‚Å“Áê‚È‚â‚ç‚êó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
		local enemy = BMvCore.GetCaptureCharaData();
		if( enemy.push() )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetAsFlag( { as_flags=_AsFlag_DamageEx, time=255, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // “Áê‚â‚ç‚ê‚É‚µ‚ÄGetBoundFlag‚ÅŒŸ’m‰Â”\‚É
			
			enemy.pop();
		}
		
		Battle_Std.PassHitMoveCodes(); // ƒqƒbƒg•ªŠò‚Å‚ÌMvCodeˆøŒp‚¬
	}
	function Update_Std() //“Š‚°‚Ê‚¯—P—\’†
	{

		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		//print("\nTh1:Frame:"+s.FrameID);
		if( s.MvCount>def_FL_Tech )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); //“Š‚°”²‚¯—P—\‚ªI‚í‚Á‚½‚Ì‚ÅI‚í‚é
			return;
		}
		
		if ( Battle_Std.ThrowTech.CheckTechCommand() ) //‘Šè‚ª“Š‚°”²‚¯ƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒh‚ğ“ü‚ê‚½
		{
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “Gî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				local delay = def_FL_Tech - s.MvCount;
				// Battle_Std.DrawDebugAttackInfo("TechSafe: "+delay+"F" );
				
				enemy.pop();
			}
			
			Battle_Std.ThrowTech.DrawTechEffect(); //“Š‚°”²‚¯‰‰o‚ğ•\¦
			Battle_Std.ThrowTech.TechRelease( { release_kyori=Def_Sys_FixPosAfterThrowTech } ); //“Š‚°”²‚¯‚³‚ê‚½‚ÌŠJ•úˆ—
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256);
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		local fin_code = BMvTbl.GetFinalizeCode();
		if( fin_code != 256 )
		{
			Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Increment(111,"’nã“Š‚°¬Œ÷");
			//“Š‚°¬Œ÷‰ñ”/(“Š‚°¬Œ÷‰ñ”+“Š‚°¸”s‰ñ”)
			Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Calc( {
				title="’nã“Š‚°¬Œ÷Šm—¦", 
				molecule=Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Count("’nã“Š‚°¬Œ÷"), denominator=(Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Count("’nã“Š‚°¬Œ÷")+Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Count("’nã“Š‚°¸”s"))
			} );
		}
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Throw_F_Hit", [256,"Mv_Teched"],[512,"Mv_Throw_F_TechMissWait"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } );	
		BMvEff.ThrowChara_SetCamera( 0 ); //“Š‚°‚Ä‚¢‚éƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ğƒJƒƒ‰ˆ—‚ÉŠÜ‚ß‚È‚¢
	}
}

			}
		}

// “Š‚°”²‚¯•s”\ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Ì@‚Æ@s“®•s”\‚ğ’Pƒ‚É’Í‚ñ‚¾ ‚ª‚ ‚é
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Throw_F_TechMissWait <- //“Š‚°”²‚¯‚³‚ê‚È‚¢‚Â‚©‚İ‚Ì
{	
	function Init_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="“Š‚°”²‚¯‚³‚ê‚È‚¢‚Â‚©‚İ¬Œ÷" });
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_Throw ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(“Š‚°)‚ğİ’è
		Battle_Std.SetNoMovableMove(); // “®‚¯‚È‚¢MV

		local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData();
		if( enemy.push() )
		{
			Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒK[ƒh¸”s" });
			Battle_Std.SoundStatus_AddFlag( def_PP_SS_NoBoundVoiceOnce ); // Ÿ‚Ì‚â‚ç‚ê‰¹º‚ğÄ¶‚µ‚È‚¢

			enemy.pop();
		}
		
		Battle_Std.ThrowTech.SetMuteki(); //‚Â‚©‚İ’†‚Í©•ª‚à‘Šè‚à–³“G‚É‚·‚é	
		
		Battle_Std.PassHitMoveCodes(); // ƒqƒbƒg•ªŠò‚Å‚ÌMvCodeˆøŒp‚¬
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		// ‚È‚º‚©GRDƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚µ‚½‚Æ‚«‚É‘Šè‚É–³“G‚ª“ü‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚È‚©‚Á‚½‚Ì‚Å‚±‚±‚ÅC³

		Battle_Std.SetThrowEnemyMuteki( 1 ); // “Š‚°’†‚Ì‘Šè‚É–³“GŠÔ‚ğİ’è
	}

	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		if( Def_Sys_ThrowCounterTechMissWaitType )
		{
			// ’Êí“Š‚°‚ÆƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[“Š‚°‚È‚Ç‚Å‰‰oŠÔ‚ª•Ï‚í‚é‚±‚Æ‚ÅAƒRƒ“ƒ{‚Ì“ü‚è‚â‚·‚³‚É·‚ªo‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤–â‘è‚ª‚ ‚é
			// UNI2‚Í“Š‚°‚Ì”äd‚ª‚‚¢‚Ì‚ÅA¡ì‚ÍƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚âƒŠƒWƒFƒNƒgƒ~ƒX‚ğ’Í‚ñ‚¾‚Æ‚«‚Ì‰‰oŠÔ‚Í’Êí‚Æ“¯‚¶‚É‚·‚é
			// ƒnƒCƒhAƒJƒOƒ„AƒoƒeƒBƒXƒ^iƒ^ƒ•ªŠ„‚ªƒYƒŒ‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤j
			if( mvs.MvCount>def_FL_Tech ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); //@“Š‚°”²‚¯—P—\‚¾‚¯‘Ò‚Á‚Äi‚Ş
		}
		else
		{
			// ‚·‚®“Š‚°‚é‚Æ‚í‚©‚è‚É‚­‚¢‚Ì‚Ådef_FL_BoundTech•ª‚Ü‚Â
			if( mvs.MvCount>def_FL_BoundTech ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); //“Š‚°”²‚¯—P—\‚ªI‚í‚Á‚½‚Ì‚ÅI‚í‚é

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Throw_F_Hit <- //“Š‚°¬—§iˆÈ~‚Í“Š‚°”²‚¯•s‰Âj
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254 } );

		// ƒnƒCƒh‚Ì“Š‚°‚Æ‚©ƒ[ƒOƒi[‚Ì‹­‰»“Š‚°‚Æ‚©‚à‚ ‚é‚Ì‚Åˆê’U‚±‚Ìİ’è‚Í‚È‚µ‚É‚·‚é
		// Battle_Std.SetNoMovableMove(); // “®‚¯‚È‚¢MV

		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_Throw ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(“Š‚°)‚ğİ’è
		BMvTbl.SetMvAction( def_MVA_Throw );
		
		//‘Šè‚Ì–³“G‚Í‰ğœ
		local enemy = BMvCore.GetCaptureCharaData();
		if( enemy.push() )
		{
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } );
			
			enemy.pop();
		}
	
		//‚Ü‚¸‘Šè‚ÌGRD‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚·
		Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue({ val=def_GRD_ThrowHit, pressure=0, boundplus=1,target=1 });
		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="“Š‚°‚Â‚©‚İ¬Œ÷" });
		
		//ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚ªÅŒã‚Ü‚Å‚«‚Ä‚ê‚ÎŸ‚Éi‚ŞB‚Ü‚¾‚È‚çUpdate’†‚àƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚·‚é‚©‚ç‚»‚±‚ÅB
		//local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾					
		//if( s.DataPattern==def_PAT_Throw_F_TechWait && s.FrameID==0 )

		//‹t“Š‚°‚¾‚Á‚½‚ç‚±‚±‚Å”½“]
		if( BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_TechReverse) == 1 )
		{
			// _dp("\n Œã‚ë“Š‚°");
			BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Reverse);	//‹t“Š‚°‚È‚ñ‚Å”½“]
			Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_ReverseThrow );
		}
		
		Battle_Std.ThrowTech.SetThrowParam(); //ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒgÀ•W‚É‘Šè‚ğ‚â‚ç‚êŠG‚ÅƒZƒbƒg

		//ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚ğ•ÏX
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Throw_F_Hit);
		
		Battle_Std.Create_TechDelayCheckObject(); //‘Šè‚Ì“Š‚°ƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒhƒ`ƒFƒbƒNƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg¶¬
		
		Battle_Std.PassHitMoveCodes(); // ƒqƒbƒg•ªŠò‚Å‚ÌMvCodeˆøŒp‚¬
		local isSidou = Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 1, def_MC1_SidouHitSkill );
		local isGRDBreakAttack = Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 2, def_MC2_GRDBreakAttack ); // Š„‚Á‚½‹Z

		if( enemy.push() )
		{
			// print("\n ó‘ÔƒNƒŠƒA:"+_AsFlag_DamageEx);
			BMvTbl.SetAsFlag( { as_flags=_AsFlag_DamageEx, time=0, flag=0 } ); // “Áê‚â‚ç‚ê‚ğÁ‚·
			
			BMvTbl.SetCaptureHitFlag( { val=_CaptureHitFlag_Parent, time=255, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // ’Í‚ñ‚¾‘ŠèˆÈŠO‚ÌUŒ‚‚ªƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚È‚¢
			
			// Š„‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Æ‚«‚É“Š‚°‚ğó‚¯‚½‚çAƒuƒŒƒCƒNŠÔ‚ª­‚µ‰ñ•œ‚·‚éd—l
			// Š„‚Á‚½‹Z‚Å‘Šè‚ÌƒuƒŒƒCƒNŠÔ‚ªŒ¸‚é‚Ì‚Í‚¨‚©‚µ‚¢‚Ì‚ÅAŠ„‚Á‚½‹Z‚Ì‚ ‚Æ‚Ìn“®‚Ì“Š‚°‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚¾‚¯
			if( !isGRDBreakAttack && Def_Sys_ThrowF_BreakTimePar && BMvEff.GRD_GetBreak( 0 )!=0 ) //ƒuƒŒƒCƒN
			{
				BMvEff.GRD_CorrectBreakTime( 0, Def_Sys_ThrowF_BreakTimePar ); //Š„‡Œ¸­
			}
			enemy.pop();
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="“Š‚°¬Œ÷" });	
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		// ƒ†ƒYƒŠƒn‚Å•ªŠò‚ ‚è‚È‚Ì‚Å—]Œv‚È‚±‚Æ‚Í‚µ‚È‚¢‚±‚Æ
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		// ƒ†ƒYƒŠƒn‚Å•ªŠò‚ ‚è‚È‚Ì‚Å—]Œv‚È‚±‚Æ‚Í‚µ‚È‚¢‚±‚Æ
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } );		
		
		//’Êí“Š‚°n“®‚ÍÅ’á•ÛØ‚É§ŒÀ‚ğ‚©‚¯‚é
		Battle_Std.SetSpHosyoHosei( { type="Throw" } );
		// Battle_Std.SetHosyoHosei_Multi( def_HosyoHosei_NormalThrow );
		// Battle_Std.SetHosyoHosei( def_HosyoHosei_NormalThrow );
		
		if( Def_Sys_SetSpGaugeLimit_Throw )
		{
			// 241212 ’Êí“Š‚°Œã‚ÌƒRƒ“ƒ{‚¾‚¯ƒQ[ƒW‘‰Á§ŒÀ‚ğ‚©‚¯‚é
	        BMvEff.SpGauge_SetLimitCombo( { val=Def_Sys_SetSpGaugeLimit_Throw, time=0 } ); //ƒQ[ƒW‘‰Á§ŒÀ
        }
	}
}

//‘Šè‘¤‚É¶¬‚³‚ê‚é
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Obj_CheckTechDelay <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender  } );	
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } )
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( {y=1024, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } );	
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		local flags = false;
		//print("\nFrame:"+s.MvCount);

		local p = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // î•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		
		if( p.IsDone ) // —LŒø‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©ˆê‰ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		{
			BMvCore.PushCharaData( p ); // 
				if( BMvTbl.CheckCommandString( { command=["A+D"], lastdelay=1 } ) ) flags = true;
			BMvCore.PopCharaData();
		}
		
		if( flags )
		{
			local frame = (s.MvCount+1);
			// Battle_Std.DrawDebugAttackInfo("TECH LATE: "+frame+"FRAME" );
			// _dm("“Š‚°”²‚¯:"+frame+"F’x‚¢");
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
		if( s.MvCount>60 ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); //‚Æ‚è‚ ‚¦‚¸’x‚·‚¬‚é‚Ì‚ÍI‚í‚é
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

//“Š‚°”²‚¯‚·‚é‘¤‚ÍBound‚Å‚·
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Teched <- //“Š‚°”²‚¯‚ç‚êiƒK[ƒhŠG‚Åƒvƒ‹ƒvƒ‹j
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_GuardS); //—§‚¿ƒK[ƒh‚ÌŒ©‚½–Ú‚Åƒvƒ‹ƒvƒ‹‚·‚é
		//BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto); //‘Šè‚Ì•û‚ğŒü‚­
		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_Throw ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(“Š‚°)‚ğİ’è (ƒo[ƒXƒg‚³‚ê‚È‚¢)
		
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254 } );		
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level=_Exist_NoCamera, mode=_ExistMode_Add } ); //ƒJƒƒ‰‚©‚çØ‚è—£‚·
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ó‘Ô•Ï”‰Šú‰»

		Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Increment(115,"’nã“Š‚°¸”s");
	}	
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto); //‘Šè‚Ì•û‚ğŒü‚­

		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		
		//ƒoƒO‘Îô
		if( s.MvCount > 60 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
		
		// ‚±‚±‚©‚ç‚Ìˆ—‚È‚ñ‚©•Ï‚È‹C‚ª‚·‚é
		// À•W‚ªŒ³‚©‚ç•Ï‰»‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‘‚¢‚Ä‚ ‚é‚¾‚¯‚©‚àH‚È‚Ì‚ÅG‚ç‚È‚¢‚Å‚¨‚­
		
		if( s.MvCount%4==0 && BMvTbl.GetLP(1)==0 ) //ƒvƒ‹ƒvƒ‹‚³‚¹‚é
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLP(1,1); //ó‘Ô•ÏX
			BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=6, y=0, flags=_Position_ChangeMuki|_Position_Add|_Position_CaptureShift } );
			BMvTbl.Frame_Proc( 0, _ValSet ); //‚P–‡–Ú‚ÌŠG‚É‚·‚é
		}
		else if( (s.MvCount+2)%4==0 && BMvTbl.GetLP(1)==1 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ó‘Ô•ÏX
			BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=-6, y=0, flags=_Position_ChangeMuki|_Position_Add|_Position_CaptureShift } );
			BMvTbl.Frame_Proc( 1, _ValSet ); //‚Q–‡–Ú‚ÌŠG‚É‚·‚é
		}
		
		//ƒAƒjƒƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚Ìƒ‹[ƒvˆ—
		if( s.MvCount%6==0 )
		{
			local frame = BMvTbl.Frame_Proc( 0, _ValAdd );
			BMvTbl.Frame_Proc( 1-frame, _ValSet ); //‚P–‡–Ú‚Æ‚Q–‡–Ú‚Åƒ‹[ƒv
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}	
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } );		
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level=_Exist_NoCamera, mode=_ExistMode_Erase } ); //ƒJƒƒ‰‚ÉŠÜ‚ß‚é
	}	
}

//“Š‚°”²‚¯‰Ÿ‚µ•Ô‚µUŒ‚
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Bound_0023 <- 
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.BoundInit();
		//print("\n“Š‚°”²‚¯UŒ‚I");
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254 } );
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //“Š‚°”²‚¯ƒtƒ‰ƒO‰Šú‰»
		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="“Š‚°”²‚¯" });
		BMvTbl.SetPrio( _CharaPrio_Near ); //ˆê”Ôè‘O‚É‚·‚é
		
		BMvEff.AddSkillCount( _SkillCount_ThrowRecover ); // —š—ğƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‚ğ‰ÁZ
		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_NoBurst ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(ƒo[ƒXƒg‚Å‚«‚È‚¢)‚ğİ’è 
		
		BMvTbl.SetAsFlag( { as_flags=_AsFlag_DamageEx, time=255, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // “Áê‚â‚ç‚ê
		
		Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Increment(122,"’nã“Š‚°”²‚¯¬Œ÷");
		//“Š‚°”²‚¯¬Œ÷‰ñ”/“Š‚°‚Å‚Â‚©‚Ü‚ê‚½‰ñ”
		Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Calc( {
			title="’nã“Š‚°”²‚¯¬Œ÷Šm—¦", 
			molecule=Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Count("’nã“Š‚°”²‚¯¬Œ÷"), denominator=(Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Count("“Š‚°‚Å‚Â‚©‚Ü‚ê‚½‰ñ”"))
		} );
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		//’nãó‘Ô‚Å‚Í•„†‚ª”½“]‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
		if( BMvTbl.CheckPosState( _PosState_Ground  ) )
		{
			//¡ƒvƒ‰ƒX‚ÌƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ÅAŸ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ªƒ}ƒCƒiƒXƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚©H
			local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector( { flags=_Vector_Bound });
			//print(format("\n x:%d addx:%d",vec.x, vec.addx));
			if( (( vec.x<0 ) && ( (vec.x + vec.addx)>=0 )) || (( vec.x>0 ) && ( (vec.x + vec.addx)<=0 )) )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, addx=0, flags=_Vector_Bound } );
			}
		}
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾					
		
		if( s.MvCount>=16 && (BMvTbl.GetLP(0)==0) )
		{
			//‘Šè‚ğ‰Ÿ‚µ•Ô‚·ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg
			BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); //“Š‚°”²‚¯ƒtƒ‰ƒO
			
			//©•ª‚Ì‚f‚q‚c‚Éƒ{[ƒiƒX
			Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue( { val=def_GRD_ThrowTech_Success, boundplus=1 } );					
			
			//‘Šè‚Ì‚f‚q‚c‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚·
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “Gî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				BMvEff.GRD_AddValue( { val=def_GRD_ThrowTech_Success_Enemy, pressure=0, boundplus=1 } );
				
				enemy.pop();
			}
			
			//‚±‚±‚Å‘Šè‚ğ‹­ˆø‚É’Í‚ñ‚ÅMv_Bound_0024‚ÅŠJ•ú‚·‚éH
			//print("\n‚±‚±H");
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				local epos = BMvTbl.GetPosition(); // ˆÊ’uæ“¾
				
				enemy.pop();

				epos = Battle_Std.GamePos2ShiftPos( epos ); //ƒIƒtƒZƒbƒgÀ•W‚É•ÏŠ·
				
				epos.x /= 128; //ƒc[ƒ‹À•W‚É
				
				//epos.x += 50; //­‚µ—£‚·

				local ret = BMvEff.CapturePlayer( { target = enemy } );
				if( ret == 1 )
				{
					BMvEff.ThrowParam( { x=epos.x });
					BMvEff.ThrowRelease( { type=24 } );
				}
				
			}
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local useval = (BMvEff.GRD_GetJudgeResult()>0)? ( (BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } )>=6)? def_GRD_BoundFU_GRD6Vorpal : def_GRD_BoundFU_Vorpal) : def_GRD_BoundFU;
		Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue({ val=useval, pressure=0, boundplus=1 });
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BtlMvStd.SetBoundFinalize();
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } );		
		BtlMvStd.ClearBound(); // ó‘Ô‰ğœ
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_SkillTechedRecover <- //“Š‚°”²‚¯‚ç‚ê‚Qi•œ‹A‚Å‚æ‚¯‚éƒ^ƒCƒvj
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		//BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto); //U‚èŒü‚«‚ğ“ü‚ê‚é‚ÆÀ•W•ÏX‘O‚È‚Ì‚Å‹tŒü‚¢‚½‚è‚·‚é‚æ
		//BMvTbl.SetPattern(410); //‚Â‚©‚İƒ|[ƒY‚Åƒvƒ‹ƒvƒ‹‚·‚é
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254 } );
	}	
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}	
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } );		
	}	
}


//ƒCƒxƒ“ƒg‚Æ‚©
//“oêƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ÅŒÄ‚Î‚ê‚Ä
// s“®•¶š—ñ‚ğ•Ô‚·As“®‚ª‘¶İ‚µ‚È‚¢‚Ü‚½‚Í""‚ª•Ô‚³‚ê‚é‚Æ Mv_Neutral ‚ªŒÄ‚Î‚ê‚é
// ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh“ª‚ÅƒXƒLƒbƒv‚µ‚½ê‡‚à‚±‚±‚É‚Æ‚ñ‚Å‚«‚ÄA‚È‚ñ‚Æround=1‚Æ‚µ‚Äˆµ‚í‚ê‚é
Std_MoveTable.GetEntranceMvName <- function()
//function Std_MoveTable::GetEntranceMvName()
{
	// print("\n --------- GetEntranceMvName");
	
	local pside = BMvTbl.GetPlayerSide();//0:1PƒTƒCƒh 1:2PƒTƒCƒh
	local tagStatus = BMvCore.GetTagStatus();//-1:’Êí 1:ƒ^ƒbƒO‚ÌƒƒCƒ“ 0:ƒ^ƒbƒO‚Ìƒp[ƒgƒi[
	local roundst = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus(); // ‚±‚ê‚Å”»•Ê

	BMvEff.Chara_ClearMessageAll();//ƒgƒŒƒ‚‚Æ‚©‚Å‚ÌŸ—˜š–‹‚Ì‰Šú‰»
	//BSound.BGM_SetFade( { val=100, time=60 } );//BGM‚Ì–ß‚µ
	
	if( roundst.Round < 0 ) return "Mv_Startup"; // ƒgƒŒ[ƒjƒ“ƒO -1H0–¢–‚¾‚Æ
	
	if( BMvTbl.TalkScript_IsDone() ) return "Mv_Startup"; // ‰ï˜b‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½‚çƒgƒŒ[ƒjƒ“ƒO‚Æ“¯‚¶ŠJnó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
	
	if( roundst.Round == 0 ) // ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚P
	{
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { time=[0,0,0] } );//ƒJƒƒ‰‰Šú‰»iƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhƒR[ƒ‹‚ÌƒJƒƒ‰ˆ—‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒgj
		BMvEff.ResetCamera();//ƒJƒƒ‰‚ğ’†‰›‚É‚·‚é

		local pside = BMvTbl.GetPlayerSide();
		
		//1P‚©‚ç‡”Ô‚ÉƒZƒŠƒt‚ğŒ¾‚¤‚Ì‚Å‘Ò‹@
		if( pside == 0 ) // 1Por3P‚È‚çStandby 2Por4P‚È‚çStandbyWait
		{
			BMvTbl.SetPP( def_PP_StandbyType, def_PP_ST_Standby );
			// print(" -> def_PP_ST_Standby");
		}
		else if( pside == 1 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetPP( def_PP_StandbyType, def_PP_ST_StandbyWait );
			// print(" -> def_PP_ST_StandbyWait");
		}
	}
	else if( roundst.Round >= 0 ) // ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚QˆÈ~i‚à‚µ‚­‚ÍƒXƒLƒbƒvj
	{
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { time=[0,0,0] } );//ƒJƒƒ‰‰Šú‰»iƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhƒR[ƒ‹‚ÌƒJƒƒ‰ˆ—‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒgj
		BMvTbl.SetPP( def_PP_StandbyType, def_PP_ST_Neutral ); // ŠJ–‹MVw’è
		BMvEff.ResetCamera();//ƒJƒƒ‰‚ğ’†‰›‚É‚·‚é
	}
	return "Mv_Startup";

	return "";
}


Std_MoveTable.Mv_Startup <- //ŠJ–‹‚©‚Ü‚¹Eâ‘Î’Ê‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚È‚Á‚½
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_AuraKanriObject", datatype=0 } );
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=600, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); //Mv’†‚Ís“®•s”\‚É
		
		if( Def_Dbg_LocalDebugScriptPath )
		{
			Battle_Std.DebugFunc.CallStartupDebugScript();
		}
		
		{
			switch( BMvTbl.GetPP( def_PP_StandbyType ) )
			{
			case def_PP_ST_Neutral:
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
				break;
			case def_PP_ST_Standby:
			case def_PP_ST_StandbyWait:
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(10);
				break;
			default:
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_StandbyNoMotionPass", [10,"Mv_Standby"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}


local setTrainingDummyClearVorpal = function()
{
	// ƒgƒŒƒ‚İ’è‚ª‘¦VP‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚Ís‚í‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
	// Œ‹‹ÇVP‚É‚È‚é‚Ì‚ÅC³‚Æ‚µ‚Ä‚Í•s\•ª‚¾‚ªAˆê’U‚Í‚±‚ê‚Å‘Î‰
	if( BMvEff.TM_IsQuickRecoverVorpalMode() )
	{
		return;
	}
	
	local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
	local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
	if( player.push() )
	{
		BMvEff.GRD_ClearVorpal( 0 ); // ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚ğ‰ğœ‚·‚é
		
		player.pop();
	}
	if( enemy.push() )
	{
		BMvEff.GRD_ClearVorpal( 0 ); // ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚ğ‰ğœ‚·‚é
		
		enemy.pop();
	}
}

local setTrainingDummyResetChara = function()
{
	local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
	local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
	if( player.push() )
	{
		BMvEff.ThrowRelease( { type="“Áê—ûKŠJ•ú", x=0, y=0 } );
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable("Mv_TrainingDummyNeutral");
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=-100*128*BMvTbl.GetMuki(), y=0, flags=0 } );
		
		player.pop();
	}
	if( enemy.push() )
	{
		BMvEff.ThrowRelease( { type="“Áê—ûKŠJ•ú", x=0, y=0 } );
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable("Mv_TrainingDummyNeutral");
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=-100*128*BMvTbl.GetMuki(), y=0, flags=0 } );
		
		enemy.pop();
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_CharaRestart <-
{
	function Init_Std() : (setTrainingDummyResetChara, setTrainingDummyClearVorpal)
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender } );	
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(990);

		//‰æ–ÊˆêuˆÃ‚­‚µ‚ÄˆÊ’uŠÖŒW‚ğ’²®
		BMvEff.FadeProc_Set({type=0, time=[0,60,15] color=0x000000}); //•‚­‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çŠJn
		
		setTrainingDummyClearVorpal(); // ˆê“x‚¾‚¯VP‰ğœi‘¦VP‚ª‚ ‚é‚Ì‚Å‰ğœ‚µ‚«‚ê‚È‚¢j
	}
	function Update_Std() : (setTrainingDummyResetChara)
	{
		// ƒoƒgƒ‹ƒLƒƒƒ‰‚Ì‚Â‚©‚İŠJ•ú‚Æd•¡‚·‚é‚Æ‚¨‚©‚µ‚­‚È‚é
		// ˆ—‚Æ‚µ‚Ä‚Í‚Ç‚¤‚©‚Æv‚¤‚ªAƒƒ‹ƒJƒ”ƒ@‚È‚Ç‚Í‰½“x‚©ŒÄ‚Î‚È‚¢‚Æ‚¤‚Ü‚­‚¢‚©‚È‚¢
		if( !Battle_Std.RoundisEnd() )
		{
			setTrainingDummyResetChara();
		}
		
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.MvCount >= 2 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize();
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		//‰æ–ÊˆêuˆÃ‚­‚µ‚ÄˆÊ’uŠÖŒW‚ğ’²®
		BMvEff.FadeProc_Set({type=0, time=[0,0,15] color=0x000000}); //•‚­‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çŠJn

		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
		
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { time=[0,0,0] } );//ƒJƒƒ‰‰Šú‰»iƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhƒR[ƒ‹‚ÌƒJƒƒ‰ˆ—‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒgj
		BMvEff.ResetCamera();//ƒJƒƒ‰‚ğ’†‰›‚É‚·‚é
	}
}



// “Áê‚ÈƒgƒŒ[ƒjƒ“ƒO‚ğ‚·‚é‚½‚ß‚ÌŠÄ‹ƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg
// ƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[‘¤‚©‚ç¶¬‚³‚êA1P‚à2P‚àƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[‚¾‚Á‚½‚Æ‚«‚Í1P‘¤‚Ì‚İ¶¬‚³‚ê‚éi‚Q‚Â‚Í¶¬‚³‚ê‚È‚¢j
Std_MoveTable.Mv_TrainingDummyMngInit <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender } );	
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(990);
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(9,0); // 0:1P 1:2P
		
		BMvTbl.SetFinalize();
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable("Mv_TrainingDummyMng");
	}
}

// “Áê‚È—ûK
Std_MoveTable.Mv_TrainingDummyMng <-
{
	function CheckInit() : (TR_CommandType)
	{
		// ‚±‚ê‚ğ“ü‚ê‚é‚ÆAƒ_ƒ~[‘€ìƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğ‰Ÿ‚µ‚½‚Æ‚«‚Ì‹““®‚ª‚¿‚å‚Á‚Æˆá˜aŠ´‚ ‚è
		// BMvEff.SetObjectRender( { type=2 } );
		// BMvEff.Cockpit_SetView( { mode=2 } ); // ƒQ[ƒW—Ş”ñ•\¦
		
		Battle_Std.GS_AddFlag( def_PP_GS_ExtraTrainingMode ); // ƒLƒƒƒ‰‘€ì•s”\ƒtƒ‰ƒO
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,-30); // ƒ`ƒFƒbƒNI—¹•ƒfƒBƒŒƒC@”½‰ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€

		// ‰Û‘è‚ª‚¢‚Â‚Å‚é‚©
		local rnd = 60 + 30 + BMvEff.Random_Limit(240);
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,rnd);

		BMvTbl.SetLP(2,0); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒX@0:ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‘O@1:ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN’†@10:ƒ`ƒFƒbƒNI—¹‘Ò‹@ @11:ƒ`ƒFƒbƒNI—¹‘Ò‹@(¸”s)@15:‘‚·‚¬‚Ä¸”s@20:KO‚ª‚¨‚«‚½‚Ì‚ÅƒGƒ‰[‰ñ”ğ
		
		local extra_training = Battle_Std.CheckExtraTraining();
		local extra_mode = extra_training-1;
		if( extra_mode < 0 ) extra_mode = 0;
		if( extra_mode > 4 ) extra_mode = 4;
		BMvTbl.SetLP(4,extra_mode); // TR_CommandType‚Ìí—Ş 0:“Š‚°”²‚¯@1:AorBorCorD@2:•KE‹ZƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒh@4:•KE‹ZƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒhiƒTƒhƒDƒ“j

		local rnd = BMvEff.Random_Limit(TR_CommandType[extra_mode].len());
		BMvTbl.SetLP(3,rnd); // TR_CommandType
		// _dpn("ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN:"+rnd+" CMD:"+TR_CommandType[rnd].command );
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(8,0); // ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv‚ÌƒGƒ‰[‚Å”²‚¯‚é‚Æ‚«—p
		
		// BMvTbl.SetLP(9,0); // 0:1P 1:2P
		
		BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_CharaRestart", datatype=1 } );
		
		local cores = GetCores();
		if( cores[0].push() )
		{
			local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat="sys_you", mvname="Mv_SystemHereYouAreTraining" } );
			
			cores[0].pop();
		}
		
		local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat="TR_TimingMark", mvname="Mv_TrainingFrameMark" } );
		if( eff.push() )
		{
			BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoCamera } );
			BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=0*128, y=-480*128 } );
			eff.pop();
		}
	}
	function GetCores()
	{
		local cores = [BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(), BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData()];
		local side = BMvTbl.GetLP(9);
		
		local ret_cores = [ cores[side] , cores[1-side] ];
		
		return ret_cores;
	}
	function EndExtraTrainingMode()
	{
		// “Š‚°‰‰o‚É‚à‚¢‚Á‚Ä‚¨‚ç‚¸ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv‚µ‚½‚Æ‚«‚Ìˆ—
		// ‹C‡‚Å“Áê—ûK‚ğ”²‚¯‚é
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
		if( player.push() )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable("Mv_StandbyNoMotion");
			BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd(); //í“¬I—¹ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é

			player.pop();
		}
		local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
		if( enemy.push() )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable("Mv_StandbyNoMotion");
			BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd(); //í“¬I—¹ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é

			enemy.pop();
		}
		BMvEff.FadeProc_Set({type=0, time=[0,2,15] color=0x000000}); // ‚±‚ê‚ğ“ü‚ê‚È‚¢‚ÆˆêuƒsƒN‚Â‚­‚Ì‚ªŒ©‚¦‚é
	}
	function Init_Std()
	{
		// BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0); // ‰½‰ñ–Ú‚Ì‰Û‘è‚©
		// BMvTbl.SetLP(6,0); // ¡‚Ü‚Å‚Ì‡Œvi•½‹Ï’l‚ğo‚·—pj

		CheckInit();
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std() : (TR_CommandType)
	{
		local input_key = 0; // 0:‰½‚à“ü‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢ 1:¬Œ÷ -1:ˆá‚¤‚Ì“ü‚ê‚½
		
		local cores = GetCores();
		local cmd_type = BMvTbl.GetLP(3); // TR_CommandType
		local cmd_mode = BMvTbl.GetLP(4); // TR_CommandType
		
		local round_is_timeup = 0;
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
		if( player.push() )
		{
			local rs =  BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus(); //ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
			player.pop();
			
			// ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv‚Ì‚Æ‚«
			if( rs.isKo && !rs.isMyKo )
			{
				round_is_timeup = 1;
			}
		}
		
		// ‚±‚±‚Ìˆ—‚¢‚éH
		if( (!Battle_Std.CheckExtraTraining() ) && BMvTbl.GetLP(8) != 25 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLP(8,25); // ‘€ì•s”\‚Ö
			
			local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
			if( player.push() )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable("Mv_StandbyNoMotion");
				BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd(); //í“¬I—¹ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é

				player.pop();
			}
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable("Mv_StandbyNoMotion");
				BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd(); //í“¬I—¹ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é

				enemy.pop();
			}
			BMvEff.FadeProc_Set({type=0, time=[0,2,15] color=0x000000}); //•‚­‚µ‚Ä‚©‚çŠJn
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
		
		if( Battle_Std.RoundisEnd() && BMvTbl.GetLP(2) != 20 && !round_is_timeup )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLP(2,20); // ‘€ì•s”\‚Ö
			
			local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
			if( player.push() )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd(); //í“¬I—¹ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é

				player.pop();
			}
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd(); //í“¬I—¹ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é

				enemy.pop();
			}
		}
			
		
		
		local player = cores[0];
		local enemy = cores[1];
		if( player.push() )
		{
			// input_key = BMvTbl.CheckCommandString( { command=[ "A+D" ], lastdelay=1, } )
			local check_cmd_table = [];
			local cmd_list = TR_CommandType[cmd_mode];
			for( local i=0; i<cmd_list.len(); i++ )
			{
				check_cmd_table.append( cmd_list[i].command );
			}
			
			// input_key = BMvTbl.CheckCommandString( { command=TR_CommandType[cmd_mode][cmd_type].command, lastdelay=1, } )
			local checkl_cmd = BMvTbl.CheckCommandString( { command=check_cmd_table, lastdelay=1, } )
			if( checkl_cmd == (cmd_type+1) )
			{
				input_key = 1;
			}
			else if( checkl_cmd || round_is_timeup )
			{
				input_key = -1;
			}
			// _dpn("checkl_cmd:"+checkl_cmd+" input_key:"+input_key);

			// ƒQ[ƒWŒn‚ÌƒAƒjƒ‚ğ‚·‚×‚Ä~‚ß‚é
			BMvEff.GRD_TS_AddValue( { val=-10 } );
			BMvEff.GRD_UseStock( { val=12 } );

			player.pop();
			
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				// ƒQ[ƒWŒn‚ÌƒAƒjƒ‚ğ‚·‚×‚Ä~‚ß‚é
				BMvEff.GRD_TS_AddValue( { val=-10 } );
				BMvEff.GRD_UseStock( { val=12 } );
				
				enemy.pop();
			}
			
			
			local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			if( mvs.CallCount != 0 ) return; // 1F‚É1‰ñ‚Ìˆ—
			
			// if( BMvTbl.IsCpu()==0 )
			{
				//LP0:ƒŠƒUƒ‹ƒg‚Ég‚¤”½‰ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€
				//LP1:Ÿ‚Ìƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚Ü‚Å‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€
				//LP2:ƒ`ƒFƒbƒNŠJn‚µ‚½‚ç1
				local result_frame = BMvTbl.GetLP(0)-1; // ŠiƒQ[“I‚É‘µ‚¦‚é@”½‰ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€
				local next_timing_frame = BMvTbl.GetLP(1); // 0‚É‚È‚é‚Æƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚É‚È‚é‚æ
				local check_status = BMvTbl.GetLP(2);
				local check_side = BMvTbl.GetLP(9);
				
				// _dpn(check_side+" : "+mvs.MvCount+" next_timing_frame:"+next_timing_frame+" result_frame:"+result_frame);
				

				// ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğ‚¨‚µ‚½‚Æ‚«
				if( input_key )
				{
					local set_status = 0;
					if( check_status==1 ) // ƒ`ƒFƒbƒNŠJnÏ‚İ
					{
						if( input_key == 1 )
						{
							if( enemy.push() )
							{
								Battle_Std.ThrowTech.TechRelease( { release_kyori=Def_Sys_FixPosAfterThrowTech } ); //“Š‚°”²‚¯‚³‚ê‚½‚ÌŠJ•úˆ—
								BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable("Mv_Teched");
								enemy.pop();
							}
							set_status = 10; // 10:¬Œ÷ 11:ƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒhƒ~ƒX 15:‘‚·‚¬
							
							// •½‹Ï‚ğo‚·
							local kadai_count = BMvTbl.AddLP(5,1); // ‰½‰ñ–Ú‚Ì‰Û‘è‚©
							local total_frame = BMvTbl.AddLP(6,result_frame); // ¡‚Ü‚Å‚Ì‡Œv
							local average_frame = total_frame * 100 / kadai_count;
							_dpn(kadai_count+": "+result_frame+"F AVE:"+average_frame);
							
							local result_str = format("%.2fF (%d/10)",average_frame/100.0,kadai_count);
							_dpn("result_str:"+result_str);
							
							// local word = format("SUCCESS! %2d/%2d",kadai_count,10);
							// _dpn(word);
							
							if( player.push() )
							{
								BMvEff.AttackInfoString_Set({ word=result_str } );
								BMvEff.AttackInfoString_Set({ word="AVERAGE" } );
								player.pop();
							}
							
							// I‚í‚è
							if( kadai_count >= 10 )
							{
								BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0); // ‰½‰ñ–Ú‚Ì‰Û‘è‚©
								BMvTbl.SetLP(6,0); // ¡‚Ü‚Å‚Ì‡Œvi•½‹Ï’l‚ğo‚·—pj
							}
						}
						else
						{
							if( player.push() )
							{
								local word = "Failed...";
								_dpn(word);
								BMvEff.AttackInfoString_Set({ word=word } );
								player.pop();
							}
						
							if( enemy.push() )
							{
								BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable("Mv_Throw_F_Hit");
								enemy.pop();
							}
							set_status = 11; // 10:¬Œ÷ 11:ƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒhƒ~ƒX 15:‘‚·‚¬
							
							// ‚±‚Ì‚ ‚Æ‚Í’Êíƒoƒgƒ‹‚É‚¢‚­{ƒŠƒXƒ^[ƒg‚È‚Ì‚ÅŠÇ—ƒ}ƒl[ƒWƒƒ‚àI—¹‚·‚é
							if( round_is_timeup )
							{
								BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
							}
						}
					}
					else if( check_status==0 ) // ‚Ü‚¾ƒ`ƒFƒbƒNŠJn‚µ‚Ä‚È‚¢
					{
						// ’Êíƒoƒgƒ‹‚É‚Í‚¢‚©‚È‚¢‚Ì‚Å–³—‚â‚èI‚í‚è‚Ìˆ—‚ğ‚¢‚ê‚é
						if( round_is_timeup )
						{
							EndExtraTrainingMode();
							BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
						}
						else
						{
							if( player.push() )
							{
								local word = "Failed... ";//+result_frame+" F";
								_dpn(word);
								BMvEff.AttackInfoString_Set({ word=word } );
								
								BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_TimeupLose); // ŠG‚¾‚¯‚»‚ê‚Á‚Û‚¢‚à‚Ì‚É‚·‚é
								player.pop();
							}
							set_status = 15; // 10:¬Œ÷ 11:ƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒhƒ~ƒX 15:‘‚·‚¬
						
						}
					

					}
					
					if( set_status )
					{
						BMvTbl.SetLP(2,set_status);//Finalize‚Ü‚Å‰½“x‚àˆ—‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É
						BMvTbl.SetLP(1,90);//Ÿ‚Éi‚ŞŠÔ
						return;
					}
				}
				
				if( next_timing_frame > 0 )
				{
					BMvTbl.AddLP(1,-1);
				}
				else
				{
					if( check_status==0 )
					{
						BMvTbl.SetLP(2,1);

						//‰Û‘è•\¦ƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO
						// _dpn("CheckStart()");
						BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); // ƒ`ƒFƒbƒNŠJn
						// BMvTbl.SetPattern(300);//•ß‚Ü‚ê
						
						if( enemy.push() )
						{
							BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable("Mv_Throw_F_TechWaitTraining");
							enemy.pop();
						}
					}
					else if( check_status==1 )
					{
						// BMvTbl.SetPattern(300);//•ß‚Ü‚ê
					}
					else if( check_status>=10 && check_status<20 )
					{
						BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100);
					}
					//–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚â‚é‚±‚Æ
					BMvTbl.AddLP(0,1);//ƒŠƒUƒ‹ƒg‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ğ‰ÁZ‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚­
				}
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("...",[100,"Mv_TrainingDummyMng"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_TrainingDummyNeutral <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.InitVector();
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(0);
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=1024, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // Mv’†s“®•s”\‚É‚·‚é
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( { y=0 } );

		BMvTbl.SetAngle( { angle=0 } ); // “Š‚°’†‚ÉŠp“x‚ğ•ÏX‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚éƒLƒƒƒ‰‘Îô
		
		// ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‘¦‚ª‚ ‚é‚Ì‚Å‚±‚±‚Å‚â‚é‚ÆƒAƒiƒEƒ“ƒX‚ª‰½d‚É‚à‚Å‚é
		// BMvEff.GRD_ClearVorpal( 0 ); // ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚ğ‰ğœ‚·‚é
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		// KO‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚½‚çŸ—˜ƒ|[ƒY‚È‚Ç‚Éi‚Ş‚½‚ß–ß‚·
		if( Battle_Std.RoundisEnd() )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize();
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral");
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_TrainingFrameMark <-
{
	function DrawKadaiCommand() : (TR_CommandType)
	{
		local oya = BMvCore.GetParentCharaData();
		if( oya.push() )
		{
			local cmd_type = BMvTbl.GetLP(3); // TR_CommandType
			local cmd_mode = BMvTbl.GetLP(4); // TR_CommandType
			oya.pop();
			
			local cmd_str = TR_CommandType[cmd_mode][cmd_type].view;
			DrawCommandStrings(cmd_str);
		}
	}
	function DrawCommandStrings( _str )
	{
		local cmd_muki_reverse = 0; // 0:’Êí 1:ƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒh‚ª”½“]
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
		if( player.push() )
		{
			local muki = BMvTbl.GetMuki();
			// _dpn("muki:"+muki);
			player.pop();
			
			if( muki == -1 ) cmd_muki_reverse = 1;
		}
		// _str = ["A","+","D"]
		local param = {
			["A"] = { pat="TR_Btn", id=0 },
			["B"] = { pat="TR_Btn", id=1 },
			["C"] = { pat="TR_Btn", id=2 },
			["D"] = { pat="TR_Btn", id=3 },
			["+"] = { pat="TR_Plus", id=0 },
			["1"] = { pat="TR_Stick", id=1, r_id=3 },
			["2"] = { pat="TR_Stick", id=2, r_id=2 },
			["3"] = { pat="TR_Stick", id=3, r_id=1 },
			["4"] = { pat="TR_Stick", id=4, r_id=6 },
			["6"] = { pat="TR_Stick", id=6, r_id=4 },
			["7"] = { pat="TR_Stick", id=7, r_id=9 },
			["8"] = { pat="TR_Stick", id=8, r_id=8 },
			["9"] = { pat="TR_Stick", id=9, r_id=7 },
		}
		
		local x_pos = -((_str.len()-1) * (32/2) * 128 );
		
		local window_id = 0;
		local word_len = _str.len();
		// _dpn("word_len:"+word_len);
		if( word_len <= 3 )
		{
			window_id = 0;
		}
		else if( word_len <= 5 )
		{
			window_id = 1;
		}
		else if( word_len <= 6 )
		{
			window_id = 2;
		}
		else if( word_len <= 7 )
		{
			window_id = 3;
		}
		else
		{
			window_id = 4;
		}
		// _dpn("word_len:"+word_len+" window_id:"+window_id)
		
		local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat="TR_Window", x= 0*128, y=80*128 } );
		if( eff.push() )
		{
			BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(window_id);
			BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoCamera|_ObjFlags_EraseParentPatChange } );
			BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv|_ObjProcFlag_EraseParentDelete } );
			BMvTbl.SetPrio(_CharaPrio_Parent_P1 ); // e+1
			
			
			for( local i=0; i<_str.len(); i++ )
			{
				if( _str[i] in param )
				{
					local use_param = param[_str[i]];
					local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat=use_param.pat, x=x_pos } );
					if( eff.push() )
					{
						local jump_id = (cmd_muki_reverse && "r_id" in use_param)? use_param.r_id : use_param.id;
						BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(jump_id);
						BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoCamera|_ObjFlags_EraseParentPatChange } );
						BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv|_ObjProcFlag_EraseParentDelete } );
						BMvTbl.SetPrio(_CharaPrio_Parent_P1 ); // e+1
						eff.pop();
					}
					x_pos += (32 * 128);
				}
			}
			eff.pop();
		}
	}
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Right); // ‰EŒü‚«ŒÅ’è
		
		BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv|_ObjProcFlag_EraseParentDelete } );
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround } );	
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		BMvTbl.SetPrio( _CharaPrio_Near ); //ƒŠƒUƒ‹ƒg‚Í‚©‚È‚èè‘O‚É‚·‚é
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(2,-1); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒX

		BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(900); // Å‰‚Í”ñ•\¦‚©‚çŠJn‚·‚é
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std() : (TR_CommandType)
	{
		local status = BMvTbl.GetLP(2);
		local oya = BMvCore.GetParentCharaData();
		if( oya.push() )
		{
			local frame = BMvTbl.GetLP(0); // 
			local oya_status = BMvTbl.GetLP(2); // 0:ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‘O@1:ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN’†@10:ƒ`ƒFƒbƒNI—¹‘Ò‹@
			local cmd_type = BMvTbl.GetLP(3); // TR_CommandType
			local cmd_mode = BMvTbl.GetLP(4); // TR_CommandType
			
			local kadai_count = BMvTbl.GetLP(5); // ‰½‰ñ–Ú‚Ì‰Û‘è‚©
			local total_frame = BMvTbl.GetLP(6); // ¡‚Ü‚Å‚Ì‡Œv
			
			/*
			// •½‹Ï‚ğo‚µI‚í‚Á‚½‚Ì‚Å‰Šú‰»
			// –Ê“|‚È‚Ì‚Åˆê’UƒAƒiƒEƒ“ƒX–½—ß‚Å“ü‚ê‚é‚¾‚¯‚É‚·‚é
			local draw_result = 0;
			local average_frame = 0;
			if( kadai_count >= 2 )
			{
				draw_result = 1;
				average_frame = total_frame * 100 / kadai_count;
				// BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0);
				// BMvTbl.SetLP(6,0);
			}
			*/
			
			oya.pop();
			
			if( status != oya_status )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetLP(2,oya_status);
				
				// _dpn("oya_status:"+oya_status);
				
				if( oya_status == 0 ) // ‘Ò‹@ŠJn ¦“ü‚Á‚Ä‚±‚È‚¢‚Í‚¸
				{
					// _dpn("‰Û‘è1");
					BMvTbl.SetPattern("TR_TimingMark");
					BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(900);
					
					if( cmd_mode == 1 || cmd_mode == 3 )
					{
						// ƒTƒhƒDƒ“‚È‚Ì‚Å•\¦‚µ‚È‚¢
					}
					else
					{
						DrawKadaiCommand();
					}
				}
				else if( oya_status == 1 ) // Œv‘ªŠJn
				{
					// _dpn("‰Û‘è2");
					BMvTbl.SetPattern("TR_TimingMark");
					BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(100);
					
					DrawKadaiCommand();
				}
				else if( oya_status == 10 ) // ƒŠƒUƒ‹ƒg‚Ö
				{
					// _dpn("‰Û‘è3");
					BMvTbl.SetPattern("TR_TimingMark");
					BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(900);
					
					frame = frame - 1; // ŠiƒQ[“I‚É‘µ‚¦‚é
					
					if( frame > 99 ) frame = 99;
					if( frame <  0 ) frame =  0;
					
					local use_count_01 =  frame%10; // 1Œ…–Ú
					local use_count_10 = (frame/10)%10; // 2Œ…–Ú
					
					DrawKadaiCommand();
					
					/*
					if( draw_result )
					{
						_dpn("•½‹Ï:"+average_frame);
					}
					*/
					
					// _dpn("use_count:"+use_count_10+"_"+use_count_01);
					
					local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat="TR_NumShadow", x= 0*128 } );
					if( eff.push() )
					{
						BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoCamera|_ObjFlags_EraseParentPatChange } );
						BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv|_ObjProcFlag_EraseParentDelete } );
						BMvTbl.SetPrio(_CharaPrio_Parent_M1 ); // e-1
						
						local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat="TR_ResultFlash", x= 0*128 } );
						if( eff.push() )
						{
							BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoCamera|_ObjFlags_EraseParentPatChange } );
							BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv|_ObjProcFlag_EraseParentDelete } );
							BMvTbl.SetPrio(_CharaPrio_Parent_P1 ); // e+1
							eff.pop();
						}

						local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat="TR_Num", x= 0*128 } );
						if( eff.push() )
						{
							BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(use_count_01);
							BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoCamera|_ObjFlags_EraseParentPatChange } );
							BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv|_ObjProcFlag_EraseParentDelete } );
							BMvTbl.SetPrio(_CharaPrio_Parent_P1 ); // e+1
							eff.pop();
						}
						local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat="TR_Num", x=-32*128 } );
						if( eff.push() )
						{
							BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(use_count_10);
							BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoCamera|_ObjFlags_EraseParentPatChange } );
							BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv|_ObjProcFlag_EraseParentDelete } );
							BMvTbl.SetPrio(_CharaPrio_Parent_P1 ); // e+1
							eff.pop();
						}
						local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat="TR_F", x= 32*128 } );
						if( eff.push() )
						{
							BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoCamera|_ObjFlags_EraseParentPatChange } );
							BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv|_ObjProcFlag_EraseParentDelete } );
							BMvTbl.SetPrio(_CharaPrio_Parent_P1 ); // e+1
							eff.pop();
						}
						eff.pop();
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}
