Std_MoveTable.Mv_StandbyNoMotionPass <- //“oê‰‰o
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); //Mv’†‚Ís“®•s”\‚É
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Neutral);
		
		//ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“ƒXƒLƒbƒv‚ª”­¶‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‘¦–½—ß‚ğŒÄ‚Ô
		//ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“ƒXƒLƒbƒv‚ª‹N‚«‚é‚ÆƒtƒŠ[ƒY‚·‚é
		BMvTbl.SetRoundStart(); //í“¬ŠJn‰Â”\ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
		
		// ‰‰o‚Å•Ï‚ÈˆÊ’u‚ÉˆÚ“®‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é‚±‚Æ‚ª‚ ‚é
		// XÀ•W‚ÍƒvƒƒOƒ‰ƒ€‚ÅXV‚³‚ê‚é‚Ì‚Å‘åä•v‚Á‚Û‚¢
		Battle_Std.InitVector();
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( { y=0 } );
		
		if( Battle_Std.CheckExtraTraining() )
		{
			BMvEff.FadeProc_Set({type=0, time=[0,2,15] color=0x000000}); //ˆÊ’u•ÏX‚ğ‰B‚·
		}
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		// 10F‚¾‚ÆƒgƒŒƒ‚‚ÅƒŠƒXƒ^[ƒg‚µ‚Ä‘¦s“®‚·‚é‚ÆACVP‚É‚È‚ç‚È‚¢ƒoƒO‚ª‚ ‚éi‚ ‚Á‚½j
		// 9F‚¾‚ÆƒgƒŒƒ‚ƒŠƒXƒ^[ƒg’¼Œã‚Ì1F‘€ì‚ª‹L˜^‚³‚ê‚È‚¢
		if( mvs.MvCount >= 8 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
		else
		{
			BMvTbl.Frame_Proc( 0, _ValSet );//¡‚Ìƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚Ì“ª‚ÉˆÚ“®
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_StandbyNoMotion" );
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		// ƒgƒŒ[ƒjƒ“ƒOŒn‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚ÍŠJ–‹‚Ìƒsƒ[ƒ“‚İ‚½‚¢‚ÈVP‰‰oŒn‚ğ“ü‚ê‚È‚¢‚½‚ß‚Éˆê’èŠÔƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ‚½‚Ä‚é
		if( Def_Sys_NoPlayTrainingRestartUpsetEffect )
		{
			local isTraining = BMvTbl.GetMvStageStatus().IsTrainingBattle(); // ƒgƒŒ[ƒjƒ“ƒOŒn‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
			if( isTraining )
			{
				BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_Null_ImmediatelyAfterRestart", datatype=1 } );
			}
		}
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_StandbyNoMotion <-
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); //Mv’†‚Ís“®•s”\‚É
		
		Battle_Std.InitVector();
		BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		
		//•‰‚¯ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚È‚çŠoÁH
		//UNI‚É‚ ‚Á‚Ä‚à—Ç‚³‚»‚¤‚È‹C‚Í‚·‚é‚ªc‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚ñ‚Ü‚·‚¬‚é
		local isTraining = BMvTbl.GetMvStageStatus().IsTrainingBattle(); // ƒgƒŒ[ƒjƒ“ƒOŒn‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		if( !isTraining )
		{
			local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus(); //ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
			// _dpn("rs.isLoseRound:"+rs.isLoseRound+" rs.isFirstLoseRound:"+rs.isFirstLoseRound );

			if( rs.isLoseRound && rs.isFirstLoseRound )//•‰‚¯ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚©‚Â‰‰ñ‚Ì‚İ
			{
				// ŠoÁ
			}
		}
		
		local round_is_timeup = 0; // ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒvŒã‚Í‰½“x‚à“ü‚ç‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É“Áê—ûK‚ğ‚â‚ç‚È‚¢
		local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus(); //ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		if( rs.isKo )
		{
			round_is_timeup = 1;
		}

		local extra_training = Battle_Std.CheckExtraTraining();
		if( extra_training && !round_is_timeup )
		{
			// _dpn("“Š‚°”²‚¯ƒeƒXƒg‚ ‚è");
			//©•ª‚ªƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[‚Å‘Šè‚ªCPU‚È‚ç¶¬
			//‚¨Œİ‚¢ƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[‚¾‚Á‚½‚è‚µ‚½‚ç‚»‚à‚»‚àˆ—‚ğ‚µ‚È‚¢
			local p_iscpu = 0;
			local e_iscpu = 0;
			local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
			if( player.push() )
			{
				p_iscpu = BMvTbl.IsCpu();
				player.pop();
			}
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				e_iscpu = BMvTbl.IsCpu();
				enemy.pop();
			}
		
			if( !p_iscpu && e_iscpu )
			{
				// ©•ª‘¤
				local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_TrainingDummyMngInit", datatype=1 } );
				Battle_Std.GS_AddFlag( def_PP_GS_ExtraTrainingMode ); // ƒLƒƒƒ‰‘€ì•s”\‚É‚·‚é
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100);
			}
			else if( p_iscpu && !e_iscpu )
			{
				// ‘Šè‘¤
				Battle_Std.GS_AddFlag( def_PP_GS_ExtraTrainingMode ); // ƒLƒƒƒ‰‘€ì•s”\‚É‚·‚é
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100);
			}
		}
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() //
	{
		BMvTbl.SetRoundStart(); //”O‚Ìˆ×í“¬ŠJn‰Â”\ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
	}	
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral", [100,"Mv_TrainingDummyNeutral"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}

// ƒŠƒXƒ^[ƒg’¼Œã‚¾‚¯¶¬‚³‚ê‚ÄAƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚Ì‰‰o‚ğ‚©‚«Á‚·‚½‚ß‚Éƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ‚½‚Ä‚é
// ƒgƒŒƒ‚Œn‚Å‚Ì‚İ—LŒø
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Null_ImmediatelyAfterRestart <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender } );	
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(990);
		Battle_Std.GS_AddFlag( def_PP_GS_ImmediatelyAfterRestart );
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0);
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus();
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0)==0 && rs.CharaMoveMode == _CharaMoveMode_Enable )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLP(0,mvs.MvCount);
		}

		local moveable_mvcount = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
		local time = mvs.MvCount - moveable_mvcount;
		// _dpn("time:"+time);
		if( mvs.MvCount >= 5 || time >= 3 ) // time == 2‚Å\•ª‚¾‚ª”O‚Ì‚½‚ß3
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize();
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.GS_DelFlag( def_PP_GS_ImmediatelyAfterRestart );
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Null_StandbyCamera <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		// _dpn("Mv_Null_StandbyCamera");

		// BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0);// ‚¾‚¢‚½‚¢‚Ì‰¹º‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ª“ü‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚ÅƒJƒƒ‰‚ÌˆÚ“®ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Ég‚¤
		local voice_frame = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
		local shift_x = 64*128;
		
		//1P‚©2P‚©‚ÅŠJnÀ•W‚ÍŒˆ‚Ü‚é
		local pos_x = ( BMvTbl.GetPlayerSide() == 0 )? -320*128 : 320*128;
		
		// BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { num=0, x=pos.x+shift_x*BMvTbl.GetMuki(), y=0, zoom=1.0, time=[10,999,0], type_in=1 } );
		// BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { num=1, x=pos.x, y=0, zoom=1.0, time=[voice_frame,255,10], type_in=1 } );

		BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { num=0, x=0, y=0, zoom=1.0, time=[0,999,0], type_in=1 } );
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { num=1, x=pos_x+shift_x*BMvTbl.GetMuki(), y=0, zoom=1.0, time=[10,255,10], type_in=1 } );

		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender } );	
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(990);

		// _dpn("ŠJn voice_frame:"+voice_frame);
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.MvCount == 10 )
		{
			local voice_frame = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
			local shift_x = 64*128;
		
			//1P‚©2P‚©‚ÅŠJnÀ•W‚ÍŒˆ‚Ü‚é
			local pos_x = ( BMvTbl.GetPlayerSide() == 0 )? -320*128 : 320*128;

			BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { num=0, x=pos_x+shift_x*BMvTbl.GetMuki(), y=0, zoom=1.0, time=[0,999,0], type_in=1 } );
			BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { num=1, x=pos_x, y=0, zoom=1.0, time=[voice_frame,255,10], type_in=1 } );
			
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

// ƒAƒCƒRƒ“‚ÍŠÔ‚Å‚àŸè‚ÉÁ‚¦‚é‚ªAƒXƒNƒŠƒvƒg‚Å‚à‹““®‚ğ‘€ì‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é
Std_MoveTable.Mv_SystemHereYouAre <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMuki( _Direction_Right );//•¶š‚È‚Ì‚Å‰EŒü‚«ŒÅ’è
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( { y=-435*128, } );
		
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoCamera } );
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0);//Á–ÅŠJn‚µ‚½‚ç1
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.MvCount>600 )//•ÛŒ¯ˆ—
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			return;
		}
		if( Battle_Std.GetUpdateFrameID( mvs ) == 900 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); // Ÿè‚ÉÁ–Å‚Ü‚Åi‚ñ‚¾
		}
		
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0)==0 )
		{
			local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus();
			if( rs.CharaMoveMode == _CharaMoveMode_Enable )
			{
				// ƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ª“®‚¯‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚È‚Á‚½‚çÁ‚·
				BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(900);
				BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); // Á–ÅŠJn‚É”ò‚ñ‚¾‚Ì‚Å1
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

// ƒgƒŒƒ‚‚Ì“Áê—ûK—p
Std_MoveTable.Mv_SystemHereYouAreTraining <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMuki( _Direction_Right );//•¶š‚È‚Ì‚Å‰EŒü‚«ŒÅ’è
		
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround|_ObjFlags_ParentMove } );
		BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv|_ObjProcFlag_EraseParentDelete } );
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=0, y=-444*128, } );
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0);//Á–ÅŠJn‚µ‚½‚ç1
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.MvCount>600 )//•ÛŒ¯ˆ—
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			return;
		}
		if( Battle_Std.GetUpdateFrameID( mvs ) == 900 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); // Ÿè‚ÉÁ–Å‚Ü‚Åi‚ñ‚¾
		}
		
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0)==0 )
		{
			local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus();
			if( mvs.MvCount >= 60 )
			{
				// ƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ª“®‚¯‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚È‚Á‚½‚çÁ‚·
				BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(900);
				BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); // Á–ÅŠJn‚É”ò‚ñ‚¾‚Ì‚Å1
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

//”Ä—p“oê
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Standby <- //“oê‰‰o1P
{
	function CheckEnemyisEnd()
	{
		local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
		if( enemy.push() )
		{
			local emv = BMvTbl.GetMvName();
			enemy.pop();
			
			if( emv == "Mv_Neutral" )
			{
				return 1;
			}
		}
		return 0;
	}
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=900, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); //Mv’†‚Ís“®•s”\‚É

		Battle_Std.InitVector();
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(0);
		
		Battle_Std.CallSystemHereYouAre();

		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,0,0); // PAT‚ÅFinalize‚µ‚½
		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,1,0); // ŠÇ—‚©‚çI—¹—\–ñ
		
		//2P‚©‚çŠÇ—ƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg‚ğŒÄ‚Ño‚·
		if( BMvTbl.GetPlayerSide() == 1 )
		{
			local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_Null_StandbyCommonManager" } );
			if( eff.push() )
			{
				BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv } );
				
				eff.pop();
			}
		}
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		// •ÛŒ¯ˆ—‚Å15•b‚Å”²‚¯‚é
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.MvCount == 900 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetRoundStart(); //í“¬ŠJn‰Â”\ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
		}
		
		if( BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,0)==1 && BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,1)==1 ) // I—¹—\–ñ
		{
			BMvTbl.SetRoundStart(); //í“¬ŠJn‰Â”\ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100);
		}
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() //
	{
		BMvTbl.SetRoundStart(); //í“¬ŠJn‰Â”\ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		if( BMvTbl.GetFinalizeCode()==0 )
		{
			// PAT0‚És‚Á‚½ŒãÄ“x•ÊPAT‚És‚­ƒP[ƒX‚à‘z’è‚µ‚ÄLPƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚Í‚µ‚È‚¢
			{
				local pside = BMvTbl.GetPlayerSide();
				_dpn((pside+1)+"PI‚í‚è‚ÅPAT0‚Ö");
				BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,0,1); // PAT‚ÅFinalize‚µ‚½
				//ƒ‹[ƒv
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(0);//—§‚¿ƒ|[ƒY‚ÖˆÚ“®‚¾‚¯‚·‚é
				// BMvTbl.SetRoundStart(); //í“¬ŠJn‰Â”\ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
				
				if( CheckEnemyisEnd() )
				{
					BMvEff.Chara_ClearMessageAll();
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			_dpn("Finalize‚Åƒjƒ…[ƒgƒ‰ƒ‹‚Ö");
			BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
			
			if( CheckEnemyisEnd() )
			{
				BMvEff.Chara_ClearMessageAll();
			}
		}
	}
}
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Null_StandbyCommonManager <- //“oê‰‰oŠÇ—ƒ}ƒl[ƒWƒƒ 2P‘¤‚©‚çŒÄ‚Î‚ê‚é
{
	function GetTimeline( player, timeline_type ) : (CommonStandbyParams)
	{
		local player_no = BMvTbl.GetLP(7+player);
		local num = BMvTbl.GetLP(0+player);
		
		if( player_no in CommonStandbyParams &&
		num in CommonStandbyParams[player_no] &&
		timeline_type in CommonStandbyParams[player_no][num]
		)
		{
			return CommonStandbyParams[player_no][num][timeline_type];
		}
		return 0;
	}
	function Init_Std() : (CommonStandbyParams)
	{
		_dpn("¡Mv_Null_StandbyCommonManager----");
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender | _ObjFlags_FromParentStop } );	
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		
		//ŠJn‚ÉƒJƒƒ‰‚ğ‰æ–Ê’†‰›‚ÅŒÅ’è‚·‚é
		//ƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ÌŠJnƒIƒtƒZƒbƒg‚ªˆÙ‚È‚é‚±‚Æ‚ª‚ ‚èAŠJnƒJƒƒ‰‚ªŒX‚­‚Ì‚ğ–h‚®
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { x=0, y=0, zoom=1.0, time=[0,999,0] } );
		
		//QÆæƒe[ƒuƒ‹‚Ì’Š‘ITL”Ô†
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,-1); //1P‚ª’Š‘I‚µ‚½TL”Ô†
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,-1); //2P‚ª’Š‘I‚µ‚½TL”Ô†
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(2,0); //ƒXƒeƒbƒv”
		BMvTbl.SetLP(3,0); // ƒXƒeƒbƒv‚ği‚Ü‚¹‚È‚¢c‚è‘Ò‹@ŠÔj
		BMvTbl.SetLP(4,0); // QÆ‚·‚é‚Ì‚ªduet‚©A‚³‚ç‚É‚Í”½“]‚µ‚ÄQÆ‚·‚é•K—v‚ª‚ ‚é‚© 1:duetQÆ 2:”½“]duetQÆ
		
		//QÆæƒe[ƒuƒ‹
		BMvTbl.SetLP(5,-1); // -1:”Ä—p 0`:‘ŠèƒLƒƒƒ‰w’è(=ƒLƒƒƒ‰”Ô†)
		BMvTbl.SetLP(6,-1); // -1:”Ä—p 0`:‘ŠèƒLƒƒƒ‰w’è(=ƒLƒƒƒ‰”Ô†)
		BMvTbl.SetLP(7,-1); //1P‚ÌƒLƒƒƒ‰”Ô†
		BMvTbl.SetLP(8,-1); //2P‚ÌƒLƒƒƒ‰”Ô†
		BMvTbl.SetLP(9,0); //1P‚ÌƒZƒŠƒt’†:0 2P‚ÌƒZƒŠƒt’†:1
		
		// ‚±‚ÌƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg‚ª2P‘¤‚©‚çŒÄ‚Î‚ê‚é‚Ì‚Åcore‚Í‹t‚É‚È‚é
		local cores = [ BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData(), BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData() ];
		local chrnos = [0,0];//1P‚Æ2P‚ÌƒLƒƒƒ‰”Ô†
		
		//1P‚Æ2P‚ÌƒLƒƒƒ‰”Ô†‚ğæ“¾‚·‚é
		for( local i=0; i<2; i++ )
		{
			if( cores[i].push() )
			{
				// chrnos[i] = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus().CharaNo;
				chrnos[i] = BMvCore.GetCharaNo();
				cores[i].pop();
			}
			BMvTbl.SetLP(7+i, chrnos[i]);
		}
		
		//0: common / enemy / twin_enemy / duet
		//1:          enemy / twin_enemy / duet
		//2:                  twin_enemy / duet
		//3:                               duet
		local common_pick_ar = [ [], [] ]; // ’Êí
		local twin_pick_ar  = [ [], [] ]; // ‚¨Œİ‚¢‚É‘Šè‚ğw’è
		local duet_pick_ar   = [ [], [] ]; // Š|‚¯‡‚¢
		local enemy_pick_ar  = [ [], [] ]; // ‘Šèw’è
		
		local duet_pick = 0;
		local enemy_pick = [0,0];
		local array_find = function( tbl, name, enemy_no )
		{
			if( name in tbl && tbl[name].len() > 0 )
			{
				foreach( slot, val in tbl[name] )
				{
					if( val == enemy_no ) // ”­Œ©
					{
						return 1;
						break;
					}
				}
				return 0;
			}
			else
			{
				return -1;
			}
		}
		//‚Ü‚¸‚ÍƒsƒbƒNƒAƒbƒvˆ—‚ğs‚¤
		for( local i=0; i<2; i++ )
		{
			local player_no = chrnos[i];
			local enemy_no = chrnos[1-i];
			
			_dpn((1+i)+"P‘¤("+player_no+") ŒŸõŠJn...");
			if( player_no in CommonStandbyParams && typeof CommonStandbyParams[player_no] == "array" )
			{
				local tbl = CommonStandbyParams[player_no];
				for( local j=0; j<tbl.len(); j++ )
				{
					//-1:–¢’è‹`‚È‚Ì‚Å¬Œ÷ 0:’è‹`‚µ‚Ä‚ ‚Á‚Ä‚È‚¢ 1:’è‹`‚µ‚Ä‚ ‚Á‚Ä”­Œ©
					local pick = ( array_find( tbl[j], "Enemy", enemy_no ) );
					
					if( pick != 0 )
					{
						if( array_find( tbl[j], "ExcludeEnemy", enemy_no ) == 1 )
						{
							pick = 0;//œŠO‚ª’è‹`‚µ‚Ä‚ ‚Á‚ÄŒ©‚Â‚©‚Á‚½‚ç
						}
						else if( "checkfunc" in tbl[j] )
						{
							// _dpn(j+":checkfunc:"+pick);
							local check_result = 1;//1‚È‚ç“ü‚ê‚é
							if( cores[i].push() )
							{
								check_result = tbl[j].checkfunc();//’e‚­ğŒ‚ª‚ ‚ê‚Î0‚É‚È‚é
								cores[i].pop();
							}
							if( check_result == 0 )
							{
								pick = 0;//•sÌ—p
							}
						}
						
						if( pick != 0 )
						{
							//Šm’è
							if( "duet_timeline" in tbl[j] )
							{
								_dpn(" -> "+j+" duet");
								duet_pick = 1;//duet‚ ‚è
								duet_pick_ar[i].append( { pl=i, num=j, type=2, param=tbl[j] } );//’Š‘I‚É’Ç‰Á
								twin_pick_ar[i].append( { pl=i, num=j, type=2, param=tbl[j] } );//’Š‘I‚É’Ç‰Á
							}
							else if( pick == 1 )//Enemy‚ª’è‹`‚µ‚Ä‚ ‚Á‚Ä¬Œ÷‚È‚ç
							{
								_dpn(" -> "+j+" enemy");
								enemy_pick[i] = 1;//—LŒø
								enemy_pick_ar[i].append( { pl=i, num=j, type=1, param=tbl[j] } );//’Š‘I‚É’Ç‰Á
								twin_pick_ar[i].append( { pl=i, num=j, type=1, param=tbl[j] } );//’Š‘I‚É’Ç‰Á
							}
							else
							{
								_dpn(" -> "+j);
								common_pick_ar[i].append( { pl=i, num=j, type=0, param=tbl[j] } );//’Š‘I‚É’Ç‰Á
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		//‚Ç‚ê‚ğg‚¤‚©ƒŒƒxƒ‹‚ğŒˆ‚ß‚é
		//twin_pick_ar‚ÍğŒ‚ª‡‚¦‚Îˆê”Ô—Ç‚¢pick
		
		local target_pick_ar = [0,0];
		local choice_mode = 0; // 0:’Êí@1:‘Šèw’è@2:duet@3:‚¨Œİ‚¢‚Éenemyw’è‚ ‚è
		if( enemy_pick[0] == 1 && enemy_pick[1] == 1 )
		{
			choice_mode = 3;
			target_pick_ar = twin_pick_ar;
		}
		else if( duet_pick )
		{
			choice_mode = 2;
			target_pick_ar = duet_pick_ar;
		}
		else if( enemy_pick[0] == 1 && enemy_pick[1] == 0 )
		{
			choice_mode = 1;
			target_pick_ar[0] = enemy_pick_ar[0];
			target_pick_ar[1] = common_pick_ar[1];
		}
		else if( enemy_pick[0] == 0 && enemy_pick[1] == 1 )
		{
			choice_mode = 1;
			target_pick_ar[0] = common_pick_ar[0];
			target_pick_ar[1] = enemy_pick_ar[1];
		}
		else
		{
			choice_mode = 0;
			target_pick_ar = common_pick_ar;
		}
		
		//ƒfƒoƒbƒO—p‚É’Š‘I‚µ‚½ƒƒbƒZ[ƒW‚ğ‘‚«o‚·
		if( Def_Dbg_LocalDebugMode )
		{
			_dpn("’Š‘I‚·‚éƒŒƒxƒ‹:"+choice_mode );
			for( local i=0; i<2; i++ )
			{
				local player_no = chrnos[i];
				local enemy_no = chrnos[1-i];
			
				
				for( local j=0; j<target_pick_ar[i].len(); j++ )
				{
					local tmp = target_pick_ar[i][j];
					local target_line = ( "duet_timeline" in tmp.param )? tmp.param.duet_timeline : ( "timeline" in tmp.param )? tmp.param.timeline : 0;
					if( target_line )
					{
						local se = -1;
						for( local k=0; k<target_line.len(); k++ )
						{
							local tmp2 = target_line[k];
							if( "SE" in tmp2 )
							{
								if( "PL" in tmp2 )
								{
									if( tmp2.PL == 0 ) se = tmp2.SE;
								}
								else
								{
									se = tmp2.SE;
								}
							}
						}
						local tag = format("battlevoice_chr%03d_%03d",player_no, se );
						local string = sqfunc.GetExStringText( tag );
						_dpn( format("%dP %d [%3d] %s : %s",(1+i), tmp.type, tmp.num, tag, string ) );
					}
				}
				_dpn("");
			}
		}
		
		//’Š‘I‚µ‚Ä€”õŠJn
		for( local i=0; i<2; i++ )
		{
			local player_no = chrnos[i];
			local enemy_no = chrnos[1-i];
			
			if( target_pick_ar[i].len() > 0 )
			{
				local use_param_no = BMvEff.Random_Limit( target_pick_ar[i].len() );
				local pick_table = target_pick_ar[i][use_param_no];//pick_ar‚©‚çƒ‰ƒ“ƒ_ƒ€‚Å‚P‚Â‚ğ’Š‘I‚·‚é
				if( "duet_timeline" in pick_table.param )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(4,1+i); // QÆ‚·‚é‚Ì‚ªduet‚©A‚³‚ç‚É‚Í”½“]‚µ‚ÄQÆ‚·‚é•K—v‚ª‚ ‚é‚© 1:duetQÆ 2:”½“]duetQÆ
					BMvTbl.SetLP(0+i, pick_table.num );
					
					if( typeof pick_table.param.WaitPAT[0] == "array" )
					{
						if( cores[i].push() )
						{
							BMvTbl.SetPattern( pick_table.param.WaitPAT[0][0] );
							cores[i].pop();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if( cores[i].push() )
						{
							BMvTbl.SetPattern( pick_table.param.WaitPAT[0] );
							cores[i].pop();
						}
					}
					
					if( typeof pick_table.param.WaitPAT[1] == "array" )
					{
						if( cores[1-i].push() )
						{
							BMvTbl.SetPattern( pick_table.param.WaitPAT[1][0] );
							cores[1-i].pop();
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if( cores[1-i].push() )
						{
							BMvTbl.SetPattern( pick_table.param.WaitPAT[1] );
							cores[1-i].pop();
						}
					}
					break;//duet‚â‚Á‚½‚ç”²‚¯‚é
				}
				else
				{
					if( cores[i].push() )
					{
						if( typeof pick_table.param.WaitPAT == "array" )
						{
							BMvTbl.SetPattern( pick_table.param.WaitPAT[0] );
						}
						else
						{
							BMvTbl.SetPattern( pick_table.param.WaitPAT );
						}
						
						if( "OffsetPosition" in pick_table.param )
						{
							local pos = pick_table.param.OffsetPosition;
							if( typeof pos == "array" )
							{
								BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=pos[0].x, y=pos[0].y, flags=_Position_CaptureShift|_Position_Add|_Position_ChangeMuki } );
							}
							else
							{
								BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=pos.x, y=pos.y, flags=_Position_CaptureShift|_Position_Add|_Position_ChangeMuki } );
							}
						}
						cores[i].pop();
			
						BMvTbl.SetLP(0+i, pick_table.num );
					}
				}
			}
		}
		// _dpn("1P:"+BMvTbl.GetLP(7)+" í:"+BMvTbl.GetLP(5)+" TL:"+BMvTbl.GetLP(0)+" 2P:"+BMvTbl.GetLP(8)+" í:"+BMvTbl.GetLP(6)+" TL:"+BMvTbl.GetLP(1)+" Duet:"+BMvTbl.GetLP(4) );
		
		// ‘½•ª‚±‚±‚Åƒe[ƒuƒ‹‚Æ‚©‚ÍŠm’è‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Í‚¸‚È‚Ì‚ÅA‚±‚Ìƒf[ƒ^‚ğ‚à‚Æ‚É1P‚Æ2P‚ğ•Ê‚É“®‚©‚·ŠÇ—ƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg‚ğ¶¬‚·‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚µ‚½‚¢
		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,1,0); // ƒm[ƒ}ƒ‹1P step
		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,2,0); // ƒm[ƒ}ƒ‹2P step

		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,3,0); // ƒm[ƒ}ƒ‹1P delay
		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,4,0); // ƒm[ƒ}ƒ‹2P delay
	}
	function UpdateNormalTimeline( use_timeline, now_pl_side )
	{
		// now_pl_side : 1P‚ÌƒZƒŠƒt’†:0 2P‚ÌƒZƒŠƒt’†:1
		// local step = BMvTbl.GetLP(2);
		local step = BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,1+now_pl_side);
		local timeline_type = BMvTbl.GetLP(4);// QÆ‚·‚éƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒ‰ƒCƒ“‚Ìí—Ş@0:common 1:duet 2:”½“]duet
		// _dpn("UpdateNormalTimeline:"+now_pl_side+" step:"+step+" delay:"+BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,3+now_pl_side) );

		if( !use_timeline || step >= use_timeline.len() )
		{
			// ‚à‚¤‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅI—¹—\–ñ‚ğ“ü‚ê‚é
			// _dpn((1+now_pl_side)+"P step over");
			return 0;
		}
		else
		{
			local target_core = 0;
			{
				target_core = (now_pl_side == 1 )? BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData() : BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
				local tmp_core = (now_pl_side == 1 )? BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData() : BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
			}
			
			if( BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,3+now_pl_side) == 0 ) // ƒXƒeƒbƒv’â~ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€’†‚Å‚È‚¢
			{
				//Init“Iƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO
				local delay = ("delay" in use_timeline[step])? use_timeline[step].delay : 0;
				_dpn("«Init----:"+step+" delay:"+delay );
				foreach( slot, val in use_timeline[step] )
				{
					_dpn(slot+" : "+val);
				}
				_dpn("ª----Init:"+step);
				
				if( target_core.push() )
				{
					if( "PAT" in use_timeline[step] )
					{
						_dpn("PATw’è‚ ‚è");
						local mvname = BMvTbl.GetMvName();
						if( mvname == "Mv_Standby" || mvname == "Mv_StandbyCommon1P" || ( Def_Dbg_LocalDebugMode && (mvname == "Mv_Neutral")) )
						{
							_dp("\n"+(now_pl_side+1)+"P‚Ìƒpƒ^[ƒ“•ÏX step:"+step );
							BMvTbl.SetPattern( use_timeline[step].PAT ); // w’è‚Ìƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚É•ÏX
						}
						else
						{
							_dp("\n ¦‚à‚¤€”õ‚Å‚«‚Ä‚é‚Ì‚Å‰½‚à‚µ‚Ü‚¹‚ñ");
						}
					}
					else
					{
						_dpn("PATw’è‚È‚µ");
					}
					if( "SE" in use_timeline[step] )
					{
						_dp("\n"+(now_pl_side+1)+"P‚Ìƒ{ƒCƒXÄ¶ step:"+step );
						
						local voice_time = ("time" in use_timeline[step])? use_timeline[step].time : delay;
						local camera_time = ("camera_time" in use_timeline[step])? use_timeline[step].camera_time : voice_time;

						local se_flags = ("flags" in use_timeline[step])? use_timeline[step].flags : 0;
						//_dpn("camera_time:"+camera_time);
						
						// š–‹‚Í‚Â‚¯‚È‚¢
						if(1)
						{
							local use_timeline_type = timeline_type;
							if( se_flags&(1<<0) )
							{
								use_timeline_type = 0;
							}
							Battle_Std.Play_SubtitleVoice( use_timeline[step].SE, voice_time, use_timeline_type );
						}
						else
						{
							BSound.SE_Play( { type=_SeType_Player, num=use_timeline[step].SE } );
						}
						
						//ƒJƒƒ‰ŒÄ‚Ño‚µˆ—
						//‡”Ô‚É˜b‚·‚Æ‚«‚Í‚ ‚Á‚Ä‚à—Ç‚³‚»‚¤‚¾‚ªAUNI‚Å‚Íƒeƒ“ƒ|d‹‚ÅŠî–{ƒIƒt‚É‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­
						local camera = ("camera" in use_timeline[step])? use_timeline[step].camera : 0;
						if( camera )
						{
							local use_voice_time = camera_time;//voice_time;
							local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_Null_StandbyCamera", datatype=1 } );
							if( eff.push() )
							{
								if( use_voice_time <= 90 ) use_voice_time = 90;//0div‰ñ”ğ‚Æ•ÛØƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Æ‚¢‚¤‚±‚Æ‚É
								BMvTbl.SetLP(0,use_voice_time);
								
								eff.pop();
							}
						}
					}			
					
					//Ÿ‚ÌPAT‚Ìhint—\–ñ‚ğ‚·‚é
					local next_step = step+1;
					// _dpn(" use_timeline.len():"+(use_timeline.len()) +" step:"+step+" -> "+next_step );
					if( use_timeline.len()-1 >= next_step )
					{
						if( "PAT" in use_timeline[next_step] )
						{
							//XXX:Ÿ‚ÌƒXƒeƒbƒv‚Ìcore‚ğƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚Å‚«‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅA‘ŠèƒLƒƒƒ‰‚Ì—\–ñ‚ğ“ü‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚é‚±‚Æ‚ª‚ ‚éc‚©‚ç•|‚¢‚Ì‚ÅƒRƒƒ“ƒgƒAƒEƒg
							// _dpn("-> hint—\–ñ:"+use_timeline[next_step].PAT+" "+delay+"FŒã");
							// BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=use_timeline[next_step].PAT, frame=0, rest=delay } );
						}
					}
					
					target_core.pop();
				}
				
				// ƒfƒBƒŒƒC‚ğİ’è
				if( delay > 0 ) // ’â~ŠÔ‚Ì’è‹`‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1, 3+now_pl_side, delay ); // ƒXƒeƒbƒv‚ği‚Ü‚¹‚È‚¢ŠÔ‚ğƒZƒbƒg
				}
				else // ’è‹`‚ª‚È‚¢‚Ì‚Åi‚ß‚é
				{
					step++;
					_dp(" -> ƒXƒeƒbƒvi‚ß‚é");
				}
			}
			else // ’â~ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Ì’è‹`‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½‚ ‚Æ
			{
				//Update“Iƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO
				local stopframe = BMvTbl.AddLPEx(1,3+now_pl_side,-1); // c‚èŠÔ‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚·
				
				if( stopframe <= 0 )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,3+now_pl_side, 0 ); // delay‰Šú‰»
					step++;
					_dp(" -> ƒXƒeƒbƒvi‚ß‚é");
				}
			}
		}
		BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,1+now_pl_side,step); // i‚ß‚½•ª‚ğ‹L‰¯
		return 1;
	}
	function UpdateDuetTimeline( use_timeline )
	{
		local step = BMvTbl.GetLP(2);
		local timeline_type = BMvTbl.GetLP(4);// QÆ‚·‚éƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒ‰ƒCƒ“‚Ìí—Ş@0:common 1:duet 2:”½“]duet
		local now_pl_side = BMvTbl.GetLP(9); //common—p@1P‚ÌƒZƒŠƒt’†:0 2P‚ÌƒZƒŠƒt’†:1

		if( !use_timeline || step >= use_timeline.len() )
		{
			// _dpn("step over");
			return 0;
		}
		else
		{
			local target_core = 0;
			{
				//duetE”½“]duet
				target_core = (use_timeline[step].PL == 1-( (timeline_type == 2)? 1 : 0 ) )? BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData() : BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
				local tmp_core = (use_timeline[step].PL == 1-( (timeline_type == 2)? 1 : 0 ) )? BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData() : BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
			}
			
			if( BMvTbl.GetLP(3) == 0 ) // ƒXƒeƒbƒv’â~ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€’†‚Å‚È‚¢
			{
				//Init“Iƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO
				local delay = ("delay" in use_timeline[step])? use_timeline[step].delay : 0;
				_dpn("«Init----:"+step+" delay:"+delay );
				foreach( slot, val in use_timeline[step] )
				{
					_dpn(slot+" : "+val);
				}
				_dpn("ª----Init:"+step);
				
				if( target_core.push() )
				{
					if( "PAT" in use_timeline[step] )
					{
						_dpn("PATw’è‚ ‚è");
						local mvname = BMvTbl.GetMvName();
						if( mvname == "Mv_Standby" || mvname == "Mv_StandbyCommon1P" || ( Def_Dbg_LocalDebugMode && (mvname == "Mv_Neutral")) )
						{
							_dp("\n"+(now_pl_side+1)+"P‚Ìƒpƒ^[ƒ“•ÏX step:"+step );
							BMvTbl.SetPattern( use_timeline[step].PAT ); // w’è‚Ìƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚É•ÏX
						}
						else
						{
							_dp("\n ¦‚à‚¤€”õ‚Å‚«‚Ä‚é‚Ì‚Å‰½‚à‚µ‚Ü‚¹‚ñ");
						}
					}
					else
					{
						_dpn("PATw’è‚È‚µ");
					}
					if( "SE" in use_timeline[step] )
					{
						_dp("\n"+(now_pl_side+1)+"P‚Ìƒ{ƒCƒXÄ¶ step:"+step );
						
						local voice_time = ("time" in use_timeline[step])? use_timeline[step].time : delay;
						local camera_time = ("camera_time" in use_timeline[step])? use_timeline[step].camera_time : voice_time;
						local se_flags = ("flags" in use_timeline[step])? use_timeline[step].flags : 0;
						//_dpn("camera_time:"+camera_time);
						
						// š–‹‚Í‚Â‚¯‚È‚¢
						if(1)
						{
							local use_timeline_type = timeline_type;
							if( se_flags&(1<<0) )
							{
								use_timeline_type = 0;
							}
							Battle_Std.Play_SubtitleVoice( use_timeline[step].SE, voice_time, use_timeline_type );
						}
						else
						{
							BSound.SE_Play( { type=_SeType_Player, num=use_timeline[step].SE } );
						}
						
						//ƒJƒƒ‰ŒÄ‚Ño‚µˆ—
						//‡”Ô‚É˜b‚·‚Æ‚«‚Í‚ ‚Á‚Ä‚à—Ç‚³‚»‚¤‚¾‚ªAUNI‚Å‚Íƒeƒ“ƒ|d‹‚ÅŠî–{ƒIƒt‚É‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­
						local camera = ("camera" in use_timeline[step])? use_timeline[step].camera : 0;
						if( camera )
						{
							local use_voice_time = camera_time;//voice_time;
							local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_Null_StandbyCamera", datatype=1 } );
							if( eff.push() )
							{
								if( use_voice_time <= 90 ) use_voice_time = 90;//0div‰ñ”ğ‚Æ•ÛØƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Æ‚¢‚¤‚±‚Æ‚É
								BMvTbl.SetLP(0,use_voice_time);
								
								eff.pop();
							}
						}
					}			
					
					//Ÿ‚ÌPAT‚Ìhint—\–ñ‚ğ‚·‚é
					local next_step = step+1;
					// _dpn(" use_timeline.len():"+(use_timeline.len()) +" step:"+step+" -> "+next_step );
					if( use_timeline.len()-1 >= next_step )
					{
						if( "PAT" in use_timeline[next_step] )
						{
							//XXX:Ÿ‚ÌƒXƒeƒbƒv‚Ìcore‚ğƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚Å‚«‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅA‘ŠèƒLƒƒƒ‰‚Ì—\–ñ‚ğ“ü‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚é‚±‚Æ‚ª‚ ‚éc‚©‚ç•|‚¢‚Ì‚ÅƒRƒƒ“ƒgƒAƒEƒg
							// _dpn("-> hint—\–ñ:"+use_timeline[next_step].PAT+" "+delay+"FŒã");
							// BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=use_timeline[next_step].PAT, frame=0, rest=delay } );
						}
					}
					
					target_core.pop();
				}
				
				// ƒfƒBƒŒƒC‚ğİ’è
				if( delay > 0 ) // ’â~ŠÔ‚Ì’è‹`‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(3, delay ); // ƒXƒeƒbƒv‚ği‚Ü‚¹‚È‚¢ŠÔ‚ğƒZƒbƒg
				}
				else // ’è‹`‚ª‚È‚¢‚Ì‚Åi‚ß‚é
				{
					step++;
					_dp(" -> ƒXƒeƒbƒvi‚ß‚é");
				}
			}
			else // ’â~ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Ì’è‹`‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½‚ ‚Æ
			{
				//Update“Iƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO
				local stopframe = BMvTbl.AddLP(3,-1); // c‚èŠÔ‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚·
				
				if( stopframe <= 0 )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(3, 0 ); // delay‰Šú‰»
					step++;
					_dp(" -> ƒXƒeƒbƒvi‚ß‚é");
				}
			}
		}
		BMvTbl.SetLP(2,step); // i‚ß‚½•ª‚ğ‹L‰¯
		return 1;
	}

	function Update_Std()
	{
		//ƒfƒoƒbƒOî•ñ•\¦
		if( _DevWindows && Def_Dbg_LocalDebugMode )
		{
			local pos = { x=250, y=150 };
			pos.ret <- function() { y+=20 };
			// debugfont.setcolor(color);
			debugfontL.draw(pos.x,pos.y,format("’Š‘I‚µ‚½‚s‚k”Ô† 1P:%d 2P:%d",BMvTbl.GetLP(0), BMvTbl.GetLP(1)));
			pos.ret();
			debugfontL.draw(pos.x,pos.y,format("DUETƒXƒeƒbƒv:%d ‘Ò‹@:%3d Duet‚©?:%d",BMvTbl.GetLP(2), BMvTbl.GetLP(3), BMvTbl.GetLP(4) ));
			pos.ret();
			debugfontL.draw(pos.x,pos.y,format("QÆæ 1P:%d %d 2P:%d %d ‚Ç‚Á‚¿‚ÌƒZƒŠƒtH:%d",BMvTbl.GetLP(5), BMvTbl.GetLP(7), BMvTbl.GetLP(6), BMvTbl.GetLP(8), BMvTbl.GetLP(9) ));
			pos.ret();
			debugfontL.draw(pos.x,pos.y,format("’ÊíƒXƒeƒbƒv 1P:%d %3d 2P:%d %3d",BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,1), BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,3), BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,2), BMvTbl.GetLPEx(1,4) ));
			pos.ret();
			
			// ‚Ü‚¾is’†‚È‚Ì‚ÉƒQ[ƒ€‚ªn‚Ü‚é‚Æ‚Ü‚¸‚¢(ƒXƒLƒbƒv‚Éš–‹‚¾‚¯c‚éBPAT‚ÉEff6-254‚ª‚ ‚é‚Æ“ü‚Á‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤)
			/*
			local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus();
			if( rs.CharaMoveMode != _CharaMoveMode_Disable )
			{
				debugfontL.draw_message( "ƒƒbƒZ[ƒW‚É‘Î‚µ‚ÄPAT‚ªI‚í‚é‚Ì‚ª‘‚·‚¬‚é" );
				_dpn("ƒƒbƒZ[ƒW‚É‘Î‚µ‚ÄPAT‚ªI‚í‚é‚Ì‚ª‘‚·‚¬‚é:"+rs.CharaMoveMode);
				// BMvTbl.SetFinalize();
				// return;
			}
			*/
		}
		
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.MvCount <= 10 )
		{
			return;
		}
		
		local timeline_type = BMvTbl.GetLP(4);// QÆ‚·‚éƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒ‰ƒCƒ“‚Ìí—Ş@0:common 1:duet 2:”½“]duet
		// ”Ä—p‚©duet‚©”½“]duet‚©‚Åg‚¤ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒ‰ƒCƒ“‚ğ‘I‚Ô
		switch( timeline_type )
		{
		case 0://common
			// _dpn("common");
			// 1P‚Æ2P‚ğ“¯‚Éi‚ß‚é
			local use_timeline = GetTimeline( 0, "timeline" );
			local update_1p_tl = UpdateNormalTimeline( use_timeline, 0 );

			local use_timeline = GetTimeline( 1, "timeline" );
			local update_2p_tl = UpdateNormalTimeline( use_timeline, 1 );
			
			// ‚à‚¤‚Ç‚Á‚¿‚àXV‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢
			if( !update_1p_tl && !update_2p_tl )
			{
				// I—¹
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize();
				return;
			}
			break;
		case 1://duet
			// _dpn("duet");
			local use_timeline = GetTimeline( 0, "duet_timeline" );
			local update_duet_tl = UpdateDuetTimeline( use_timeline );
			if( !update_duet_tl )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize();
			}
			break;
		case 2://”½“]duet
			// _dpn("rev duet");
			local use_timeline = GetTimeline( 1, "duet_timeline" );
			local update_duet_tl = UpdateDuetTimeline( use_timeline );
			if( !update_duet_tl )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize();
			}
			break;
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		_dp("\n ÅŒã‚ÉƒtƒF[ƒh‚Â‚«‚Åí“¬ŠJn‰Â”\ó‘Ô‚ÖˆÚs");
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
		if( player.push() )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,1,1); // ŠÇ—‚©‚çI—¹—\–ñ
			player.pop();
		}
		local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
		if( enemy.push() )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLPEx(1,1,1); // ŠÇ—‚©‚çI—¹—\–ñ
			enemy.pop();
		}
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { time=[0,0,0] } );//ƒJƒƒ‰ŒÅ’è‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
		BMvEff.Chara_ClearMessageAll();//ƒgƒŒƒ‚‚Æ‚©‚Å‚ÌŸ—˜š–‹‚Ì‰Šú‰»
	}
}
// ‚Ç‚ñ‚ÈŸ—˜‚È‚Ì‚©‚ğæ“¾
// ‚Q‰ñæ“¾‚·‚é‚©‚ç‚à‚µ‚©‚µ‚½‚çƒYƒŒ‚é‚±‚Æ‚à‚ ‚é‚©‚à‚µ‚ê‚È‚¢
local get_wintype = function()
{
	local ret_wintype="ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜";
	local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus();
	
	local is_ko = Battle_Std.GS_CheckFlag( def_PP_GS_KoAtk ); // ‰½‚©‚µ‚ç‚ÅKO‚µ‚½H
	if( !is_ko && rs.isWinRound )
	{
		// Œˆ’…ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒvŸ—˜
		ret_wintype = "Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜_ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv"; // ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒvŒˆ’…
	}
	else if( rs.isWinRound )
	{
		// Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜
		ret_wintype = "Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜"; // Œˆ’…
	}
	else if( !is_ko )
	{
		// ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒvƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜
		ret_wintype = "ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜_ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv";
	}
	else
	{
		// ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜
		ret_wintype = "ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜";
	}
	// _dpn("ret_wintype:"+ret_wintype+" is_ko:"+is_ko);
	
	return ret_wintype;
}
local pic_winlife = function()
{
	local ret_winlife = "’Êí";
	
	local hp_ratio = BCMDTbl.GetHPRatio();
	local rand = BMvEff.Random_Limit(100);
	
	//HP‚ª100%‚È‚çˆ³Ÿƒ{ƒCƒXA0%i1%j‚È‚çhŸƒ{ƒCƒX‚É•K‚¸‚È‚é
	//HP‚ª70%ˆÈã‚È‚çˆ³Ÿƒ{ƒCƒXA30%ˆÈ‰º‚È‚çhŸƒ{ƒCƒX‚É‚·‚é‚ªAƒ‰ƒ“ƒ_ƒ€‚Å’Êíƒ{ƒCƒX‚É‚à‚È‚é
	
	if( hp_ratio>=100 )
	{
		ret_winlife = "ˆ³Ÿ";
	}
	else if( hp_ratio<=0 )
	{
		ret_winlife = "hŸ";
	}
	else if( hp_ratio>=70 && rand>=50 )
	{
		ret_winlife = "ˆ³Ÿ";
	}
	else if( hp_ratio<=30 && rand>=50 )
	{
		ret_winlife = "hŸ";
	}
	else
	{
		ret_winlife = "’Êí";
	}	
	
	// _dpn("hp_ratio:"+hp_ratio+" rand:"+rand+" -> "+ret_winlife );
	return ret_winlife;
}
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Win <- //Ÿ—˜•ªŠò
{
	function Init_Std() : (get_wintype)
	{
		Battle_Std.InitVector();
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		
		BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=0, mvname="Mv_WinCamera" } ); //‰‰oŠJn
		local wintype = get_wintype(); // Ÿ—˜î•ñæ“¾
		
		switch( wintype )
		{
		case "Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜_ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv":
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100);
			Battle_Std.Achievement_Unlock(29);//yƒgƒƒtƒB[zƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv‚ÅŸ—˜‚µ‚½
			break;
		case "Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜":
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(200);
			break;
		case "ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜":
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100);
			break;
		case "ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜_ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv":
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100);
			Battle_Std.Achievement_Unlock(29);//yƒgƒƒtƒB[zƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv‚ÅŸ—˜‚µ‚½
			break;
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_WinPattern", [100,"Mv_RoundWinPattern"], [200,"Mv_WinPattern"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}

//Ÿ—˜‚ÌƒJƒƒ‰’–Ú
Std_MoveTable.Mv_WinCamera <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		//ŠJnˆÊ’u‚Í‰æ–Ê’†‰›H
		local pos = BMvEff.GetCameraPosition( { x=0, y=0, flags=_GetPos_DispCamera } );
		BMvTbl.SetPosition(pos);
		
		//‚±‚ÌƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg‚É’–Ú‚·‚é
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { charapos=1, zoom=1.0, time=[45,999,60], type_in=3 } );
		
		
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround|_ObjFlags_NoRender } ); //_ObjFlags_NoRender
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetCamera_FocusKeep( 999 ); // ƒ`ƒ…[ƒgƒŠƒAƒ‹‚Å’·‚¢‚±‚Æ•ú’u‚³‚ê‚é‚Ì‚Å–³ŒÀƒ‹[ƒv‚É‚·‚é
		
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		local maxlen = def_POS_GamenHajiX - (512*128);
		//‘€ìe‚ğƒz[ƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚µ‚ÄƒJƒƒ‰‚Ì–Ú•W‚É‚·‚é
		local p = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // î•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		
		if( p.IsDone ) // —LŒø‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©ˆê‰ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		{
			BMvCore.PushCharaData( p ); // 
				local pos = BMvTbl.GetPosition( 0 );
			BMvCore.PopCharaData();
			local posst;
			local tpos = BMvTbl.CCharaPosition(); // ˆÊ’uî•ñŒ^‚Ì¶¬
			tpos.y = 0;
			if( pos.x <= -maxlen )
			{
				tpos.x = -maxlen;
				posst = BMvEff.GetPointStatus( { position=tpos } ); // ˆÊ’uî•ñæ“¾
				Battle_Std.SetMuki_CCharaPosition( tpos );
			}
			else if(pos.x >= maxlen)
			{
				tpos.x = maxlen;				
				posst = BMvEff.GetPointStatus( { position=tpos } ); // ˆÊ’uî•ñæ“¾
				Battle_Std.SetMuki_CCharaPosition( tpos );
			}
			else
			{
				posst = BMvEff.GetPointStatus( { target=p } ); // ˆÊ’uî•ñæ“¾
				//í‚Ée‚Ì•û‚ğŒü‚«‘±‚¯‚é
				Battle_Std.SetMuki_PlayerPosition();				
			}
			
			if( posst.distance_x>=512 )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=posst.distance_x/20,} );
			}
			else
			{
				Battle_Std.InitVector();
			}
		}
		else
		{
			Battle_Std.InitVector();		
			//print("x");
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.InitVector();
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "..." );
	}
} 
Std_MoveTable.Mv_WinPattern <- //Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜ƒAƒjƒ
{
	function Init_Std() : (pic_winlife)
	{
		Battle_Std.InitVector();
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Win);
		
		local win_life = pic_winlife();
		
		BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_TMP0,0); // ‚Ç‚Ì‰¹º‚ğÄ¶‚µ‚½‚© 0:’Êí 1:ˆ³Ÿ@2:hŸ
	
		if( Def_Dbg_RoundStatus ) _dp1p("\n‚¯‚Á‚¿‚á‚±Ÿ—˜");
		if( Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜", flags=(1<<1) }) == 0 ) //‰½‚àÄ¶‚³‚ê‚È‚©‚Á‚½
		{
			//c‚è‘Ì—Í‚ğŒ©‚Ä•ªŠò
			switch( win_life )
			{
			case "ˆ³Ÿ":
				Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜_ˆ³Ÿ", flags=(1<<1) });
				BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_TMP0,1); // ‚Ç‚Ì‰¹º‚ğÄ¶‚µ‚½‚© 0:’Êí 1:ˆ³Ÿ@2:hŸ
				break;
			case "hŸ":
				Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜_hŸ", flags=(1<<1) });
				BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_TMP0,2); // ‚Ç‚Ì‰¹º‚ğÄ¶‚µ‚½‚© 0:’Êí 1:ˆ³Ÿ@2:hŸ
				break;
			case "’Êí":
				Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜_’Êí", flags=(1<<1) });
				BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_TMP0,0); // ‚Ç‚Ì‰¹º‚ğÄ¶‚µ‚½‚© 0:’Êí 1:ˆ³Ÿ@2:hŸ
				break;
			}
		}		
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() //
	{
		//”O‚Ì‚½‚ß
		BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd(); //í“¬I—¹ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
	}	
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		//Ÿ‚Ìƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚Ö‚Ìˆ—H
		BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd(); //í“¬I—¹ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚éH
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_WinPatternEnd" );
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_RoundWinPattern <- //Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜ƒAƒjƒ
{
	function Init_Std() : (get_wintype,pic_winlife)
	{
		Battle_Std.InitVector();
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_RoundWin);
		
		local wintype = get_wintype(); // Ÿ—˜î•ñæ“¾
		local win_life = pic_winlife();
		
		if( wintype == "Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜_ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv" )
		{
			if( Def_Dbg_RoundStatus ) _dpn("Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜_ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv");
			Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Œˆ’…Ÿ—˜_ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv", flags=(1<<1) });
		}
		else
		{
			if( Def_Dbg_RoundStatus ) _dp1p("\nƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhæ“¾");
			
			if( wintype == "ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜_ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv" && Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜_ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv", flags=(1<<1) }) )
			{
				// _dpn("ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv‚Å‚©‚Âƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv‚Ì‰¹º‚ª’è‹`‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½‚Ì‚ÅÄ¶");
			}
			else if( Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜", flags=(1<<1) }) )
			{
				// _dpn("ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚Í‚í‚©‚ç‚È‚¢‚ªAƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜‚Ì‰¹º‚ª’è‹`‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½‚Ì‚ÅÄ¶");
			}
			else
			{
				// _dpn("‰½‚àÄ¶‚³‚ê‚È‚©‚Á‚½‚Ì‚Å‚«‚Á‚Æ×‚©‚­’è‹`‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Í‚¸‚È‚Ì‚ÅAc‚è‘Ì—Í‚ğŒ©‚Ä•ªŠò‚µ‚ÄÄ¶");
				switch( win_life )
				{
				case "ˆ³Ÿ":
					Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜_ˆ³Ÿ", flags=(1<<1) });
					break;
				case "hŸ":
					Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜_hŸ", flags=(1<<1) });
					break;
				case "’Êí":
					Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhŸ—˜_’Êí", flags=(1<<1) });
					break;
				}
			}
		}		
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() //
	{
		//”O‚Ì‚½‚ß
		BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd(); //í“¬I—¹ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é
	}	
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		//Ÿ‚Ìƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚Ö‚Ìˆ—H
		BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd(); //í“¬I—¹ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚éH
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_RoundWinPatternEnd" );
	}
}

//‚r‚d‚ğ’x‚ç‚¹‚ÄÄ¶‚·‚éƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Obj_DelayPlaySE <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		// _dpn("Mv_Obj_DelayPlaySE:"+BMvTbl.GetLP(0)+" - "+BMvTbl.GetLP(1) );
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender | _ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );		
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( {y=1024, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } );
		BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv } );
		//LP0 ƒfƒBƒŒƒC
		//LP1 Ä¶‚·‚é”Ô†
		//LP2 Ä¶ƒtƒ‰ƒO
		//LP3 ‰¹º‚Ì’·‚³@¦š–‹–³‚µ‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚Í0‚ª“ü‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é
	}
	function Update_Std() //Update‚¶‚á‚È‚¢‚Æ“Š‚°ŠÖŒW‚Å•s‹ï‡‚Å‚é
	{
		//print(".");
		//LP‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚Á‚Ä0‚É‚È‚Á‚½‚çÄ¶‚·‚é
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0)>0 )
		{
			local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
			if( player.push() )
			{
				local isUpdate = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus().isUpdate;
				local mvhs = BMvTbl.GetMvHitStatus();
				local isAtkHitStop = (!isUpdate && mvhs.Type&_HitType_Hit && mvhs.Count>0);
				player.pop();
				
				// _dpn("isUpdate:"+isUpdate+" isAtkHitStop:"+isAtkHitStop+" HitStop:"+mvhs.Count );
				
				if( isUpdate || isAtkHitStop )
				{
					//ŠÔ’â~’†‚¶‚á‚È‚¢‚Æ‚«‚Ì‚İi‚ß‚é
					//update‚µ‚Ä‚È‚¢‚Æ‚«‚àUŒ‚‚ª‚ ‚½‚Á‚Ä‚ÄƒqƒbƒgƒXƒgƒbƒv‚ª‚©‚©‚Á‚Ä‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÍŠÔ’â~‚¶‚á‚È‚­‚ÄUŒ‚ƒqƒbƒg‚È‚Ì‚Åi‚ß‚é
					BMvTbl.AddLP(0,-1);
				}
				// else
				// {
					// _dpn("----------------------------------ˆÈ‘O‚Æˆá‚¤:"+BMvTbl.GetLP(0)+" - "+BMvTbl.GetLP(1) );
				// }
			}
		}
		else //ƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ª‚«‚½
		{
			//Ä¶
			local num = BMvTbl.GetLP(1);
			local flags = BMvTbl.GetLP(2);
			local frame = BMvTbl.GetLP(3);
			
			// _dpn("frame:"+frame);
			
			_vdm(format("PlaySE [%3d] -- Delay",num));
			if( frame != 0 )
			{
				local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
				if( player.push() )
				{
					Battle_Std.Play_SubtitleVoice( num, frame+10 ); // 10Fƒ}[ƒWƒ“
					player.pop();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				Battle_Std.PlayerSE_Play( num, flags ); //_SeType_Player‚Ìnum‚ğÄ¶‚µ‚Ä‹L‰¯
			}
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}
//‚r‚d‚ğ’x‚ç‚¹‚ÄÄ¶‚·‚éƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg2 Param0‚Ü‚¿
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Obj_DelayParam0PlaySE <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		//print("\nŠJn");
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );		
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( {y=1024, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } );
		BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv } );
		//LP0 ‚Ü‚ÂParam0
		//LP1 Ä¶‚·‚é”Ô†
		//LP2 Ä¶ƒtƒ‰ƒO
		//LP3 ‰¹º‚Ì’·‚³@¦š–‹–³‚µ‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚Í0‚ª“ü‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é
	}
	function Update_Std() //Update‚¶‚á‚È‚¢‚Æ“Š‚°ŠÖŒW‚Å•s‹ï‡‚Å‚é
	{
		local mvs = Battle_Std.GetPlayerMvStatus();
		// _dpn("."+mvs.Param0+" LP:"+BMvTbl.GetLP(0) );
		//‘€ìe‚ÌParam0‚ğŠÄ‹‚µ‚Äˆê’v‚µ‚½‚çÄ¶
		
		if( mvs.Param0 == BMvTbl.GetLP(0) )
		{
			//Ä¶
			local num = BMvTbl.GetLP(1);
			local flags = BMvTbl.GetLP(2);
			local frame = BMvTbl.GetLP(3);
			
			_vdm(format("PlaySE [%3d] -- DelayParam0",num));
			if( frame != 0 )
			{
				local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
				if( player.push() )
				{
					Battle_Std.Play_SubtitleVoice( num, frame+10 ); // 10Fƒ}[ƒWƒ“
					player.pop();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				Battle_Std.PlayerSE_Play( num, flags ); //_SeType_Player‚Ìnum‚ğÄ¶‚µ‚Ä‹L‰¯
			}
			
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}
//‹¤’ÊƒAƒNƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚±‚±‚Ü‚Å	


local pat_OkiagariFreeMotionAr = chrparam.Get( { type="‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è‚ÌƒtƒŠ[ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ƒpƒ^[ƒ“", chrnum=_ChrNo } );
local pat_OkiagariFreeMotionFinalizeMv = chrparam.Get( { type="‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è‚ÌƒtƒŠ[ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“Finalizeæ", chrnum=_ChrNo } );

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Modori_Okiagari <- // ‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è‚ÌƒtƒŠ[ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“
{
	function Init_Std() : (pat_OkiagariFreeMotionAr, _ChrNo )
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=def_FL_OkiagariNageMuteki, muteki_dage=0, muteki_dageX=0, muteki_nageX=def_FL_OkiagariNageMuteki } ); //‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è–³“GŠÔ‚ğİ’è
		
		if( _ChrNo == Def_ChrNo_Chr018 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // Mv’†s“®•s”\‚É‚·‚é
		}
		else
		{
			BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=1, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // Mv’†s“®‰Â”\‚É‚·‚é
		}
		
		//‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è‚ÌƒtƒŠ[ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		switch(mvs.DataPattern)
		{
		case 593:
			if( pat_OkiagariFreeMotionAr[0] )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(pat_OkiagariFreeMotionAr[0]);
			}
			else
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			}
			break;
		case 594:
			if( pat_OkiagariFreeMotionAr[1] )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(pat_OkiagariFreeMotionAr[1]);
			}
			else
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			}
		default:
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);//ƒGƒ‰[
			break;		
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() : (pat_OkiagariFreeMotionFinalizeMv)
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( pat_OkiagariFreeMotionFinalizeMv );
	}
}

// s“®•¶š—ñ‚ğ•Ô‚·As“®‚ª‘¶İ‚µ‚È‚¢‚Ü‚½‚Í""‚ª•Ô‚³‚ê‚é‚Æ‚È‚É‚à‚µ‚È‚¢
Std_MoveTable.GetResultMvName <- function()
//function Std_MoveTable::GetResultMvName()
{
	//ƒfƒoƒbƒOˆ—
	if( 0 )
	{
		local pside = BMvTbl.GetPlayerSide();//0:1PƒTƒCƒh 1:2PƒTƒCƒh
		local tagStatus = BMvCore.GetTagStatus();//-1:’Êí 1:ƒ^ƒbƒO‚ÌƒƒCƒ“ 0:ƒ^ƒbƒO‚Ìƒp[ƒgƒi[
		local roundst = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus(); // ‚±‚ê‚Å”»•Ê
		local tag_str = { [-1]="’ÊíƒLƒƒƒ‰", [1]="ƒƒCƒ“", [0]="ƒp[ƒgƒi[" };
	
		_dpn("šGetResultMvName Round:"+roundst.Round+" "+(pside+1)+"PƒTƒCƒh "+tag_str[tagStatus] );
	}
	
	//_dem("I—¹:GetResultMvName");
	Battle_Std.PcAuraEffect_AllAuraEnd(); //Ÿ—˜ƒLƒƒƒ‰orƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv‚Ì—¼Ò‚Í‚±‚Ìƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ÅƒI[ƒ‰‚ğÁ‚·

	local p = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus(); // ‚±‚ê‚Å”»•Ê
	//print("\n::GetResultMvName" + p.Round + "," + p.isMyKo + "," + p.WinType + " : " + p.isWinRound );

	//if( p.Round < 0 ) return ""; // ƒgƒŒ[ƒjƒ“ƒO
	if( p.isMyKo )
	{
		// KO‚³‚ê‚½‚ç“|‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚Åƒ|[ƒY‚ğæ‚ç‚È‚¢
		//‚±‚±‚É‚Í‚¢‚Á‚Ä‚­‚é‚±‚Æ‚Í‚È‚¢‚Ì‚Å‚Í‚È‚¢‚©H‚¾‚Ô‚é‚j‚n‚Å‚à“ü‚Á‚Ä‚±‚È‚¢
		//_dem("”s–k")
		return "";
	}
	else
	{
		// ‹N‚«‚Ä‚¢‚éAŸ‚¿ˆÈŠO‚Íƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv
		if( p.WinType == 1 ) // Ÿ‚¿
		{
			if( Def_Dbg_RoundStatus ) _dm("Ÿ—˜orƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒvŸ—˜");
			return "Mv_Win";
		}
		else
		if( p.WinType == 0 ) // •‰‚¯
		{
			if( Def_Dbg_RoundStatus ) _dm("ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv”s–k");
			return "Mv_TimeupLose";
		}
		else
		{
			// ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒvƒhƒ[
			if( Def_Dbg_RoundStatus ) _dm("ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒvƒhƒ[");
			return "Mv_TimeupLose";
		}
	}
	return "";
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Obj_CharaAura <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoRender|_ObjFlags_NoGround } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( {y=1024, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } ); //‰B‚·‚—
		BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv } );
		
		// local PSFlag = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0) > 0 )
		{
			BMvTbl.AddLP(0,-1);
		}
		else
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); //ƒI[ƒ‰‚Ì—\’è‚³‚ê‚Ä‚½‘±ŠÔI—¹
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable(); //‚Î‚¢‚Î[‚¢
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		//”²‚¯‚é‚Éƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ‚È‚ñ‚¿‚á‚ç‚·‚é
		Battle_Std.PcAuraEffect_CommonAuraEnd();
	}
}

//ƒŠƒo[ƒTƒ‹ŠÔ‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚·‚¾‚¯‚ÌƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Obj_ReversalTime <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		//print("\nŠJn");
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender | _ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );		
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( {y=1024, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } );
	}
	function Update_Std() //Update‚¶‚á‚È‚¢‚Æ“Š‚°ŠÖŒW‚Å•s‹ï‡‚Å‚é
	{
		local p = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ‘€ìe‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		BMvCore.PushCharaData( p ); //
			local pp = BMvTbl.GetPP( def_PP_ReversalLeftTime );
			if( pp>0 ) pp--;
			BMvTbl.SetPP( def_PP_ReversalLeftTime, pp );
		BMvCore.PopCharaData();
		
		//print("\nc‚è:"+pp);
		
		if( pp <= 0) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

//í‚É‘±‚·‚éƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒgi‡‚ªI‚í‚é‚ÆÁ–Åj
//ƒLƒƒƒ‰ƒNƒ^[‚ÌƒI[ƒ‰‚ğŠÇ—‚·‚é
Std_MoveTable.Mv_AuraKanriObject <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoRender|_ObjFlags_NoGround } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( {y=1024, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } ); //‰B‚·‚—
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		if( mvs.CallCount!=0 ) return; // 1ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚É1‰ñ‚Ì‚İ‚É•ÏX
		
		if( mvs.MvCount%7==0 ) //‹C‚¿Œy‚­‚·‚é AC:6 PS3:7
		{
			local noaura = Battle_Std.GetPSFlag(def_PP_PS_NoAura); //ƒI[ƒ‰‚Í’£‚ç‚È‚¢ƒtƒ‰ƒOƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
			if( !noaura ) Battle_Std.PcAuraEffect_AuraCheck(); //ƒI[ƒ‰‚ª•K—v‚È‚ç‚Í‚é	
		}
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		//¡‰½‚ÌƒI[ƒ‰‚ªo‚Ä‚¢‚é‚©H‚ÍPSƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ÅŠÇ—‚·‚é
		//GRD‚ÅƒI[ƒ‰‚ğo‚·‚È‚çAƒI[ƒ‰o‚Ä‚¢‚éŠÔ‘±‚·‚éƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg‚ğ¶¬
		//‰½‚©‚Åã‘‚«‚³‚ê‚½‚ ‚ÆAI‚í‚éƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ÅPSƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ÍƒŠƒZƒbƒg‚³‚ê‚é
		//‚¢‚ÂI‚í‚é‚©‚ª•s–¾‚¾‚Ë
		//”Ä—pã‘‚«ƒI[ƒ‰ŒÄ‚Ño‚µŠÖ”FƒI[ƒ‰‚Â‚¯‚é‚¾‚¯
		//”Ä—pã‘‚«ƒI[ƒ‰I—¹ŠÖ”@@FƒI[ƒ‰Á‚µ‚Äƒtƒ‰ƒO‚àÁ‚·		
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ‘€ìe‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		if( player.push() )
		{
			local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus(); //ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhæ“¾ó‘Ô
			local endflag = ((BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_PlayerStatus)&def_PP_PS_AuraKanriEnd)!=0);
			
			player.pop();
			if( rs.isKo || endflag ) // Œˆ’…orI‚í‚êƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½‚çI—¹
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.PcAuraEffect_AllAuraEnd();
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

// ƒRƒ“ƒZƒ“ƒgƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“ƒ|[ƒY‚ÅUŒ‚‚·‚éƒ^ƒCƒv
Std_MoveTable.Mv_DyingIWEAtk <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		// _dp("\n Mv_DyingIWEAtk Init()");
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_SPAction ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(‚r‚o)‚ğ’Ç‰Á
		
		Battle_Std.SetCharaFlash_LiberateInit(); //Œõ‚Á‚Ä
		
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Convert);	

		Battle_Std.InitVector(); // ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( { y=0 } ); // ’n–Ê‚É‚­‚Á‚Â‚¯‚é
	
		Battle_Std.Reversal.CheckTime_DrawInfo(); // ƒŠƒo[ƒTƒ‹‚È‚ç•¶š•\¦
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
		
		BMvEff.Liberate_End(); // VO‚ğI—¹‚³‚¹‚é
		
		if( Def_Sys_FixSetHosyoHosei_IWtoIWE )
		{
			local comboUsedSpSkill = Battle_Std.EnemyDamageFlag_Check( def_DF_UseSpSkill );
			if( comboUsedSpSkill )
			{
				_dpn("IWE:SP‚Ì•ÛØ•â³");
				Battle_Std.SetSpHosyoHosei( { type="SP" } );//‚Q‰ñ–Ú‚Ì•â³‚ğ‚©‚¯‚é
			}
		}

		//”­“®‚ÉGRD‚Í‹ó‚É‚È‚è‚Ü‚·
		local isVorpal = (BMvEff.GRD_GetJudgeResult()>0);
		
		Battle_Std.GS_DelFlag( def_PP_GS_VorpalDyingIWEAtk ); // VP’†‚ÌIWE‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©

		local stock = BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } );;
		if( Def_Sys_IWEGRDBreakType == 0 )
		{
			BMvEff.GRD_UseStock( { val=stock } ); // ‘SÁ”ï
			if( isVorpal )
			{
				BMvEff.GRD_ClearVorpal( 0 ); // ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚ğ‰ğœ‚·‚é
				Battle_Std.GS_AddFlag( def_PP_GS_VorpalDyingIWEAtk ); // VP’†‚ÌIWE‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
			}
			else
			{
				BMvEff.GRD_SetBreak( 0, def_GRDBF_IWE, def_GRDBF_IWE ); // ƒuƒŒƒCƒN
			}
		}
		else
		{
			if( isVorpal )
			{
				Battle_Std.GS_AddFlag( def_PP_GS_VorpalDyingIWEAtk ); // VP’†‚ÌIWE‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
			}
		}
		
		if( Def_Dbg_LocalDebugScriptPath ) //ƒ[ƒJƒ‹ŠÂ‹«‚Å‚ÌƒfƒoƒbƒO
		{
			Battle_Std.DebugFunc.CallAtkInitScript();
		}
		
		BMvEff.SpGauge_SetLimit( { time=def_FL_EXSLimit_IWEAtk } ); //ˆê’èŠÔƒQ[ƒW‘‰Á‚É§ŒÀ‚ğ‚©‚¯‚é
		BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { mode=_SpGauge_UseBuffer } ); 
		
		//EXƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“‚ÆŠÔ’â~‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚é@‚Å‚à‹Z‚ÍSPƒAƒNƒVƒ‡ƒ“H
		Battle_Std.Init_ExistAtkSkill(); //SPƒQ[ƒWÁ”ïEƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“ŒÄ‚Ño‚µ
		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_Skill|def_MC_EXAction ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(•KE‹Z)‚ğİ’è
		BMvTbl.SetMvAction( def_MVA_EXSystem );
		
		Battle_Std.PlayerSE_StopLastPlaySound(); // “¯‰Ÿ‚µ‘¼‚Ì‹Z‚ªo‚é‚Ì‚Å~‚ß‚é(IWE‰¹º‚ª‚È‚¢‚©‚à‚È‚Ì‚Å)
		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="IWEUŒ‚" });
		
		local muteki_f = 25 + def_FL_EXCutinStopTime;
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=muteki_f, muteki_dage=muteki_f, muteki_nageX=muteki_f, muteki_dageX=muteki_f } ); // ‚¿‚å‚Á‚Æ’·‚ß‚É–³“G
		
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); // ƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚½‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); // ƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“Á‹‚µ‚½‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		
		// ƒ‹[ƒv‚Ì”²‚¯æ‚ğ—\–ñ
		local stopframe = BMvEff.GetFrameIDNum( 256 );
		if( stopframe != -1 ) // FrameID‚ª‚ ‚é‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		{
			BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=def_PAT_Convert, frame=stopframe, rest=8 } );
		}
		
		BMvTbl.SetFinalizeCode(0); // ‰Šú‰»
	}
	function Start_Std()
	{
		// –³“GUŒ‚‚È‚Ì‚Å
		BMvEff.AttackInfoString_Set({ word=def_AISW_Invincibility,} );
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.CallLoopEndCache_FrameID( def_PAT_Convert, 256, 10 ); // ƒLƒƒƒbƒVƒ…—\–ñPat, —\–ñFrameID, ‰½F–ˆ‚ÉŒÄ‚Ô‚©

		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		local mvcount = mvs.MvCount;
		local frameid = mvs.FrameID;
		local mode = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
		
		// XXX:s“®•s”\¨s“®‰Â”\‚Ìƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ÅA+B+C+D‚ÅVO¨IWE‚Æ‚µ‚½‚Æ‚«A‚È‚º‚©2F–Ú‚ÅVO‚ÌƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ª“ü‚Á‚Ä•‚‚­‚Ì‚ğ–h‚®
		if( mvcount <= 1 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetPosition( { y=0 } ); // ’n–Ê‚É‚­‚Á‚Â‚¯‚é
		}
		
		if( frameid == 200 ) BMvTbl.JumpFrameID( 100 ); // –³ŒÀƒ‹[ƒv
		if( mvs.MvCount > (65 + def_FL_EXCutinStopTime) )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize( 256 ); // ”²‚¯‚é
		}
		if( mvs.MvCount > (45 + def_FL_EXCutinStopTime) )
		{
			if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0) == 1 )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(512);	
			}
		}
		if( mvs.MvCount == (10 + def_FL_EXCutinStopTime ) && mvs.CallCount == 0 ) // ÀÛ‚É‚Í +5F‚®‚ç‚¢‚Ì”­¶‚É‚È‚é
		{
			local vo_eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=-260, mvname="Mv_DyingIWEAtk_AtkEff", datatype=1, start_pat=99, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
			if( vo_eff.push() )
			{
				BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_ToParentHitStatus } );
				
				// ‘Šè‚ª‚â‚ç‚ê’†‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚Í”»’è‚ğ‘å‚«‚­‚·‚é
				if( Def_Sys_IWE_IncreaseDamageAtkRectSize && Battle_Std.CheckEnemyisDamage() )
				{
					BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(1000);
				}
				vo_eff.pop();
			}
		}
		
		if( mvs.MvCount > (25 + def_FL_EXCutinStopTime) )
		{
			if( BMvTbl.GetLP(1) == 0 )
			{
				BMvEff.CutInProc_Erase(); //ƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“Á‹
				BMvTbl.SetLP(1,1); // Á‚µ‚½‹L‰¯
			}		
		}

	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		local fc = BMvTbl.GetFinalizeCode(); //¡‚Ìƒtƒ@ƒCƒiƒ‰ƒCƒYƒR[ƒh‚ğæ“¾
		if( fc != 512 ) // Mv_Skill_IWEXIST ˆÈŠO
		{
			BMvEff.CutInProc_Erase(); //ƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“Á‹
		}
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral",[256,"Mv_DyingIWEAtk_Modori"], [512,"Mv_Skill_IWEXIST"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } ); //”²‚¯‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÉÁ‚·
		if( !BMvTbl.FromFinalize() )
		{
			BMvEff.CutInProc_Erase(); //ƒJƒbƒgƒCƒ“Á‹
		}

		if( Def_Sys_IWEGRDBreakType == 1 && BMvTbl.GetLP(0) == 0 )
		{
			_dpn("ƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚È‚¢‚Å”²‚¯‚½");
			local isFromVorpal = Battle_Std.GS_CheckFlag( def_PP_GS_VorpalDyingIWEAtk ); // VP’†‚ÌIWE‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
			if( isFromVorpal )
			{
				BMvEff.GRD_ClearVorpal( 0 ); // ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚ğ‰ğœ‚·‚é
			}
			else
			{
				BMvEff.GRD_SetBreak( 0, def_GRDBF_IWE, def_GRDBF_IWE ); // ƒuƒŒƒCƒN
			}
		}
	}
}

//ƒRƒ“ƒZƒ“ƒgƒŒ[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚Ì–ß‚è
//‚±‚ê‚Ís“®•s‰Â‚É‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­‚Ædef_PAT_Convert‚ÉƒLƒƒƒ‰·‚ª‚ ‚é‚Ì‚Åƒ_ƒ‚¾‚æ

	}	
}

// UŒ‚”»’è¨’ÇŒ‚”»’è‚Ì2ƒqƒbƒg‹Z‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é
// ——R‚Í‚©‚È‚è“ä‚¾‚ªA‘½•ª–³ˆó‘ã‚É‘Š‘Å‚¿‚Æ‚©•Ê‹Z‚Æ‚Ì“¯ƒqƒbƒg‚ÅƒXƒbƒ|”²‚¯‚é‚±‚Æ‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½‚±‚Æ‚Ì‘Î‰‚¾‚Æv‚í‚ê‚é
// ‚Å‚«‚ê‚Îˆ—‚ğ®—‚µ‚½‚¢‚ªˆê’U‚Í‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü‚É‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é
Std_MoveTable.Mv_DyingIWEAtk_AtkEff <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); // ƒY[ƒ€‚Æ‚©‚Í‚P‰ñ‚¾‚¯‚É‚·‚é
	}
	function HitInterrupt_Std()
	{
		// ƒ_ƒ[ƒW‚ğ—^‚¦‚½‚Æ‚«‚Ìˆ—
		// ˆ—‚ª‚©‚È‚è•|‚­Œ©‚¦‚é‚Ì‚ÅAe‚ÌMV‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚½‚Æ‚«‚Íˆ—‚ğ‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É•ÏX
		if( Battle_Std.CheckDamageTiming() && BMvTbl.CheckMvUniqueID( { datatype=_DataType_Player } ) != 0 )
		{
			local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			local effhit = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
			
			local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
			if( player.push() )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); // ƒqƒbƒg
				// _dp("\n VOeffƒqƒbƒg");
				
				if( effhit == 0 )
				{
					BMvEff.Slowmotion_Set( { time=60, power=6666, power_minus=10000/60 } );
					BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { charapos=1, zoom=1.25, time=[8,40,15], type_in=1, type_out=2 } );
					BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { time=35, type=2, clear=1 } );
				}

				// ’eƒqƒbƒg‚Å–{‘Ì–³“G‚É‚µ‚ÄAƒqƒbƒgŒã‘Š‘Å‚¿‚É‚È‚ç‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
				local muteki_f = 60;
				BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=muteki_f, muteki_dage=muteki_f, muteki_nageX=muteki_f, muteki_dageX=muteki_f } ); // ‚¿‚å‚Á‚Æ’·‚ß‚É–³“G
				
				player.pop();
				
				if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0)==0 && mvs.FrameID == 110 )
				{
					// ‘±•”•ª‚¾‚¯ƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚½‚Ì‚ÅSE‚Æ‚©ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‚Æ‚©ŒÄ‚Ô
					_dpn("š¡‚Ü‚ÅIWEƒqƒbƒg‚Å–³‰¹‚¾‚Á‚½‰ÓŠ");
					BSound.SE_Play( { num=5 } ); // ƒLƒbƒNSE
					local createPos = Battle_Std.GetCreatePosition( BMvEff.GetAttackHitPos() );
					BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat="Grp_Hit_XDagekiCLimit", x=createPos.x, y=createPos.y } );
				}
				
				BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); // effhit‚ğ1‚É‚µ‚ÄA‹Êƒqƒbƒgˆ—‚µ‚½‚æ
			}
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				// ˆÈ~‚Ì‹Ê‚Æ‚©ƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‹Ê–³“G‚É‚·‚é
				BMvTbl.SetHitCheckFlag( { type=0, val=_HitCheckFlag_FireBall, time=8, flag=0 } ); // –³“G
				BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=8, } ); // ‚¿‚å‚Á‚Æ–³“G
				
				// ‚±‚ê‚ğ‚â‚é‚ÆŠÌS‚ÌŠÔˆá‚¢ƒqƒbƒg‚ÌƒtƒHƒ[‚ª‚«‚©‚È‚¢
				/*
				local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
				if( bs.isBound && bs.Num == 239 ) // ƒCƒOƒWƒXƒg•‚‚©‚¹‚ª‚¿‚á‚ñ‚Æ‚ ‚½‚Á‚Ä‚½‚çI
				{
				}
				*/
				
				enemy.pop();
			}
		}
	}	
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_IWEAtk_Eff <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
	}
	function HitInterrupt_Std()
	{
		if( Battle_Std.CheckDamageTiming() )
		{
			local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
			if( player.push() )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); // ƒqƒbƒg
				// _dp("\n VOeffƒqƒbƒg");
				
				player.pop();
			}
			
			local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			//“ñ“xƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚·‚é•K—v‚ ‚é‚©H@‚Ü‚Ÿ”O‚Ì‚½‚ß
			//i‚»‚à‚»‚à‚±‚ê‚Í‘Šè‚ª_MvStFlag_DangerHPDef ‚Ì‚¾‚¯ŒÄ‚Î‚ê‚é‚Ì‚Å‘åä•v‚È‚Í‚¸‚¾‚¯‚ÇA‰ñ•œ‚³‚ê‚é‚©‚à‚µ‚ê‚È‚¢‚µcj
			//‹t‚Ìó‹µ‚É‚Í‘Î‰‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢B”­“®‘Ì—Í‚ ‚Á‚ÄAƒqƒbƒg‚Q‚T“–¢–‚¾‚Æƒ_ƒB
			if( mvs.flags & _MvStFlag_DangerHPAtk ) //HP‚ªŠëŒ¯ó‘Ô‚È‘Šè‚ğ‰£‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é
			{
				local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ‘€ìe‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
				if( player.push() )
				{
					BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_IWEXS_able" } );
					
					player.pop();
				}
			}			
		}
	}	
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Liberate <- 	//ƒŠƒxƒŒ[ƒg@ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt
{
	function StepMode()
	{
		//print("\nStepMode");
		BMvTbl.AddLP(0,1); //ƒ‚[ƒh‚ği‚ß‚é
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
		
	}
	function Init_Std() //
	{
		// _dp("\n Mv_Liberate_Init()");
		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_EXAction ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(‚d‚w)‚ğ’Ç‰Á
		BMvTbl.SetMvAction( def_MVA_EXSystem );
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 1, def_MC1_CmdLvDZ_OK );
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_SysAtk ); // ƒVƒXƒeƒ€“I‚ÈUŒ‚
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
		
		Battle_Std.PlayerSE_StopLastPlaySound(); // “¯‰Ÿ‚µ‘¼‚Ì‹Z‚ªo‚é‚Ì‚Å~‚ß‚é
	
		//Battle_Std.PcAuraEffect_LiberateInit(); //ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt‚ÌƒI[ƒ‰
		Battle_Std.SetCharaFlash_LiberateInit(); //Œõ‚Á‚Ä
		
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Liberate);
		
		Battle_Std.InitVector(); // ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, y=-64, addx=0, addy=0, flags=_Vector_Normal } );
		
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254 } );
		
		BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_Temp,0); //”Ä—p•Ï”‰Šú‰»

		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //ó‘Ô
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ó‘ÔƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^
		BMvTbl.SetLP(2,0); //Å‘åƒ^ƒ‚Å‚Í‚È‚¢ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt‚©
		//BMvTbl.SetLP(3,0); //‚h‚e‚w‰Â”\ğŒ‚Á‚·H
		BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0); // ÕŒ‚‚ªƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚½‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		
		/*
		local isOverLiberate = ( BMvEff.Liberate_Get() == _SpGaugeMode_OverLiberate)? true : false;
		local enemyisDangar = false;
		local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // ‚Ä‚«
		if( enemy.IsDone )
		{
			BMvCore.PushCharaData( enemy ); //	
				enemyisDangar = (BCMDTbl.GetHPRatio()<=30);
			BMvCore.PopCharaData(); //
		}
		if( isOverLiberate && enemyisDangar )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLP(3,1); //IFX‰Â”\ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt‚Å‚·‚Ì‚¤
		}
		*/
		
		//BMvEff.Slowmotion_Set( { time=999, power=5000 } );
		//BMvTbl.SetLP(9,0);
		
		// FrameIDƒƒ‚
		// modeƒƒ‚(LP0) ver1.02
		// 10 0 0`8F:ƒ^ƒ‚P–‡–Úi0F–Új
		// 20 1 0`2F:ƒ^ƒ‚Q–‡–Ú
		// 30 2 0`2F:”­“®’¼‘O
		// 40 3 0`2F:”š”­ŠG
		// xx 4 0F:ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒgŒÄ‚Ño‚µƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO
		// 9+3+3+3 18 +2 = 20F
		
		Battle_Std.Reversal.CheckTime_DrawInfo(); // ƒŠƒo[ƒTƒ‹‚È‚ç•¶š•\¦
		
		if( Def_Dbg_LocalDebugScriptPath ) //ƒ[ƒJƒ‹ŠÂ‹«‚Å‚ÌƒfƒoƒbƒO
		{
			Battle_Std.DebugFunc.CallAtkInitScript();
		}
		
		local checkframe_20 = BMvEff.GetFrameIDNum( 20 );
		local checkframe_30 = BMvEff.GetFrameIDNum( 30 );
		local checkframe_40 = BMvEff.GetFrameIDNum( 40 );
		BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=def_PAT_Liberate, frame=checkframe_20, rest=4 } ); // ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“—£‚µ‚Å”ò‚Ô‚Ì‚Å—\–ñ
		BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=def_PAT_Liberate, frame=checkframe_30, rest=6 } ); // ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“—£‚µ‚Å”ò‚Ô‚Ì‚Å—\–ñ
		BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=def_PAT_Liberate, frame=checkframe_40, rest=8 } ); // ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“—£‚µ‚Å”ò‚Ô‚Ì‚Å—\–ñ
		
		BMvTbl.SetFinalizeCode(0); // ‰Šú‰»
		if( Def_Sys_SetCounterHitFlagToVeilOff )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetCounterHitFlag( { val=1, time=9999, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );//”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚é
		}
	}
	function Start_Std()
	{
		// ƒRƒXƒgÁ”ïEg—p‰ñ”Œn‚ÍStartƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚É•ÏX
		BMvEff.AddSkillCount( _SkillCount_VeilOff ); // —š—ğƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‚ğ‰ÁZ
		BMvEff.GRD_ClearBreak( 0 ); //Œø‰Ê‚Æ‚µ‚ÄƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚ğƒNƒŠƒA
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		local mode = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
		local cnt = BMvTbl.GetLP(1);
		//local IFX = BMvTbl.GetLP(3);
		BMvTbl.AddLP(1,1); //ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ƒJƒEƒ“ƒgi‚ß‚é
		
		// _dpn("mode:"+mode+" cnt:"+cnt+" ƒ^ƒ‰ğœ:"+BMvTbl.GetLP(2) );
	
		switch( mode ) //mode:LP0
		{
		case 0: //‰Šú
			if( s.FrameID == 20 ) //Ÿ‚ÌŠG‚Ü‚Åi‚ñ‚Å‚µ‚Ü‚Á‚½‚æ‚¤‚¾
			{
				StepMode();
			}
			else if( cnt>=9 )
			{
				StepMode();
			}
			else if( cnt>=6 ) //ver1.02:6
			{
				//ƒz[ƒ‹ƒhƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
				local mask = (1<<0)|(1<<1)|(1<<2);
				// ƒ^ƒŠJ•ú‚ğƒiƒV‚É‚·‚é
				// if( BMvTbl.CheckButtonHold( mask ) < 100 ) //—v‹‚Æˆê’v–¢–
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(2,1); //“r’†‚Å”²‚¯‚½‚Ì‚Åƒ^ƒ‰ğœÏ‚İƒtƒ‰ƒO
					StepMode();
				}
			}
			break;
		case 1: //Ÿ‚Ìƒ^ƒ‚ÌŠG
			if( s.FrameID == 30 ) //Ÿ‚ÌŠG‚Ü‚Åi‚ñ‚Å‚µ‚Ü‚Á‚½‚æ‚¤‚¾
			{
				StepMode();
			}		
			else if( cnt>=0+2 ) //ver1.02:cnt>1 UNI2ƒÀ:0
			{
				//ƒz[ƒ‹ƒhƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
				local mask = (1<<0)|(1<<1)|(1<<2);
				//‚½‚ß‚Ä‚È‚¢or‘O‚ÌŠG‚Åƒ^ƒ‚ğ‰ğœ‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚½‚ç”²‚¯‚é
				if( BMvTbl.CheckButtonHold( mask ) < 100 || BMvTbl.GetLP(2)==1 ) //—v‹‚Æˆê’v–¢–orƒ^ƒ‰ğœÏ
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(2,1); //“r’†‚Å”²‚¯‚½‚Ì‚Åƒ^ƒ‰ğœÏ‚İƒtƒ‰ƒO
					StepMode();
				}
				else if( cnt>=2 )
				{
					// _dm("‚½‚ß‚«‚Á‚½");
					//ƒ^ƒ‚«‚Á‚½
					BMvTbl.SetFinalize(512); //Å‘åƒ^ƒƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt
					BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(20); //‚±‚ÌŠG‚Å‚à‚¤‚¿‚å‚¢‚Ü‚Á‚ÄI
					StepMode();
				}
			}
			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(20);
			}
			break;
		case 2: //”²‚¯æ@‚½‚Ô‚ñƒ^ƒ‚¶‚á‚È‚¢‚ÌŠm’è
			if( cnt>=0 ) StepMode();  //ver1.02:cnt>1@ver1.03loc:cnt>=0
			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(30);
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { y=-512, flags=_Vector_Normal|_VecFlag_Add } );
			}
			break;
		case 3: //”š”­ŠJn
			if( cnt>=0 ) StepMode();  //ver1.02:cnt>1@ver1.03loc:cnt>=0
			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(40);
			}
			break;
		case 4: //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ ‚½‚¦‚½‚è
			if( cnt>=33+4 ) StepMode();
			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
				local isDying = ( mvs.flags & _MvStFlag_DangerHPDef )? 1 : 0; // •m€ó‘Ô
				
				Battle_Std.SetSpHosyoHosei( { type="VO" } );
				
				// ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv‚ÍÁ”ï‚·‚éiŠÔ‰Ò‚¬‚Ég‚¦‚é‚Ì‚Åj
				if( !Battle_Std.CharaisKO() ) // KOŒã‚É”­“®‚·‚é‚ÆŸ‚Ìƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚ÅƒQ[ƒW‚ª‹ó‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚Ì‚Å’e‚­
				{
					BMvEff.Liberate_Set(); // ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒtó‘Ô‚ğƒZƒbƒg
					BMvEff.Liberate_SetType( _LiberateType_Normal ); // ’Êíƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt
					BMvEff.Liberate_SetMinusValue( def_NormalVO_MinusValue );
				}
				
				// ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt‚É‚È‚Á‚½‚Ìƒ{[ƒiƒX
				if( Def_Sys_VeilOff_GRDBonus )
				{
					local addGRD = ( isDying )? 20000 : 10000; // ‘‰Á’l

					// ”­“®‚ÉGRD‚ğ‘‰Á‚³‚¹‚é
					BMvEff.GRD_AddValue( { val=addGRD, pressure=0, boundplus=0 } );
					//_dp("\n addGRD:"+addGRD);
				}
				BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { mode=_SpGauge_UseAll } ); //‚r‚oƒQ[ƒW‘SÁ”ï
			
				local isOverLiberate = (BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_VeilOffGaugeType)==1)? 1 : 0; //0:100%ŠJ•ú 1:200%ŠJ•ú
				
				//‘Šè‚ª‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚è’†‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚Åƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚é
				local enemy_isDamage = Battle_Std.CheckEnemyisDamage()? 1 : 0; //

				// if( enemy_isDamage==0 ) //¶”­“®‚È‚ç
				// {
					// //”­“®‚É‘Ì—Í‚ğÁ”ï‚·‚é
					// if( isOverLiberate )
					// {
						// BMvEff.SetHpGauge( { value=def_HPV_OverLiberate } );
					// }
					// else
					// {
						// BMvEff.SetHpGauge( { value=def_HPV_Liberate } );
					// }
				// }
				
				local vo_eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=-260, mvname="Mv_Liberate_Eff", datatype=1, start_pat=92, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
				if( vo_eff.push() )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(0,enemy_isDamage);
					BMvTbl.SetLP(1,isOverLiberate);
					BMvTbl.SetLP(2,0); // 0:’Êí@1:‚½‚ß
					
					vo_eff.pop();
				}
				
				// Œã‚ë‚Ì‚à‚â‚à‚â
				local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=0, mvname="", datatype=1, start_pat=83, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
				if( eff.push() )
				{
					//ˆÃ“]‚Æ‚©‚Í•\¦‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É
					BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoRenderBlackOut|_ObjFlags_NoRenderOrder } );
					
					eff.pop();
				}

				Battle_Std.InitVector(); //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»i‰½‚©c‚Á‚Ä‚é‚Æƒ„ƒo‚»‚¤‚È‚Ì‚Åj
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, y=-800, addx=0, addy=50, flags=_Vector_Div } );
				Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt" });
				BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { type=1, time=30 } ); //‰æ–Ê‚ğ‰¡‚É—h‚ç‚·

				BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=13, muteki_dage=13 } );
				
				Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Increment(90,"ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒtg—p");
			}
			break;
		case 5: //—‰ºŠJn
			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); //—‰ºMv‚ÖˆÚs
			}
			break;			
		}
		if( s.isLanding ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256);
		
		// ÕŒ‚”g‚ªƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚Ä‚½‚ç–³“G‚Åã‘‚«‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚­
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(5)==1 )
		{
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254 } );
		}		
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Liberate_End",[256,"Mv_Liberate_Land"],[512,"Mv_MaxLiberate"]); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } ); //”²‚¯‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÉÁ‚·
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Liberate_End <- //—‰º•”•ª
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector(0);
		if( vec.y <= 0 ) //‚Ü‚¾ã¸‚µ‚Ä‚é‚æ‚¤‚È‚ç‰º‚°‚æ‚¤
		{
			Battle_Std.InitVector(); //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, y=0, addx=0, addy=250, flags=_Vector_Normal } );
		}
		
		// ÕŒ‚”g‚ªƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚Ä‚½‚ç–³“G‚Å—‰º
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(5)==1 )
		{
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254 } );
		}
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_SysAtk ); // ƒVƒXƒeƒ€“I‚ÈUŒ‚
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
		if( Def_Sys_SetCounterHitFlagToVeilOff )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetCounterHitFlag( { val=1, time=9999, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );//”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚é
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		if( s.isLanding ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256);
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral",[256,"Mv_Liberate_Land"]); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...	
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } ); //”²‚¯‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÉÁ‚·
	}	
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Liberate_Land <- 	//ƒŠƒxƒŒ[ƒg’…’n
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.InitVector(); //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( { y=0 } ); //‚±‚ê‚Å‰½‚Æ‚©
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(21); //’…’nƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚É‚µ‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤
		local frame = (BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_Temp)==1)? def_FL_LiberateHit_LandRecovery : def_FL_Liberate_LandRecovery;
		//’…’nƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚É‚Íi‚ñ‚Å‚é‚¾‚ë‚¤‚µAó‘Ô‚ÌˆÚs‚¾‚¯s‚Á‚Ä’…’nd’¼‚ğİ’è‚·‚é
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag({ move=0, time=frame, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangePattern|_ClearFlag_ChangeMv }); //’…’nd’¼
		
		// ÕŒ‚”g‚ªƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚Ä‚½‚çs“®‰Â”\‚É‚È‚é‚Ü‚Å‚ğ–³“G‚É‚·‚é
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(5)==1 )
		{
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=frame, muteki_dage=frame } );
		}
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_SysAtk ); // ƒVƒXƒeƒ€“I‚ÈUŒ‚
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
		if( Def_Sys_SetCounterHitFlagToVeilOff )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetCounterHitFlag( { val=1, time=9999, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );//”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚é
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } ); //”²‚¯‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÉÁ‚·
	}	
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_ComboLiberate <- 	//ƒŠƒxƒŒ[ƒg@ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt
{
	function Init_Std() //
	{
		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_EXAction ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(‚d‚w)‚ğ’Ç‰Á
		BMvTbl.SetMvAction( def_MVA_EXSystem );
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_SysAtk ); // ƒVƒXƒeƒ€“I‚ÈUŒ‚
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 1, def_MC1_CmdLvDZ_OK ); // “¯‰Ÿ‚µ‚Å‘¼‚Ìs“®‚ÉˆÚ‚ê‚és“®
	
		Battle_Std.SetEXCutinGrp( { x=0, y=-200, frame=def_FL_EXCutinBGChangeTime } ); // @@@@@@@@ HYPE @@@@@@@@ //
	
		Battle_Std.SetCharaFlash_LiberateInit(); //Œõ‚Á‚Ä
		
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_Liberate);
		
		// ”­“®‚Ì‚‚³‚ğ‹L‰¯‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚«ƒYƒ‰‚µ‰Ÿ‚µ‚ÅIWE‚És‚¯‚é‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©Œˆ‚ß‚é
		local pos = BMvTbl.GetPosition();
		if( pos.y >= 0 ) // B–†‚É‚µ‚Ä‚à‚¢‚¢‚Æ‚Ív‚¤‚¯‚Ç•|‚¢‚Ì‚ÅƒVƒrƒA‚É
		{
			Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 1, def_MC1_GenFlag ); // MvŒÅ—Lƒtƒ‰ƒO
		}

		Battle_Std.InitVector(); // ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, y=-1800, addx=0, addy=128, flags=_Vector_Div } );
		
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254 } );
		
		// FrameIDƒƒ‚
		// modeƒƒ‚(LP0) ver1.02
		// 10 0 0`8F:ƒ^ƒ‚P–‡–Úi0F–Új
		// 20 1 0`2F:ƒ^ƒ‚Q–‡–Ú
		// 30 2 0`2F:”­“®’¼‘O
		// 40 3 0`2F:”š”­ŠG
		// xx 4 0F:ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒgŒÄ‚Ño‚µƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO
		// 9+3+3+3 18 +2 = 20F
		
		Battle_Std.Reversal.CheckTime_DrawInfo(); // ƒŠƒo[ƒTƒ‹‚È‚ç•¶š•\¦
		
		if( Def_Dbg_LocalDebugScriptPath ) //ƒ[ƒJƒ‹ŠÂ‹«‚Å‚ÌƒfƒoƒbƒO
		{
			Battle_Std.DebugFunc.CallAtkInitScript();
		}
		
		// local checkframe_20 = BMvEff.GetFrameIDNum( 20 );
		// local checkframe_30 = BMvEff.GetFrameIDNum( 30 );
		// local checkframe_40 = BMvEff.GetFrameIDNum( 40 );
		// BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=def_PAT_Liberate, frame=checkframe_20, rest=4 } ); // ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“—£‚µ‚Å”ò‚Ô‚Ì‚Å—\–ñ
		// BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=def_PAT_Liberate, frame=checkframe_30, rest=6 } ); // ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“—£‚µ‚Å”ò‚Ô‚Ì‚Å—\–ñ
		// BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=def_PAT_Liberate, frame=checkframe_40, rest=8 } ); // ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“—£‚µ‚Å”ò‚Ô‚Ì‚Å—\–ñ
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); // ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^

		BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0); // ÕŒ‚‚ªƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚½‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		
		Battle_Std.PlayerSE_StopLastPlaySound(); // “¯‰Ÿ‚µ‘¼‚Ì‹Z‚ªo‚½‚è‘O‚Ì‹Z‚Ì‰¹º‚ª‚ ‚é‚Ì‚Å~‚ß‚é
		
		BMvTbl.SetFinalizeCode(0); // ‰Šú‰»
		if( Def_Sys_SetCounterHitFlagToVeilOff )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetCounterHitFlag( { val=1, time=9999, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );//”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚é
		}
	}
	function Start_Std()
	{
		//ƒYƒ‰‚µ‰Ÿ‚µ‚É‚â‚â•Ï‚É‚È‚é‚Ì‚Åƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ğStart‚É•ÏX
		
		//CVO”­“®E‘Šè‚ª“®‚¯‚È‚¢ƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚Å‰‰o
		//‘Šè‚Í‘½•ª•Ê‚Ìˆ—‚ÅEXS Limit‚É‚È‚é‚ªA‚»‚ê‚Ìà–¾‚Ì‚½‚ß
		local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
		if( enemy.push() )
		{
			Battle_Std.DrawInActiveEffect(0); // ƒ_ƒ‚»‚¤‚È‰‰o‚¾‚¯“ü‚ê‚é
			enemy.pop();
		}
		
		// ƒRƒXƒgÁ”ïEg—p‰ñ”Œn‚ÍStartƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚É•ÏX
		BMvEff.AddSkillCount( _SkillCount_VeilOff ); // —š—ğƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‚ğ‰ÁZ
		BMvEff.GRD_ClearBreak( 0 ); //Œø‰Ê‚Æ‚µ‚ÄƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚ğƒNƒŠƒA

		//yƒgƒƒtƒB[zÀí‚ÅƒNƒƒXƒLƒƒƒXƒgƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt‚ğg—p‚µ‚½B
		// BMvTbl.Achievement_Unlock(2);

		Battle_Std.GS_DelFlag( def_PP_GS_CelecVorpal ); // ƒZƒŒƒXƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚È‚Ì‚ğÁ‚·
		BMvEff.GRD_SetConvertBreak( { time=0 } );
		BMvEff.GRD_UseStock( { val=20 } );
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		
		if( mvs.FrameID != 0 )
		{
			local now_fra = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
			if( mvs.CallCount == 0 ) BMvTbl.AddLP(0,1);
			
			// local frames = [2,2,2,2,3,3,3,3,3];
			local frames = [9,8,6,3,2,3,3,2,2];
			
			local now_total = 0;
			
			if( now_fra >= frames[ mvs.DataFrame%frames.len() ] )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); // ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‰Šú‰»
				
				if( mvs.DataFrame+1 >= frames.len() )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetFinalize( 0 ); // ‘«‚è‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅƒGƒ‰[‰ñ”ğ
					return;
				}
				else
				{
					BMvTbl.Frame_Proc( 1, _ValAdd ); // i‚ß‚é
				}
			}
			
			switch( Battle_Std.GetUpdateFrameID( mvs ) )
			{
			case 10:
				BMvEff.SetStopTime( { time=28 } ); //ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg‚ÌŠÔ’â~
				
				local isOverLiberate = (BCMDTbl.GetComboGauge() >= 20000)? 1 : 0;
				
				local usepat = 104; // •m€‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚Å•Ï‚¦‚Ä‚à‘å·‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅŒÅ’è
				local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=-300, datatype=1, start_pat=usepat, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
				if( eff.push() )
				{
					// 200%‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚ÅŒ©‚½–Ú‚ğ•Ï‚¦‚é
					local mask = (1<<1);
					if( Def_Sys_ChangeMaxCVOEffect && isOverLiberate )
					{
						mask = (1<<0);
					}
					BMvTbl.SetHan6LayerMask( {  val = mask } ); // ƒrƒbƒg‚ğ—§‚Ä‚½ƒŒƒCƒ„[‚ğ”ñ•\¦
					BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv } ); // MV•ÏX‚ÅÁ–Å
					eff.pop();
				}
				BMvEff.CutInProc_Set({ time=[3,37,10], cutin_mv="",erasetype=0, bgtype=1 });
				
				if( Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒNƒƒXƒLƒƒƒXƒgƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt" }) )
				{
				}
				else
				{
					Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt" });
				}
				break;
			case 20:

		
				break;
			case 40: // ŠJ•ú
				local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
				local isDying = ( mvs.flags & _MvStFlag_DangerHPDef )? 1 : 0; // •m€ó‘Ô
				local isOverLiberate = (BCMDTbl.GetComboGauge() >= 20000)? 1 : 0;
				
				Battle_Std.SetSpHosyoHosei( { type="CVO" } );
				
				// •m€‚ÅA‚¶‚á‚È‚­‚Ä‘Ì—Í‚ÌŠ„‡‚ÅŒvZ‚ÅA
				// •m€‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚½‚ç‹¤’ÊiˆÈ‘O‚Æ“¯‚¶j‚¾‚ÆAŠoÁ”ò‚Î‚µ‚ª–³‚­‚È‚é
				// •m€‚Å‚Ì‚İ‹­‚¢‚¾‚ÆAˆê‰‹C‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚é‚±‚Æ‚ª‚Å‚«‚éA‚Í‚¸

				BMvTbl.SetVector( { y=0, addy=0, flags=_Vector_Div } );
				local usepat = ( isDying )? 103 : 102;
				local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, mvname="Mv_ComboVO_HitEffect", start_pat=usepat, x=0, y=-200*128 } );
				if( eff.push() )
				{
					// 200%‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚ÅŒ©‚½–Ú‚ğ•Ï‚¦‚é
					local mask = (1<<1);
					if( Def_Sys_ChangeMaxCVOEffect && isOverLiberate )
					{
						mask = (1<<0);
					}
					BMvTbl.SetHan6LayerMask( {  val = mask } ); // ƒrƒbƒg‚ğ—§‚Ä‚½ƒŒƒCƒ„[‚ğ”ñ•\¦
					eff.pop();
				}
				
				if( !Battle_Std.RoundisEnd() ) // KO‚âƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒvŒã‚É”­“®‚·‚é‚ÆŸ‚Ìƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚ÅƒQ[ƒW‚ª‹ó‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚Ì‚Å’e‚­
				{
					BMvEff.Liberate_Set(); // ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒtó‘Ô‚ğƒZƒbƒg
					BMvEff.Liberate_SetType( _LiberateType_Combo ); // ƒRƒ“ƒ{ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt
				}
				
				// def_DyingComboVO_MinusValue ‚à def_ComboVO_MinusValue ‚à140‚È‚Ì‚ÅˆÓ–¡‚ª‚È‚¢
				// local useVal = ( isDying )? def_DyingComboVO_MinusValue : def_ComboVO_MinusValue;
				
				BMvEff.Liberate_SetMinusValue( def_ComboVO_MinusValue );
				
				// ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt‚É‚È‚Á‚½‚Ìƒ{[ƒiƒX
				if( Def_Sys_VeilOff_GRDBonus )
				{
					local addGRD = ( isDying )? 20000 : 10000; // ‘‰Á’l

					// ”­“®‚ÉGRD‚ğ‘‰Á‚³‚¹‚é
					BMvEff.GRD_AddValue( { val=addGRD, pressure=0, boundplus=0 } );
					//_dp("\n addGRD:"+addGRD);
				}
				BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { mode=_SpGauge_UseAll } ); //‚r‚oƒQ[ƒW‘SÁ”ï
				BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { type=1, time=30 } ); //‰æ–Ê‚ğ‰¡‚É—h‚ç‚·
				
				Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Increment(90,"ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒtg—p"); // CVO‚Í•Ê‚È‹C‚à‚·‚é‚¯‚ÇVO‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚±‚Æ‚ÍŠm‚©
				
				// CS“¯—l‚ÉƒŠƒZƒbƒg
				if( Def_Sys_CVO_ResetAnyLimit )
				{
					Battle_Std.EnemyDamageFlag_Del( def_DF_JumpCanseled ); // uƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹‚ğ‚³‚ê‚½v‚ğ‘Šè‚©‚çÁ‚·
					Battle_Std.EnemyDamageFlag_Del( def_DF_AerialJumpCanseled ); // uƒGƒŠƒAƒ‹ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹‚ğ‚³‚ê‚½v‚ğ‘Šè‚©‚çÁ‚·
				}
				break;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
		if( mvs.isLanding ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256);
		
		// ÕŒ‚”g‚ªƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚Ä‚½‚ç–³“G‚Åã‘‚«‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚­
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(5)==1 )
		{
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254 } );
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_ComboLiberate_End",[256,"Mv_ComboLiberate_Land"]); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } ); //”²‚¯‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÉÁ‚·
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_ComboLiberate_End <- //—‰º•”•ª
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector(0);
		if( vec.y <= 0 ) //‚Ü‚¾ã¸‚µ‚Ä‚é‚æ‚¤‚È‚ç‰º‚°‚æ‚¤
		{
			Battle_Std.InitVector(); //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, y=1500, addx=0, addy=250, flags=_Vector_Normal } );
		}
		
		// ÕŒ‚”g‚ªƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚Ä‚½‚ç–³“G‚Å—‰º
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(5)==1 )
		{
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254 } );
		}
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_SysAtk ); // ƒVƒXƒeƒ€“I‚ÈUŒ‚
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
		if( Def_Sys_SetCounterHitFlagToVeilOff )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetCounterHitFlag( { val=1, time=9999, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );//”íƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^[‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚é
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		if( mvs.isLanding ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256);
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral",[256,"Mv_ComboLiberate_Land"]); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...	
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } ); //”²‚¯‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÉÁ‚·
	}
}
