Std_MoveTable.Mv_ComboVO_HitEffect <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_ToParentHitStatus|_ObjFlags_NoGround } );
	}
	function HitInterrupt_Std()
	{
		// ƒqƒbƒg‚Ìˆ—‚ğƒfƒƒŠƒbƒgˆ—‚ğ‘‚­‚ÆAƒRƒ“ƒ{VO‹óU‚è‚ª—¬s‚é‚Ì‚Å’ˆÓ
		
		if( Battle_Std.CheckDamageTiming() ) //ƒ_ƒ[ƒW‚ğ—^‚¦‚½
		{
			// ‚ ‚Ä‚½‚çƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ğ–³“G‚É‚·‚é
			if( BMvTbl.CheckMvUniqueID( { datatype=_DataType_Player } ) != 0 )
			{
				// ƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[‚ª©•ª©g”­¶‚³‚¹‚½s“®’†
				local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
				if( player.push() )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(5,1); // ƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚½‚Ì‚ğ“`‚¦‚é
					
					//CVOƒqƒbƒg‚ÍEXSLimit‚ğ—^‚¦‚é
					//CSƒJƒ“ƒjƒ“ƒO‚ÆˆÙ‚È‚èA‚±‚Á‚¿‚Ís“®‰Â”\‚É‚ ‚Ä‚Ä‚àŒø‰Ê‚ ‚è
					Battle_Std.SetSpecialEXSLimit_Enemy( def_SP_CVO_EXSLimitVal );

					player.pop();
				}
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}


//Å‘åƒ^ƒƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt
Std_MoveTable.Mv_MaxLiberate <-
{
	function StepMode()
	{
		BMvTbl.AddLP(0,1); //ƒ‚[ƒh‚ği‚ß‚é
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
	}
	function Init_Std() //
	{
		//Ä“x–³“G‚É‚·‚é
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254 } );
	
		//•Ï”‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,2); //ó‘Ô
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ó‘ÔƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^
		BMvTbl.SetLP(2,0); //
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(3,0); //‚h‚e‚w‰Â”\ğŒ‚Á‚·H
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(5,0); // ÕŒ‚‚ªƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚½‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		
		
		local isOverLiberate = (BCMDTbl.GetComboGauge() >= 20000)? true : false;
		local enemyisDangar = false;
		local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // ‚Ä‚«
		if( enemy.IsDone )
		{
			BMvCore.PushCharaData( enemy ); //	
				local es = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
				enemyisDangar = (es.flags & _MvStFlag_DangerHPDef);
			BMvCore.PopCharaData(); //
		}
		
		// FrameIDƒƒ‚
		// 10:ƒ^ƒ‚P–‡–Úi0F–Új
		// 20:ƒ^ƒ‚Q–‡–Ú
		// 30:”­“®’¼‘O		
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_SysAtk ); // ƒVƒXƒeƒ€“I‚ÈUŒ‚
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		local mode = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
		local cnt = BMvTbl.GetLP(1);
		BMvTbl.AddLP(1,1); //ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ƒJƒEƒ“ƒgi‚ß‚é
		
		switch( mode )
		{
		case 2: //”²‚¯æ
			if( cnt>=4 ) StepMode();

			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				//‚l‚`‚w‚½‚ß‚ªŠm’è‚µ‚½‚Ì‚ÅƒoƒŠƒoƒŠ‚·‚é
				BSound.SE_Play( { num=74 } ); // ‚¿‚è‚¿‚è‰¹
				BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=-260, mvname="", datatype=1, start_pat=77, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );						
				BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { type=2, time=60 } ); //‰æ–Ê‚ğ—h‚ç‚·

				BMvTbl.SetVector( { y=-512, flags=_Vector_Normal|_VecFlag_Add } );

				//BMvEff.Slowmotion_Set( { time=40, power=5000 } ); //ƒXƒ[‰‰o
				
				//Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt" });		
				
				BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(20);
			}
			break;
		case 3: //”š”­ŠJn
			if( cnt>=2 ) StepMode();

			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(30);
			}
			break;
		case 4: //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ ‚½‚¦‚½‚è
			if( cnt>=33 ) StepMode();
			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
				local isDying = ( mvs.flags & _MvStFlag_DangerHPDef )? 1 : 0; // •m€ó‘Ô
				
				Battle_Std.SetSpHosyoHosei( { type="VO" } );
			
				// ƒ^ƒCƒ€ƒAƒbƒv‚ÍÁ”ï‚·‚éiŠÔ‰Ò‚¬‚Ég‚¦‚é‚Ì‚Åj
				if( !Battle_Std.CharaisKO() ) // KOŒã‚É”­“®‚·‚é‚ÆŸ‚Ìƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚ÅƒQ[ƒW‚ª‹ó‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚Ì‚Å’e‚­
				{
					BMvEff.Liberate_Set(); // ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒtó‘Ô‚ğƒZƒbƒg
					BMvEff.Liberate_SetType( _LiberateType_Max ); // Å‘åƒ^ƒƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt
					BMvEff.Liberate_SetMinusValue( def_MaxVO_MinusValue );
				}
				
				// ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt‚É‚È‚Á‚½‚Ìƒ{[ƒiƒX
				if( Def_Sys_VeilOff_GRDBonus )
				{
					local addGRD = ( isDying )? 20000 : 10000; // ‘‰Á’l

					// ”­“®‚ÉGRD‚ğ‘‰Á‚³‚¹‚é
					BMvEff.GRD_AddValue( { val=addGRD, pressure=0, boundplus=0 } );
					//_dp("\n addGRD:"+addGRD);
				}
				BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { mode=_SpGauge_UseAll } ); //‚r‚oƒQ[ƒW‘SÁ”ï
			
				BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(40);

				local isOverLiberate = (BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_VeilOffGaugeType)==1)? 1 : 0; //0:100%ŠJ•ú 1:200%ŠJ•ú
				
				//Å‘åƒ^ƒ‚Ì‚Í‘Ì—ÍÁ”ï‚È‚µ

				//‘Šè‚ª‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚è’†‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚Åƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚é
				local enemy_isDamage = Battle_Std.CheckEnemyisDamage()? 1 : 0;
				
				//92:100%¶
				//93:100%ƒRƒ“ƒ{
				//94:200%orƒ^ƒ¶
				//95:200%orƒ^ƒƒRƒ“ƒ{
				//96:IWX¶
				//97:IWXƒRƒ“ƒ{

				//ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚ğƒRƒ“ƒ{’†‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚Å•ª‚¯‚é
				local vo_eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=-260, mvname="Mv_Liberate_Eff", datatype=1, start_pat=93, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
				if( vo_eff.push() )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(0,enemy_isDamage);
					BMvTbl.SetLP(1,isOverLiberate);
					BMvTbl.SetLP(2,1); // 0:’Êí@1:‚½‚ß
					
					vo_eff.pop();
				}				
				
				local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=0, mvname="", datatype=1, start_pat=83, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
				if( eff.IsDone )
				{
					BMvCore.PushCharaData( eff ); //	
						//ˆÃ“]‚Æ‚©‚Í•\¦‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É
						BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoRenderBlackOut|_ObjFlags_NoRenderOrder } );
					BMvCore.PopCharaData(); //
				}

				Battle_Std.InitVector(); //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»i‰½‚©c‚Á‚Ä‚é‚Æƒ„ƒo‚»‚¤‚È‚Ì‚Åj
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, y=-800, addx=0, addy=50, flags=_Vector_Div } );
				Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt" });
				BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { type=1, time=30 } ); //‰æ–Ê‚ğ‰¡‚É—h‚ç‚·
				
				Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Increment(90,"ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒtg—p");
			}
			break;
		case 5: //—‰ºŠJn
			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); //—‰ºMv‚ÖˆÚs
			}
			break;			
		}
		if( s.isLanding ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256);
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Liberate_End",[256,"Mv_Liberate_Land"]); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0 } ); //”²‚¯‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÉÁ‚·
	}	
}


//ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒtÕŒ‚”gƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Liberate_Eff <-
{
	function Init_Std() //
	{
		//LP0 ... 1:‘Šè‚Í‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚è’†‚Éo‚³‚ê‚½ 0:‚Ó‚¤‚¤
		//LP1 ... 0:100%ŠJ•ú 1:200%ŠJ•ú

		//LP2 ... 0:’Êí 1:‚½‚ß
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_ShieldWeakGRDAdd );//”íƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚ÅGRD‚ª‘½‚­‘‚¦‚éUŒ‚
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_SysAtk ); // ƒVƒXƒeƒ€“I‚ÈUŒ‚
	}
	function HitInterrupt_Std()
	{
		if( Battle_Std.CheckDamageTiming() ) //ƒ_ƒ[ƒW‚ğ—^‚¦‚½
		{
			if(Def_Sys_VeilOff_SubEnemyGRDStock)
			{
				//‘Šè‚ÌGRD‚ÌŒÂ”‚ğ”¼•ª‚É‚·‚é
				local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
				if( enemy.push() )
				{
					local enemy_grd_stock = BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } );
					local sub_stock = enemy_grd_stock/2;
					BMvEff.GRD_UseStock( { val=sub_stock } );
					_dpn("ƒXƒgƒbƒN:"+enemy_grd_stock+" Œ¸‚ç‚·”:"+sub_stock);
					enemy.pop();
				}
			}
			
			if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0)==1 ) //ƒRƒ“ƒ{’†‚Éo‚µ‚Äƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚½ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt
			{
				if( Def_Sys_VeilOff_GRDBreak == 1 )
				{
					// “G‚ÌGRDó‘Ôæ“¾ 
					if( BMvEff.GRD_GetBreak( 1 ) == 0 ) // 0 ƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢ 1 ƒuƒŒƒCƒN 100 ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg’† 255 ƒI[ƒo[ƒuƒŒƒCƒN
					{
						BMvEff.GRD_ClearVorpal( 1 ); // ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚ğ‰ğœ‚·‚é
					}
				}
				BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_Temp,1); //’…’nd’¼‚ğƒqƒbƒg‚Ì‚à‚Ì‚É‚·‚é
			}
			else //¶‚Åƒqƒbƒg‚³‚¹‚½ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒt
			{
				if( Def_Sys_VeilOff_GRDBreak == 1 )
				{
					local break_time = ( BMvTbl.GetLP(2)==1 )? def_GRDBF_MaxVeilOffDmg : def_GRDBF_VeilOffDmg; // ‚½‚ßVO‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
					BMvEff.GRD_SetBreak( 1, break_time, break_time ); //‘Šè‚ğƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚É‚·‚é
				}
				else if( Def_Sys_VeilOff_GRDBreak == 2 )
				{
					// “G‚ÌGRDó‘Ôæ“¾ 
					if( BMvEff.GRD_GetBreak( 1 ) == 0 ) // 0 ƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢ 1 ƒuƒŒƒCƒN 100 ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg’† 255 ƒI[ƒo[ƒuƒŒƒCƒN
					{
						BMvEff.GRD_ClearVorpal( 1 ); // ƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚ğ‰ğœ‚·‚é
					}
				}
				
				// VO‚ªƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚½‚çVO‚ÌŠÔ‚ğ­‚µ’Z‚­‚·‚éiØ‚è‘Ö‚¦‚¹‚½‚Ì‚Å\•ªj
				if( Def_Sys_StartHitVO_MinusTime )
				{
					local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
					if( player.push() )
					{
						local gauge_status = BMvEff.Liberate_Get();
						if( gauge_status != 0 )
						{
							_dpn("VOŠÔ‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚·:"+Def_Sys_StartHitVO_MinusTime);
							BMvEff.Liberate_Calc( { value=Def_Sys_StartHitVO_MinusTime, mode=0 } );
						}
						player.pop();
					}
				}
				
				BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_Temp,1); //’…’nd’¼‚ğƒqƒbƒg‚Ì‚à‚Ì‚É‚·‚é
			}
			
			// ‚Ç‚Á‚¿‚Å‚à
			
			// ‚ ‚Ä‚½‚çƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ğ–³“G‚É‚·‚é
			if( BMvTbl.CheckMvUniqueID( { datatype=_DataType_Player } ) != 0 )
			{
				// ƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[‚ª©•ª©g”­¶‚³‚¹‚½s“®’†
				local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData();
				if( player.push() )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(5,1); // ƒqƒbƒg‚µ‚½‚Ì‚ğ“`‚¦‚é
					
					player.pop();
				}
			}
			
			// ‚ ‚Ä‚½‘Šè‚Éƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ‚½‚Ä‚é
			Battle_Std.EnemyDamageFlag_Add( def_DF_HitedVO );//VO‚ğ‚ ‚Ä‚ç‚ê‚½
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_IWEXS_able <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		// _dm("IFWXSg—p‰Â”\ó‘ÔŠJn");
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoRender|_ObjFlags_NoGround } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( {y=-100, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } );
		
		BMvEff.Slowmotion_Set( { time=60, power=6666, power_minus=10000/60 } );

		local p = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ‘€ìe‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		if( p.IsDone )
		{
			BMvCore.PushCharaData( p ); //	
				BMvEff.SetCamera_Focus( { charapos=1, zoom=1.25, time=[8,30,15], type_in=1, type_out=2 } );
			BMvCore.PopCharaData(); //
		}
		
		BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { time=35, type=2, clear=1 } );
		
		BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_IWEXS_ChgVO_Hit,1); //g—p‰Â”\		
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		//ƒRƒ“ƒ{ó‘Ô‚ª“rØ‚ê‚½‚çI—¹
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		//_dm("."+s.MvCount);
		if( !Battle_Std.CheckEnemyisDamage() )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		// _dm("IFWXSg—p‰Â”\ó‘ÔI—¹");
		BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_IWEXS_ChgVO_Hit,0); //g—p•s‰Â”\		
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();		
	}
}





//ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg‚·‚é‚Æ¶¬‚³‚ê‚é
//ŠÔ‚ÅGRD‚ªSP‚É•ÏŠ·‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚­
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Convert_Object <-
{
	//LP0:—\–ñGRDÁ”ïƒXƒgƒbƒN@”­“®‚É‚¢‚­‚Â‚ ‚é‚©
	//LP1:SP‚Ç‚ñ‚¾‚¯‘‚¦‚½‚© Œ©‚½–Ú‚Í‚¢‚¢‰ÁŒ¸‚Å‚à‚¢‚¢‚©‚ç”’l‚ ‚é’ö“x‚»‚ë‚¦‚é
	
	//LP2:–ˆƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚¢‚­‚Â‘‚â‚·‚©
	//LP3:•ÏŠ·ŠÔ
	
	function Init_Std()
	{
		// BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0);	// ”­“®‚É‚¢‚­‚Â‚ ‚é‚©‚Ì—\–ñ
		
		local stock = BMvTbl.GetLP(0); // BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } ); // ”­“®‚É‚¢‚­‚Â‚ ‚é‚©æ“¾
		
		local isCanselCS = (BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_ChainShiftStatus)==1); //ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg
		local enemyisBound = Battle_Std.CheckEnemyisBound(); // ‘Šè‚ª‚â‚ç‚ê’†‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©

		local cs_time = stock * 25; //ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg•ÏŠ·ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€@ŒÂ”‚Å•Ï‰»
		local sp_frameAdd = 80; //  1F‚Å‘‚¦‚é—Ê 40=4%
		
		// GRD0ŒÂ‚Å‚à10%‚®‚ç‚¢‚Í‘‚¦‚éBCSƒQ[ƒW‚Í25F‚ÍÅ’á‚Å‚àoŒ»‚·‚éB
		if( stock == 0 )
		{
			cs_time = 25;
			sp_frameAdd = 40;
		}

		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		
		if( isCanselCS )
		{
			if( enemyisBound ) // ‘Šè‚ªBound’†
			{
				cs_time = cs_time * 60 / 100;
			}
			else
			{
				cs_time = cs_time * 40 / 100;
			}
			
			if( player.push() )
			{
				local use_val = def_NormalVO_MinusValue;
				switch( BMvEff.Liberate_GetType() )
				{
				case _LiberateType_Max:
					use_val = def_MaxVO_MinusValue;
					break;
				case _LiberateType_Combo:
					use_val = def_ComboVO_MinusValue;
					break;
				case _LiberateType_Normal:
				default:
					use_val = def_NormalVO_MinusValue;
					break;
				}
				
				BMvEff.Liberate_SetMinusValue( use_val/2 ); // CS’†‚ÌVO‚ÌŒ¸­Š„‡
				
				player.pop();
			}
		}
		else //ƒm[ƒLƒƒƒ“
		{
			if( enemyisBound ) // ‘Šè‚ªBound’†
			{
				cs_time = cs_time * 80 / 100;
			}
			else
			{
				// sp_frameAdd = sp_frameAdd * 100 / 100;
			}		
		
			if( player.push() )
			{
				BMvEff.Liberate_SetMinusValue( 0 ); // CS’†‚ÌVO‚ÌŒ¸­Š„‡
				
				player.pop();
			}
		}
		// _dp("\n CS:"+stock+" cancel:"+isCanselCS+" bound:"+enemyisBound+" time:"+cs_time+" plus:"+(cs_time*sp_frameAdd)/100 );

		//‹L‰¯
		BMvTbl.SetLP(2, sp_frameAdd);
		BMvTbl.SetLP(3,cs_time);

		BMvEff.GRD_ClearBreak( 0 );
		BMvEff.GRD_SetConvertBreak( { time=cs_time } ); // ŠJn¦‚È‚ñ‚©”÷–­‚É10F‚®‚ç‚¢’·‚­‚È‚é‚æ‚¤‚È‚Ì‚ÅLP‚Å”²‚¯‚é

		//”­“®‚ÉGRD‚Í‹ó‚É‚È‚è‚Ü‚·
		if( player.push() )
		{
			local stock = BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } );
			BMvEff.GRD_UseStock( { val=stock } ); // ‘SÁ”ï
			
			player.pop();
		}
		
		// o—Íƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		//_dm( format( "ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒgŠJn[ stock:%d time:%d ]", stock, convert_time ) );		
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		if( s.MvCount<=2 ) return; //Å‰‚¿‚å‚ë‚Á‚ÆEXS‚ª‘‚¦‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚Ì‚ğ–h~
		
		//
		local isconvert = 1; // Œp‘±’†‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		BMvTbl.AddLP(3,-1); //c‚èƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒgŠÔ‚ğŒ¸‚ç‚·
		
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
	
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(3)<0 || BMvEff.GRD_GetBreak(0) != 100 ) // ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒgó‘Ô‚Å‚È‚­‚È‚éğŒ
		{
			if( player.push() )
			{
				local stock = BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } );
				BMvEff.GRD_UseStock( { val=stock } ); // ‘S•”Á”ï
				BMvEff.GRD_ClearBreak( 0 ); // ƒNƒŠƒA‚Í‚³‚ê‚Ä‚é‚¯‚Ç
				
				player.pop();
			}

			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			isconvert = 0; //
			//print("\nI‚í‚è");
		}

		//ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg‚Ìƒ{[ƒiƒX‘O‚Éƒ^ƒCƒ}[ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		//I—¹ğŒ‚Íˆê‰‚±‚ê‚Ì‘O‚Éƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚·‚é
		local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus();
		//print("\nrs:"+rs.TimeUpdate);
		if( rs.TimeUpdate==0 ) //ƒ^ƒCƒ}[‚ªi‚ñ‚Å‚¢‚È‚¢‚Ì‚Å
		{
			//print("\n‚Æ‚Ü‚ê");
			//return;
		}		

		if( isconvert ) // ƒRƒ“ƒo[ƒgó‘ÔŒp‘±’†
		{
			// ƒQ[ƒW‚Ó‚â‚µ
			
			local SPadd = BMvTbl.GetLP(2); //‚PƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚É‘‚â‚·—Ê
			
			if( player.push() )
			{
				BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { value=SPadd } ); // ‚r‚o‚ğ‘‰Á
				
				player.pop();
			}
			//BMvTbl.AddLP(1,-1);
			//local lp = BMvTbl.GetLP(1);
			//if( lp<=0 ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);

			//local z = BMvEff.GRD_GetBreak(0);				
			//print(format(" GB:%d %d : %d",z, s.MvCount, lp));
		}

	}	
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		if( player.push() )
		{
			local use_val = def_NormalVO_MinusValue;
			switch( BMvEff.Liberate_GetType() )
			{
			case _LiberateType_Max:
				use_val = def_MaxVO_MinusValue;
				break;
			case _LiberateType_Combo:
				use_val = def_ComboVO_MinusValue;
				break;
			case _LiberateType_Normal:
			default:
				use_val = def_NormalVO_MinusValue;
				break;
			}
			
			BMvEff.Liberate_SetMinusValue( use_val ); // CS’†‚ÌVO‚ÌŒ¸­Š„‡
			
			player.pop();
		}
	}
}


//ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Convert <- 	//ƒŠƒo[ƒXƒRƒ“ƒo[ƒg
{
	function Init_Std() //
	{
		BMvEff.AddSkillCount( _SkillCount_ChainShift ); // —š—ğƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‚ğ‰ÁZ
	
		//¡‚ÌƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ğ‹L‰¯
		local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector(0);
		BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=vec.x, y=vec.y, addx=vec.addx, addy=vec.addy, flags=_Vector_Keep } ); //•Û‘¶ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚É—^‚¦‚é
		
		//s“®•s”\‚Å–³“G‚É‚·‚é
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangePattern|_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=254, muteki_dage=254, muteki_dageX=254, muteki_nageX=254 } );			
		
		// EŠÔ–³“G‚ğİ’è‚·‚é
		// EŠÔ’â~‚ğİ’è‚·‚éi‚±‚¤‚È‚é‚Æ©•ª‚ÌŠÔ–³“G‚ÍŠÔ’â~’†‚ài‚Şj
		// E‘Šè‚à“¯‚ÉŠÔ’â~‚ğ”­¶‚³‚¹‚é
		// Eæ‚É©•ª‚ÌŠÔ’â~‚ªI‚í‚é¨ŠÔ–³“G‚Í“rØ‚ê‚½ó‘Ô‚Å‘Šè‚ÌŠÔ’â~ó‘Ô‚ª‘±‚­
		// ‚Æ‚¢‚¤ó‹µ‚ª‚ ‚é‚Ì‚ÅA–³“GŠÔ‚Í’·‚ß‚É“ü‚ê‚Ä‚¨‚­‚±‚Æ
	
		//‹¤’Êˆ—
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto); //U‚èŒü‚­
		
		//‚f‚q‚c‚Ì’[”‚ğí‚Á‚Ä‰½ŒÂ‚à‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚©æ“¾‚·‚é
		//‚f‚q‚c‚ª‚UŒÂˆÈã‚¾‚Á‚½‚ç‚·‚²‚¢ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg‚Æ‚·‚é
		//BMvEff.GRD_UseStock( { val=0 } ); //’[”‚ğí‚é@¦‚±‚Ìˆ—•s—v
		local stock = BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } );

		if( stock>Def_Sys_ChainShiftGRDLimit ) stock=Def_Sys_ChainShiftGRDLimit; //ŒÀŠE‚ğ’´‚¦‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚éiclr‚Ü‚Å‚Í6ŒÂ§ŒÀj
		
		// if( stock<1 ) stock=1; //Å’á‚Å‚à1ŒÂˆµ‚¢‚É‚·‚éH 1ŒÂ‚ ‚é‚Æ‚«‚ÌƒƒŠƒbƒg‚ª–³‚¢‚©‚çNG

		local isSuperChainShift = (stock>=6)? 1 : 0; //‚·‚²‚¢ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg‚ÌğŒ
		
		local is_celes_vp = Battle_Std.GS_CheckFlag( def_PP_GS_CelecVorpal ); // ƒZƒŒƒXƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚¾‚Á‚½‚©
		Battle_Std.GS_DelFlag( def_PP_GS_CelecVorpal ); // ƒZƒŒƒXƒ”ƒH[ƒpƒ‹‚È‚Ì‚ğÁ‚·
		if( is_celes_vp )
		{
			isSuperChainShift = 1;
		}
		
		//print("\n isSuperChainShift:"+isSuperChainShift);

		if( isSuperChainShift ) //‚·‚²‚¢ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg
		{
			// BMvTbl.Achievement_Unlock(4); // yƒgƒƒtƒB[zƒQ[ƒW‰ñû120“I
			
			//•â³‚ğ‚ä‚é‚­‚·‚éi­‚µ‰ñ•œ‚·‚éƒCƒ[ƒWj
			local hosei = BMvEff.ComboView_Set();
			if( hosei < def_HOSEI_ChainShiftMax )
			{
				BMvEff.ComboView_Set( { val=def_HOSEI_ChainShift, type=1 } ); // æZ
				BMvEff.ComboView_Set( { val=def_HOSEI_ChainShiftMax, type=0 } ); // ã‘‚«
			}
		}
		else // ’Êíƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg
		{
		
		}
		
		// CS‚É‚æ‚éCP‰ñ•œƒeƒXƒg
		local par = (isSuperChainShift)? def_CPH_SuperChainShift : def_CPH_ChainShift; // ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg‚Ì¦‚³‚Å‰ñ•œŠ„‡‚ª•Ï‰»
		Battle_Std.ComboPoint_Multi( par ); // ƒRƒ“ƒ{ƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ğæZ
		
		//ƒoƒEƒ“ƒh‰ñ”‚ğ‰ñ•œ‚·‚éi‚R‰ñ‚Ü‚Å‚¢‚Á‚Ä‚é‚Æƒ_ƒj
		//ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv‰ñ”§ŒÀ‚àƒŠƒZƒbƒg
		//‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚èC³’l‚àƒNƒŠƒA‚·‚é
		local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “Gî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		local enemy_is_damage = false;
		if( enemy.push() )
		{
			local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
			// short BtlMvStd::MvBoundStatus::isBound
			// ‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚èƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ª‚ ‚éó‘Ô‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©i‚â‚ç‚êó‘Ô‚©‚Å‚Í‚È‚¢
			// 0 --- ‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚èó‘Ô‚Å‚È‚¢
			// 1 --- ‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚èó‘Ô
			// 2 --- ‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚èó‘Ô‚ÅƒK[ƒh‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é
			//print("\n bs.isBound:"+bs.isBound);
			enemy_is_damage = ( bs.isBound==1 );
			if( enemy_is_damage ) // ‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚èó‘ÔiƒK[ƒh‚ÍœŠO‚·‚é‚æj
			{
				if( isSuperChainShift ) //‚·‚²‚¢ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg
				{
					if( bs.WallCount<3 )
					{
						BMvTbl.SetWallCount( 0 ); //ƒoƒEƒ“ƒh‰ñ”‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
					}
					
					//‚Ì‚¯‚¼‚èC³’l‚ğƒNƒŠƒA
					//isBound==1‚Ì‚¾‚¯‚É‚µ‚È‚¢‚ÆƒK[ƒh’†‚É”­“®‚³‚ê‚Äƒnƒ‚ç‚ê‚é
					BMvTbl.BoundCorrect_Calc( { num=-10000 } );  // ’Êí‚Í10‚Å‚Pƒqƒbƒg•ªA-10000‚Ì‚¾‚¯ƒNƒŠƒAˆ—
				}
				
				BMvTbl.DamageFlag_Func( def_DF_JumpCanseled, _ValErase );// uƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹‚ğ‚³‚ê‚½v‚ğ‘Šè‚©‚çÁ‚·
				BMvTbl.DamageFlag_Func( def_DF_AerialJumpCanseled, _ValErase );// uƒGƒŠƒAƒ‹ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹‚ğ‚³‚ê‚½v‚ğ‘Šè‚©‚çÁ‚·
			}
			
			enemy.pop();
		}
		
		//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‰ñ”§ŒÀ‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
		BMvTbl.AddAirCount( def_AC_Assault, -10 ); // ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‰ñ”ƒŠƒZƒbƒg
		
		// ƒLƒƒƒ‰ŒÂ•Ê‚Ì‹ó’†•KE‹Z§ŒÀ‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg
		if( Def_Sys_ChainShift_ResetCharaAirCount )
		{
			BMvTbl.ClearAllAirCounts(); // ƒXƒƒbƒg0`16‚Ü‚Å‚ğ‰Šú‰»‚·‚é
			
			// “ñ’iƒWƒƒƒ“ƒv§ŒÀ‚àƒŠƒZƒbƒg‚·‚é
			BMvTbl.AddAirJumpCount( -10 ); // 0‰ñˆÈ‰º‚É‚Í‚È‚ç‚È‚¢‚Ì‚Å‘½‚ß‚Éˆø‚­
		}

		
		local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_Convert_Object" } );
		if( eff.push() )
		{
			BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoRender|_ObjFlags_NoGround } );
			BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
			BMvTbl.SetLP(0,stock); // GRD‚ÌŒÂ”‚ğ‹L‰¯
			
			eff.pop();
		}
		
		local isCanselCS = (BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_ChainShiftStatus)==1); //ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg
		local voiceType = ( isCanselCS )? "ƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg" : "ƒm[ƒ}ƒ‹ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg";
		// _dp("\n voiceType:"+voiceType );
		if( Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type=voiceType }) )
		{
		}
		else
		{
			Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg" });
		}
		

		//ƒƒbƒZ[ƒW‚Ì•\¦
		BMvEff.AttackInfoString_Set({ word=def_AISW_Convert,} );
		
		//‘Šè‚ÉAˆÃ“]Œã“®‚¯‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚È‚é‚ÆÁ‚¦‚éƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg‚ğŒÄ‚Ô
		Battle_Std.CallAntenStopObject();
		
		//ŠÔ’â~‚ÆˆÃ“]‚Ìˆ—
		BMvEff.SetStopTime( { time=def_FL_ConvertStopTime } ); //ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg‚ÌŠÔ’â~
		
		// ¦ˆÈ‘O‚ÍCSI—¹Œã‚àˆÃ“]ŠÔ‚ªc‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚ÆA“Š‚°‚ç‚ê‚Ä‚àGRDƒuƒŒƒCƒN‚µ‚È‚¢ƒoƒO‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½‚ª¡‚Í‘åä•v‚Ì‚Í‚¸
		BMvEff.CutInProc_Set({ time=[3,def_FL_ConvertStopTime-3,10], cutin_mv="",erasetype=0, bgtype=1 });

		BMvEff.SetSpGauge( {value=def_SP_Convert_Init,} );
		
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_ChainShift); //ƒpƒ^[ƒ“ƒZƒbƒg
		BMvTbl.SetPrio( _CharaPrio_Near ); //–Ú—§‚½‚¹‚é‚æ‚¤‚Éè‘O‚É‚·‚é
		
		BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, y=0, addx=0, addy=0, flags=_Vector_Normal|_Vector_Div|_Vector_Bound|_Vector_DivKeep } ); //KeepˆÈŠO‚ğ‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetVector_MaxX( 0 ); //XMAX‚ğ‰Šú‰»
		
		local rc = BMvEff.GetHanteiRect( { check=[ _Hantei_Etc, 0 ], flags=_HanteiFlag_NoMukiChange | _HanteiFlag_Tool  } );
		if( rc.sx != _Hantei_Error ) // ‘¶İ‚·‚é‚©
		{
			local usepat = ( isSuperChainShift )? 76 : 82; //ƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‚Ìƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚ğŒˆ‚ß‚é
			BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=rc.sx, y=rc.sy, datatype=1, start_pat=usepat, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
			local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=rc.sx, y=0, mvname="", datatype=1, start_pat=83, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
			if( eff.push() )
			{
				BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_ParentMove } );	
				BMvTbl.SetPosition( { y=0 } );
				
				eff.pop();
			}
		}
		local pos = BMvTbl.GetPosition(0);
		if( pos.y >= -64 ) //’nã•t‹ß‚¾‚Á‚½‚ço‚·
		{
			BMvEff.CreateObject( { x=0, y=0, datatype=1, start_pat=87, flags=_Position_ToolShift } );
			BMvTbl.SetPosition( { y=0 } ); // ‰Œ‚ğo‚µ‚½‚Æ‚«’nã‚Å‚È‚¢‚Æ”»•Ê‚Â‚©‚È‚¢
		}
		
		Battle_Std.Reversal.CheckTime_DrawInfo(); // ƒŠƒo[ƒTƒ‹‚È‚ç•¶š•\¦
		
		// –ß‚è‚Ìƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğƒLƒƒƒbƒVƒ…‚É‚Ì‚¹‚Ä‚¨‚­
		local cache_f = def_FL_ConvertStopTime -3; // ‘å‘Ì40F‚®‚ç‚¢‚¾‚©‚ç—]—T‚Á‚µ‚å
		if( cache_f < 0 ) cache_f = 3;
		Battle_Std.CallSkillSoonCaches( [100,cache_f], [200,cache_f] ); // FrameID, rest

		Battle_Std.CharaBattleActivity_Increment(95,"ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg‰ñ”");
		
		Battle_Std.EnemyDamageFlag_Add( def_DF_UseComboChainShift ); // CS‚µ‚½‚±‚Æ‚ğ‹L‰¯
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.MvCount==1 )
		{
			// 190415 ‚Å‚©‚©‚è‚¾‚¯‚Åˆ—‚ğ‚µ‚½‚¢
			// 190410 ‚±‚±‚Åˆ—‚µ‚È‚¢‚Æprio‰œ‚Ì‘Šè‚Ìs“®1F–Ú‚Ì‚Æ‚«‚ÉŒŸ’m‚³‚ê‚È‚¢
			// 181113 CS‚É‘Šè‚ªs“®•s”\‚¾‚Á‚½‚çÔ‚­Œõ‚ç‚¹‚æ‚¤
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				local isCancelNone = BCMDTbl.CheckCancel( _SkillType_None );
				local isBound = Battle_Std.CheckPlayerisBound();
				local isMuteki = BMvEff.GetPlayerMuteki( 0 ); // ‘ÅŒ‚–³“G
				local existKuraiHantei = 0;
				local cmdInfo = BCMDTbl.GetCmdInfoEx(0);
				
				local isInActiveMv = ( Battle_Std.MoveCode.CheckFlag(def_MC_Atk|def_MC_Skill) || Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 7, def_MC7_StdAssult|def_MC7_AirAssult|def_MC7_InActive ) )? 1 : 0;
				{
					for(local i=0; i<8; i++)
					{
						local rc = BMvEff.GetHanteiRect( { check=[ _Hantei_Kurai , i ], } );
						if( rc.sx != _Hantei_Error )
						{
							existKuraiHantei = 1; //‘¶İ‚µ‚½
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if( !isCancelNone && !isBound && isMuteki==0 && existKuraiHantei && cmdInfo.GetFrame() <=10 && isInActiveMv)
				{
					Battle_Std.SetInActiveEffect(); // s“®•s”\‚Ì”íCS‚Å‚Ì‰‰o
					
					
					if( Def_Sys_EnemyAntenStopNoCancel )
					{
						local mvname = BMvTbl.GetMvName();
						local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
						if( mvname == "Mv_Assault_Std" || mvname == "Mv_Assault_Air" )
						{
							_dpn("ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg’†‚Ì”íCS‚Å‚à‚â‚ª‚Â‚¢‚½‚Ì‚ÅƒLƒƒƒ“ƒZƒ‹ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğÁ‚·");
							BMvTbl.SetAsFlag( { as_flags=_AsFlag_ChainShift, time=0, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
						}
					}
		
				}
				enemy.pop();
			}
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		if( s.MvCount>=def_FL_ConvertStopTime) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		if( s.FrameID==100 ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Convert_Modori" );
	}				
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		//”O‚Ì‚½‚ß–³“G‰ğœ
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0, muteki_dageX=0, muteki_nageX=0 } );	
	}
}

//ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒgƒtƒŠ[ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Convert_Modori <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		//’nã‚©‹ó’†‚©‚Å•ªŠò
		//’´’á‹ó‚¾‚Á‚½‚ç’nã‚É‚µ‚½•û‚ª‚¢‚¢‚Ì‚Å‘®«‚Å‚Í‚È‚­À•W‚Å•ªŠò‚·‚é
		local pos = BMvTbl.GetPosition(0);
		if( pos.y> -64 ) //’nã
		{
			BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(100);
			BMvTbl.SetPosition( { y=0 } ); //’n–Ê‚É‚Â‚¯‚é
			Battle_Std.InitVector(); //’nã‚È‚Ì‚Å•Û‘¶ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ÌÄ¶‚Í‚µ‚È‚¢‚Å‰Šú‰»
		}
		else //‹ó’†
		{
			BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(200);
			local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector( { flags=_Vector_Keep } ); //•Û‘¶ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ğŒÄ‚Ño‚·
			
			//‚»‚Ì‚Ü‚Ü‚¾‚Æ‹ó’†•‚—V‚Æ‚©‚©‚Á‚Æ‚Ñ‚·‚¬‚é‚Æ‚©‚ ‚é‚Æv‚¤‚Ì‚Å§ŒÀ‚ğ‚©‚¯‚é
			if( vec.x> 3000 ) vec.x=3000;
			if( vec.x< -3000 ) vec.x=-3000;

			if( vec.y> 3000 ) vec.y=3000;
			if( vec.y< -3000 ) vec.y=-3000;
			
			//addx‚ÍŠë‚È‚¢‹C‚ª‚·‚é‚Ì‚Åg‚í‚È‚¢
			//ƒ`ƒFƒCƒ“ƒVƒtƒg‚ğg‚¤‚±‚Æ‚Å‚ÌŒ¸‘¬‚É‚æ‚é¶‰E“ñ‘ğ‚Æ‚©”­¶‚µ‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚í‚¯‚¾‚ª‚µ‚å‚¤‚ª‚È‚¢
			vec.addx = 0;
			
			//addy‚Í190ˆÈãA250ˆÈ‰º‚É‚·‚é‚·‚é
			if( vec.addy <= 190 ) vec.addy=190;
			if( vec.addy >= 250 ) vec.addy=250;
			
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=vec.x, y=vec.y, addx=vec.addx, addy=vec.addy } );
			
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, y=0, addx=0, addy=0, flags=_Vector_Keep } ); // •Û‘¶‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚½‚à‚Ì‚ğ”jŠü
		}
		
		//s“®‰Â”\‚Å“®‚¯‚é‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=1, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangePattern|_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0, muteki_dageX=0, muteki_nageX=0 } ); //LU‚Å‚à‚â‚é‚¯‚Çˆê‰‚±‚Á‚¿ƒƒCƒ“
		
		BMvTbl.SetCommandLongDelay(1); //ó•tŠÔ‘‰Áiæs“ü—Í—pj
		
		BMvTbl.SetFinalizeCode(0); // ‰Šú‰»
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.isLanding )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(256); //’…’n‚Ö
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral", [256,"Mv_Convert_Modori_Land"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Convert_Modori_Land <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetPattern(21); //’…’n
		Battle_Std.InitVector();
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}
}

local set_Nagemukite_GuardModori = function()
{
	if( BMvTbl.ChangeMv_GetMoveCodeEx( 1 )&def_MC1_ShieldFailed )
	{
		_dp("\n ‘O‚ª¸”sƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚¾‚Á‚½‚Ì‚Å“Š‚°–³“G–³‚µ");
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0 } ); //–³“GŠÔ‚Í–³‚µ
	}
	else
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=def_FL_ModoriGuardNageMuteki } ); //–³“GŠÔ‚ğİ’è
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Modori_GuardA <- 
{
	function Init_Std() : (set_Nagemukite_GuardModori)
	{
		//BMvTbl.SetPattern(def_PAT_GuardA); //¦
		// –ß‚è‚ª‚Qƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚ ‚é
		if( BMvTbl.CheckPosState( _PosState_Air ) )//‹ó’†ó‘Ô‚ÅƒK[ƒh‰ğœ
		{
			if (BMvTbl.JumpFrameID( 120 ) == -1)
			{
				_dem("yŒxz”ò‚Ñæ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ID‚ª–³‚¢");
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			}
		}
		else	//’nãó‘Ô‚ÅƒK[ƒh‰ğœ
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(200); // Mv_Modori_GuardS@‚Ö
			return;
		}
		
		set_Nagemukite_GuardModori(); // ¸”sƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚¶‚á‚È‚¯‚è‚á“Š‚°–³“G‚ğİ’è
		
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=1, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // Mv’†s“®‰Â”\‚É‚·‚é
		BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Auto);
		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒK[ƒh–ß‚è" });
		
		// ƒK[ƒhƒtƒ[ƒg‚©‚ç‚Ì–ß‚è
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(8) ==1 ) // –³“G‚ğƒZƒbƒg‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚½
		{
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=254, muteki_dageX=254 } );
		}	
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.isLanding )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100);
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Modori_GuardAFall", [100,"Mv_Modori_GuardAFallLand"], [200,"Mv_Modori_GuardS"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=0, muteki_dageX=0 } );
	}	
}

//VectorI—¹Œãƒ_ƒEƒ“ŠG‚Ìƒtƒ@ƒCƒiƒ‰ƒCƒY‚Å‚±‚±‚É‚â‚Á‚Ä‚­‚éiŠ®‘S‚ÉQ‚«‚Á‚½ŠG‚Ü‚Å‚­‚éuŠÔ‚É‚±‚±‚Ö‚­‚éj
//‚±‚±‚Å‚j‚nƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚Å‘åä•v‚©H
//‚±‚±‚©‚çæ‚Íbound‘®«‚ª‰ğœ‚³‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚Å–³“GŠÔ‚Æ‚©‚ğ‚Â‚¯‚È‚¢‚Æ‚Ü‚¸‚»‚¤
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Modori_Down <- // ƒ_ƒEƒ“‚©‚ç‚Ì‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		//‚±‚±‚Ü‚Å‚«‚½‚çƒ_ƒEƒ“’Ç‚¢“¢‚¿‚³‚ê‚È‚©‚Á‚½
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=254, muteki_nage=254, muteki_dageX=254, muteki_nageX=254 } ); //–³“GŠÔ‚ğİ’è
	
		local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus(); //ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾		
		// _dp("\n rs.Round:"+rs.Round );
		if (rs.isMyKo && rs.Round != -2 ) //KOó‘Ô‚È‚çQ‚½‚«‚èAƒ`ƒ…[ƒgƒŠƒAƒ‹‚Æ‚©‚ÍœŠO
		{
			BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_nage=0, muteki_dage=0, muteki_dageX=0, muteki_nageX=0 } ); //‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è–³“GŠÔ‚ğÁ‹
			
			//‚Ü‚¸‹N‚«ã‚ª‚èæ‚Ìƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚Öi‚Ş
			local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			switch(s.DataPattern)
			{
			case 290:
			case 350:
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(293);
				break;
			case 291:
			case 352:
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(294);
				break;
			case 292:
			case 354:
			case 356:
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(295);
				break;
			default:
				_dem("yŒxz‚¨‚©‚µ‚¢‚Æ‚±‚ë‚©‚ç”ò‚ñ‚Å‚«‚½‚æ‚O");
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(295);
				break;		
			}
			//‚Ü‚¸256‚É”ò‚ñ‚Å‹N‚«ã‚ª‚ç‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É
			//E•Ê‚ÌMv‚Éó‘ÔˆÚs
			//‚»‚ÌŒãˆê’èƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‘Ò‚Á‚ÄA‹N‚«ã‚ª‚é‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚ğƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
			//ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚Æ‚ç‚ê‚½‚¾‚¯‚©”s–k‚©‚É”ò‚Ô
			if( BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(256)==-1 )
			{
				_dm("yŒxz”ò‚Ñæ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ID‚ª–³‚¢256");
			}
			if( rs.isLoseRound )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(def_FC_Lose);
			}
			else if( rs.WinType == -1) //©•ª‚ªŸ‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©iKOŒã‚Ì‚İjA0-•‰‚¯ 1-Ÿ‚¿ -1-ˆø‚«•ª‚¯
			{
				//ˆø‚«•ª‚¯iŸ”sƒ|ƒCƒ“ƒg‚ª—¼Ò‚É“ü‚éj‚Í”s–kMv‚É‚µ‚Ä‹N‚«ã‚ª‚ç‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚É‚·‚é
				//TODO:‚l‚–Œ©‚Ä‰½‚©‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é‚à‚Ì‚ª‚ ‚é‚Æ‚¿‚å‚Á‚Æ“s‡‚ªˆ«‚¢‚©‚à
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(def_FC_Lose);			
			}
			else
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(def_FC_RoundLose);
			}
		}
		else //KO‚³‚ê‚Ä‚È‚¢‚Ì‚ÅQ‚Á‚Ï‚È‚µ‚ÖA‚à‚µ‚­‚ÍKO‚³‚ê‚½‚¯‚Çƒ`ƒ…[ƒgƒŠƒAƒ‹“à
		{
			//‚Ü‚¸‚ÍQ‚Ä‚¢‚éŠG‚Ü‚Åi‚ß‚æ‚¤@¦“¯‚¶ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚ğ‚Q‰ñ‚â‚é
			local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			switch(s.DataPattern)
			{
			case 290:
			case 350:
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(290);
				break;
			case 291:
			case 352:
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(291);
				break;
			case 292:
			case 354:
			case 356:
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(292);
				break;
			default:
				_dem("yŒxz‚¨‚©‚µ‚¢‚Æ‚±‚ë‚©‚ç”ò‚ñ‚Å‚«‚½‚æ‚O");
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(292);
				break;		
			}
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); // ‚Ë‚Á‚Ï‚Ö
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neppa", [def_FC_RoundLose,"Mv_RoundLose"], [def_FC_Lose,"Mv_Lose"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}

// ƒvƒƒOƒ‰ƒ€‚Ì‹Ù‹}ó‚¯g‚Ìˆ—‚ÅuMv_Neppav‚ğQÆ‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚Å–¼‘O‚Í•ÏX‚µ‚È‚¢‚±‚Æ
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Neppa <- // ƒ_ƒEƒ“‚©‚ç‚ÌQ‚Á‚Ï‚È‚µó‘Ô@Ÿ‚É‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è‚ÖˆÚs
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=254, muteki_nage=254, muteki_dageX=254, muteki_nageX=254 } ); //–³“GŠÔ‚ğİ’è
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		
		// 0`10 ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“ó‚¯gó•t 11`29:Q‚Ä‚é 30:‹N‚«ã‚ª‚é
		local mask = (1<<0)|(1<<1)|(1<<2)|(1<<3); // D‚àŠÜ‚Ş
		local push_button = ( BMvTbl.IsCpu()==0 )? BMvTbl.CheckButtonHoldFrame( mask, 2 ) : BMvTbl.CheckButtonHold( mask ); // ƒvƒŒƒCƒ„[‚Ì‚İ2F‘O‚Ì“ü—Í‚ğŒ©‚éiƒ[ƒ‹ƒoƒbƒN‘Îôj
		if( mvs.MvCount >= 20 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); // ‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è‚Öi‚Ş
		}
		else if( mvs.MvCount <= 10 )
		{
			if( push_button ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100); // ó‚¯g‚Öi‚Ş
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Okiagari", [100,"Mv_NeppaRecover"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Okiagari <- // Q‚Á‚Ï‚È‚µ‚©‚ç‚Ì‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dage=254, muteki_nage=254, muteki_dageX=254, muteki_nageX=254 } ); //–³“GŠÔ‚ğİ’è
		BMvTbl.TM_SetDownStatus(); // ƒ_ƒEƒ“ó‘Ô‘Ò‹@BƒgƒŒƒ‚ƒŠƒo[ƒTƒ‹‹ZŒÄ‚Ño‚µ—pB
		
		//‚Ü‚¸‹N‚«ã‚ª‚èæ‚Ìƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚Öi‚Ş
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		switch(s.DataPattern)
		{
		case 290:
			BMvTbl.SetPattern(293);
			BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=296, frame=0, rest=10 } ); // ƒtƒŠ[ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚P‚e–Ú—\–ñ
			break;
		case 291:
			BMvTbl.SetPattern(294);
			BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=297, frame=0, rest=10 } ); // ƒtƒŠ[ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚P‚e–Ú—\–ñ
			break;
		case 292:
			BMvTbl.SetPattern(295);
			BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=298, frame=0, rest=10 } ); // ƒtƒŠ[ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚P‚e–Ú—\–ñ
			break;
		default:
			_dem("yŒxz‚¨‚©‚µ‚¢‚Æ‚±‚ë‚©‚ç”ò‚ñ‚Å‚«‚½‚æ‚P");
			BMvCore.CallEntryBCCachePreTransfer( { pat=298, frame=0, rest=10 } ); // ƒtƒŠ[ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚P‚e–Ú—\–ñ
			BMvTbl.SetPattern(295);
			break;		
		}			
		
		//‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è‚Ìƒ{ƒCƒX•ªŠò
		if( Battle_Std.RoundisEnd() ) // Œˆ’…‚Â‚¢‚½Œã‚Íƒ{ƒCƒX‚ğ–³‚­‚·
		{
		
		}
		else
		{
			local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			switch(s.DataPattern)
			{
			case 293:
				if( Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è" }) == 0 )
				{
					Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="‚ ‚¨Œü‚¯‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è" });
				}
				break;
			case 294:
				if( Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è" }) == 0 )
				{
					Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="‚¤‚Â•š‚¹‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è" });
				}
				break;
			case 295:
				Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="d‹N‚«ã‚ª‚è" });
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_OkiagariFreeMotion" );
	}	
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetPlayerTimer( { muteki_dageX=0, muteki_nageX=0 } ); //‚±‚Ì‚Q‚Â‚Í•|‚¢‚Ì‚ÅÁ‚µ‚Ä‚¨‚­
		Battle_Std.Reversal.SetTime(); //ƒŠƒo[ƒTƒ‹ó•t{”»’èƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg¶¬
	}		
}

		}

		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); //Mv’†‚Ís“®•s”\‚É
		
		BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd();
	}
	function Finalize_Std() //

	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level=_Exist_NoKasanariHantei, mode=_ExistMode_Erase } ); //d‚È‚è”»’èÁ‹–ß‚µ

//ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh”s–k‚Å‹N‚«ã‚ª‚é
//ˆø‚«•ª‚¯‚Ì‚Í‚±‚±‚É‚±‚È‚¢‚æ
Std_MoveTable.Mv_RoundLose <-
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		_dm("ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚Ì‘±‚«‚ª‚ ‚é‚Ì‚Å‹N‚«‚é");
		if (BMvTbl.JumpFrameID( 512 ) == -1)
		{
			_dm("yŒxz”ò‚Ñæ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ID‚ª–³‚¢512");
		}
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level=_Exist_NoKasanariHantei, mode=_ExistMode_Add } ); //d‚È‚è”»’è–³‚µ
		BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); //Mv’†‚Ís“®•s”\‚É
		
		BMvTbl.SetRoundEnd(); //ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhI—¹–½—ß‚ğ“ü‚ê‚é
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //‰¹ºÄ¶ƒtƒ‰ƒO		
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus(); //ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒhƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾		
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus(); // ƒXƒe[ƒ^ƒXæ“¾
		
		local mvcnt = BMvTbl.GetLP(0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^æ“¾
		BMvTbl.AddLP(0,1); //1Fi‚ß‚é		

		//‚µ‚Î‚ç‚­‚Í‹N‚«ã‚ª‚ç‚È‚¢
		if( s.MvCount<120 && s.FrameID==512)
		{
			BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(512); //512‚ÌQ‚½ŠG‚ÅŒÅ‚Ü‚Á‚Ä‚Ä
		}

		//120FŒã‚Éƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh”s–k‰¹º‚ğÄ¶‚·‚é
		if( s.MvCount>=120 && BMvTbl.GetLP(1)==0 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLP(1,1); //Ä¶Ï‚İƒtƒ‰ƒOiÄ¶¸”s‚µ‚Ä‚àƒtƒ‰ƒO‚Í‚½‚Âj
			
			//ƒJƒƒ‰‹éŒ`ŠO‚É©•ª‚ª‚¢‚é‚©ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
			local inCamera = true; //ƒJƒƒ‰“à‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
			
			//¡‚ÌƒJƒƒ‰‹éŒ`‚ğæ“¾i‚ä‚ê‚â’–Ú‚Ì‰e‹¿‚ğó‚¯‚éƒJƒƒ‰ˆÊ’uj
			local pos = BMvEff.GetCameraRect( { flags=_GetPos_DispCamera } );

			//©•ª‚ÌÀ•W‚ğæ“¾
			local my_pos = BMvTbl.GetPosition(0);
			if( my_pos.x < pos.sx || my_pos.x > pos.ex ) inCamera = false;
			
			//Œ‹‰Ê
			if( inCamera )
			{
				_dm("›@ƒJƒƒ‰“à‚È‚Ì‚Åƒ{ƒCƒX‚ğÄ¶");
				Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh”s–k" });
			}
			else
			{
				_dm("~@ƒJƒƒ‰ŠO‚È‚Ì‚Åƒ{ƒCƒX‚ÍÄ¶‚µ‚È‚¢");
			}
		}
	}	
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		_dem("yŒxz‚Ö‚ñ‚Èƒtƒ@ƒCƒiƒ‰ƒCƒY");
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );	//”­¶‚µ‚È‚¢‚Í‚¸
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level=_Exist_NoKasanariHantei, mode=_ExistMode_Erase } ); //d‚È‚è”»’èÁ‹–ß‚µ
	}	
}

		//_dm("‹N‚«ã‚ª‚èƒtƒŠ[");

//iƒLƒƒƒ‰‚ÌŒü‚«‚ÆƒxƒNƒgƒ‹Œü‚«‚ªˆê’v‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢‚Æƒ_ƒ‚È‚à‚Ìj

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Bound_0009 <- 
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.BoundInit();
		Battle_Std.SetMuki_BoundVectorMuki();
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.BoundUpdate();
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local useval = (BMvEff.GRD_GetJudgeResult()>0)? ( (BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } )>=6)? def_GRD_BoundFU_GRD6Vorpal : def_GRD_BoundFU_Vorpal) : def_GRD_BoundFU;
		Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue({ val=useval, pressure=0, boundplus=1 });
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		//”²‚¯æ‚ª–³‚¢‚©‚àH
		//ó‘Ô‚ÌˆÚs‚¾‚¯s‚¤
		BtlMvStd.SetBoundFinalize();
		// print("\nMv_Bound Finalize()");
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		BtlMvStd.ClearBound(); // ó‘Ô‰ğœ
		//print("\nMv_Bound LastUpdate()");
	}
}

local makemv_BoundSirimoti = function( frame1=9, frame2=15, frame3=25, boundpow=100 )
{
	local retmv = {};
	retmv.Init_Std <- function() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.BoundInit(); //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»E‚â‚ç‚êó‘ÔƒZƒbƒg

		//‚â‚ç‚êŠG‚ÆƒxƒNƒgƒ‹Œü‚«‚ªâ‘Î‚É“™‚µ‚­‚È‚¢‚Æ‚¨‚©‚µ‚¢‚Ì‚Å
		Battle_Std.SetMuki_BoundVectorMuki(); //Œü‚«‚ğƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ÌŒü‚«‚É‡‚í‚¹‚é
			
		//LP0 ‚µ‚è‚à‚¿‚Ìó‘Ô@0:ƒqƒbƒg’¼Œã`@1F‚µ‚è‚à‚¿’…’n`@2F•œ‹Aƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“`@(3:I‚í‚è)
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //•Ï”‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒg
	}
	retmv.FrameUpdate_Std <- function() : (frame1, frame2, frame3, boundpow)
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		local mode = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
		local cnt = BMvTbl.GetLP(1);
		BMvTbl.AddLP(1,1); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒgƒAƒbƒv
		local useval = (BMvEff.GRD_GetJudgeResult()>0)? ( (BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } )>=6)? def_GRD_BoundFU_GRD6Vorpal : def_GRD_BoundFU_Vorpal) : def_GRD_BoundFU;
		Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue({ val=useval, pressure=0, boundplus=1 });	

		switch( mode )
		{
		case 0:
			if( s.MvCount>frame1 )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); //ó‘ÔˆÚs
				BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^ƒŠƒZƒbƒg
			}
			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				//H‚ç‚Á‚½’¼Œã

				//‚µ‚è‚à‚¿‚â‚ç‚êó‘Ô‚È‚Ì‚ğƒZƒbƒg(‰ğœ‚Í©“®)
			}
			break;
		case 1:
			if( s.MvCount>frame2 )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetLP(0,2); //ó‘ÔˆÚs
				BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^ƒŠƒZƒbƒg
			}
			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				//K‚©‚ç’…’n‚·‚é‚Æ‚±‚ë
				BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(100);
				
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=-1000*boundpow/100, y=0, addx=100*boundpow/100, addy=0, flags=_Vector_Bound } );
				
				//ƒoƒEƒ“ƒhƒGƒtƒFƒNƒgEŒø‰Ê‰¹ŒÄ‚Ñ
				// callLandEffect(); // ’…’nƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg ‚±‚±‚Í‚¤‚é‚³‚¢‚Ì‚Åg‚í‚È‚¢
				// BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat=20 } ); //»‰Œ
				// ’n–Ê’…’n‚Ì‚â‚ç‚êƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg‚ğo‚»‚¤
				BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat=51 } ); //‚µ‚¿‚à‚¿ƒ_ƒEƒ“
				BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { time=12, type=0, clear=0, } ); //—h‚ç‚µ
			}
			break;
		case 2:
			{
				// –ß‚éƒ^ƒCƒ~ƒ“ƒO‚ğƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ğŒ©‚Äs‚¤
				local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
				if( bs.isBound )
				{
					local left_frame = bs.GetCountNow(); // ‚ ‚Æ‰½ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ÅI‚í‚é‚©
					
					if( left_frame <= 9 )
					{
						//–ß‚èƒAƒjƒ‚Éi‚Ş
						BMvTbl.SetLP(0,3); //ó‘ÔˆÚs
						BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^ƒŠƒZƒbƒg
						BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(256);
						// _dpn("‚±‚±‚Å–ß‚é");
					}
				}				
			}
			if( cnt==0 )
			{
				//•œ‹A‚ÌŠGŠJnEEE‚Í•Êˆ—‚ÉˆÚ“®(ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ğŒ©‚Ä‚â‚é‚æ‚¤‚É)
				// BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(256);
				
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, y=0, addx=0, addy=0, flags=_Vector_Bound } );				
			}
			break;		
		}
	}
	retmv.Finalize_Std <- function() // 
	{
		//ó‘Ô‚ÌˆÚs‚¾‚¯s‚¤
		BtlMvStd.SetBoundFinalize(); //ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚ª•Ï‰»‚·‚é
		// print("\nMv_Bound Finalize()");
	}
	retmv.LastUpdate_Std <- function() // 
	{
		BtlMvStd.ClearBound(); // ó‘Ô‰ğœ
	}
	return retmv;
}

//‚µ‚è‚à‚¿iì‚è’¼‚µj
//ƒqƒ‹ƒ_‚ÌˆÖqˆ—‚Å‚±‚Ì”Ô†Œˆ‚ß‘Å‚¿‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚Å’ˆÓ
//HilCheck.isSirimoti()‚àˆê‚É‘Î‰‚·‚é‚±‚Æ
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Bound_0197 <- makemv_BoundSirimoti( 7, 12, 20, 100 ); // ‚µ‚è‚à‚¿ã

local makemv_BoundAirSirimoti = function( type=0 )
{
	local retmv = {};
	
	local mvparam =
	{
		xvec = -1000,
		xadd = 100,
	}
	
	switch( type )
	{
	case 0:
		break;
	case 1:
		mvparam.xvec = -3000;
		mvparam.xadd = 200;
		break;
	default:
		break;
	}
	
	retmv.Init_Std <- function() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.BoundInit(); //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»E‚â‚ç‚êó‘ÔƒZƒbƒg
		
		//‚â‚ç‚êŠG‚ÆƒxƒNƒgƒ‹Œü‚«‚ªâ‘Î‚É“™‚µ‚­‚È‚¢‚Æ‚¨‚©‚µ‚¢‚Ì‚Å
		Battle_Std.SetMuki_BoundVectorMuki(); //Œü‚«‚ğƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‚ÌŒü‚«‚É‡‚í‚¹‚é
		
		//LP0 ‚µ‚è‚à‚¿‚Ìó‘Ô@0:ƒqƒbƒg’¼Œã`@1F‚µ‚è‚à‚¿’…’n`@2F•œ‹Aƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“`@(3:I‚í‚è)
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //•Ï”‰Šú‰»
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒg
	}
	
	
	retmv.FrameUpdate_Std <- function() : (mvparam)
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		
		//ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹ƒe[ƒuƒ‹‚Ì“Á’è‚Ì‚Æ‚±‚ë‚¾‚¯‚Ìˆ—‚·‚é
		local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
		// bs.VecCount 0:‹ó’†ƒqƒbƒg`’…’n‚Ü‚Å
		// bs.VecCount 1:‚µ‚è‚à‚¿’…’n`
		if( bs.isBound && bs.VecCount==1 )
		{
			local mode = BMvTbl.GetLP(0);
			local cnt = BMvTbl.GetLP(1);
			BMvTbl.AddLP(1,1); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒgƒAƒbƒv
			local useval = (BMvEff.GRD_GetJudgeResult()>0)? ( (BMvEff.GRD_CheckStock( { val=-1 } )>=6)? def_GRD_BoundFU_GRD6Vorpal : def_GRD_BoundFU_Vorpal) : def_GRD_BoundFU;
			Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue({ val=useval, pressure=0, boundplus=1 });	

			switch( mode )
			{
			case 0:
				if( s.MvCount>10 )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); //ó‘ÔˆÚs
					BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^ƒŠƒZƒbƒg
				}
				if( cnt==0 )
				{
					//H‚ç‚Á‚Ä’…’n‚µ‚½’¼Œã
					BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(100);

					//‚µ‚è‚à‚¿‚â‚ç‚êó‘Ô‚È‚Ì‚ğƒZƒbƒg(‰ğœ‚Í©“®)
					
					//ƒoƒEƒ“ƒhƒGƒtƒFƒNƒgEŒø‰Ê‰¹ŒÄ‚Ñ
					BMvEff.CreateObject( { datatype=1, start_pat=20 } ); //»‰Œ
					BMvEff.SetCamera_Quake( { time=12, type=0, clear=0, } ); //—h‚ç‚µ
					BSound.SE_Play( { type=_SeType_Normal, num=37 } ); //_SeType_Normal ---”Ä—p _SeType_Player ---ƒLƒƒƒ‰				
					
					BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=mvparam.xvec, y=0, addx=mvparam.xadd, addy=0, flags=_Vector_Bound } );
				}
				break;
			case 1:
				if( s.MvCount>20 )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetLP(0,2); //ó‘ÔˆÚs
					BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^ƒŠƒZƒbƒg
				}
				if( cnt==0 )
				{
					//•œ‹A‚ÌŠGŠJn
					BMvTbl.JumpFrameID(256);
					
					BMvTbl.SetVector( { x=0, y=0, addx=0, addy=0, flags=_Vector_Bound } );				
				}
				break;
			}
		}		
	}
	retmv.Finalize_Std <- function() // 
	{
		//ó‘Ô‚ÌˆÚs‚¾‚¯s‚¤
		BtlMvStd.SetBoundFinalize(); //ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚ª•Ï‰»‚·‚é
		// print("\nMv_Bound Finalize()");
	}
	retmv.LastUpdate_Std <- function() // 
	{
		BtlMvStd.ClearBound(); // ó‘Ô‰ğœ
	}
	
	return retmv;
}// Šî–{“®ìƒe[ƒuƒ‹

// ƒXƒ^ƒ“Œn
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Bound_0120 <- 
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.BoundInit(); //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»E‚â‚ç‚êó‘ÔƒZƒbƒg
		
		//ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹ƒe[ƒuƒ‹‚ÌÅ‰‚¾‚¯ˆ—‚·‚é
		local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
		if( bs.isBound && bs.VecCount==0 )
		{
			local enemyChrNo = Battle_Std.GetEnemyCharaNo();
			if( enemyChrNo==Def_ChrNo_Wag ) //‘Šè
			{
				local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “G‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ‚©‚¦‚é
				if( enemy.push() )
				{
					// ‘Šè‚ª¶¬
					local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { start_pat="stun_hamon", mvname="Mv_BoundLockEff"} );
					if( eff.push() )
					{
						Battle_Std.SetPos_MarkingEnemy(); // ƒ[ƒOƒi[‚©‚çŒ©‚Ä“G‚É‚­‚Á‚Â‚¯‚é
						
						eff.pop();
					}
					
					enemy.pop();
				}
				BMvEff.SetCharaColor( { color = 0x2856A2, type = 4, intime = 4, time = 255 } );			
			}
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
		if( bs.isBound && bs.VecCount==0 )
		{
			//‚Æ‚è‚ ‚¦‚¸ˆê–‡–Ú‚ÌŠG‚ÅŒÅ’è‚·‚é
			local frame = BMvTbl.JumpFrameID( 0, _ValAdd );
			BMvTbl.Frame_Proc( frame, _ValSet  );
		
			//’èŠú“I‚É‰½‚©o‚·‚Æ‚©c
			local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			
			local enemyChrNo = Battle_Std.GetEnemyCharaNo();
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData();
			if( enemyChrNo==Def_ChrNo_Wag ) //‘Šè‚ª
			{
				//
				if( mvs.MvCount%25==0 )
				{
					if( enemy.push() )
					{
						// ‘Šè‚ª¶¬
						local eff = BMvEff.CreateObject( { start_pat="stun_hit", mvname="Mv_BoundLockEff" } );
						if( eff.push() )
						{
							Battle_Std.SetPos_MarkingEnemy(); // ƒ[ƒOƒi[‚©‚çŒ©‚Ä“G‚É‚­‚Á‚Â‚¯‚é
							
							eff.pop();
						}
						enemy.pop();
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BtlMvStd.SetBoundFinalize();
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		local enemyChrNo = Battle_Std.GetEnemyCharaNo();
		if( enemyChrNo == Def_ChrNo_Wag )
		{
			local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
			if( bs.isBound && bs.VecCount==0 )
			{
				//ƒtƒF[ƒh‚Å”²‚¯‚é
				BMvEff.SetCharaColor( { color = 0x2856A2, type = 0, intime = 10, time = 10 } );
			}
			else
			{
				//‰Šú‰»H
				BMvEff.SetCharaColor( { color = 0xFFFFFF, type = 0, intime = 0, time = 0 } );
			}
		}

		//‹¤’Ê
		BtlMvStd.ClearBound(); // ó‘Ô‰ğœ
	}
}

// ƒEƒFƒu‚â‚ç‚ê
local webeff_size = chrparam.Get( { type="ƒEƒFƒu‚â‚ç‚êƒGƒtƒFƒNƒgƒTƒCƒY", chrnum=_ChrNo } );

// ƒrƒƒƒNƒ„‚©‚ç¶¬‚³‚ê‚éƒEƒFƒuƒGƒtƒFƒNƒg
Std_MoveTable.Mv_WebLockEffect <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetPattern( "web_lock" );
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround } );
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); // I‚í‚è‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		// _dp("\n ƒEƒFƒu‚â‚ç‚êŠJn");
		
		// •ÊƒLƒƒƒ‰“¯m‚Ì‚ÍAƒŒƒCƒ„[0‚ğg‚¤
		// “¯ƒLƒƒƒ‰í‚ÌA1P‚È‚çƒŒƒCƒ„[0A2P‚È‚çƒŒƒCƒ„[1‚ğg‚¤
		local norender_layer = (1<<1); // ”ñ•\¦‚É‚·‚éƒŒƒCƒ„[
		if( Battle_Std.IsDoukyara() && BMvTbl.GetPlayerSide()==1 )
		{
			norender_layer = (1<<0); // ”ñ•\¦‚É‚·‚éƒŒƒCƒ„[
		}
		BMvTbl.SetHan6LayerMask( {  val = norender_layer } ); // ƒrƒbƒg‚ğ—§‚Ä‚½ƒŒƒCƒ„[‚ª”ñ•\¦‚É‚È‚é		
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		// ‘Šè‚É‚­‚Á‚Â‚­
		// ‘Šè‚Ìs“®‚ª•Ï‚í‚Á‚½‚çÁ‚¦‚é
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0)==0 ) // ƒƒbƒN‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ìˆ—
		{
			// _dp("\n ƒƒbƒN’†");
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “G‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ‚©‚¦‚é
			if( enemy.push() )
			{
				local pl_pos = BMvTbl.GetPosition(0);
				local pl_kasanari = BMvEff.GetHanteiRect( { check=[ _Hantei_Kasanari, 0 ] } );
				local set_posy = pl_pos.y+(-150<<7); // Šî–{’l
				if( pl_kasanari.sx != _Hantei_Error )
				{
					set_posy = pl_kasanari.ey + ( (pl_kasanari.sy - pl_kasanari.ey) * 6 / 10 );
				}
				local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
				enemy.pop();

				BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=pl_pos.x, y=set_posy } ); // ‚Æ‚è‚ ‚¦‚¸À•W‚ğƒZƒbƒg
				
				if( bs.isBound && ( bs.Num == 235 || bs.Num == 236 ) && bs.VecCount == 0 ) // ƒAƒjƒ’â~‚·‚é‚Ì‚Í‚±‚±‚¾‚¯
				{
				}
				else
				{
					// _dp("\n ƒEƒFƒu‚â‚ç‚ê‚¨‚í‚è:"+bs.Num );
					BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); // ƒƒbƒNI‚í‚è
					BMvTbl.JumpFrameID( 900 ); // Á–ÅƒAƒjƒ‚Öi‚Ş
				}
			}
			else
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize( 0 );
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

{
	function Init_Std() : (webeff_size)
	{
		Battle_Std.BoundInit(); //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»E‚â‚ç‚êó‘ÔƒZƒbƒg
		
		//ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹ƒe[ƒuƒ‹‚ÌÅ‰‚¾‚¯ˆ—‚·‚é
		local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();

		{
			local enemyChrNo = Battle_Std.GetEnemyCharaNo();
			if( enemyChrNo==Def_ChrNo_Bya )
			{

				if( enemy.push() )
				{
					local eff =BMvEff.CreateObject( { mvname="Mv_WebLockEffect" });
					if( eff.push() )
					{
						// _dp("\n ƒTƒCƒY•ÏX:"+webeff_size[0]+", "+webeff_size[1] );
						BMvTbl.SetScale( { x=webeff_size[0]*100, y=webeff_size[1]*100 } );
						eff.pop();
					}
					
					enemy.pop();
				}
			}
			BMvEff.SetCharaColor( { color = 0x808080, type = 4, intime = 4, time = 254 } );			
		}
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‰Šú‰»
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		local mvcnt = BMvTbl.GetLP(0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^æ“¾
		BMvTbl.AddLP(0,1); //1Fi‚ß‚é

		local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
		if( bs.isBound && bs.VecCount==0 )
		{
			//‚Æ‚è‚ ‚¦‚¸ˆê–‡–Ú‚ÌŠG‚ÅŒÅ’è‚·‚é
			local frame = BMvTbl.JumpFrameID( 0, _ValAdd );
			BMvTbl.Frame_Proc( frame, _ValSet  );
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BtlMvStd.SetBoundFinalize();
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
		if( bs.isBound && bs.VecCount==0 )
		{
			//ƒtƒF[ƒh‚Å”²‚¯‚é
			BMvEff.SetCharaColor( { color = 0x808080, type = 0, intime = 10, time = 10 } );
		}
		else
		{
			//‰Šú‰»H
			BMvEff.SetCharaColor( { color = 0xFFFFFF, type = 0, intime = 0, time = 0 } );
		}
		BtlMvStd.ClearBound(); // ó‘Ô‰ğœ
	}
}

// ¦ª‚ÌƒxƒNƒgƒ‹”Ô†ƒLƒ‚¤‚¿‚ÅƒGƒtƒFƒNƒgˆ—‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚Å’ˆÓ

//ô”›ŒÅ’è•‚‚«
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Bound_0247 <- 
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.BoundInit(); //ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹‰Šú‰»E‚â‚ç‚êó‘ÔƒZƒbƒg
		
		//ƒxƒNƒgƒ‹ƒe[ƒuƒ‹‚ÌÅ‰‚¾‚¯ˆ—‚·‚é
		local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
		if( bs.isBound && bs.VecCount==0 )
		{
			local enemyChrNo = Battle_Std.GetEnemyCharaNo();
			if( enemyChrNo==Def_ChrNo_Set ) //‘Šè‚ªƒZƒg‚È‚çH
			{
				local e = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “G‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ‚©‚¦‚é
				if( e.IsDone )
				{
					BMvCore.PushCharaData( e ); //
						local pos = Battle_Std.GetNearEnemyToolShiftPosition();
						Battle_Std.CreateObjectEX({ x=pos.x, y=pos.y-200, flags=_Position_ToolShift,
						pat="Spell_Hit", FrameID=[1,2], angle="rand" });
						
						Battle_Std.CreateObjectEX({ x=pos.x, y=pos.y-200, flags=_Position_ToolShift,
						pat="Spell_Biribiri", FrameID=[1,2,3], angle="rand" });
					BMvCore.PopCharaData(); //						
				}
			}
			BMvEff.SetCharaColor( { color = 0x2856A2, type = 4, intime = 4, time = 254 } );			
		}
		
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^‰Šú‰»
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		local mvcnt = BMvTbl.GetLP(0); //ƒJƒEƒ“ƒ^æ“¾
		BMvTbl.AddLP(0,1); //1Fi‚ß‚é

		local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
		if( bs.isBound && bs.VecCount==0 )
		{
			//‚Æ‚è‚ ‚¦‚¸ˆê–‡–Ú‚ÌŠG‚ÅŒÅ’è‚·‚é
			local frame = BMvTbl.JumpFrameID( 0, _ValAdd );
			BMvTbl.Frame_Proc( frame, _ValSet  );
		
			//’èŠú“I‚É‰½‚©o‚·‚Æ‚©c
			local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			//print("\nƒtƒŒ[ƒ€:"+s.MvCount);
			
			local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “G‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ‚©‚¦‚é
			if( enemy.IsDone )
			{
				local enemyChrNo = Battle_Std.GetEnemyCharaNo();
				//print("\n”Ô†F"+enemyChrNo);
				if( enemyChrNo==Def_ChrNo_Set ) //‘Šè‚ªƒZƒg‚È‚çH
				{
					if (mvcnt%2==0 && mvcnt<=4 ) //”ƒtƒŒ‚É‚P‰ñ
					{
						BMvCore.PushCharaData( enemy ); //
							local pos = Battle_Std.GetNearEnemyToolShiftPosition();
							Battle_Std.CreateObjectEX({ x=pos.x, y=pos.y-200, flags=_Position_ToolShift,
							pat="Spell_Hit", FrameID=[1,2], angle="rand" });
						BMvCore.PopCharaData(); //
					}
					if( mvcnt%10==0 )
					{
						BMvCore.PushCharaData( enemy ); //
							local pos = Battle_Std.GetNearEnemyToolShiftPosition();
							Battle_Std.CreateObjectEX({ x=pos.x, y=pos.y-200, flags=_Position_ToolShift,
							pat="Spell_Biribiri", FrameID=[1,2,3], angle="rand" });
						BMvCore.PopCharaData(); //
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std() // 
	{
		BtlMvStd.SetBoundFinalize();
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std() // 
	{
		//BMvEff.SetCharaColor( { color = 0x2856A2, type = 4, intime = 1, time = 1 } );
		local bs = BtlMvStd.GetBoundStatus();
		if( bs.isBound && bs.VecCount==0 )
		{
			//ƒtƒF[ƒh‚Å”²‚¯‚é
			BMvEff.SetCharaColor( { color = 0x2856A2, type = 0, intime = 10, time = 10 } );
		}
		else
		{
			//‰Šú‰»H
			BMvEff.SetCharaColor( { color = 0xFFFFFF, type = 0, intime = 0, time = 0 } );
		}
		BtlMvStd.ClearBound(); // ó‘Ô‰ğœ
	}
}

//‚j‚n‚³‚ê‚½uŠÔŒÄ‚Î‚ê‚Ä­‚µ‚·‚é‚ÆÁ–Å‚·‚é‚l‚–
//‚j‚nƒ{ƒCƒX‚ÌÄ¶‚Ég‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é
//ƒLƒƒƒ‰•Ê‚É‘‚«Š·‚¦‚½‚¯‚ê‚Î‚±‚ê‚ğ‚¢‚¶‚ë‚¤
Std_MoveTable.Mv_KOImpact_Object <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoRender|_ObjFlags_NoGround } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		//LP0 : ‚j‚n‰¹º‚ğÄ¶‚µ‚½‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); // ‰Šú‰»
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		//1FŒã‚É‘€ìe‚Ìƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚ğŒ©‚Ä•ªŠò
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0)==0 && s.MvCount>2  )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1);

			local p = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ‘€ìe‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
			if( p.IsDone )
			{
				if( BMvCore.PushCharaData( p ) )
				{
					local rs = BMvTbl.GetMvRoundStatus();
					// _dp("\n ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh:"+rs.Round );
					if( rs.Round != -2 ) // ƒ`ƒ…[ƒgƒŠƒAƒ‹ˆÈŠO
					{
						local oya_s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
						//_dm("s:"+oya_s.DataPattern);
						//‚Ğ‚´‚­‚¸‚ê‚Æ‚ÍŒ¾‚¤‚ªƒŠƒ“ƒl‚Ì“Š‚°‚Å‚à“¯—l‚É‚â‚ç‚È‚¢‚Æ‚Ü‚¸‚»‚¤‚È‚Ì‚Å290‚àŠÜ‚ß‚é
						local isHizakuzure = ( oya_s.DataPattern==327 || oya_s.DataPattern==290 ); //ƒpƒ^[ƒ“‚Å•G•ö‚ê‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚ğƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚µ‚Ä‚é‚—
						if( rs.isLoseRound )
						{
							local seplay = 0; // ’è‹`‚µ‚Ä‚ ‚Á‚ÄÄ¶‚³‚ê‚½‚ç1
							if( isHizakuzure )
							{
								seplay = Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Œˆ’…‚j‚n_•G•ö‚ê" });
							}
							else
							{
								seplay = Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Œˆ’…‚j‚n_’Êí" });
							}
							if( seplay==0 ) Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Œˆ’…‚j‚n" });
						}
						else
						{
							local seplay = 0; // ’è‹`‚µ‚Ä‚ ‚Á‚ÄÄ¶‚³‚ê‚½‚ç1
							if( isHizakuzure )
							{
								seplay = Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚j‚n_•G•ö‚ê" });
							}
							else
							{
								seplay = Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚j‚n_’Êí" });			
							}
							if( seplay==0 ) Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="ƒ‰ƒEƒ“ƒh‚j‚n" });
						}
					}
				}
				BMvCore.PopCharaData();
			}
		}
		if( s.MvCount>60 ) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}







//skill


Std_MoveTable.Mv_DebugPause <- 
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		switch( Def_Dbg_DebugButtonMode )
		{
			case 1: //ƒQ[ƒW‘‰Á
			case 3: //ƒgƒŒƒ‚Œü‚¯
			{
				btl_debPrint2("yƒfƒoƒbƒOzƒQ[ƒW‘‰Á");
				local exs = BCMDTbl.GetComboGauge();
				BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { value=5000, correct=0 } );
				BMvEff.Liberate_End(); //ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒtó‘Ô‚¾‚Á‚½‚çI—¹‚·‚é
				
				//–ƒ^ƒ“‚Á‚Û‚©‚Á‚½‚ç‹ó‚É‚·‚é
				if( exs==20000 || exs==40000 )
				{
					BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { value=-40000, correct=0 } );
				}
				
				BMvTbl.SetPattern( 0 ); //—§‚¿ƒ|[ƒY‚É–ß‚é
				Battle_Std.InitVector();
				BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
				break;
			}
			case 2: //‘Šè‚Ì‚İŠÔ’â~
			{
				btl_debPrint2("yƒfƒoƒbƒOz‘Šè‚¾‚¯ŠÔ’â~");
				BMvEff.SetStopTime( { time=999, stopme=0 } );			
				BMvTbl.SetPattern( 0 ); //—§‚¿ƒ|[ƒY‚É–ß‚é
				Battle_Std.InitVector();
				BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=0, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
				//BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
				break;
			}
			case 7: // ƒXƒe[ƒWŠm”F
			{
				Battle_Std.InitVector();
				BMvTbl.SetMoveableFlag( { move=1, time=254, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } );
				local pos = BMvTbl.GetPosition();
				local enemy = BMvCore.GetEnemyCharaData();
				if( enemy.push() )
				{
					BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=pos.x, y=pos.y } );
					
					enemy.pop();
				}
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			}
			case 8:
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100); // “oê
				break;
			}
			case 10:
			{
				//IWE‚ğŠm”F‚·‚é
				BMvTbl.SetPattern(1);
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(10000); // öİ
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		// E‚ğ‰Ÿ‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚éŠÔŒp‘±
		if( BMvTbl.CheckButtonHold( (1<<4) ) == 0 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			return;
		}
		// ‰Ÿ‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚éŠÔ‚Ìˆ—
		if( BMvTbl.CheckStickHold( (1<<2) ) )
		{
			// EXS‚ğ‹ó‚É‚·‚é
			BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { value=-40000, correct=0 } );
		}
	}
	function Finalize()
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral", [100,"Mv_Standby"], [10000,"Mv_Skill_IWEXIST"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}	
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_DebugButton_4 <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		switch( Def_Dbg_DebugButtonMode )
		{
			case 1: //
			{
			
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
				break;
			}
			case 2: // 
			{
				local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “G‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
				if( enemy.push() )
				{
					BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { value=2500, correct=0 } );

					enemy.pop();
				}			
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
				break;
			}
			case 3: //
			{
				local muki = BMvTbl.GetMuki();
				BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=-def_POS_GamenHajiX*muki, y=0 } );
				local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “G‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
				if( enemy.IsDone )
				{
					if( BMvCore.PushCharaData( enemy ) )
					{
						BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=-def_POS_GamenHajiX*muki, y=0 } );
						BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { value=-50000, correct=0 } );
					}
					BMvCore.PopCharaData(); //
				}
				BMvTbl.SetPattern("DUkemi_0"); //‚±‚ê‚ÅˆÀS
				Battle_Std.InitVector();
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { y=-1000, addy=190, flags=_Vector_Normal } );
				BMvTbl.SetFinalizeCode(0);
				break;
			}
			case 6:
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
				break;			
			}
			case 7:
			{
				BMvTbl.SetPosition( { x=-500, flags=_Position_ChangeMuki|_Position_Add|_Position_CaptureShift } );
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
				break;			
			}
			case 8:
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(100); // Ÿ—˜
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize()
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral", [100,"Mv_RoundWinPattern"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}

			case 3: //
			{
				btl_debPrint2("yƒfƒoƒbƒOz‘Šè‚ÌƒQ[ƒW‘‰Á");
				local enemy = BMvCore.GetNearEnemyCharaData(); // “G‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
				if( enemy.IsDone )
				{
					if( BMvCore.PushCharaData( enemy ) )
					{
						local exs = BCMDTbl.GetComboGauge();
						BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { value=5000, correct=0 } );
						BMvEff.Liberate_End(); //ƒ”ƒF[ƒ‹ƒIƒtó‘Ô‚¾‚Á‚½‚çI—¹‚·‚é
						
						//–ƒ^ƒ“‚Á‚Û‚©‚Á‚½‚ç‹ó‚É‚·‚é
						if( exs==20000 || exs==40000 )
						{
							BMvEff.SetSpGauge( { value=-50000, correct=0 } );
						}
					}
					BMvCore.PopCharaData(); //
				}
				BMvTbl.SetPattern("DUkemi_0"); //‚±‚ê‚ÅˆÀS
				Battle_Std.InitVector();
				BMvTbl.SetVector( { y=-1000, addy=190, flags=_Vector_Normal } );				
				BMvTbl.SetFinalizeCode(0);
				break;
			}
			case 6:
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
				break;			
			}
			case 8:
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(512); // Ÿ—˜
				break;
			}				
			case 9:
			{
				break;
			}				
		}
	}
	function Finalize()
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral", [512,"Mv_TimeupLose"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}

	
//“Š‚°”²‚¯—P—\•“Š‚°”²‚¯¸”sƒtƒŒ[ƒ€ŠÇ—
//def_PP_TechHit_FrameManager‚É’l‚ª“ü‚é‚Æ‚«‚Í•K‚¸‚±‚ê‚ÆƒZƒbƒg‚É‚È‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é
//’l‚ª“ü‚éƒJƒEƒ“ƒgƒ_ƒEƒ“‚µ‚Ä‚¢‚­
//‹­ˆø‚É“Š‚°”²‚¯•s”\ó‘Ô‚É‚·‚é‚Æ‚«‚Ídef_FL_Tech+1‚Ìó‘Ô‚É‚È‚é‚Ì‚ÅA‹­§ƒXƒƒEƒŠƒWƒFƒNƒgƒ~ƒXó‘Ô‚Æ‚È‚é
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Obj_TechHit_FrameManager <- 
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		//_dm("FM:“Š‚°”²‚¯ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN‚«‚½‚æ");
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender | _ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );		
		//BMvTbl.SetPP( def_PP_TechHit_FrameManager, 0 ); //“Š‚°”²‚¯ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( {y=1024, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } );
	}
	function Update()
	{
		local end_manage = 0;//ŠÇ—I—¹‚·‚éH
		local techman_frame = BMvTbl.GetPP( def_PP_TechHit_FrameManager );
		// 0 : “Š‚°or“Š‚°”²‚¯‚ğ‚»‚à‚»‚à“ü‚ê‚Ä‚¢‚È‚¢‚©A“ü—Í‚µ‚Ä‚©‚ç‚©‚È‚èŒo‰ß‚µ‚½
		// 1`40 : “Š‚°”²‚¯‚ğ“ü—Í‚µ‚Ä‰½ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€Œo‰ß‚µ‚½‚©
		//def_FL_Tech					= 11;	//‚Â‚©‚Ü‚ê‚Ä‚©‚ç‚Ì“Š‚°”²‚¯—P—\ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€
		//def_FL_TechSenkou			= 5;		//“Š‚°ƒRƒ}ƒ“ƒh“ü—Í‚µ‚Ä‚©‚ç‚Ì“Š‚°”²‚¯—P—\ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€@‚±‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Ü‚Å‚Í“Š‚°”²‚¯		
		//def_FL_TechMiss				= 30;	//“Š‚°‚ğ“ü‚ê‚Ä‚©‚ç‚Ì“Š‚°”²‚¯¸”sƒtƒŒ[ƒ€@‚±‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Ü‚Å‚Í“Š‚°”²‚¯ƒ~ƒX		
		if (techman_frame<def_FL_TechMiss)
		{
			techman_frame++;
			BMvTbl.SetPP( def_PP_TechHit_FrameManager, techman_frame ); //¸”sƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚É‚È‚é‚Ü‚Å‚ÍŠÔ‚ğ‚µ‚Ü‚Á‚Ä‚¨‚±‚¤
		}
		else
		{
			end_manage = 1; // I—¹
		}
		
		/*
		//ƒK[ƒh’†‚É“Š‚°”²‚¯‚ğ“ü‚ê‚ÄAd’¼Œã‚Ìƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…“Š‚°‚Æ‚©‚Å‚à‰ğœ‚³‚ê‚Ä‚µ‚Ü‚¤‚Ì‚Í‚¨‚©‚µ‚¢c‚Ì‚Åˆê’U–ß‚·
		//“Š‚°‚Â‚©‚İ‚Ìˆ—‚ªs“®‰Â”\‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©‚ğŒ©‚ÄÔ“Š‚°”»’f‚µ‚Ä‚é‚Ì‚ÅA‚±‚±‚à“¯‚¶ğŒ‚¶‚á‚È‚¢‚Æ‚¨‚©‚µ‚¢
		if( Battle_Std.CheckPlayerisMovable() )
		{
			end_manage = 1; // I—¹
		}
		*/
		
		if( end_manage )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetPP( def_PP_TechHit_FrameManager, 0 ); //I—¹
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
		}
		//_dp(format("\n“Š‚°”²‚¯F%d",BMvTbl.GetPP( def_PP_TechHit_FrameManager ) ))
	}
	function Finalize()
	{
		//_dm("FM:‚Î‚¢‚Î‚¢");
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

//e‚ÌMv‚ª•Ï‰»or“Á’èƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Å“Š‚°‘®«‚ğÁ‹‚·‚é
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Obj_SetAutoThrowMv <-
{
	function Init()
	{
		//_dm("Ÿ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Éƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğÁ‹");
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender } );//‚±‚ê‚ÍŠÔ’â~‚Ì‰e‹¿‚ğó‚¯‚Ü‚·
		BMvEff.ObjProcFlags_Set( { flags=_ObjProcFlags_EraseChangeParentMv } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );		
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( {y=1024, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } );
		//LP0 ::w’èƒtƒŒ[ƒ€
	}
	function Update()
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();	
		local frame = BMvTbl.GetLP(0); //‰½ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€Œã‚ÉÁ‚·‚Ì‚©
		local end_flag = ( s.MvCount>frame);
		if( end_flag) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
	}
	function Finalize()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
	function LastUpdate()
	{
		//print("\nƒtƒ‰ƒO‚¯‚·‚¨");
		BMvEff.CreateObject( {  mvname="Mv_Obj_DelThrowMv" } ); //1FŒã‚Éƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğÁ‚·Mv‚ğì¬
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Obj_DelThrowMv <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		//_dm("Ÿ‚ÌƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚Éƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğÁ‹");
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoGround | _ObjFlags_NoRender | _ObjFlags_MoveTimeStopAll } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );		
//		BMvTbl.SetPP( def_PP_TechHit_FrameManager, 0 ); //“Š‚°”²‚¯ƒ`ƒFƒbƒN
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( {y=1024, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } );
	}
	function Update()
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();	
		if( s.MvCount>2) BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0)
	}
	function Finalize()
	{
		Battle_Std.DelPSFlag( def_PP_PS_isThrowMv ); //“Š‚°‘®«ƒ‚[ƒVƒ‡ƒ“‚ğíœ
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
}

// Std_MoveTable‚É“Áê‹ZŠÖŒW‚ğƒK[ƒb‚Æ’Ç‰Á‚·‚éŠÖ”
local Add_EXAtks = function( stdmv ) : (set_AirAtkLandStatus, set_AirAtk_LandMoveableFrame)
{
	//ŠÖ”“àŠÖ”
	local makemv_GroundEXAtk = function( param={} )
	{
		local retmv = {};
		
		local limit = param.ComboLimit;
		local pat = param.Pattern;
		local nextmv = param.NextMv;
		local movecode = param.MoveCode;
		local ground_atk_flags = ( "AtkFlags" in param )? param.AtkFlags : 0;
		local atkLv = param.AtkLv;
		
		local atkTable = ( "atkTable" in param )? param.atkTable : {};
		local isAdd = ( "isAdd" in atkTable && atkTable.isAdd );
		
		retmv.Init_Std <- function() : ( limit, pat, movecode, ground_atk_flags, atkLv, isAdd )
		{
			Battle_Std.InitGroundAtk( { type=atkLv, flags=ground_atk_flags } ); //’ÊíUŒ‚‹¤’ÊInit
			
			if( movecode )
			{
				// s“®ƒR[ƒh’Ç‰Á
				// _dp("\n ’Ç‰Á:"+movecode);
				Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( movecode );
			}
			Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 3, def_MC3_UniqAttack ); // “Áê‹Z
			
			if( isAdd )
			{
				Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_AddMv );
				Battle_Std.PassAddSkillMoveCodes();
			}
		
			BMvTbl.SetComboLimit( limit );
			BMvTbl.SetPattern( pat );
		}

		retmv.Start_Std <- function()
		{
			Battle_Std.StartGroundAtk();
		}

		local fu_voice = param.FUVoice;

		retmv.FrameUpdate_Std <- function() : (fu_voice)
		{
			Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type=fu_voice });
		}
		
		retmv.HitInterrupt_Std <- function()
		{
			Battle_Std.MvRule_Atk_HitInterrupt();
		}	

		retmv.Finalize_Std <- function() : (nextmv)
		{
			BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( nextmv );
		}
		return retmv;
	}


	local makemv_AirEXAtk = function( param={} ) : (set_AirAtkLandStatus)
	{
		local retmv = {};
		
		local limit = param.ComboLimit;
		local pat = param.Pattern;
		local movecode = param.MoveCode;
		local atkLv = param.AtkLv;
		
		local atkTable = ( "atkTable" in param )? param.atkTable : {};
		local isAdd = ( "isAdd" in atkTable && atkTable.isAdd );
		
		retmv.Init_Std <- function() : ( limit, pat, movecode, atkLv, isAdd )
		{
			Battle_Std.InitAirAtk( { type=atkLv } ); //’ÊíUŒ‚‹¤’ÊInit
			
			if( movecode )
			{
				// s“®ƒR[ƒh’Ç‰Á
				// _dp("\n ’Ç‰Á:"+movecode);
				Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( movecode );
			}			

			Battle_Std.SetStatus_AirAtkStatus(); //ƒWƒƒƒ“ƒvUŒ‚‚ÌŠeíƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğƒŠƒZƒbƒg{—§‚Ä‚é
			
			if( isAdd )
			{
				Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_AddMv );
				Battle_Std.PassAddSkillMoveCodes();
			}
		
			BMvTbl.SetComboLimit( limit );
			BMvTbl.SetPattern( pat );
		}
		
		retmv.Start_Std <- function()
		{
			Battle_Std.StartAirAtk();
		}		

		local fu_voice = param.FUVoice;

		retmv.FrameUpdate_Std <- function() : (set_AirAtkLandStatus, fu_voice)
		{
			Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type=fu_voice });
			local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
			if( s.isLanding )
			{
				set_AirAtkLandStatus(); // def_PP_AirAtkStatus ‚É’…’nî•ñ‚ğ“ü‚ê‚é
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(9999); //’…’n‚µ‚½‚çLand‚Ö
			}
			Battle_Std.CheckAirAtkFsiki(); // Ø‚è‘Ö‚¦’†’i‚Íã’i‚É•ÏX
		}
		
		retmv.HitInterrupt_Std <- function() : (atkLv)
		{
			Battle_Std.MvRule_Atk_HitInterrupt();
		}	

		local landmv = param.LandMv;
		retmv.Finalize_Std <- function() : ( landmv )
		{
			Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral", [9999,landmv] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
		}
		
		retmv.GetFinalizeCode_Std <- function() : (landmv)
		{
			return [ [0,"Mv_Neutral"], [9999,landmv] ];//XXX:9999‚Í‚â‚ß‚é
		}

		return retmv;
	}

	local makemv_AirEXAtkLand = function( param={} ) : (set_AirAtk_LandMoveableFrame)
	{
		local retmv = {};
		
		switch( param.AtkLv )
		{
		case "A":
			retmv.Init_Std <- function() : (set_AirAtk_LandMoveableFrame)
			{
				Battle_Std.InitAitAtkLand();
				
				set_AirAtk_LandMoveableFrame(			//’…’nd’¼‚ğİ’è
				def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery,			//‹óU‚è‚µ‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery_Shielded,	//ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery_Damage,		//ƒ_ƒ[ƒW‚ğ—^‚¦‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery_Guard,		//ƒK[ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery_Assault		//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚Ä‹óU‚è‚µ‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery_AssaultShielded,	//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚ÄƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery_AssaultDamage,		//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚Äƒ_ƒ[ƒW‚ğ—^‚¦‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirA_LandRecovery_AssaultGuard		//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚ÄƒK[ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				);
			}
			break;
		case "B":
			retmv.Init_Std <- function() : (set_AirAtk_LandMoveableFrame)
			{
				Battle_Std.InitAitAtkLand();
				
				set_AirAtk_LandMoveableFrame(			//’…’nd’¼‚ğİ’è
				def_FL_AtkAirB_LandRecovery,			//‹óU‚è‚µ‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirB_LandRecovery_Shielded,	//ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirB_LandRecovery_Damage,		//ƒ_ƒ[ƒW‚ğ—^‚¦‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirB_LandRecovery_Guard,		//ƒK[ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirB_LandRecovery_Assault		//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚Ä‹óU‚è‚µ‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirB_LandRecovery_AssaultShielded,	//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚ÄƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirB_LandRecovery_AssaultDamage,		//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚Äƒ_ƒ[ƒW‚ğ—^‚¦‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirB_LandRecovery_AssaultGuard		//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚ÄƒK[ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				);
			}
			break;
		case "C":
			retmv.Init_Std <- function() : (set_AirAtk_LandMoveableFrame)
			{
				Battle_Std.InitAitAtkLand();
				
				set_AirAtk_LandMoveableFrame(			//’…’nd’¼‚ğİ’è
				def_FL_AtkAirC_LandRecovery,			//‹óU‚è‚µ‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirC_LandRecovery_Shielded,	//ƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirC_LandRecovery_Damage,		//ƒ_ƒ[ƒW‚ğ—^‚¦‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirC_LandRecovery_Guard,		//ƒK[ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirC_LandRecovery_Assault		//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚Ä‹óU‚è‚µ‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirC_LandRecovery_AssaultShielded,	//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚ÄƒV[ƒ‹ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirC_LandRecovery_AssaultDamage,		//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚Äƒ_ƒ[ƒW‚ğ—^‚¦‚½
				def_FL_AtkAirC_LandRecovery_AssaultGuard		//ƒAƒTƒ‹ƒg‚©‚ço‚µ‚ÄƒK[ƒh‚³‚ê‚½
				);
			}
			break;
		}
		
		retmv.Finalize_Std <- function()
		{
			BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
		}
		
		return retmv;
	}




	local std_atk_list = [

		["Mv_Atk_Cro3B", "3B", DEF_BS_CL_ATK3B, "Mv_Atk_Cro3B_FU", "Mv_Crouch_Wait", 0, 0, "B", {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Cro3C", "3C", DEF_BS_CL_ATK3C, "Mv_Atk_Cro3C_FU", "Mv_Crouch_Wait", 0, 0, "C", {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Cro1A", "1A", DEF_BS_CL_ATK1A, "Mv_Atk_Cro1A_FU", "Mv_Crouch_Wait", def_MC_GRDLowBreak, 0, "A", {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Cro1B", "1B", DEF_BS_CL_ATK1B, "Mv_Atk_Cro1B_FU", "Mv_Crouch_Wait", 0, 0, "B", {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Cro1C", "1C", DEF_BS_CL_ATK1C, "Mv_Atk_Cro1C_FU", "Mv_Crouch_Wait", 0, 0, "C", {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_2A_2A", "2A_2A", DEF_BS_CL_ATK2A_2A, "Mv_Atk_2A_2A_FU", "Mv_Crouch_Wait", def_MC_GRDLowBreak, (1<<0), "A", { isAdd=1 } ],
		["Mv_Atk_2B_2B", "2B_2B", DEF_BS_CL_ATK2B_2B, "Mv_Atk_2B_2B_FU", "Mv_Crouch_Wait", 0, (1<<0), "B", { isAdd=1 } ],
		["Mv_Atk_2B_2B_2B", "2B_2B_2B", DEF_BS_CL_ATK2B_2B_2B, "Mv_Atk_2B_2B_2B_FU", "Mv_Crouch_Wait", 0, (1<<0), "B", { isAdd=1 } ],
		["Mv_Atk_2C_2C", "2C_2C", DEF_BS_CL_ATK2C_2C, "Mv_Atk_2C_2C_FU", "Mv_Crouch_Wait", 0, (1<<0), "C", { isAdd=1 } ],
	];
	
	for( local i=0; i<std_atk_list.len(); i++ )
	{
		local param = std_atk_list[i];
		local mv = param[0];
		local pat = param[1];
		local limit = param[2];
		local fu_se = param[3];
		local nextmv = param[4];
		local movecode = param[5];
		local atkflags = param[6];
		local atklv = param[7];
		local atkTable = param[8];
		
		stdmv[mv] <- makemv_GroundEXAtk( { ComboLimit=limit, Pattern=pat, FUVoice=fu_se, NextMv=nextmv, MoveCode=movecode, AtkFlags=atkflags, AtkLv=atklv, atkTable=atkTable } );
	}
	
	/*
	local cro_atk_list = [
		["Mv_Atk_Cro6A", "6A", DEF_BS_CL_ATK6A, "Mv_Atk_Std6A_FU" ],
		
	];

	for( local i=0; i<cro_atk_list.len(); i++ )
	{
		local param = cro_atk_list[i];
		local mv = param[0];
		local pat = param[1];
		local limit = param[2];
		local fu_se = param[3];
		
		stdmv[mv] <- makemv_CroEXAtk( { ComboLimit=limit, Pattern=pat, FUVoice=fu_se } );
	}
	*/

	local air_atk_list = [
		["Mv_Atk_Air2A", "J2A", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJ2A, "Mv_Atk_Air2A_FU", "A", "Mv_Atk_Air2A_Land", def_MC_GRDLowBreak, {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Air2B", "J2B", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJ2B, "Mv_Atk_Air2B_FU", "B", "Mv_Atk_Air2B_Land", 0, {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Air2C", "J2C", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJ2C, "Mv_Atk_Air2C_FU", "C", "Mv_Atk_Air2C_Land", 0, {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Air4A", "J4A", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJ4A, "Mv_Atk_Air4A_FU", "A", "Mv_Atk_Air4A_Land", def_MC_GRDLowBreak, {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Air4B", "J4B", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJ4B, "Mv_Atk_Air4B_FU", "B", "Mv_Atk_Air4B_Land", 0, {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Air4C", "J4C", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJ4C, "Mv_Atk_Air4C_FU", "C", "Mv_Atk_Air4C_Land", 0, {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Air6A", "J6A", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJ6A, "Mv_Atk_Air6A_FU", "A", "Mv_Atk_Air6A_Land", def_MC_GRDLowBreak, {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Air6B", "J6B", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJ6B, "Mv_Atk_Air6B_FU", "B", "Mv_Atk_Air6B_Land", 0, {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_Air6C", "J6C", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJ6C, "Mv_Atk_Air6C_FU", "C", "Mv_Atk_Air6C_Land", 0, {} ],
		["Mv_Atk_JA_JA", "JA_JA", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJA_JA, "Mv_Atk_JA_JA_FU", "A", "Mv_Atk_JA_JA_Land", def_MC_GRDLowBreak, { isAdd=1 } ],
		["Mv_Atk_JB_JB", "JB_JB", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJB_JB, "Mv_Atk_JB_JB_FU", "B", "Mv_Atk_JB_JB_Land", 0, { isAdd=1 } ],
		["Mv_Atk_JC_JC", "JC_JC", DEF_BS_CL_ATKJC_JC, "Mv_Atk_JC_JC_FU", "C", "Mv_Atk_JC_JC_Land", 0, { isAdd=1 } ],
	];

	
	for( local i=0; i<air_atk_list.len(); i++ )
	{
		local param = air_atk_list[i];
		local mv = param[0];
		local pat = param[1];
		local limit = param[2];
		local fu_se = param[3];
		local land_lv = param[4];
		local land_mv = param[5];
		local movecode = param[6];
		local atkTable = param[7];
		
		stdmv[mv] <- makemv_AirEXAtk( { ComboLimit=limit, Pattern=pat, FUVoice=fu_se, LandMv=land_mv, MoveCode=movecode, AtkLv=land_lv, atkTable=atkTable } );
		if( land_mv in stdmv )
		{
			// Šù‚É‚ ‚é‚Ì‚ÅƒXƒLƒbƒv
		}
		else
		{
			stdmv[land_mv] <- makemv_AirEXAtkLand( { AtkLv=land_lv } );
		}
	}
}

		BMvTbl.SetComboLimit(DEF_BS_CL_ATKJAANDB);
		BMvTbl.SetPattern("JA+B");

			Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.DelFlag( 7, def_MC7_LightLegsMuteki );//¬‘«‘®«‚Ö‹óU‚è—\’è‚ÌMV
			BMvTbl.SetHitCheckFlag( { type=0, val=Def_HitCheckFlag_LightLegs, time=0, flag=_ClearFlag_ChangeMv } ); // ¬‘«–³“G‚ÉƒXƒJ‚é‚ğíœ
		}
	}	
	function Finalize() // 
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}	
}


local grd_action_init = function()
{
	// Á”ï—\–ñ‚ğs‚¤
	local useval = ( BMvEff.GRD_GetJudgeResult() > 0 )? def_PP_GAU_VORPALUse : def_PP_GAU_NormalUse;
	BMvTbl.SetPP( def_PP_GRDAction_UseGRD, useval ); // ‰Šú‰»iƒtƒ‰ƒOˆ—‚¾‚¯‚ÇA‰Šú‰»{İ’è‚È‚Ì‚Åã‘‚«‚ÅOKj
	_dp("\n GRDÁ”ï—\–ñ:"+useval );
	
	// ƒAƒiƒEƒ“ƒX•\¦
	// BMvEff.AttackInfoString_Set({ word=def_AISW_GRDAction,} );	
	
	BMvTbl.SetMvAction(def_MVA_EXAtk);
	Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_BCAction ); // BC‹Z
}

local grd_action_useGRD = function()
{
	local grd_st = BMvTbl.GetPP( def_PP_GRDAction_UseGRD );
	// Á”ïÏ‚İorÁ”ï‚µ‚È‚¢ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½‚ç‚â‚ç‚È‚¢
	if( !(grd_st&def_PP_GAU_Used) && !(grd_st&def_PP_GAU_NoUse) )
	{
		if( grd_st&def_PP_GAU_VORPALUse )
		{
			// ‚P‚Â–³‚­‚Ä‚à‚n‚j‚ÅA©•ª‚ª0.5Œ¸‚é
			Battle_Std.GRD_AddValue( { val= -5000, boundplus=1 } );

			Battle_Std.PP_AddFlag( def_PP_GRDAction_UseGRD, def_PP_GAU_Used ); // Œ¸‚ç‚µ‚½ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ’Ç‰Á
			Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_PaySkillCost ); // ƒRƒXƒgÁ”ïÏ‚İ@¦‚±‚Ìˆ—‚Å‚Í–¢g—p‚¾‚ª“ˆê‚Ì‚½‚ß’Ç‰Á
		}
		else if( grd_st&def_PP_GAU_NormalUse )
		{
			// ‚P‚ÂŒ¸‚Á‚ÄA‚P‚Â–³‚¢‚Æ‘Šè‚ª‚P‚Â‘‚¦‚é
			Battle_Std.UseGRDStock( 1, 10000 ); // Á”ïGRD”, ‘Šè‘‰ÁGRD’l(’PˆÊˆá’ˆÓ)

			Battle_Std.PP_AddFlag( def_PP_GRDAction_UseGRD, def_PP_GAU_Used ); // Œ¸‚ç‚µ‚½ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğ’Ç‰Á
			Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_PaySkillCost ); // ƒRƒXƒgÁ”ïÏ‚İ@¦‚±‚Ìˆ—‚Å‚Í–¢g—p‚¾‚ª“ˆê‚Ì‚½‚ß’Ç‰Á
		}
	}
}

local grd_action_update = function() : (grd_action_useGRD)
{
	local grd_st = BMvTbl.GetPP( def_PP_GRDAction_UseGRD );
	if( !(grd_st&def_PP_GAU_Used) && !(grd_st&def_PP_GAU_NoUse) && !(grd_st&def_PP_GAU_NoUpdateUse) )
	{
		local mvs = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( mvs.MvCount == 4 && mvs.CallCount==0 )
		{
			grd_action_useGRD();
		}
	}
}

local grd_action_lastupdate = function() : (grd_action_useGRD)
{
	local grd_st = BMvTbl.GetPP( def_PP_GRDAction_UseGRD );
	// Á”ïÏ‚İorÁ”ï‚µ‚È‚¢ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ª‚ ‚Á‚½‚ç‚â‚ç‚È‚¢
	if( !(grd_st&def_PP_GAU_Used) && !(grd_st&def_PP_GAU_NoUse) )
	{
		// œŠOğŒ
		if( Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.CheckFlag( 1, def_MC1_DoujiEndYoyaku ) && !Battle_Std.CheckPlayerisBound() )
		{
			_dp("\n “¯‰Ÿ‚µ‚ÅˆÚ‚Á‚½s“®•‚â‚ç‚ê‚¶‚á‚È‚¢‚Ì‚Åˆ—‚µ‚È‚¢");
		}
		else
		{
			grd_action_useGRD(); // GRDÁ”ïˆ—
		}
	}
}

// B+C

{
	function Init_Std() : (grd_action_init)
	{
		Battle_Std.InitGroundAtk( { type="FF" } ); //’ÊíUŒ‚‹¤’ÊInit
		grd_action_init(); // GRDƒAƒNƒVƒ‡ƒ“‹¤’ÊInit

		//’Êí‹Z‚Æ•KE‹Z‚ÌŠÔ‚Ìq‚Å‚¢‚ë‚¢‚ë“s‡‚ªˆ«‚¢‚Ì‚ÅƒRƒ“ƒ{ƒŠƒ~ƒbƒg‚Í—¼•û—§‚Â

		BMvTbl.SetComboLimit(DEF_BS_CL_ATKCBANDC);
		BMvTbl.SetPattern("B+C");
		
		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Mv_Atk_StdBandC_Init" });		
		
	}

	}

	{
		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Mv_Atk_StdBandC_FU" });
		
		grd_action_update();
	}	
	function Finalize() //

		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}

	{
		grd_action_lastupdate();
	}
}

//‹ó’†“Š‚°
Std_MoveTable.Mv_Throw_A <- 
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetPattern("Throw_A");
		BMvTbl.ClearHitStatus();

		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_Throw ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(“Š‚°)‚ğİ’è
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 7, def_MC7_InActive );
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_SysAtk ); // ƒVƒXƒeƒ€“I‚ÈUŒ‚
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
		
		// ƒRƒ“ƒ{ƒŒ[ƒg‰ÁZ
		// ’nã”Å‚É‚Í‚È‚¢B’nã”Å‚É‚Â‚¯‚é‚Æ˜AƒLƒƒƒ“‚Ì“–‚Ä“Š‚°‚ªˆÀ‚­‚È‚é
		BMvTbl.AddComboRate(); // ƒRƒ“ƒ{ƒŒ[ƒg‰ÁZ
		
		// ‹ó’†•‚—V‚µ‚È‚¢‚æ‚¤‚ÉƒxƒNƒgƒ‹İ’è
		local vec = BMvTbl.GetVector( { flags=_Vector_Normal } );
		if( vec.addy < 190 )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetVector( { addy=190, flags=_Vector_Normal } );
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Mv_Throw_A_FU" });
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		if( s.isLanding )
		{
			BMvTbl.SetFinalize(512); //’…’n‚µ‚½‚çLand‚Ö
		}		
	}
	function HitInterrupt_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.SetThrowHitFinalize(256); // ƒqƒbƒg•ªŠòƒtƒ@ƒCƒiƒ‰ƒCƒY
	}	
	function Finalize() // 
	{
		Battle_Std.SwitchNextMoveTable("Mv_Neutral", [256,"Mv_Throw_A_Hit"],[512,"Mv_Throw_A_Land"] ); //ƒfƒtƒH,[code,mv]...
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Throw_A_Land <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.InitCharaVector();
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 2, def_MC2_SysAtk ); // ƒVƒXƒeƒ€“I‚ÈUŒ‚
		Battle_Std.MoveCodeEx.AddFlag( 4, def_MC4_EnablePanishAnnounce ); //ƒpƒjƒbƒVƒ…‚ªo‚és“®
	}
	function Finalize()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}
}

Std_MoveTable.Mv_Throw_A_Hit <- 
{
	function Init_Std() // 
	{
		
		//‹t“Š‚°‚¾‚Á‚½‚ç‚±‚±‚Å”½“]
		if( BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_TechReverse) == 1 ) BMvTbl.SetMuki(_Direction_Reverse);	//‹t“Š‚°‚È‚ñ‚Å”½“]		
		Battle_Std.InitVector(); //Šµ«íœ		

		//”½“]Œã‚Éƒpƒ^[ƒ“İ’è
		BMvTbl.SetPattern("Throw_A_Hit");
		
		Battle_Std.PassHitMoveCodes(); // ƒqƒbƒg•ªŠò‚Å‚ÌMvCodeˆøŒp‚¬
		
		Battle_Std.MoveCode.AddFlag( def_MC_Throw ); // s“®ƒR[ƒh(“Š‚°)‚ğİ’è
		BMvTbl.SetMvAction( def_MVA_Throw );
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		Battle_Std.TypeSE_Play({ type="Mv_Throw_A_Hit_FU" });
	}
	function Finalize() //

		BMvTbl.SetNextMoveTable( "Mv_Neutral" );
	}		
}

// “¯‰Ÿ‚µƒ_ƒbƒVƒ…‚Å¶¬‚³‚ê‚é
// 
Std_MoveTable.Mv_DoujiDash_F_HoldCheckObject <-
{
	function Init_Std()
	{
		BMvEff.SetObjectFlags( { flags=_ObjFlags_NoRender|_ObjFlags_NoGround|_ObjFlags_ParentMove } );
		BMvEff.SetExist( { level = _Exist_NoHantei } );
		BMvTbl.SetPosition( {y=1024, flags=_Position_CaptureShift } ); //‰B‚·
		
		BMvTbl.SetLP(0,0); // ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğ—£‚µ‚½‚©‚Ç‚¤‚©
		BMvTbl.SetLP(1,0); // ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğ—£‚µ‚½‚ ‚Æ‰½ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€Œo‰ß‚µ‚½‚©B‚±‚ê‚ª2‚É‚È‚é‚ÆƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg‚ªÁ–Å‚·‚éBilastdelay‘Îôj
		// 6+A+B ¨ 1F‚Åƒjƒ…[ƒgƒ‰ƒ‹ ¨@‚Æ‚·‚é‚ÆA‚±‚ÌƒIƒuƒWƒFƒNƒg‚ªÁ‚¦‚Ä‹Z§ŒÀ‚ªÁ‚¦‚é‚ªA6+A+B‚ÌƒL[‚Íc‚Á‚Ä‚¢‚é‚Ì‚Ålastdelay‚Åó‚¯•t‚¯‚Ä‚¢‚é‚à‚Ì‚ª‚ ‚é‚Æo‚Ä‚½
	}
	function Update_Std()
	{
		if( BMvTbl.GetLP(0) == 1 ) // ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğ—£‚µ‚½
		{
			local frame = BMvTbl.AddLP(1,1); // Œo‰ß‚µ‚½ƒtƒŒ[ƒ€‚ğ‹L‰¯
			//_dp("\n frame:"+frame);
			if( frame >= 2+2 ) // lastdelay‚ª2F‘‚¦‚½‚Ì‚Å‘Î‰
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0);
			}
		}
	}
	function FrameUpdate_Std()
	{
		local s = BMvTbl.GetMvStatus();
		
		local player = BMvCore.GetPlayerCharaData(); // ‘€ìe‚Ìî•ñ‚ğ“¾‚é
		if( player.push() )
		{
			local button_release = (BMvTbl.CheckButtonHold( (1<<0)|(1<<1) ) == 0)? 1 : 0;
			player.pop();

			if( s.MvCount>6+2 ) // lastdelay‚ª2F‘‚¦‚½‚Ì‚Å‘Î‰
			{
				BMvTbl.SetFinalize(0); //’·‚·‚¬
			}
			if( button_release )
			{
				BMvTbl.SetLP(0,1); // ƒ{ƒ^ƒ“‚ğ—£‚µ‚½‚±‚Æ‚ğ‹L‰¯
			}
		}
	}
	function Finalize_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetDeleteMoveTable();
	}
	function LastUpdate_Std()
	{
		BMvTbl.SetPP(def_PP_CmdStatus, BMvTbl.GetPP(def_PP_CmdStatus)&~def_PP_CS_DoujiDash_F ); //ƒtƒ‰ƒO‚ğÁ‚·
	}
}
